### [1] [Item Representations Matter: Bridging Hard Semantic IDs and Soft Prototype Mixtures for Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05799)
*Vicente Balmaseda,Dongqi Fu,Jiarui Feng,Ren chen,Hanqing Zeng,Tianxin Wei,Yinglong Xia,Tianbao Yang,Hong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CAP-Mix 是一种流式推荐方法，将物品表示为共享原型上的软混合加物品专属向量，在保留连续表示的同时引入共享结构，避免离散量化瓶颈，并在多个基准上提升 HR@5/NDCG@5 且推理吞吐更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索存在两类权衡：Semantic IDs 等离散码共享参数并带预训练语义结构，但硬离散化会丢失原始内容嵌入的信息；连续模型无量化损失，却多用扁平物品查找表，缺乏跨物品的显式参数共享。作者希望让连续推荐器获得共享结构，同时不引入离散瓶颈。

Method: 提出 CAP-Mix：每个物品表示为若干共享原型的软混合（soft assignment）再加一个物品专属向量；分配权重、原型与物品向量联合训练，且推理时保留软表示。预训练信息可选：SID 责任度可作为分配先验，完整内容嵌入可初始化物品专属向量而不改变初始扁平表结构。模型基于 flow recommender。

Result: 三个数据集的匹配对比中：无预训练信息时，在稀疏 Amazon 数据集上优于扁平表，Steam 上有小幅增益；仅给定离线 SID 时，相比同一 tokenizer 构建的硬表 HR@5 提升 4.45–14.90%；给定完整内容嵌入时，与强扁平表持平或更优，而硬分配在稀疏 Amazon 上退化。六个基准平均，相对各数据集各指标的最佳基线，HR@5 与 NDCG@5 分别相对提升 6.9% 和 13.6%，推理吞吐为离散基线的 1.4–161 倍。

Conclusion: 软混合式连续表示可在不引入离散瓶颈的前提下实现跨物品参数共享，兼具精度提升与显著推理效率优势，且能灵活兼容 SID 先验与内容嵌入等预训练信号。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems rely on generative retrieval techniques to predict the next item either as discrete codes or as a continuous vector. On the one hand, Semantic IDs (SIDs) give discrete models shared parameters and pretrained semantic structure, but discretizing items into hard codes discards some information from the original content embeddings. On the other hand, continuous models avoid quantization, yet typically use flat item lookup tables with no explicit parameter tying across items. We ask whether a continuous recommender can introduce shared structure without making it a discrete bottleneck. We propose CAP-Mix, a flow recommender that represents each item as a soft mixture of shared prototypes plus an item-specific vector. The assignments, prototypes, and item vectors are trained jointly, and the soft representation is retained at inference. Pretrained information is optional: SID responsibilities can serve as a prior over assignments, while a full content embedding can initialize the item-specific vector without changing the initial flat table. In matched comparisons on three datasets, CAP-Mix improves over a flat table on the two sparse Amazon datasets and yields a small gain on Steam when trained without pretrained information. Given only an offline SID, it outperforms a hard table built from the same tokenizer by 4.45-14.90% in HR@5. Given the full content embedding, it matches or improves a strong flat table while hard assignments regress on the sparse Amazon datasets. Overall, averaged across six benchmarks, CAP-Mix improves HR@5 and NDCG@5 by 6.9% and 13.6% relative to the best evaluated baseline for each dataset and metric, while delivering 1.4-161x the inference throughput of the discrete baselines in our experiments.

</details>


### [2] [SciToT: Multimodal Tip-of-the-Tongue Retrieval for Scientific Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05829)
*Stan Fris,Sijie Tao,Makoto P. Kato,Maarten de Rijke*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向科学文档的多模态"舌尖现象"(ToT)检索基准，包含108,512篇开源科学文档与11,499条合成查询，并给出可扩展的查询生成流程与多种检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者常记得论文内容的某些方面却无法回忆标题或作者等可靠标识，这种ToT式再查找任务在科学文档中尤为困难，因为记忆线索可能同时涉及文本信息与图表等视觉元素，而现有ToT检索基准均未覆盖科学搜索场景。

Method: 构建跨5个领域的108,512篇开源科学文档语料；提出可扩展的查询引出(elicitation)流程，从源文档中独立抽取文本与视觉记忆线索，并利用大语言模型生成类似人类不完整记忆的查询；通过对公开再查找请求的实证分析刻画科学ToT信息需求特征，并用人工标注与自动化质量控制评估生成查询；提供词法、稠密、晚期交互及视觉 grounding 四类检索基线。

Result: 建成含11,499条合成查询的多模态科学ToT检索基准，验证了生成查询与真实再查找需求的一致性，并给出多类检索基线的性能参照；数据集、文档表示、记忆线索、评测划分及生成与检索流程均以MIT协议开源。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了科学领域多模态ToT检索基准的空白，为科学论文的多模态再查找研究提供了可复现的数据与工具基础。

Abstract: Researchers frequently encounter difficulties re-finding scientific papers when they remember aspects of the content but cannot recall reliable identifiers, such as titles or author names. This type of re-finding task, known as tip-of-the-tongue (ToT) retrieval, is particularly challenging for scientific documents, where recollections may involve textual information and visual elements, including figures, diagrams, and plots. Existing ToT retrieval benchmarks do not address scientific search.
  We introduce a multimodal benchmark for scientific ToT retrieval, comprising 108,512 open-access scientific documents across five domains and 11,499 synthetically generated queries. To construct the benchmark, we propose a scalable query elicitation pipeline that independently extracts textual and visual memory cues from source documents and uses large language models to generate queries resembling incomplete human recollections. We characterize scientific ToT information needs through an empirical analysis of publicly available re-finding requests and use the outcomes to assess the generated queries using human annotations and automated quality control. The benchmark includes lexical, dense, late-interaction, and visually grounded retrieval baselines. We release the dataset, extracted document representations, generated memory cues, evaluation splits, and accompanying generation and retrieval pipelines under an MIT license to facilitate research on multimodal scientific paper re-finding.

</details>


### [3] [Weaver: Weaving Preference Evidence through Incremental Reasoning for Personalized Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05900)
*Linyao Zheng,Zhifang Mao,Xuhang Shi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Weaver框架，通过双层检索推理图将意图依赖与记忆条件检索轨迹耦合，实现个性化RAG中偏好证据的增量整合与情境化推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化检索增强生成需从长交互历史中提取并应用用户偏好，但偏好证据分散于过往对话且绑定特定时间与情境：孤立片段难以完整支撑回答，不同条件下的偏好又易被混淆。

Method: 构建双层检索与推理图，上层为意图依赖图、下层为记忆条件检索轨迹图。包含三个组件：依赖信息缺口追踪（记录回答当前请求所需信息及前置依赖）、记忆条件增量检索（利用已确立前提与线索定位缺失证据并记录检索轨迹）、范围感知组合推理（识别互补与替代支撑、区分同一时空情境内的冲突主张），结论迭代更新任务前提与剩余信息缺口以引导后续检索。

Result: 在个性化RAG基准（尤以PersonaMem为主）上，Weaver在证据完整性与答案质量两方面均优于领先基线。

Conclusion: 通过将检索轨迹与组合式偏好推理耦合、并以信息缺口驱动的增量检索闭环，Weaver能建立连贯的上下文依据，有效提升个性化响应的证据完整度与生成质量。

Abstract: Personalized retrieval-augmented generation requires extracting and applying user preferences from extended interaction histories. This task is challenging because preference evidence is scattered across past conversations and remains bound to specific temporal and situational contexts. Consequently, isolated fragments rarely provide complete support for a response, while preferences expressed under different conditions are easily conflated. To address these limitations, we propose Weaver, a framework that incrementally weaves memory-conditioned retrieval trajectories with compositional preference reasoning for personalized responses. Weaver operates on a Dual-Layer Retrieval and Reasoning Graph that couples an upper Intent Dependency Graph with a lower Memory-Conditioned Retrieval Trajectory Graph. Dependency Information Gap Tracking represents the information needed to answer the current request and its prerequisite dependencies in the upper graph. Memory-Conditioned Incremental Retrieval uses established premises and clues from retrieved conversations to locate missing evidence, recording the resulting retrieval trajectories in the lower graph. Scope-Aware Compositional Reasoning jointly interprets the accumulated evidence to determine which preferences apply to the request. It identifies complementary and alternative support and distinguishes conflicting claims within the same temporal and situational context. These conclusions iteratively update task premises and remaining information gaps, guiding subsequent retrieval to establish a coherent contextual basis for the final response. Experiments on personalized RAG benchmarks, particularly PersonaMem, demonstrate that Weaver outperforms leading baselines in both evidence completeness and answer quality.

</details>


### [4] [Optimal Re-Ranking Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05908)
*Siqing Huo,Andrew Parry,Debasis Ganguly,Charles L. A. Clarke*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文质疑固定重排深度的惯例，利用LLM相关性判断证明查询级最优重排深度存在且可预测（IR-DCG@10），可在保持效果的同时将平均重排深度缩减最多3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第二阶段神经重排器通常采用固定重排深度，隐含"效果随深度饱和"的假设；已有研究指出深度增益非线性且最优深度因查询而异，但人工标注规模限制了此类研究。

Method: 借助LLM生成的大规模稠密相关性判断，在典型重排流水线上逐查询确定最优重排深度；将重排深度建模为查询级属性，并检验能否由第一阶段检索特征先验预测，比较多种标准QPP方法与基于LLM评估第一阶段排序质量的IR-DCG@10预测器。

Result: 许多查询存在明确的最优重排深度，超过后效果停滞或下降；多数标准QPP方法对此任务无效；IR-DCG@10可在保持整体效果的同时将平均重排深度最多缩减3倍；在MSMARCO DEV上，oracle选择最优深度可使效果提升超7%并将平均深度缩减5倍。

Conclusion: 重排深度是查询级可预测属性，LLM自动判断可支撑此前人工标注不可行的研究；作者公开相关性判断、代码库与实验产物以促进可复现研究与后续工作。

Abstract: Second-stage neural rankers are commonly applied with a fixed re-ranking depth, assuming that retrieval effectiveness saturates as depth increases. Prior research has questioned the assumption that increasing the re-ranking depth yields linear performance gains, further suggesting that optimal re-ranking depth varies considerably from query to query. In the past, studying such phenomena was methodologically difficult given the scale of manual annotation required. With the advent of LLM-based relevance judgments we can now more easily undertake such studies, in this case to pinpoint the optimal re-ranking depth on a per-query basis. Using dense LLM-based relevance judgments over a typical re-ranking pipeline, we show that many queries exhibit a well-defined optimal re-ranking depth, beyond which effectiveness stagnates or degrades. We formulate re-ranking depth as a query-specific property and study whether it can be predicted a priori from first-stage retrieval characteristics. Through a large-scale analysis, we find that most standard query performance prediction (QPP) methods are ineffective for this task. In contrast, a predictor derived from LLM-assessed first-stage ranking quality, which we term IR-DCG@10, can reduce the average re-ranking depth by up to a factor of 3 while preserving overall effectiveness, depending on the specific first- and second-stage rankers used. Under oracle selection of optimal depths, we further show that retrieval effectiveness can improve by more than 7% while reducing the average re-ranking depth by a factor of 5 on the MSMARCO DEV collection. Given our promising preliminary findings, we would encourage the use of automatic judgments to facilitate research otherwise infeasible under manual annotation. Towards this point, we release relevance judgments, our codebase, and experimental artefacts to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [5] [One Basket, Many Experts: Decorrelated Re-ranking for Financial Asset Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05952)
*Ryuki Unno,Keigo Sakurai,Koshi Watanabe,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Decorrelated-Satellite Expert Re-ranking (DSER)：保留专家推荐列表中最相关的部分，用收益去相关资产替换其余槽位，以单一整数m显式控制相关性-绩效权衡，在FAR-Trans上实现小幅相关性损失换取风险调整收益提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融资产推荐通常只优化"相关性"（推荐是否匹配用户购买行为），但服务商价值还取决于组合绩效（风险调整收益），后者影响用户参与度与佣金收入。两目标难以兼得：最匹配用户行为的推荐集中于少数高相关性的热门资产，从而限制了风险调整收益上限。

Method: DSER重排序框架：从"专家跟随"列表中保留最相关的前若干推荐，将剩余m个槽位替换为与已选资产收益去相关的候选资产；m为服务商设定的整数旋钮，使权衡成为可审计的显式选择而非隐于模型内部的属性。在公开零售券商数据集FAR-Trans上，采用主要协议并考虑真实交易成本进行实验。

Result: 替换少量槽位可在较小的相关性代价下提升风险调整收益，且在典型零售组合规模下该增益在计入交易成本后依然成立。最小化相关性虽可降低组合波动，但数据中相关性最低的资产本身波动更高、收益更高，其波动性抵消了波动的下降——增益源于收益而非波动降低。权衡呈非对称性：相关性代价在各市场阶段保持一致，而收益增益依赖市场阶段。

Conclusion: 相关性-绩效权衡可通过单一整数m显式暴露并可审计，服务商可据此读取每个运营点的相关性成本；去相关卫星式替换是提升风险调整收益的可行、低成本机制。

Abstract: Financial asset recommendation for retail investors is typically optimized for relevance, that is, how well a recommendation matches what each user will buy. From the service provider's point of view, the value of a recommendation also depends on portfolio performance: the risk-adjusted return the recommended assets (the basket) deliver, which drives user engagement and fee revenue. The two goals are rarely achieved jointly, because the recommendations that best match user behavior concentrate on a few popular assets whose returns are highly correlated, which caps the risk-adjusted return a provider can offer. We propose Decorrelated-Satellite Expert Re-ranking (\textbf{DSER}), which retains the most relevant recommendations from an expert-following list and replaces the rest with return-decorrelated alternatives. The number of replaced slots is a single integer~$m$ (a knob) that the provider sets, so the relevance--performance trade-off becomes an explicit, auditable choice rather than a property hidden inside a learned model. In experiments on the public FAR-Trans retail-brokerage dataset, under the primary protocol, replacing a few slots improves risk-adjusted return at a small relevance cost, and the gain survives realistic trading costs at typical retail portfolio sizes. Minimizing correlation would lower basket volatility, but in our data the least-correlated assets are more volatile and higher-return, so their volatility offsets that reduction while their return drives the gain, not lower volatility. The resulting trade-off is asymmetric, with the relevance cost consistent across market phases while the gain is phase-dependent. The provider sets $m$ to read off exactly how much relevance each operating point costs.

</details>


### [6] [Do Search-Augmented Recommenders Use the Latest Search Episode?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06044)
*Jiandong Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 FocalSwap 审计方法，通过"中性掩码"与"匹配观测搜索替换"两类对照替换最新搜索槽或全部搜索输入，检验搜索增强推荐中搜索信号的贡献；发现全源贡献的有符号估计高度依赖对照选择，且"最新搜索槽"的正贡献在 SESRec 与 UniSAR 上均未确立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索增强推荐器同时使用最新搜索会话与更早的搜索历史，但既有工作多以掩码（masking）方式归因搜索输入贡献，缺少对"贡献估计是否依赖对照设计"的系统检验，可能将掩码伪影误判为真实搜索收益。

Method: 提出 FocalSwap：对每次推荐，在固定目标物品、候选集、搜索前历史与打分器的条件下，仅替换最新搜索槽或全部搜索输入；对比中性掩码与类别/画像匹配的观测搜索序列替换，记录目标物品排名变化、相对各对照的 RR 增益及对真实焦点搜索的偏好。用植入信号与 GenSAR 当前查询任务验证审计有效性；在 KuaiSAR、Qilin 数据集及 SESRec、UniSAR 三套独立训练检查点上做行为与词法证据评估，并对每个检查点做五种替代捐赠者分配以检验稳健性。

Result: 同一 66,139 行数据上，全部搜索输入替换为中性 ID 得到正的有符号贡献，而匹配观测搜索序列替换的估计更小，其中一个检查点区间下界低于零；配对 RR 差距为 +2.770e-3 至 +6.604e-3，所有校正区间均在零以上。中性掩码导致 59.7%–61.7% 目标排名变化，匹配替换为 44.1%–44.8%；对照差异在五种捐赠者分配下持续存在。在主要的中性掩码与类别匹配对照下，SESRec 与 UniSAR 均未能确立最新搜索槽的正贡献。

Conclusion: 将搜索收益归因于更广的搜索状态或最新搜索会话时，掩码证据必须与观测搜索替换对照相结合；否则贡献估计的有符号性与量级会因对照设计而系统性偏移。

Abstract: Search-augmented recommenders can use the latest search episode and earlier searches. We introduce FocalSwap to test how these inputs contribute to the next recommendation. For each recommendation, it changes the latest search slot or all search inputs while holding the target, candidates, pre-search history, and scorer fixed. It compares neutral masks with matched observed-search replacements, recording rank changes, RR gains relative to each control, and preference for the true focal search. A planted signal and GenSAR's current-query task confirm that the audit detects known input dependence. We evaluate behavioral and lexical evidence on KuaiSAR and Qilin, and recommendation checkpoints trained using the released SESRec and UniSAR implementations.
  Across three independently trained UniSAR checkpoints, the estimated all-source benefit depends on the control. On the same 66,139 rows, replacing all search inputs with a neutral ID yields a positive signed contribution, whereas replacing them with category/profile-matched observed search sequences gives smaller estimates, with an interval below zero for one checkpoint. The paired RR gaps range from $+2.770 \times 10^{-3}$ to $+6.604 \times 10^{-3}$, and every adjusted interval is above zero. Neutral masks change 59.7-61.7% of target ranks, while matched replacements change 44.1-44.8%. The control contrast persists across five alternative donor assignments per checkpoint. Under the primary neutral-mask and category-matched controls, positive latest-slot contribution is not established in SESRec or UniSAR. These results show why masking evidence should be compared with observed-search replacements when attributing a benefit to broader search state or the latest episode.

</details>


### [7] [Generative Retrieval under Real-Time Supply Constraints in Local Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06323)
*Shaopeng Liang,Biao Zhang,Hongzhen Ye,Zeyang Hou,Ma Jiang,Jintao Tu,yunxi. hz,Zhijia Fang,Jianqiang Cao,Junxue Rao,Jia Jia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RTP-LLM流水线，将请求时供给快照中合法的残差量化标识编译为压缩稀疏行前缀索引，与模型prefill重叠执行并跨beam共享，实现带约束的生成式检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地零售与外卖场景中，候选不仅需语义匹配，还须在该用户该时刻可服务；位置、天气、交通会改变供给，目录有效标识对当前请求可能不可行，而事后过滤已消耗beam容量与推理算力，短输出序列又难以摊销在线约束准备开销。

Method: 在显式物化的请求时供给快照上做检索；将合格RQ标识编译为CSR前缀索引，CPU编译与model prefill重叠，上传索引在beam间共享；合法扩展保留原模型log概率，约束状态随所选beam父节点传递；形式化"快照相对标识有效性"并与实体级资格、新鲜度区分；实现三token商户标识生成。

Result: 匹配beam的日约束案例中被过滤商户由299中的170降至299中的29；残差距离过滤说明请求级约束的必要性；异构主机下3万条目比较报告服务端延迟增量0.6 ms，客户端测量显示额外请求开销；三类地理设置下的捕获请求刻画了不同供给规模与输出宽度下的行为。

Conclusion: 提供了可行实现并识别瓶颈，但未能在匹配检索质量下证明计算节省；证据跨越不同开发阶段与测量边界。

Abstract: Generative retrieval predicts semantic identifiers for candidate entities. In local retail and food delivery, candidates must also be serviceable for a particular user at a particular time. Location, weather, and traffic reshape merchant and product supply, so a catalog-valid identifier may be infeasible for the current request. Post-generation filtering removes such candidates only after beam capacity and inference computation have been spent. We study retrieval over an explicitly materialized, request-time supply snapshot, where short output sequences leave little opportunity to amortize online constraint preparation. Our RTP-LLM pipeline compiles eligible residual-quantization identifiers into a compressed sparse row prefix index, overlaps CPU compilation with model prefill, and shares the uploaded index across beams. Legal expansions retain original-model log probabilities, and constraint states follow selected beam parents. We formalize snapshot-relative identifier validity and distinguish it from entity-level eligibility and freshness. The implementation generates three-token merchant identifiers. Historical evidence covers daily catalog constraints and subsequent request-scoped serving evaluations. In one matched-beam daily-constraint case, filtered merchants decrease from 170 of 299 candidates to 29 of 299; residual distance filtering motivates request-specific constraints. A different-host comparison with 30,000 supplied entries reports a 0.6 ms service-side latency increment, while client measurements expose additional request costs. Captured requests from three geographic settings characterize behavior across supply sizes and output widths. These observations span development stages and measurement boundaries. They establish a practical implementation and identify bottlenecks, but do not establish compute savings at matched retrieval quality.

</details>


### [8] [PMRecBench: A Benchmark for Personalization in Recommendation Agents under Multimodal Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06349)
*Xiaoyu Zhang,Yougang Lyu,Bin Wu,Sahan Bulathwela,Emine Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出首个面向多模态查询的个性化推荐基准PMRecBench（510条图文查询配对真实用户历史），揭示推荐智能体存在"个性化失衡"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有购物与娱乐平台的推荐智能体在多模态查询下需同时满足用户视觉化表达的需求与由历史行为推断的偏好，但已有基准存在两点不足：仅使用纯文本查询；多模态基准只评估查询匹配能力，不基于真实用户历史评估个性化。

Method: 构建PMRecBench：覆盖服装与电影两领域，包含510条经质量控制、与对应用户真实历史配对的图文查询。两项关键设计：(1) 查询源自真实用户记录中一致的行为模式，参考图像与目标物品与真实交互关联；(2) 构造具有诊断性的候选池——包含用户高评分但不满足需求物品，以及满足需求但从未被高评分的物品，分别检验需求理解与偏好遵循能力。

Result: 诊断池实验显示推荐智能体存在个性化失衡：相对仅使用个性化信号，加入多模态查询虽提升整体排序表现（需求信息增益），却削弱了偏好遵循；进一步分析表明该下降与图像解读有关——智能体可能优先依据与参考图像的相似度而非用户历史证据；仅更换更强的商用基座模型也无法消除该失衡。

Conclusion: 个性化失衡揭示了推荐智能体的核心挑战：在响应多模态查询时，需在提升排序性能的同时保持对用户偏好的遵循。

Abstract: On Web platforms for shopping and entertainment, recommendation agents under multimodal queries aim to deliver personalized recommendations that satisfy users' visually specified requirements and reflect preferences inferred from user histories. However, existing personalized recommendation benchmarks use text-only queries, while multimodal ones assess query matching without evaluating personalization based on real user histories. We introduce the Personalized Multimodal Recommendation Benchmark (PMRecBench), the first benchmark to evaluate the personalization of recommendation agents under multimodal queries. It pairs 510 quality-controlled image-text queries across clothing and movies with the corresponding users' real histories. PMRecBench features two key designs: (1) queries derived from consistent behavioral patterns in real user records, with reference images and targets linked to actual interactions; and (2) challenging diagnostic pools with candidate items rated highly by the user but violating requirements, and items satisfying requirements but never rated highly, testing requirement understanding and preference following, respectively. Extensive experiments on diagnostic pools reveal a personalization imbalance in recommendation agents: relative to personalization signals alone, adding multimodal queries weakens preference following, even though the additional information about query requirements improves overall ranking performance. Further analysis links this decline in preference following to image interpretation, where agents can prioritize resemblance to reference images over evidence from user histories, and stronger commercial backbones alone fail to resolve this imbalance. This personalization imbalance highlights a central challenge for recommendation agents: improving ranking performance while preserving preference following when responding to multimodal user queries

</details>


### [9] [Deliberate Latent Reasoning for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06521)
*Haobo Zhang,Qiannan Zhu,Yutao Zhu,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出DESRec：一种个性化潜在推理框架，通过推理步数选择、自蒸馏压缩和渐进偏好抽取，为不同用户自适应确定推理深度，提升序列推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的序列推荐通过自回归生成潜在推理向量来精炼用户表示，但对所有用户使用固定推理步数（"一刀切"策略），导致简单历史过度推理、复杂历史推理不足，表示学习次优。

Method: 1) 推理步选择模块：训练阶段为每个用户序列识别最优推理步数；2) 自蒸馏机制：将最具信息量的推理状态压缩到最终推理步，作为推理阶段用于物品排序的最终用户表示；3) 渐进偏好抽取机制：将用户历史动态划分为可变数量的时间段，各段汇总为潜在"里程碑"表示该时期偏好，并作为后续推理输入，里程碑数量随历史长度变化。

Result: 在六个公开数据集和四种骨干推荐模型上的大量实验表明，DESRec优于八个基线模型。

Conclusion: 个性化潜在推理（自适应推理深度+渐进偏好抽取）可有效提升序列推荐中的用户建模质量，验证了推理深度个性化的重要性。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders are widely applied in real-world scenarios to model user preferences and behavior patterns based on user interactions. Recent advances in sequential recommendation enhance transformer-based models with latent reasoning to refine user representations by autoregressively generating intermediate reasoning vectors. However, exploring the optimal reasoning depth for diverse user histories remains challenging: existing methods typically employ a fixed number of reasoning steps for all users. This ``one-size-fits-all'' strategy often leads to suboptimal representation learning due to over-thinking in simple histories or under-thinking in complex ones. To address this issue, we propose DESRec, a deliberate latent reasoning framework that performs personalized latent reasoning to learn more informative reasoning states and enhance user modeling for sequential recommendation. Specifically, it designs a reasoning step selection module that identifies the optimal reasoning step for each user sequence during the training phase. Through a self-distillation mechanism, it compresses this most informative state into the final reasoning step, which serves as the final user representation for item ranking during inference. Moreover, it introduces a progressive preference extraction mechanism for personalized reasoning, which dynamically segments a user's history into a variable number of periods and summarizes each into a latent milestone representing the user's preference for that period. It will be used as the input of the subsequent reasoning. The number of milestones varies with the users' history, enabling a personalized reasoning over users' evolving interests. Extensive experimental results on six public datasets and four backbone recommenders demonstrate the superiority of DESRec over eight baseline models.

</details>


### [10] [WISE: An Agentic Framework for Whole-page Multimodal Search Experience Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06777)
*Wenyi Xu,Renzhe Zhou,Songyang Li,Feiran Zhu,Ruoqi Zhao,Chenglei Dai,Xuanping Li,Kaiqiao Zhan,Jingwei Zhuo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出新任务“系统质量诊断”（SQD），并给出面向工业搜索的智能体诊断框架WISE，配套ReTrace轨迹监督与AHPO优化方法，以及真实场景基准WISE-BENCH，实现从发现用户可见问题到定位底层算法组件根因的端到端诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法驱动的搜索、推荐、广告服务依赖多阶段模型流水线。现有研究侧重单阶段模型优化，上线后监控只关注运行可靠性，导致“运行健康但结果质量差”的系统难以被发现与归因，缺少系统化的问题定义与评测基准。

Method: 形式化定义SQD任务，将质量评估从识别坏结果扩展到跨组件因果归因；提出智能体框架WISE，端到端发现用户侧问题并追溯至底层算法组件；引入ReTrace构建证据一致性的轨迹监督，并提出Adaptive Harness-Policy Optimization（AHPO）基于失败信号联合优化智能体外壳与模型策略；构建WISE-BENCH多模态搜索基准，融合用户侧证据、内部流水线轨迹与专家诊断标注。

Result: 多模型规模下均取得稳定提升，Qwen3.5-35B-A3B上端到端诊断准确率达75.20%；在生产的质量审计、在线质量保障与模型优化场景中可降低诊断成本，并提升下游相关性模型效果。

Conclusion: SQD将质量评估与根因归因统一，WISE及其配套监督、优化方法和基准为算法服务的质量诊断提供了可系统评测的方案，并在工业部署中验证了实用价值。

Abstract: Algorithm-driven services, including search, recommendation, and advertising, rely on multi-stage model pipelines. While research prioritizes model optimization, post-deployment monitoring focuses on operational reliability. Yet operationally healthy systems may still produce poor-quality results, exposing a gap in existing evaluation and monitoring practices. However, a systematic problem formulation and benchmark for this task remain lacking. To bridge this gap, we formulate System Quality Diagnosis (SQD) for algorithm-driven services, a new task that extends quality evaluation beyond identifying poor outcomes to attributing their causes across system components. Focusing on industrial search, we propose WISE, an agentic framework for end-to-end quality diagnosis that discovers user-facing issues and traces their origins to underlying algorithmic components. To improve diagnostic reliability, we introduce ReTrace for constructing evidence-consistent trajectory supervision and Adaptive Harness-Policy Optimization (AHPO) for failure-guided optimization of the agent harness and model policy. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct WISE-BENCH, a real-world multimodal search benchmark that combines user-facing evidence, internal pipeline traces, and expert diagnosis annotations. Extensive experiments show consistent gains across model scales, with WISE achieving 75.20% end-to-end accuracy on Qwen3.5-35B-A3B. In production, WISE supports quality auditing, online quality assurance, and model optimization, reducing diagnosis costs and improving downstream relevance models.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [11] [Constraint-Preserving Flow Matching for Spectral Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05436)
*Anita Yang,Ken-ichi Kawarabayashi,Yohan Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LaSFM：在Stiefel流形上做流匹配，同时约束Laplacian零空间、特征向量正交性与正特征值，实现谱图生成及性质引导生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱图生成通过Laplacian的特征值与特征向量表征图，但其特征系统须保持Laplacian的结构性质。现有方法只保留特征向量正交归一性，未施加Laplacian零空间约束（即特征向量与全1向量正交），可能生成与Laplacian结构不一致的特征系统。

Method: 提出Laplacian-Constrained Stiefel Flow Matching (LaSFM)：在生成过程中同时保持零空间条件、特征向量正交归一性与正特征值；刻画了相应的约束Stiefel流形并在其上形式化流匹配；进一步扩展至性质引导生成，在维持约束的同时将样本导向目标图性质。

Result: 实验在谱层面与图层面指标上均取得更优表现，且生成结果与目标性质的吻合度更高。

Conclusion: 显式编码Laplacian零空间约束可提升谱图生成的结构一致性与质量，并支持可控的性质引导生成。

Abstract: Spectral graph generation models graphs through the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of their Laplacians, providing a compact and continuous alternative to direct graph construction. However, these eigensystems must retain the structural properties of graph Laplacians. Existing approaches preserve eigenvector orthonormality but do not enforce the Laplacian nullspace constraint, potentially producing eigensystems inconsistent with Laplacian structure. To address this, we introduce Laplacian-Constrained Stiefel Flow Matching (LaSFM), which preserves the nullspace condition alongside eigenvector orthonormality and positive eigenvalues during generation. We characterize the underlying constrained Stiefel manifold and formulate flow matching on it. We further extend LaSFM to property-guided generation, steering samples toward desired graph properties while maintaining these constraints. Experiments demonstrate improved performance on both spectral and graph-level metrics, along with better alignment with target properties.

</details>


### [12] [Learning Implicit Solves for Dynamic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05439)
*Chawit Kaewnuratchadasorn,Jiaji Wang,Xin Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INOS将预训练的神经算子嵌入Newmark-β时间积分，把每步动力学求解转化为等效静力平衡问题，实现无需动态轨迹监督的高保真动力学仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真动力学系统仿真在科学发现与工程设计中至关重要，但计算代价高昂；现有神经求解器通常依赖动态轨迹数据监督，泛化与复用性受限。

Method: 先由静态平衡解预训练神经算子，再将其复用为Newmark-β积分中的隐式求解器：每个时间步由积分器构造等效平衡问题，神经算子推断下一步解场，随后由Newmark关系推进动态状态。

Result: 在线性与非线性弹性动力学算例中实现准确全场预测且无需动态轨迹监督；零样本评估精度优于以动态仿真训练的神经求解器基线；非线性仿真相对串行经典有限元求解器加速超过4600倍；支持跨空间与时间离散复用、动态条件变化，并完成稳定的百万步滚动。

Conclusion: INOS是一种可复用的神经隐式求解器，能够实现准确、高效且鲁棒的动力学建模。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulation of dynamical systems is central to scientific discovery and engineering design, yet remains computationally demanding. Here we introduce the Implicit Neural Operator Solver (INOS), which learns the implicit solve and encodes a time integrator. INOS pretrains a neural operator from static equilibrium solutions and reuses it within Newmark-$β$ integration. At each time increment, the integrator forms an effective equilibrium problem, the pretrained neural operator infers the next-step solution field, and the Newmark relations advance the dynamic state. Numerical examples in linear and nonlinear elastodynamics demonstrate accurate full-field predictions without dynamic trajectory supervision. Comparative results show that INOS, evaluated zero-shot, achieves higher predictive accuracy than neural solver baselines trained on dynamic simulations. In addition, INOS delivers over 4,600$\times$ speedup in nonlinear simulations over sequential classical finite-element solvers. INOS natively accommodates changes in dynamic conditions and enables reuse of the pretrained operator across spatial and temporal discretizations. Further evaluation demonstrates a stable million-step rollout. These findings establish INOS as a reusable neural implicit solver for accurate, efficient, and robust dynamic modeling.

</details>


### [13] [Matched but Not Better: An Equivalence-Tested Benchmark of Quantum Machine Learning on Six Clinical Datasets and the QML-Health-READY Reporting Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05442)
*Mohammed Siraj B,Gousia Habib,Ruban S*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 正确配置的量子分类器（QSVM、VQC）在六个临床数据集上接近但未超越调优后的经典模型；主要实用代价来自有限测量次数（而非门噪声），配置错误可逆转结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习（QML）常被宣传用于临床预测，但与经典模型的比较很少采用配对设计，也缺乏等价性检验，导致其真实增益缺乏可信证据。

Method: 在六个公开临床数据集上，将量子核SVM（QSVM，4量子比特，带宽调优）与变分量子分类器（VQC）同五个调优经典模型对比：输入统一为四分量特征，25次交叉验证划分；采用校正重采样t检验、Holm校正等价性检验（裕度±0.02与±0.05）及随机效应合并；并做消融、噪声、有限测量次数与实现缺陷分析；提出八维度报告框架QML-Health-READY。

Result: 量子模型均未显著优于任何经典对照。合并后QSVM较核匹配RBF-SVM为−0.006 AUROC（95% CI −0.017~+0.004），落在±0.02等价域内；VQC较最佳经典模型为−0.007（−0.019~+0.004）。QSVM在四/六个数据集中于±0.05内与RBF-SVM等价，但在NHANES 2017-18显著劣于最佳经典模型（−0.076）。经典平凡乘积态核与纠缠核表现相当。去极化噪声p=0.01且每核元素1,024次测量时，QSVM AUROC下降0.054（0.036~0.071）。带宽未调优时AUROC接近随机（0.48–0.59）。

Conclusion: QML的临床优势未获证据支持；其瓶颈是有限测量估计而非门噪声，配置误差可导致结论反转。QML-Health-READY为可信宣称提供报告规范。

Abstract: Background: Quantum machine learning (QML) is promoted for clinical prediction, but comparisons with classical models are rarely paired or tested for equivalence. Methods: We compared a quantum-kernel support vector machine (QSVM; 4 qubits, tuned bandwidth) and a variational quantum classifier (VQC) with five tuned classical models across six public clinical datasets. All models used identical four-component inputs and 25 cross-validation splits. Paired AUROC differences were assessed using a corrected resampled t-test, Holm-adjusted equivalence tests (margins: +/-0.02 and +/-0.05), and random-effects pooling. Ablation, noise, finite-shot, and implementation-fault analyses were conducted. We propose QML-Health-READY, an eight-domain reporting framework. Results: Neither quantum model significantly outperformed any classical comparator. Pooled across datasets, QSVM differed from the kernel-matched radial basis function (RBF) SVM by -0.006 AUROC (95% CI: -0.017 to +0.004), equivalent to +/-0.02. VQC differed from the best classical model by -0.007 (95% CI: -0.019 to +0.004). QSVM was equivalent to RBF SVM within +/-0.05 in four of six datasets but was significantly worse than the best classical model on NHANES 2017-18 (-0.076). A classically trivial product-state kernel performed as well as the entangling kernel. With depolarizing noise (p = 0.01) and 1,024 shots per kernel entry, QSVM AUROC decreased by 0.054 (95% CI: 0.036 to 0.071). Untuned bandwidth produced near-chance AUROC (0.48-0.59). Conclusions: Correctly configured quantum classifiers approached but did not exceed tuned classical models. Finite-shot estimation, rather than gate noise, was their main practical cost, while configuration errors could reverse conclusions. QML-Health-READY defines reporting requirements for credible claims.

</details>


### [14] [Objective-Conditioned Active Preference Learning for Multi-Objective Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05506)
*Duc Anh Vu,Thong Nguyen,Hoang Pham,Ponhvoan Srey,Luu Anh Tuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OGC-DPO：将每个(响应对,目标)组合视为独立查询，用策略自身的隐式梯度度量信息量，选取最能扩展各目标梯度覆盖的查询，实现多目标对齐下的主动偏好学习，降低标注成本并证明冲突型偏好是有益训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标对齐（有用性、无害性、诚实性等）通常对每个目标分别做偏好判断，标注成本随目标数线性增长；主动偏好学习虽可降低标注成本，但现有方法在多目标场景下缺乏目标感知的查询选择机制，且将冲突偏好视为有害而从训练数据中剔除，造成信息浪费。

Method: 提出OGC-DPO（Objective-conditioned Gradient Coverage DPO）主动学习框架：将每个(响应对, 目标)组合作为单独查询；不依赖外部奖励模型，直接利用策略自身的隐式梯度衡量查询信息量；依据对各目标梯度覆盖的扩展程度进行查询选择，从而实现目标感知的多目标主动偏好学习。

Result: 在不同目标数量的实验中，相同标注成本下OGC-DPO优于现有主动偏好学习方法；进一步分析表明，信息量高的冲突偏好并非一律有害，反而能为策略训练提供有效信号。

Conclusion: 多目标对齐中的偏好标注可通过目标条件化、基于梯度的覆盖式主动选择显著提效；冲突偏好应被有选择地利用而非简单剔除，为多目标主动偏好学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with several objectives, such as helpfulness, harmlessness, and honesty, typically relies on preference judgment for each objective, growing the annotation cost linearly with the number of objectives. Active preference learning can reduce this cost by selecting the comparisons whose labels are expected to change the policy most. Existing remedies either lack an objective-aware query selection mechanism for the multi-objective setting or regard conflicting preferences as harmful, therefore excluding them from the training data. We introduce OGC-DPO (Objective-conditioned Gradient Coverage DPO), an active learning framework that treats each (response pair, objective) combination as a separate query and measures its informativeness through the policy's own implicit gradient, without relying on an external reward model. OGC-DPO then selects the queries that most expand each objective's gradient coverage. Empirical experiments across varying numbers of objectives demonstrate the effectiveness of OGC-DPO over existing active preference learning methods under the same annotation cost. Further analysis demonstrates that informative conflicts, rather than being uniformly harmful, provide a useful policy training signal.

</details>


### [15] [TSFM Meets LLM: Context as Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05515)
*Hemanth Ram,Sai Shankar Narasimhan,Shubhankar Agarwal,Sandeep Chinchali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可扩展的多模态数据构造方法（用LLM为真实时间序列打文本标注），并提出CoCo框架：LLM将文本推理蒸馏为数值化的预测与置信度协变量，引导TSFM骨干做预测；通过GRPO对齐LLM、再用其协变量微调TSFM，在金融、经济、天气、交通、安全等真实基准上取得最高12%的MAE提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实时间序列中常伴随新闻、报告、注释等文本信息，蕴含数值历史无法推断的预测信号；但LLM缺乏数值精度，TSFM虽定量准确却无法推理文本影响；同时缺少大规模配对多模态数据集，阻碍了二者的融合研究。

Method: 1）数据层面：用LLM对真实时间序列进行标注，规模化生成逼真的多模态（数值+文本）数据集；2）模型层面：提出CoCo（Context as Covariates）框架，由LLM将文本推理蒸馏为数值化的预测协变量与置信度协变量，作为协变量引导TSFM骨干网络预测；3）分阶段对齐：先用GRPO微调LLM，奖励设为协变量带来的预测性能提升；再以对齐后LLM生成的协变量微调TSFM。

Result: 在合成多模态语料上训练的模型可泛化到金融、经济、天气、交通、安全等真实基准；CoCo（Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507作LLM、Chronos2作TSFM）相较SOTA TSFM及GPT-4o等LLM预测器，MAE最高提升12%。代码、权重与评测数据集已公开。

Conclusion: 通过“LLM推理数值化+TSFM定量预测”的协变量式耦合与分阶段对齐，可在无大规模真实配对数据的情况下有效融合文本与数值信息；合成多模态语料具备向真实基准迁移的泛化能力。

Abstract: Textual information is ubiquitous in real-world time series datasets, appearing as news, reports, and annotations that hold key predictive signals not deducible from numerical history alone. Large Language Models (LLMs) interpret text but lack numerical precision, while Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) are quantitatively accurate but cannot reason about the effects of text. Moreover, the lack of large-scale paired multimodal datasets limits progress in bridging this gap. In this work, we address this challenge by providing a scalable approach for generating realistic multimodal datasets by annotating real-world time series with LLMs. Building on this, we propose Context as Covariates (CoCo), a forecasting framework in which an LLM distills its textual reasoning into numerically grounded forecast and confidence covariates that guide a TSFM backbone, thereby exploiting the strengths of both LLMs and TSFMs. We align the two models in stages, first fine-tuning the LLM via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a reward tied to covariate-induced improvement in forecasting performance, and then fine-tuning the TSFM on covariates generated by the aligned LLM. Our experiments show that models trained on our synthetic multimodal corpus generalize to real benchmarks spanning finance, economics, weather, traffic, and security. Notably, CoCo with Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 as the LLM and Chronos2 as the TSFM achieves up to 12% MAE improvement over state-of-the-art TSFMs and LLM forecasters, such as GPT-4o. Code, checkpoints, and the evaluation datasets are publicly available.

</details>


### [16] [The Confidence Score: a New Measure on Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05528)
*Elizabeth Hofer,Martin v. Mohrenschildt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种新的核度量"Confidence Score"，用于预测核分类器的准确率，可更好地评估核与分类任务之间的适配性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核度量（如与理想核的对齐度）要求同类样本彼此相似，这一假设对内部结构丰富的类别而言过于严格，限制了其对核分类性能的预测能力。

Method: 提出 Confidence Score：度量"真值标签的编码"与"该真值经核投影后的结果"之间的对齐程度，而非与理想核的对齐，从而允许保留类内结构的核获得高分。

Result: 在真实数据与合成数据上，跨多种核的实验中，Confidence Score 与分类准确率的相关性高于若干对比核度量。

Conclusion: Confidence Score 是一种更适用于分类任务核评价的度量，能够更准确地预测核分类器的泛化表现。

Abstract: Across a wide range of applications, kernel measures are used to tune hyperparameters, select among candidate kernels, and assess the suitability of kernels for specific learning tasks. For classification, kernel measures are used to predict which kernels may produce accurate kernel classifiers. Many existing kernel measures, such as alignment to the ideal kernel, are designed to reward kernels which captures all samples from the same class as similar. While effective in many settings, this requirement is unnecessarily restrictive for classification, where classes may exhibit substantial internal structure. In this work, the "Confidence Score", a new kernel measure, is proposed. Unlike alignment-to-the-ideal kernel measures, the Confidence Score measures the alignment between an encoding of the ground truth and that ground truth projected by the kernel, allowing kernels that preserve intra-class structure to achieve high scores. Through experiments, it is demonstrated that the Confidence Score exhibits a higher correlation with classification accuracy across a variety of kernels on both real-world and synthetic datasets, compared to select other kernel measures.

</details>


### [17] [Recasting Visual Language Models as EEG Foundation Models for Clinical Neurodiagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05538)
*Xin Zhou,Elena Sergeeva,Nan Bi,Wenyu Song,Lijun Yin,Hao Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出并验证：无需大规模EEG预训练的通用视觉语言模型（VLM）可将EEG以视觉方式解读，在多项临床神经诊断任务上性能与专用基础模型相当，少样本适配能力更强，且具备更好的模型规模扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化EEG依赖任务专用、需人工标注的监督模型；新兴范式通过大规模无标注EEG域内预训练构建统一表征（基础模型），但其表征质量受限于模态专用数据集的可得性。作者由此追问：通用VLM能否像临床医生那样"目视"阅读与解读EEG，从而规避对大规模EEG预训练的依赖。

Method: 将EEG信号转化为可视化表征输入通用VLM，不进行大规模EEG域内预训练；在一组临床神经诊断任务上评测其零/少样本与微调表现，并与EEG专用基础模型对比，同时考察随模型规模扩大的性能变化趋势。

Result: 通用VLM在多项临床神经诊断任务上表现具有竞争力；在少样本学习设定下跨任务适配优于专用基础模型；随模型规模增大性能持续提升，而模态专用模型未表现出该扩展性。

Conclusion: VLM可作为一种可行的EEG视觉解读范式，具备跨任务泛化与良好可扩展性，为摆脱对大规模模态专用标注/预训练数据的依赖提供了新路径。

Abstract: Historically automated electroencephalography (EEG) systems was done using task-specific supervised models trained on purposely annotated data. A more recent modeling paradigm relies on creating a unified representation, or a 'foundation model', for multiple modality-specific tasks through large-scale domain-specific pretraining on the unannotated EEG signal. The quality of this unified representation directly depends on the availability of the modality-specific datasets. At the same time, generalist Vision Language Models (VLMs) exhibit broad few-shot learning capacities as well as good fine-tuning adaptability to different visual and language tasks. Can VLMs learn how to read and interpret EEG visually, emulating to a degree the way human clinicians inspect the EEG patterns? In this work we make a case that they can: we show that generalist VLMs without large-scale EEG pretraining perform competitively across a collection of clinical neuro-diagnostic tasks, can adapt to different tasks in a few-shot learning setting better than specialist foundation models, and exhibit better performance with model scaling when modality-specific models fail to exhibit.

</details>


### [18] [FedSharp: Byzantine Robust Decentralized Federated Learning Under Noisy Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05546)
*Ojaswi Sinha,Amin Farajzadeh,Animesh Yadav,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedSharp鲁棒去中心化联邦学习框架，同时应对数据异构、拜占庭攻击与信道噪声，在100节点含33拜占庭节点场景下显著优于单模型选择基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFL无中心聚合服务器，但面临数据异构、拜占庭攻击与信道噪声三重威胁。作者指出：噪声随模型在节点间传播而累积，且在学习率衰减时愈发显著；累积噪声还会让拜占庭攻击者通过模仿诚实模型均值来操纵基于损失的模型选择。

Method: FedSharp = screened, hop-aware, ranked, proximal averaging：①每个节点保留2b个前驱模型，保证至多b个拜占庭节点时至少有b个诚实候选；②用逐坐标中位数偏差筛选可疑模型；③对损失最低的b个候选取平均，将新信道噪声方差降低b倍；④引入噪声自适应梯度范数正则与近端训练，缓解累积噪声与非IID漂移。

Result: 100节点环形拓扑、33个拜占庭节点下，MNIST精度93.5–94.9%，SOTA单模型选择基线仅29.4–38.1%；CIFAR-10为32.8–48.8%，基线10.0–30.5%；覆盖高斯与隐藏攻击、IID与非IID数据。MNIST上FedSharp与无信道噪声的基线精度差距约1个百分点以内。

Conclusion: 通过跳数感知候选池、中位数筛选、低损失Top-b平均与噪声自适应近端正则，FedSharp在不依赖中心聚合的前提下有效抑制噪声累积与拜占庭操纵，逼近无噪声上界。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) eliminates the central aggregation server but remains vulnerable to data heterogeneity, Byzantine attacks, and channel noise. We show that noise accumulates as models propagate across nodes, becoming increasingly significant as the learning rate decays. Moreover, accumulated noise enables Byzantine attackers to exploit loss-based model selection by mimicking honest model averages. To address these challenges, we propose screened, hop-aware, ranked, proximal averaging, a robust DFL framework (FedSharp). In this approach, each node maintains models from its 2b predecessors, ensuring at least b honest candidates when at most b nodes are Byzantine. FedSharp screens suspicious models using coordinate-wise median deviations, averages the b lowest-loss candidates to reduce fresh channel-noise variance by a factor of b, and applies noise-adaptive gradient-norm regularization and proximal training to mitigate accumulated noise and non-IID drift. Experiments on a 100-node ring with 33 Byzantine nodes show that FedSharp achieves 93.5-94.9% accuracy on MNIST, compared with 29.4-38.1% for a state-of-the-art single-model-selection benchmark, and 32.8-48.8% on CIFAR-10, compared with 10.0-30.5%, across Gaussian and hidden attacks under IID and non-IID data. On MNIST, FedSharp also achieves accuracy within approximately one percentage point of the benchmark operating without channel noise.

</details>


### [19] [Data-Efficient Scaling-Law Discovery for Recommendation Systems via Coupled Hyperparameter Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05643)
*John Martabano Landy,Louis C. Tiao,Yao Xuan,Luc Kien Hang,Rupinder P. Khandpur,Arvind M. Vepa,Srinidhi Raghavendran,Anirudh Agrawal,Xiaoyi Leo Liu,Jiang Liu,Peggy Yao,Alireza Vahdatpour,Djordje Gligorijevic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自动化系统 Axia，用"scaling layer"将排序模型架构参数映射到低维超超参数空间，配合代理模型与后验采集准则高效发现各类排序模型的缩放律，在 Meta 广告排序/检索系统 34 次模型迭代中取得 0.66% 离线归一化熵提升与 0.36% 累计在线增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web 平台的大规模排序与推荐模型同样受益于算力增长，缩放行为近似幂律；但排序模型架构异构，缩放律不像 LLM 那样直接，穷举式扫描代价过高，需要高效发现任意排序模型的缩放律。

Method: 引入 scaling layer：将原始架构参数分组为语义一致的组并定义其协同变化规律，从而建立从低维超超参数空间到完整架构空间的映射；在 Axia 中拟合 scaling-informed 代理模型，用基于后验的采集准则选择配置，输出 Pareto 前沿并逐模型验证工作点；同时设计舰队级评估协议，在固定代表性模型集与匹配试验预算下度量改进。

Result: 在 Meta 广告排序与检索系统的两个开发周期、34 次模型迭代中，该流程平均带来 0.66% 的离线归一化熵改进与 0.36% 的累计在线提升。

Conclusion: Axia 是一个自动化系统，可学习模型特定的复杂度—质量关系，并在异构模型生态中提供不确定性感知的算力分配指导，以少量高信息量试验替代穷举扫描。

Abstract: Large-scale ranking and recommendation models of Web platforms benefit from increased compute much as large language models do, and their scaling behavior is broadly power-law in form. Yet ranking models have heterogeneous architectures which makes their scaling law less straightforward than that of LLMs. We address how to discover the scaling law of an arbitrary ranking model efficiently. We introduce a scaling layer, which groups primitive architecture parameters into semantically coherent groups and defines how they co-vary, inducing a mapping from a low-dimensional space of hyper-hyperparameters into the full architecture space. On top of it, Axia fits scaling-informed surrogates, selects configurations by a posterior-based acquisition criterion, and returns a Pareto frontier from which per-model operating points are validated -- replacing exhaustive sweeps with a small number of informative runs. A fleet-level evaluation protocol, computed on a fixed representative model set under matched trial budgets, makes refinements to either component measurable. Our contribution is an automated system that learns model-specific complexity-quality relationships and gives uncertainty-aware guidance for allocating compute across a heterogeneous model ecosystem. Across 34 model iterations in two development cycles on Meta's ads ranking and retrieval system, the workflow yielded a mean 0.66% offline normalized-entropy improvement and 0.36% cumulative online lift.

</details>


### [20] [Support Vector Machines Directly Minimise KL Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05557)
*Ivan Silajev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明支持向量分类与回归的对偶目标等价于两个高斯测度间带约束的精确KL散度最小化，从而为SVM建立概率解释，并统一核机器方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVM基于几何间隔最大化，缺乏主流概率解释，使其在理论上与可比较、可组合的概率机器学习模型相隔离。

Method: 将支持向量分类与支持向量回归的对偶目标形式化，证明其为两个高斯测度之间的精确、带约束的Kullback-Leibler散度最小化任务；并考察去掉域约束、施加L2正则时该散度形式是否能还原核岭回归的精确目标函数。

Result: 支持向量分类与回归的对偶目标均可被精确表述为约束KL散度最小化；解除域约束并加入L2正则后，同一散度表述可精确恢复核岭回归的目标函数。

Conclusion: SVM对偶直接最小化相对熵，为标准核机器提供了统一的信息论模型，并为在概率论框架下统一更广泛的核算法奠定基础。

Abstract: Support vector machines (SVMs) are fundamentally grounded in geometric margin maximisation and currently lack a mainstream probabilistic interpretation. This keeps them theoretically isolated from probabilistic machine learning models that are naturally comparable and combinable. In this paper, we bridge this gap by proving that the dual objectives of both support vector classification and support vector regression are exact, constrained Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence minimisation tasks between two Gaussian measures. We further show that this same divergence formulation recovers the exact objective function for kernel ridge regression when domain constraints are removed and $L^2$ regularisation is applied. By demonstrating that SVM duals directly minimise relative entropy, our results provide a unified information-theoretic model for standard kernel machines and lay the foundation for unifying broader kernel-based algorithms under probability theory.

</details>


### [21] [Readable Is Not Steerable: Pre-Registered Tests of Physical Structure in Learned Fluid Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05577)
*Manvendra Modgil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文以2D衰减Navier-Stokes流为测试床，通过三项预注册研究检验"自监督世界模型潜空间围绕物理量组织"这一说法，发现潜空间的表征内容与可控性可以分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "自监督世界模型其潜变量围绕物理量组织"是常见宣称，但缺乏严格、可复现的检验。作者希望在受控的物理系统上用预注册、密封留出数据的方式直接检验该主张，并区分"潜空间包含什么信息"与"能否通过潜空间控制/干预"。

Method: 在2D衰减Navier-Stokes流上开展三项各只测试一次的预注册研究：(1) 比较JEPA与同架构未训练网络、非物理线性动力学JEPA、静态不变模型的潜空间组织结构；(2) 用普通自编码器与加入VICReg的自编码器，考察流速场渲染与最慢无监督潜方向（如涡度、enstrophy）的关联及相关性r；(3) 通过路由构造可操纵的"旋钮"方向——解码器仅经小型条件码获得整体尺度，另一通路携带退化谱形状，用无标签规则寻找loudness/形状旋钮并设对照。全部实验约70 GPU小时。

Result: 研究1：JEPA未达预注册标准，未训练网络、非物理JEPA与静态不变模型表现相当或更好（不支持）。研究2：普通自编码器最慢无监督方向能显著呈现enstrophy（最佳r 0.80–0.86 vs 未训练0.38），VICReg在每项比较中都降低该显著性（涡度未达预注册幅度），但提升线性可解码性（部分支持）。研究3：8个训练模型中全部找到loudness旋钮，形状旋钮在5个新种子中3个成功（预注册门槛为4），两个对照均无形状旋钮（部分支持）。探索中，近乎完美读取谱形状的监督方向在操纵时几乎不产生变化。

Conclusion: 潜空间"包含什么"与"能通过它控制什么"并不一致：高可解码的物理量方向未必可操纵，而可操纵旋钮需按输出效应而非数据方差来识别。自监督世界模型围绕物理量组织的说法在上述设定下未获有力支持。所有预注册、偏离记录、代码与输出均公开。

Abstract: Self-supervised world models are often said to organize their latents around physical quantities. We test this on 2D decaying Navier-Stokes flow in three pre-registered studies, each tested once on held-out data. In Study 1, a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) trained to predict the flow fails the pre-registered criteria for organizing its latent around fluid quantities better than an untrained network of the same architecture (not supported); a JEPA trained on non-physical linear dynamics and a static invariance model that never sees time do as well or better. In Study 2, plain autoencoders that render the field make enstrophy prominent in their slowest unsupervised latent directions (best r 0.80-0.86 vs 0.38 untrained; fresh seeds, sealed data). Adding VICReg lowers this prominence in every comparison, though not by the pre-registered margin for vorticity, while raising the autoencoders' linear decodability (partially supported). In Study 3, we build steerable directions ("dials") by routing: the decoder gets overall scale only through a small conditioning code, while its other pathway carries a degraded copy of spectral shape. Label-free rules find a loudness dial in all 8 trained models and a shape dial in 3 of 5 fresh seeds on sealed data, against a pre-registered bar of 4; neither control shows a shape dial (partially supported). Across the studies, what a latent contains and what can be controlled through it come apart. In exploration, a supervised direction reading spectral shape almost perfectly moved nothing when steered. During the build, the loudness dial had to be found by its effect on the output (closest to a pure rescaling), not by data variance; for shape, a variance rule and an effect-constrained rule each worked in 3 of 5 seeds. The program used about 70 logged hours on one consumer GPU; all pre-registrations, deviations, code and outputs are public.

</details>


### [22] [Exploring the Privacy--Size Tradeoff for Differentially Private Self-Attentive Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05582)
*Rohit Sadavarte,Dhruvi Kothari,Sparsh Gupta,Eric Van Oeveren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAVANT——首个结合注意力机制、VAE与DP-SGD的表格数据合成模型，并在隐私预算与数据规模两个维度上系统评估，发现同等DP保障下数据越多效用越高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据在共享时最有价值，但最值得共享的数据往往受隐私义务限制。差分隐私（DP）合成数据可调和二者矛盾，然而其效用同时取决于隐私预算ε与生成器可见的数据量，而已有研究多为在数据量上固定、只研究预算，缺乏对二者的联合刻画。

Method: 提出SAVANT：一种用DP-SGD训练的表格数据自注意力变分自编码器（据称是首个将注意力、VAE与DP统一于一个表格模型的工作）。在公开数据集（两个）上，与两种边缘（marginal）机制PrivBayes和MST在隐私预算与数据规模构成的二维网格上联合比较，以下游分类器因改用合成数据而损失的效用作为度量；并通过噪声缩放分析比较DP-SGD与边缘机制在数据与预算之间的交换率；另用两种独立的成员推断攻击并以实测零假设为基线进行检验。

Result: 在相同DP保证下，数据越多通常效用越高；对边缘机制而言，数据量增长可吸收严格预算带来的大部分效用损失，而噪声缩放论证表明DP-SGD以数据换预算的交换率更差。两种成员推断攻击相对于实测零假设均未发现聚合层面的成员信号。

Conclusion: 数据规模是与隐私预算同等重要的合成数据效用决定因素，评估时应联合考虑；SAVANT提供了结合注意力、VAE与DP的表格建模方案，且在所用攻击下未检出可测的成员泄露。后续应用与基准应在预算与数据量两轴上报告效用。

Abstract: Data tends to be most useful when it is shared, and the data most worth sharing is often the data that privacy obligations restrict. Synthetic data from a generator trained under differential privacy (DP) is one way to reconcile the two, but its utility depends on the privacy budget $\varepsilon$ and on how much data the generator sees - a parameter that most data sharing applications of synthetic data control. We present SAVANT, a self-attentive variational autoencoder for tabular data trained with DP-SGD that, to our knowledge, is the first to combine attention, a VAE, and DP in one tabular model. We evaluate it against two marginal mechanisms, PrivBayes and MST, jointly over the privacy budget and the dataset size on two public datasets, measuring the utility a downstream classifier loses by training on synthetic rather than real data. We find that, under the same DP guarantee, more data generally means more utility. For the marginal mechanisms it absorbs most of what a strict budget costs, and a noise-scaling argument suggests DP-SGD exchanges data fo budget at a worse rate. Two independent membership-inference attacks, judged against a measured null, found no aggregate membership signal.

</details>


### [23] [C-GRPO: Conformal Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05591)
*Arya Fayyazi,Seyedarmin Azizi,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CGRPO 用校准的共形阈值替代 GRPO 固定 rollout 预算，实现有限样本覆盖保证的自适应采样，训练 rollout 减少 49–70% 且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 GRPO 采用固定组大小（group size）进行多路 on-policy rollout，在简单提示上浪费算力、在困难提示上探索不足。现有自适应预算方法虽缓解该低效问题，但其停止规则是启发式的，缺乏有限样本校准保证。

Method: 提出 CGRPO：以任务特定的非一致性分数（nonconformity score）上的共形阈值替代固定 rollout 预算。具体包括：面向闭式推理的 APS 式校准、面向执行式代码生成的首成功分数（first-success score）、自动误覆盖率选择，以及周期性重校准；并证明该停止规则的有限样本覆盖性。

Result: 在数学、研究生级科学与代码生成六个基准上，相对固定预算 GRPO 平均减少 49–70% 训练 rollout，同时保留较强的固定预算精度；在不同数据集上于静态与动态基线中取得最佳 pass@1；跨模型族迁移可带来最高 82% 的 rollout 节省。

Conclusion: CGRPO 将自适应 rollout 预算纳入共形预测框架，兼具统计保证与效率收益；在策略单调改进假设下重校准使期望训练预算非增，验证了理论性与实用性。

Abstract: Reasoning-oriented language model training increasingly relies on multiple on-policy rollouts, but standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) uses a fixed group size, wasting compute on easy prompts and under-exploring hard ones. Existing adaptive-budget methods alleviate this inefficiency, yet their stopping rules are heuristic and do not provide a finite-sample calibration guarantee. We propose CGRPO, which replaces the fixed rollout budget of standard GPRO with calibrated conformal thresholds over task-specific nonconformity scores, combining Adaptive Prediction Set (APS)-style calibration for closed-form reasoning, a first-success score for execution-based code, automatic miscoverage selection, and periodic re-calibration. We prove finite-sample coverage for the resulting stopping rule and show that, under monotone policy improvement, recalibration induces a non-increasing expected training budget. Across six benchmarks spanning mathematics, graduate-level science, and code generation, CGRPO reduces mean training rollouts by 49-70% relative to fixed-budget GRPO while retaining strong fixed-budget accuracy, achieves the strongest pass@1 among evaluated static and dynamic baselines on different datasets, and transfers across model families with up to 82% rollout savings.

</details>


### [24] [Alternatives to Shallow Autoencoders for Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06610)
*Mario Mallea,Angela Nebot,Francisco Mugica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于用户-用户信息的EASE替代形式及融合用户-用户与物品-物品邻接分布的混合模型，在标准推荐基准上超越EASE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EASE依靠物品间关系拟合满秩高维线性映射，假设其难以捕捉相似但不完全等价的细粒度用户关系，从而限制推荐精度。

Method: 在EASE框架下提出利用用户-用户信息的替代公式，并构建融合用户-用户与物品-物品邻接分布的混合模型。

Result: 在知名推荐基准上，所提模型性能优于EASE。

Conclusion: CF浅层自编码器研究应纳入用户侧信息，用户-用户信息对提升排序精度具有显著价值。

Abstract: Collaborative filtering (CF) is a cornerstone of recommender systems and plays a relevant role in many modern applications. CF uses user-item interaction data to discover future preferences. Although deep learning models have shown promise in CF, Embarrassingly Shallow Autoencoders for Sparse Data (EASE) has gained attention for its outstanding ranking accuracy provided by its closed-form solution. EASE relies primarily on relationships among items to fit a full-rank high-dimensional linear mapping. We hypothesize that this design limits its capacity to capture similar but not equivalent fine-grained user relationships, consequently limiting its recommendation accuracy. This paper introduces an alternative formulation based on EASE that takes advantage of user-user information. Furthermore, we propose a hybrid model that combines both user-user and item-item adjacency distributions.
  Our experiments reveal that the proposed models outperform EASE on well-known recommendation benchmarks, highlighting the significance of including the user alternative in shallow autoencoder studies for CF.

</details>


### [25] [Relevance Propagation as Entropic Optimal Transport on Filippov Neural Networks: Sinkhorn Propagation and Region Relevance in Image Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05594)
*Olivier Gibaru,Eric Nyiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文证明逐层相关性传播（LRP）并非启发式，而是单一变分问题的解：前向激活流所诱导的后向路径上的相对熵最小（散度为零）流；LRP$_0$、LRP$_{z^+}$、LRP$_{α,β}$ 皆由此原理经不同参考选取导出，并可统一为权重随节点变化的 LRPN$_{α,β}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRP 及相关性传播长期被当作经验性/启发式方法，缺乏统一的理论依据，各规则之间关系不清，也缺少对不连续、分段光滑架构的严格刻画。

Method: 以 Filippov 算子有向无环图对前馈网络作测度论形式化，配合 Clarke–Filippov 广义梯度微积分处理分段光滑与不连续结构（如硬路由 MoE）；将 LRP 表述为前向激活流的"后向转移律"下、零相对熵的散度自由流（非负参考时即后向路径 Markov 测度间的相对熵）。由此导出各规则，提出逐边熵版本（Sinkhorn Relevance Propagation）与 Hebb 式节点自适应权重的 LRPN$_{α,β}$；并刻画反向传播为 Lagrangian 松弛的伴随方程。

Result: 在 ReLU 与 max-pooling 网络上，LRP$_0$ 等于 Gradient×Input，并在折点处有显式跳跃，而 LRP$_{z^+}$ 保持连续。对输入区域聚合时，非负参考的流可精确核算哪些隐单元承载证据，条件流为 Schrödinger 桥。VGG-16 上守恒恒等式达到舍入误差精度，各规则可由映射符号、偏置节点分得的输出份额、忠实性、定位性与类别依赖性区分；ConvNeXt-Tiny 上 LRPN$_{α,β}$ 保持输出守恒，其 Hebb 权重取得所测规则中最高的 ABPC。

Conclusion: 相关性传播具有统一变分/信息几何解释，规则选择等价于参考测度的选择；该框架可推广至不连续架构，并给出新规则与可解释的守恒与证据归因性质。

Abstract: Relevance propagation, which redistributes a network's prediction backward through the layers, is usually treated as a heuristic. We show that it is the solution of a single variational problem: Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP) is the divergence-free flow on the active computational graph of zero relative entropy to the backward transition law of the forward activation flow; for a non-negative reference, this is a relative entropy between Markov measures on backward paths. The rules LRP$_0$, LRP$_{z^+}$ and LRP$_{α,β}$ follow from this principle by the choice of a reference, and are instances of one rule, LRPN$_{α,β}$, whose weights may vary from node to node, for instance with the node's activity, in the spirit of Hebb's principle. The principle rests on a measure-theoretic formalization of feed-forward networks as directed acyclic graphs of Filippov operators, which covers piecewise-smooth and discontinuous architectures (e.g. hard-routed Mixture-of-Experts) with a Clarke-Filippov generalized-gradient calculus; it also characterizes backpropagation as the adjoint equation of a Lagrangian relaxation. On networks of ReLU and max-pooling nodes, LRP$_0$ is Gradient$\times$Input, with an explicit jump across a kink where LRP$_{z^+}$ stays continuous. Taking the entropy edge by edge yields a new rule, Sinkhorn Relevance Propagation. Aggregated over an input region, the flow of a non-negative reference tells, with exact accounting, which hidden units carry its evidence, the conditioned flow being a Schrödinger bridge. On VGG-16, the conservation identities hold to round-off, and the rules are told apart by the sign of their maps, the share of the output left to the bias nodes, faithfulness, localization and class dependence; on ConvNeXt-Tiny, LRPN$_{α,β}$ conserves the output and its Hebbian weights give the highest ABPC of the rules tested.

</details>


### [26] [Navigating Disagreement Among Criteria for Evaluating Explanation Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06846)
*Grant Shanklin,Jiří Němeček,Asia Biega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"faithfulness功能式"(faithfulness functional)框架，将现有一致性/忠实性评价准则的分歧定位于三个可选设计选择，论证忠实性是模型与解释之间"共同但未完全规定"的关系，并以不利行动通知(adverse action notices)案例说明具体情境如何收窄这些选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有忠实性(faithfulness)的操作化标准常对同一解释给出相互矛盾的判断，使开发者与审计者陷入选择困境，论文旨在厘清分歧来源并提供可操作的评估路径。

Method: 1) 构建faithfulness functional，将各操作化的分歧拆解为三处：(a)解释对模型作出了何种断言；(b)哪些比较与该断言相关；(c)这些比较之间的一致性如何聚合；2) 将现有评价准则在该功能式内重构；3) 以不利行动通知的行业提出程序为案例，重构其隐含的评价逻辑。

Result: 证明了现有准则的差异实质上是通用忠实性要求下未规定的选择差异；在案例中识别出情境可正当化的选择项与仍悬而未决的选择项。

Conclusion: 忠实性文献中出现多种操作化是预期之内的；操作化的正当性取决于对上述选择的证成，需援引解释性、制度性与规范性考量；具体情境可有效收窄选择范围，从而在分歧中形成情境化正当的评价。

Abstract: Faithfulness requires that algorithmic explanations accurately reflect the model's behavior. Yet existing operationalizations of faithfulness can disagree about whether the same explanation is faithful, leaving developers and auditors to choose among conflicting evaluations. In this paper, we investigate disagreement among evaluation criteria and offer a practical path forward for evaluating explanation faithfulness. We first identify how operationalizations of faithfulness differ. We introduce a faithfulness functional that locates their disagreements in what an explanation claims about the model, which comparisons bear on that claim, and how agreement across those comparisons should be aggregated, and we reconstruct existing evaluation criteria within it. We argue these reconstructions support understanding faithfulness as a common though incompletely specified relation between the model and explanation, so the differences reflect choices left open by the general requirement. Justifying an operationalization then comes down to justifying these choices, which, on our view, draws on explanatory, institutional, and normative considerations. We support this argument with a case study of adverse action notices, reconstructing the evaluation implicit in an industry-proposed procedure and identifying which of its choices the setting justifies and which remain open. Our work gives auditors and practitioners a way to navigate disagreeing evaluations of faithfulness. It also explains why the literature should expect multiple operationalizations, while showing how concrete settings can narrow the choice among them. More broadly, our analysis shows how a shared requirement of faithfulness can support contextually justified evaluations despite disagreement among existing metrics.

</details>


### [27] [RIDGE: State-Conditioned Reward Blending for Behavioral Coverage in Deep RL Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05601)
*AL Muqshith Mohammed Shifan,Kevin Christopher Chua,Ali Neshati,Loutfouz Zaman,Cristiano Politowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RIDGE：一个状态条件混合器加多头评论家的单智能体架构，使一个智能体在 Crafter 上同时习得多种人格化行为，规避非平稳奖励下 PPO 退化的“切换稳定性困境”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定人格奖励训练出的智能体行为僵化（Explorer 饥饿仍探索、Survivor 空地图囤积），为覆盖 N 种风格需训练 N 个智能体；而在回合内动态改变奖励组合会遭遇切换稳定性困境——PPO 在非平稳奖励下性能退化。作者希望用单一智能体、单一训练过程实现多风格行为。

Method: RIDGE 的两个设计要点：(1) 状态条件混合器，由慢变状态变量计算经 softmax 归一化的 sigmoid 权重，实现人格奖励的平滑加权混合；(2) 多头评论家，每个人格对应一个价值头，各自在其平稳的奖励信号上训练，避免共享批评器受非平稳目标污染。

Result: 在 Crafter 上设置四个人格，单个 RIDGE 智能体同时掌握木质层级“制作/战斗”分裂的两个分支，而任何固定人格基线都无法联合求解；锐度消融（sharpness ablation）表明状态条件是性能增益的驱动因素。结果基于每条件 n=3 随机种子，属机制动态性演示。

Conclusion: 以状态条件奖励混合 + 多头价值分解可在不切换奖励的前提下获得多风格行为，绕开非平稳奖励导致的策略退化；但当前证据仅为 n=3 的演示性结果，更大规模多种子扫描留待后续工作。

Abstract: A deep RL agent trained on a fixed persona reward plays one way: an Explorer keeps exploring while starving, a Survivor hoards on an empty map. Since each persona fixes its own reward, automated playtesting trains N agents for N styles. Changing reward composition mid-episode runs into the switching-stability dilemma: PPO degrades under non-stationary rewards. We present RIDGE, a single-agent architecture that sidesteps the dilemma with two design choices: a state-conditioned blender that computes softmax-normalized sigmoid weights from slow-moving state variables, and a multi-head critic that trains one value head per persona on its own stationary signal. On Crafter with four personas, one \ridge agent acquires both branches of the wood-tier crafting/combat split that no fixed-persona baseline solves jointly, and a sharpness ablation confirms state-conditioning drives the gain. We position these results as demonstrations of the blending mechanism's dynamics across n=3 seeds per condition; larger multi-seed sweeps remain future work.

</details>


### [28] [Transport along Local Annealing Paths: Wasserstein Geometry and Multimodal Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05605)
*Han Chen,Sifan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出几何退火路径在Wasserstein空间中可能大幅绕行，导致模式间质量传输负担过重；提出分量退火传输（CAT），通过模式感知的混合参考与精确混合分解，沿分量退火路径分别学习传输映射，从而缓解模式坍塌并提升采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分方法学习多模态采样的传输映射时易发生模式坍塌。常用缓解手段是沿几何退火定义的中间分布路径学习传输，但退火路径本身的性质及其与传输学习的关系尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 首先分析退火路径在Wasserstein空间中的几何性质，将路径几何与传输学习难度（总体优化的收敛速度）建立联系。据此提出CAT：以模式信息引导的混合分布作为参考，构造目标分布的精确混合分解，在分量退火路径上分别学习独立的传输映射；分量权重单独估计；并提出由传输代价（transport effort）引导的自适应退火调度。

Result: 理论表明：当质量在模式间迁移时，退火路径可能远长于端点间距离；长路径会拖慢总体优化，而足够正则的路径可实现快速收敛。同时证明分量权重估计在学习到的流不精确时仍保持一致性。实验在多模态合成分布与贝叶斯推断任务上显示传输学习与采样效率均有提升。

Conclusion: 退火路径的几何是影响传输学习效率的关键因素；通过分量级分解与退火，CAT减少了对模式间质量传输的需求，配合自适应退火调度可有效缓解模式坍塌并提升多模态采样表现。

Abstract: Variational approaches to learning transport maps for multimodal sampling can suffer from mode collapse. One way to mitigate this problem is to learn transport along a prescribed path of intermediate distributions defined by geometric annealing. We show that annealing can take a substantial detour in Wasserstein space: when mass moves between modes, the path can be much longer than the distance between its endpoints. We further link path geometry to transport learning, showing that long annealing paths can slow population optimization, while sufficiently regular paths permit rapid convergence. Motivated by these findings, we propose componentwise annealed transport (CAT) to reduce the need for mass transfer between modes. Starting from a mode-informed mixture reference, CAT constructs an exact mixture decomposition of the target and learns separate transport maps along componentwise annealing paths. We estimate the component weights separately and show that these estimates remain consistent even when the learned flows are inexact. We also develop adaptive annealing schedules guided by transport effort. Experiments on multimodal synthetic distributions and Bayesian inference problems demonstrate improvements in transport learning and sampling efficiency.

</details>


### [29] [On the Generalization Limitation of On-Policy Distillation for Post-RL Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05609)
*Yichen Wang,Yufa Zhou,Siqi Zhu,Haozheng Luo,Jingzhi Bao,Zhichao Liu,Zijun Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出多教师on-policy蒸馏(MOPD)虽在分布内(IND)表现近乎完美，但掩盖了OOD能力整合不完整的问题，并分析瓶颈与更新几何，提出改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作报告(M)OPD在分布内蒸馏性能近乎完美，但作者怀疑这可能掩盖了监督分布之外能力整合的不完整，即教师泛化能力未被充分迁移。

Method: 先在合成推理任务上做初步研究，再扩展到数学推理、代码、智能体、长上下文等真实任务，用Qwen3模型评估外推式与组合式泛化；从教师能力和数据覆盖两个潜在瓶颈切入分析，并考察相对于RL基线的更新几何（幅度、谱集中度、共享结构），据此提出geometry-based patching与base-preserving replay。

Result: 外推设置下OPD/MOPD达教师IND分数的96.04–102.73%，但OOD分数比基座模型低2.45–16.25%；三种组合设置中MOPD提升IND但组合任务OOD比最佳教师低18.19–24.34%。联合缓解教师能力与数据覆盖瓶颈可消除OOD遗忘；(M)OPD平均OOD遗忘低于RL基线，其更新更小、谱更集中且共享结构更强。

Conclusion: (M)OPD存在能力整合的盲点，需明确支持OOD保持与泛化的算法；所提geometry-based patching与base-preserving replay可在基本保持IND的同时改善OOD。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) extends on-policy distillation (OPD) to consolidate capabilities from multiple teachers. Although prior work reports near-perfect in-distribution (IND) distillation performance, we find that this can mask incomplete capability consolidation beyond the supervised distribution. A preliminary study on synthetic reasoning shows that OPD does not fully transfer teachers' generalization capabilities. We then extend this investigation to real-world tasks, evaluating extrapolative and compositional generalization across mathematical reasoning, coding, agent, and long-context capabilities using Qwen3 models. Across extrapolative settings, OPD and MOPD reach 96.04-102.73% of their teachers' IND scores. Yet their out-of-distribution (OOD) scores remain 2.45-16.25% below the base model's. Across three compositional settings, MOPD improves IND performance over the base model, but its performance on composed tasks as OOD remains 18.19-24.34% below that of the best teachers. To understand these gaps, we examine teacher capability and data coverage as two potential bottlenecks. Jointly alleviating these bottlenecks eliminates OOD forgetting in our experiments and suggests the potential for generalization beyond the teacher. (M)OPD also reduces OOD forgetting on average relative to its reinforcement learning (RL) baselines. Analysis of the associated update geometry reveals smaller and more spectrally concentrated updates on average than those of the RL baselines, along with greater shared update structure under MOPD. Motivated by these findings, we propose geometry-based patching and base-preserving replay to improve OOD performance while largely preserving IND performance. Our findings identify a blind spot in capability consolidation via (M)OPD, highlighting the need for algorithms that explicitly support OOD retention and generalization.

</details>


### [30] [LACS: Distribution-Sensitive Label-Agnostic Confidence Selection for Dynamic Data Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05616)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LACS（Label-Agnostic Confidence Selector）：以最大预测置信度为依据、基于log-mean-exp种群相对参考值加margin的免标签、免逐样本梯度选择规则，可在自适应确定阈值与保留数量的同时减少训练计算；七个设置中反向活跃行减少10-35%、精度基本持平，但因额外评分前向，尚未转化为实际墙钟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模数据训练使"选择哪些样本承担昂贵训练计算"成为关键问题。固定配额需预先决定剪枝量，绝对置信度阈值依赖分数尺度，二者均缺乏自适应性；因此需要一种同时自适应调整阈值与保留数量的种群相对替代方案，且选择时不需要标签或逐样本梯度。

Method: 提出LACS：训练期规则，基于最大预测置信度，移除分数达到或超过"log-mean-exp参考值 + margin"的样本。分析其对分数间隙与质量的敏感性，并推导精确的无剪枝条件；证明对0<ρ<1，移除比例至多ρ且至少保留一个样本。因分数无符号，置信正确与置信错误的样本均可被移除。评估覆盖七个设置，包括神经训练、语言建模与boosting；神经网络在CIFAR-10上比较反向行数与精度，并在匹配反向预算与优化步数下与小批量训练、随机选择对比；另在14个表格数据集上考察二次boosting，并进行RBF-SVM案例研究。

Result: 神经执行研究将反向活跃行减少10-35%；CIFAR-10上反向行减少29%，精度均值89.77% vs 全CE的89.82%。在匹配反向预算与优化更新次数下，五seed平均比小批量训练高1.0 pp、比随机选择高1.2 pp。14个表格数据集上，二次boosting平均测试误差由23.36%降至22.96%，完整训练重放使用拟合行约少30%。RBF-SVM案例中耗时下降37%，误差略低。

Conclusion: 研究区分了"选择性工作量缩减"与"实际加速"：当前基于精确当前分数的神经实现需额外一次评分前向，故反向稀疏尚未转化为墙钟加速；该开销属于执行方式选择而非选择规则的固有要求。LACS在保持精度的同时有效减少训练计算，是免标签、免逐样本梯度的自适应选择规则。

Abstract: Training on large datasets motivates selecting which examples receive expensive training computation. Fixed quotas determine the amount of pruning in advance, while absolute confidence cutoffs depend on the score scale. We study a population-relative alternative that adapts the threshold and retained count together. We introduce LACS (Label-Agnostic Confidence Selector), a training-time rule based on maximum predictive confidence, requiring neither labels at selection time nor per-example gradients. LACS removes examples at or above a log-mean-exp reference plus a margin. We characterize its sensitivity to score gaps and mass, and derive an exact no-pruning condition. For $0<ρ<1$, the removed fraction is at most $ρ$ and at least one example remains. The unsigned score permits both confidently correct and confidently wrong examples to be removed. Seven evaluation settings include neural training, language modeling, and boosting. Neural execution studies reduce backward-active rows by 10-35%; on CIFAR-10, 29% fewer backward rows accompany accuracy means of 89.77% versus 89.82% for full CE. At a matched backward budget and number of optimizer updates, LACS is 1.0 pp above small-batch training and 1.2 pp above random selection over five seeds. Across 14 tabular datasets, quadratic boosting reduces mean test error from 23.36% to 22.96%. Its full training replays use about 30% fewer fitting rows; these counters span a different horizon from validation-selected accuracy. In one RBF-SVM case study, elapsed time falls by 37% with slightly lower error. These findings separate selective-work reduction from realized acceleration: the current exact-current-score neural implementation uses an additional scoring forward, so its backward sparsity does not yet translate into wall-clock acceleration. This overhead is an execution choice rather than a requirement of the selection rule.

</details>


### [31] [When Are Two SAE Latents the Same Feature?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05655)
*Alexandra Suvorikova,Ilgam Latypov,Anton Trukhan,Yuriy Dorn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以能量加权最优传输计划为核心的分析框架，统一刻画稀疏自编码器（SAE）的特征对应、字典级差异与有限数据不确定性，并导出传输代价与质量分数的联合渐近推断及联合自助法置信带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较稀疏自编码器需要同时处理三项任务：特征（潜在单元）之间的对应关系、整体字典层面差异的度量，以及由有限评估数据带来的不确定性；现有方法缺乏将三者联系起来的统一工具。

Method: 在指定相似度几何下构造能量加权最优传输（OT）计划：同一计划既定义字典级差异度量，又将其分解为各潜在单元的贡献，并量化每个潜在单元的质量在对应单元上的分布。分析解码器相似度、激活相似度与一阶因果相似度之间的关系，用理想化模型厘清"分裂"与"吸收"现象的解释。对固定已训练 SAE，推导传输代价与传输分数的联合渐近推断，同时考虑激活相似性以及由同一批文档估计的两侧能量边缘分布；提出联合文档自助法（joint document bootstrap）给出渐近有效的同时置信带。

Result: 在四个语言模型的实验中定位了跨随机种子的差异，并分离出依赖上下文的变化成分；理论上给出传输代价与分数的联合渐近分布与有效同时置信带。

Conclusion: 最优传输计划可作为 SAE 比较的统一统计基础，兼顾特征对应、字典级差异分解与不确定性量化，为跨种子与跨上下文条件下的 SAE 差异分析提供有推断保证的工具。

Abstract: Comparing sparse autoencoders (SAEs) requires connecting feature correspondence, differences between whole dictionaries, and uncertainty from finite evaluation data. We develop a framework that connects these tasks through an energy-weighted optimal-transport plan under a specified similarity geometry. The same plan defines a dictionary-level discrepancy, decomposes it into latent contributions, and quantifies how each latent's mass is distributed across counterparts. We characterize relationships between decoder, activation, and first-order causal similarities; idealized models clarify the interpretation of splitting and absorption. For fixed trained SAEs, we derive joint asymptotic inference for transport costs and fractions, accounting for activation similarities and both energy marginals estimated from the same documents. A joint document bootstrap gives asymptotically valid simultaneous confidence bands. Experiments across four language models locate cross-seed discrepancy and separate context-dependent changes.

</details>


### [32] [Beyond Accuracy: Information-Theoretic Decoder Evaluation Under Latent Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05658)
*Tasha Kim,Benjamin Ballyk,Anson Lei,Ingmar Posner,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将脑-机器人辅助控制形式化为POMDP，证明后悔值存在由最差目标对Chernoff信息Γ_min决定的相变边界，并给出可从混淆矩阵O(K²)计算的闭式后悔预测器U_id；实验表明U_id能解释准确率之外的累积规划后悔，并揭示"准确率陷阱"（相同35.1%验证准确率下留出后悔相差9.6倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解码器评估沿用单试次分类与准确率指标，忽略了辅助控制的内在序列性：规划器需在时间上累积间歇、含噪的解码输出，单步准确率只是下游任务性能的代理指标，可能严重误导传感器与解码器选择。

Method: 将脑-机器人控制建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）；理论上证明后悔值的相变性质——只要所有目标对均解码可区分则后悔在全时域一致有界，否则线性增长，并以最差目标对的Chernoff信息Γ_min刻画该边界；进而推导闭式后悔预测器U_id(C)，可由任意混淆矩阵在O(K²)时间内计算。

Result: 在两个信息通道正交的领域上，U_id能够预测准确率无法解释的累积规划后悔；在真实EEG数据（BNCI2014001 受试者5）上出现"准确率陷阱"：两个解码器验证准确率同为35.1%，但留出后悔相差9.6倍，且该差异可由U_id预测；同信号控制实验以跨越独立特征通道的解码器进一步确认U_id的优势。

Conclusion: 单步准确率不足以评价序列辅助控制中的解码器；基于Γ_min的U_id提供了一种即插即用的诊断工具，可用于在序列化脑-机器人控制中评估与选择传感器及解码器。

Abstract: A central challenge in assistive brain-to-robot control is decoding a user's latent intent from complex and noisy brain signals. Prior approaches evaluate decoders as single-trial classifiers with accuracy-based metrics, but this neglects the inherently sequential nature of assistive control. Since a planner must integrate intermittent, noisy decoder outputs over time, single-step accuracy can only be a proxy when predicting downstream task performance. We formalize brain-to-robot control as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), and prove that there is a sharp phase transition: regret is uniformly bounded across the horizon as long as pairs of goals are all decoder-distinguishable, but grows linearly otherwise. We show that a single channel statistic, the worst-pair Chernoff information $Γ_{\min}$ sets this boundary. Then, we derive a closed-form regret predictor $U_{\mathrm{id}}(C)$, that can be computed from any confusion matrix in $O(K^2)$ time. Across two domains spanning orthogonal information channels, we show that $U_{\mathrm{id}}$ predicts cumulative planning regret beyond what accuracy can explain. We then highlight a pathological accuracy trap, in which two decoders have identical validation accuracy 35.1% on real EEG data (BNCI2014001 subject 5) but result in a difference of $9.6\times$ in held-out regret, which $U_{\mathrm{id}}$ predicts. Same-signal control confirms $U_{\mathrm{id}}$'s advantage with decoders that span independent feature channels. Ultimately, our work provides a drop-in diagnostic to evaluate and select sensors and decoders in sequential assistive control.

</details>


### [33] [Adaptive Domain Decomposition for Finite Basis Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05659)
*Sarah Helfert,Wanxin Chen,Amanda A. Howard,Panos Stinis,Alexander Heinlein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应域分解有限基物理信息神经网络（ADD-FBPINNs），将数据驱动的域划分学习网络POUNets与FBPINNs结合，实现自动、可解释且稳定的域划分，在多个二维及一维偏微分方程问题上精度优于固定域分解的FBPINNs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINN精度有限；FBPINNs通过用户设定的子域划分提升精度，但划分方式依赖人工经验，无法随解的特征自适应调整，导致子域划分不稳定或不够高效。

Method: 将可分离的Partition of Unity Networks（POUNets）用于学习域划分，与finite Basis PINNs（FBPINNs）耦合：POUNet依据解的特征生成子域划分，FBPINNs在各子域上以有限基展开求解，从而形成自适应域分解的训练框架。

Result: 在二维Helmholtz方程、具多尺度特征的二维Poisson方程以及黏性Burgers方程上测试，所提方法相比固定域分解的FBPINNs取得更高精度。

Conclusion: 自适应、可学习的域分解能够自动获得稳定且具几何可解释性的子域划分，并有效提升FBPINNs类方法的近似精度。

Abstract: In this work, we present adaptive domain decomposition finite basis physics-informed neural networks (ADD-FBPINNs). The proposed method integrates Partition of Unity Networks (POUNets), a data-driven approach for learning domain partitions, with finite Basis physics-informed neural networks (FBPINNs) to perform more efficient domain decomposition and achieve more accurate approximations. FBPINNs improve upon the accuracy of standard physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) through user-defined subdivisions of the computational domain. By adapting the domain decomposition based on the solution's features using a separable POUNet, the framework can automatically learn partitions that are stable and can be geometrically interpretable. We test this approach on the two-dimensional Helmholtz equation, two-dimensional Poisson equation with multiscale features, and viscous Burgers equation and observe improved accuracy compared with FBPINNs with fixed domain decomposition.

</details>


### [34] [Revisiting Critic Estimation in Soft Actor-Critic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05666)
*Jan Felix Kleuker,Koen Ponse,Aske Plaat,Thomas M. Moerland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地重新审视SAC中三类被忽视的critic设计选择（软优势学习、多步回报目标、从回放缓冲训练多步critic），在14个任务、完整随机超参搜索下比较六种critic变体，发现唯一有效改进是在回放轨迹片段上训练λ-return critic。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAC作为结合熵正则与actor-critic的深度强化学习算法被广泛使用，但标准实现几乎都依赖Q函数的一步时序差分学习，其他critic设计选择的潜力尚未被充分探索。作者希望系统性厘清这些替代方案是否真正带来收益。

Method: 固定策略目标不变，仅改变critic设计，生成六个变体：(i) 优势critic、(ii) dueling优势critic（均基于一步转移）；(iii, iv) λ-return与软GAE critic（on-policy）；(v, vi) 同样两种critic但从回放轨迹片段训练。每个变体在14个任务上跨完整随机超参数搜索进行评估，并将超参数视为算法的一部分。

Result: 用软优势替代软Q值并未提升性能；软GAE critic相对更简单的λ-return critic没有额外收益。真正提升性能（在连续控制任务上最明显）的是在回放轨迹片段上训练λ-return critic，且无需重要性加权或trace cutting。

Conclusion: 该结果将"未修正的多步目标在回放下有效"的早期证据扩展到包含熵项的完整软λ-return。所研究的思想本质上是通用的，作者希望这些实证结论能推广到更广泛的强化学习算法类别中。
