Abstract: Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) is a widely used deep reinforcement learning algorithm that combines entropy regularization with an actor-critic architecture. Standard implementations rely on one-step temporal-difference learning of Q-functions, yet alternative critic design choices remain underexplored. In this work, we revisit three such choices: learning soft advantages instead of soft Q-values, the use of multi-step return targets, and the training of multi-step critics from replay buffer data. Keeping the policy objective fixed, we compare the six critic variants these choices generate: (i) advantage and (ii) dueling-advantage critics learned from one-step transitions, and $λ$-return and soft Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE) critics learned either (iii, iv) on-policy or (v, vi) from replayed trajectory segments. Each variant is evaluated across an entire random hyperparameter search on fourteen tasks, treating the hyperparameters as part of the algorithm. Learning soft advantages instead of soft Q-values does not improve performance, and the soft GAE critic adds nothing over the simpler $λ$-return critic. What does improve performance, most clearly on continuous control, is training a $λ$-return critic on replayed trajectory segments, even without importance weighting or trace cutting. This extends earlier evidence that uncorrected multi-step targets work well under replay to the full soft $λ$-return, entropy terms included. By design, the underlying ideas studied here extend beyond SAC, and our empirical findings aim to encourage their application to a broader class of reinforcement learning algorithms.

</details>


### [35] [CoReFlow: Consistency-Relaxed Flow Maps for Probabilistic Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05715)
*Pengnan Chi,Zhiguo Zhang,Xiaoliang Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一致性松弛流映射模型CoReFlow，利用流映射的一步生成能力实现高精度、低推理成本的概率交通预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流模型通过多步采样建模不确定性，但推理慢，且其设计目标是捕捉复杂多峰分布，与条件交通数据分布的有限复杂度不匹配，导致性能不如确定性基线。

Method: 首次将概率流ODE解算子（流映射）的一步生成能力用于概率交通预测；采用简洁训练目标，通过有限时间区间上插值端点对模型进行监督；以条件均值约束一致性（而非自蒸馏、MeanFlow Identity等保持轨迹一致性的做法）。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上取得SOTA预测精度，优于近期基线；不确定性建模具有竞争力，且相比现有生成式基线降低推理开销。

Conclusion: 放松一致性约束的流映射方法在概率交通预测中兼顾精度与效率，优于多步生成的扩散/流模型基线。

Abstract: Probabilistic traffic forecasting is essential for reliable Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), as it captures uncertainties in complex road networks. Recent diffusion- and flow-based generative models have facilitated uncertainty modeling through a multi-step sampling process. However, beyond slow inference, this process is designed to capture complex multimodal distributions, which misaligns with the limited complexity of the conditional traffic data distribution, causing models to underperform deterministic baselines. To address this, we introduce the \underline{Co}nsistency-\underline{Re}laxed \underline{Flow} Map (CoReFlow) model, presenting the first effort to leverage the one-step generation capability of flow maps, known as solution operators of probability-flow ordinary differential equations. Besides, we improve accuracy using a concise training objective that supervises the model via interpolation endpoints over finite time intervals. Unlike existing methods (e.g., self-distillation and MeanFlow Identity) that maintain trajectory consistency to model multimodal distributions, our objective leverages conditional mean to restrict the consistency, thereby achieving higher accuracy in the probabilistic traffic forecasting task. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets show CoReFlow achieves state-of-the-art forecasting accuracy. It surpasses recent baselines, provides competitive uncertainty modeling, and reduces inference costs compared to existing generative baselines.

</details>


### [36] [Give the Drafter a Second Chance to Guess: Parallel Reconsideration for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05724)
*Hoang Anh Duy Le,Huibing Dong,Alireza Khadem,Ziteng Sun,Xiang Song,Yizheng Huang,Soroush Radpour,Anshumali Shrivastava,Ye He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Parallel Reconsideration机制：利用草稿模型自身的token级不确定性，对犹豫的猜测并行追加额外前向pass作为精化候选并入草稿树，从而提升接受长度与加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码的加速上限取决于每个猜测token的接受率。现有特征级草稿模型对所有token（无论简单或困难）一律采用单次前向pass生成，而被目标模型拒绝的token中有不少正是草稿模型自身不确定、目标模型却能明确判定的部分，单pass设计未能利用这一不确定性信号。

Method: 以草稿模型自身的不确定性为引导，对"犹豫"位置的猜测再执行额外的前向pass；这些额外pass与原单次草稿pass并行运行，几乎不增加延迟。同时设计了配套的训练配方与serving端的草稿算法，将重考虑得到的猜测作为精化候选追加到原始草稿树中。

Result: 在多种模型与基准上，相比基线平均将接受长度提升5.3%–10.2%，加速比提升3.5%–9.2%。

Conclusion: 将不确定度驱动的并行重考虑与草稿树候选扩充相结合，可在几乎零额延迟开销下有效提高投机解码的接受率与端到端加速，且具备跨模型、跨任务的通用性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference with a small drafter, whose per-guess acceptance rate determines the achievable speedup. Feature-level drafters share a design choice: every token, easy or hard, is drafted under a single forward pass. Many of the tokens the target rejects are ones the drafter is itself unsure about yet the target answers decisively. To address this, we introduce Parallel Reconsideration: guided by its own uncertainty, the drafter reconsiders its hesitant guesses with extra passes that run in parallel with the one-pass draft, adding negligible latency. A co-designed training recipe and serving-time drafting algorithm append these reconsidered guesses to the original draft tree as refined candidates. Across models and benchmarks, on average, Parallel Reconsideration improves over the baselines by 5.3% to 10.2% on acceptance length and by 3.5% to 9.2% on speedup.

</details>


### [37] [Do Language Models Know Which Constraints Belong Together?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05726)
*Junbo Jacob Lian,Kaixuan Chen,Hanzhang Qin,Huiling Chen,Chaoyu Zhang,Chung-Piaw Teo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Dantzig-Wolfe分解中选择约束族进行凸化的问题，证明仅观察界的搜索方法在零边际组上需要覆盖数级（Θ(k^w)）评估，提出分阶段搜索将成本降至Θ(k^e)以度量预测价值，并提出JointDW将语言模型预测与LP对偶前瞻结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dantzig-Wolfe重构可强化线性松弛，但每个候选约束族都需运行一次列生成，代价高；当多个约束族仅联合才能强化界时（如lot sizing、网络设计、设施选址中的经典不等式），逐个添加并停止的搜索会停在原线性松弛上，选择问题因此困难。

Method: 证明下界：若每次评估最多凸化k个族中的s个，仅观察界的方法需约k^w次评估才能找到隐藏的w族组，且在真实DW松弛上成立；提出一种无需已知e的分阶段搜索，使漏掉e个成员的预测将成本降至Θ(k^e)。研究两类预测来源：最优LP对偶上的一趟前瞻（对无共享变量的族组可证明"盲"）与读取模型名与文本的语言模型。JointDW用分阶段搜索验证语言模型预测，并以有界损失与前瞻交错。

Result: 在MIPLIB-NL中界可改进的模型里约四分之一存在仅联合强化界的组；最强语言模型识别这些组的频率约与前瞻相当，其对教科书模型的预测依赖名称，且推理可使同一模型准确率翻倍。在含干扰族的大型模型上，前瞻在三个族的一次评估中找到该组；仅给文本的语言模型很少命名它，而给出全部系数的推理模型几乎全部答对，且对族对所需评估少于前瞻。

Conclusion: 仅基于界的DW族选择在零边际组上存在固有的覆盖数复杂度下界；分阶段搜索可量化预测的信息价值，并实现可控的预测-搜索权衡；语言模型与LP对偶前瞻互补，JointDW在验证预测的同时保持有界损失，实证支持其在真实MIPLIB-NL模型上的有效性。

Abstract: Dantzig-Wolfe reformulation strengthens the linear relaxation of a mixed-integer program by convexifying a chosen set of constraint families, and every candidate costs a column generation run. The choice is hard when families strengthen the bound only together, because a search that adds one family at a time then stops at the linear relaxation. The classical inequalities of lot sizing, network design and facility location rest on such zero-marginal groups. We prove that when each evaluation convexifies at most $s$ of $k$ families, any method that observes only bounds needs a covering number of evaluations, of order $k^w$, to find a hidden group of $w$ families, also on actual Dantzig-Wolfe relaxations. A prediction that misses $e$ members reduces the cost to $Θ(k^e)$, through one staged search that does not need to know $e$, so the evaluations a prediction saves measure what the predictor knows. We study two sources of predictions. A one-pass lookahead at an optimal LP dual is provably blind to groups whose families share no variable, and a language model reads the names and text of the model. JointDW verifies the prediction of the language model with the staged search and interleaves it with the lookahead at a bounded loss. Groups that strengthen the bound only jointly appear in a quarter of the MIPLIB-NL models whose bound can be improved. There the strongest language models identify them about as often as the lookahead, their predictions rest on the names for textbook models, and reasoning about doubles the accuracy of the same model. On larger models with distracting families, the lookahead finds the group in one evaluation of three families. Language models given only the text seldom name it, while one reasoning model given every coefficient names it in all but one answer and then needs fewer evaluations than the lookahead for pairs of families.

</details>


### [38] [RESHPart: Feedback-Reshaped Spectral Embedding for Hypergraph Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05729)
*Rongjian Liang,Zhuo Feng,Brucek Khailany*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FRESHPart通过分析谱嵌入几何（类射线结构），利用拉普拉斯设计与超边重加权，以几何反馈与搜索反馈迭代重塑谱嵌入，在Titan23上取得优于先前最强结果的超图划分效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展拉普拉斯求解器已可用，下一步是更有效地利用它们实现高质量超图划分；需从谱嵌入几何角度重新审视谱超图划分，解释基于投影的划分为何有效并加以改进。

Method: 系统分析电路超图与受控合成（超）图，识别出类射线（ray-like）嵌入结构；借助拉普拉斯设计与超边重加权系统性地调控谱嵌入几何；提出FRESHPart反馈重塑框架，通过几何反馈（Geometry Feedback）与搜索反馈（Search Feedback）精炼谱嵌入，使其更契合投影式划分。

Result: 在Titan23基准上，ε=2%时相对hMETIS平均割降13.09%，ε=20%时降25.21%，均超过此前最强结果（12.16%与22.53%）；单GPU上每个基准均在120秒内完成。

Conclusion: 谱嵌入几何可被系统性设计与调控，反馈式嵌入重塑能显著提升投影式谱划分质量与效率，FRESHPart为可扩展的高质量超图划分提供了有效范式。

Abstract: With scalable Laplacian solvers now available, the next frontier is to exploit them more effectively for high-quality hypergraph partitioning. In this work, we revisit spectral hypergraph partitioning from the perspective of spectral embedding geometry. Through a systematic analysis of circuit hypergraphs and controlled synthetic (hyper)graphs, we identify a ray-like embedding structure that helps explain the effectiveness of projection-based partitioning. We further show that the geometry of spectral embeddings can be systematically influenced through Laplacian design and hyperedge reweighting. Guided by these insights, we propose FRESHPart, a feedback-reshaped spectral partitioning framework that refines the spectral embedding through Geometry Feedback and Search Feedback, producing embeddings that are better aligned with projection-based partitioning. On the Titan23 benchmark, FRESHPart achieves average cut reductions over hMETIS of 13.09% at $ε=2\%$ and 25.21% at $ε=20\%$, surpassing the strongest previously reported results (12.16% and 22.53%, respectively), while completing every benchmark in under 120 s on a single GPU.

</details>


### [39] [Self-Supervised Learning of Universal Latent Spaces for Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05735)
*Sepehr Mousavi,Levi Lingsch,Siddhartha Mishra,Laura De Lorenzis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“函数自编码器 + 冻结潜空间”的解耦范式：在合成解析函数上无标签自监督预训练表示，再在缓存嵌入上训练神经算子，实现与任务无关、精确等变、可解释且高效的PDE求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子把函数表示与具体任务耦合，每个新任务都要用标注数据重建低维约化空间，浪费计算与内存，表示无法复用；借鉴NLP/CV中自监督预训练使表示可共享的成功经验，作者希望将表示学习与算子学习解耦。

Method: 在运行时合成的解析函数（无求解器、无标签）上预训练一个函数自编码器，用于在任意点云上表示函数；其诱导的潜空间被冻结并跨任务复用。该表示对平移、伸缩（dilation）和幅值精确等变，可重构未见几何上的PDE解与自然图像。下游神经算子仅在缓存嵌入上训练，并支持分块推理处理超大规模点云。

Result: 潜空间整体可用且解码为有效场；保持物理场间距离序关系；测地线可插值物理演化；编辑方向可跨场迁移。流体、固体力学、声学及Helmholtz/Poisson共28个任务上，精度可超过SOTA端到端架构，吞吐量最高提升一个数量级，内存节省使模型规模可扩大近两个数量级并适配消费级GPU；推理时潜空间rollout快至一个数量级，分块推理可在单块消费级GPU上处理超5亿点。

Conclusion: 表示学习与算子学习的解耦是可行且高收益的：冻结的任务无关潜空间兼具等变性、可解释性与可迁移性，并在精度、速度、内存与规模上全面优于端到端神经算子，为科学机器学习提供了“预训练表示+轻量算子”的新范式。

Abstract: Neural operators fit their representation of functions to the task they are trained on, so every new task rebuilds a reduced space from labeled data. Following the self-supervised pre-training that made representations shareable in natural language processing and computer vision, we decouple representation learning from operator learning. A functional autoencoder is pre-trained on analytic functions synthesized on the fly, with no solver and no labels, to represent functions on arbitrary point clouds, and the latent space it induces is frozen and reused across tasks. The representation is exactly equivariant to translation, dilation, and amplitude, and reconstructs solutions of partial differential equations (PDEs) and natural images on geometries it never saw. Being task-agnostic, the latent space can be interpreted in its own right. The whole space is used and decodes to valid fields, it preserves the ordering of distances between physical fields, its geodesics interpolate physical evolutions, and edit directions transfer across fields. With frozen representation maps, a dataset is encoded once and a neural operator is trained entirely on cached embeddings, which saves memory and training time. On 28 tasks in fluid dynamics, solid mechanics, acoustics, and the Helmholtz and Poisson equations, this paradigm can exceed the accuracy of state-of-the-art end-to-end architectures, with up to an order of magnitude higher throughput and memory savings that fit models nearly two orders of magnitude larger on consumer-grade GPUs. At inference, latent rollouts run up to an order of magnitude faster than regular rollouts, and chunked inference processes functions of over half a billion points on a single consumer-grade GPU.

</details>


### [40] [Text-Only Domain Adaptation for Low-Resource Medical ASR: Shallow Fusion and Density Ratio Rescoring for Hausa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05744)
*Oche David Ankeli,Hassan Adelani Luqman,Theodora Ngozi Egbunike,Joshua Gottschalk,Bonaventure F. P. Dossou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在固定MMS-1B-FL102声学模型上，用仅文本的领域自适应方法（Shallow Fusion与Density Ratio Approach）改进豪萨语医疗语音识别，在提示词不相交的五折交叉验证下将WER从29.56%降至23.55%/23.33%，但医学语言模型相对通用语言模型的增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR系统在专业医学词汇上表现不佳，尤其低资源语言缺乏领域特定语音数据，无法通过采集领域语音来做自适应，因此需要探索仅利用文本的轻量级领域自适应方案。

Method: 保持声学模型MMS-1B-FL102固定，在解码阶段进行语言模型融合：以19,421句豪萨语健康领域句子训练4-gram KenLM作为医学目标模型，以75,372句通用领域模型作为DRA的源先验；对比Shallow Fusion（SF）与Density Ratio Approach（DRA）两种融合方式。评测数据为10位说话人、来自尼日利亚北部六州、25条经医师审核的提示词共250条录音，采用提示词不相交的五折交叉验证。

Result: SF与DRA分别将WER从29.56%降至23.55%与23.33%，二者均显著优于贪心解码（p < 0.001），但两者之间差异不显著（p = 0.423）。仅使用通用LM的SF即达到23.96% WER，说明医学LM相对通用LM的额外增益有限。医学术语错误率（MTER）在融合下亦有改善，但未能确立明确的医学领域优势。

Conclusion: 轻量级、仅文本的语言模型融合可在不额外采集领域语音数据的前提下有效提升豪萨语医疗ASR，但其收益更多来自通用语言模型先验而非医学领域知识；SF与DRA在此设定下效果相当。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems often struggle with specialised medical vocabulary, especially in low-resource languages where domain-specific speech data are scarce. We investigate text-only domain adaptation for Hausa medical ASR using the fixed MMS-1B-FL102 acoustic model and two decoding-time language-model fusion methods: Shallow Fusion (SF) and the Density Ratio Approach (DRA). A 4-gram KenLM trained on 19,421 Hausa health sentences serves as the medical target model, while a 75,372-sentence general-domain model provides the source prior for DRA. Evaluation uses 250 recordings of 25 physician-reviewed prompts from 10 speakers across six northern Nigerian states. Under prompt-disjoint five-fold cross-validation, SF and DRA reduce WER from 29.56% to 23.55% and 23.33%, respectively. Both significantly outperform greedy decoding (p < 0.001), while their difference is not significant (p = 0.423). However, general-LM-only SF achieves 23.96% WER, indicating that the medical LM's additional improvement over a general LM is modest. Medical Term Error Rate also improves under fusion, but does not establish a distinct medical-domain advantage. These findings demonstrate that lightweight, text-only language-model fusion can improve Hausa medical ASR without additional domain-specific speech collection.

</details>


### [41] [Optimizing Recourse Under Predictive Multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05774)
*Shahin Jabbari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将预测多重性下的鲁棒反事实解释（recourse）形式化为：在预算约束下，最小化竞争模型集合中的最坏情况有效性损失，并提出oracle高效算法ORPM及其收敛条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测多重性意味着性能相近的模型可能对个体预测产生分歧，因此针对单一模型生成的反事实建议在另一模型上可能失效，需要跨模型（含不同架构、不同假设类）都稳健的recourse。

Method: 将鲁棒recourse建模为预算约束下的最坏情况有效性损失最小化问题，竞争集可包含线性、神经网络、混合及决策树模型；提出oracle高效算法ORPM，并证明其收敛的充分条件。

Result: 在1个合成数据集和5个真实数据集上评估，竞争集涵盖线性、神经、混合与决策树模型；实验显示优化间隙上界递减、多设置下有效性高，优于针对特定模型类或架构设计的基线方法；并量化了在给定预算下要求跨模型采取共同动作对recourse成功率的影响，以及该成功率在不同人口群体间的差异。

Conclusion: ORPM能有效求解预测多重性下的鲁棒recourse问题，在最坏情况有效性上表现良好且具跨模型类泛化能力；同时揭示了共同动作约束与群体间成功率差异等权衡问题。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse identifies modifications that can change an unfavorable model prediction into a favorable one. Under predictive multiplicity, similarly performing models can disagree on individual predictions, so recourse valid for one model may fail for another. We formulate robust recourse under predictive multiplicity as a budget-constrained minimization of the worst-case validity loss across a competing set of models, which can include models with different architectures or from different hypothesis classes. We propose an oracle-efficient algorithm called ORPM and prove sufficient conditions for its convergence. We evaluate ORPM on one synthetic and five real datasets, and competing sets consist of linear, neural, mixed, and decision-tree models. Our experiments show decreasing optimization-gap bounds and high validity in many tested settings, with favorable performance against baselines designed for specific model classes or architectures. We also quantify how requiring a common action across competing models changes recourse success at a given budget and examine disparities in this success across demographic groups.

</details>


### [42] [Pathwise-Budgeted LLM Routing: Online Allocation with K-Bit Tail-Cost Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05835)
*Hanzhang Qin,Sophie H. Yu,Yuewei Zhang,Zhi-Hai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出上下文赌博机策略ADP-HT，在固定预算下同时决定调用哪个大模型以及是否重试失败任务，以最大化服务停止前解决的任务数；在预算最紧时，其MATH500与GPQA解出任务数超过最优基线两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定预算场景中，调用费用只有在完成后才揭晓，一次长响应可能耗尽剩余预算，导致后续任务无法服务；因此需要在"成功率—成本"之间权衡，并处理成本不确定性。核心目标是最大化预算耗尽前解出的任务数。

Method: 构建上下文赌博机策略ADP-HT：估计各模型在具体任务上的成功概率；将响应长度粗化为分位网格上的$2^K$个区间标签，据此构造保守成本估计；以预测成功与保守成本之比进行决策，并依据实际支出与剩余每任务可用预算动态调整成本惩罚系数。理论分析在标定、局部正则与网格覆盖条件下，给出保守成本估计的"超额允许量"界，揭示固定样本量下$K$的偏差—估计权衡。

Result: 在最紧的测试预算下，ADP-HT在MATH500和GPQA上解出的任务数是最佳已实现基线的两倍以上；在RouterBench的14个预算中，有10个取得最高的平均累积得分；在LiveCodeBench三个最低测试预算上，均优于已部署的商用路由器（默认与省钱两种模式）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将成本估计保守化并与预算自适应耦合，可有效缓解事后计费带来的预算风险；ADP-HT在多基准、多预算档位上稳定优于强基线，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Firms using large language models under fixed budgets must decide which model to call and whether to retry a failed task. Because a call's charge is revealed only after completion, one long response can exhaust the remaining budget and leave later tasks unserved. Our objective is to maximize the number of tasks solved before service stops. We develop a contextual bandit policy, ADP-HT, which learns each model's task-specific success probability and constructs conservative cost estimates from coarsened response-length labels on a quantile grid with $2^K$ intervals. The policy weighs predicted success against these costs, adjusting the cost penalty to realized spending and the budget available per remaining task. Under calibration, local regularity, and grid-coverage conditions, we bound the excess allowance in the policy's conservative cost estimates. At a fixed sample size, increasing $K$ reduces the discretization term but increases the estimation allowance. At the tightest tested budgets, ADP-HT solves more than twice as many MATH500 and GPQA tasks as the best implemented baseline. It achieves the highest mean cumulative score at ten of fourteen RouterBench budgets. On LiveCodeBench, it outperforms a deployed commercial router in both default and cost-saving modes at the three lowest test budgets.

</details>


### [43] [PEARL: Prediction Error-Adaptive Regularized Learning for Selective Forgetting under Partial Preference Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05842)
*Rashmi Gangadharaiah,Balakrishnan Narayanaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEARL提出一种按预测误差定向的Gram矩阵“逆向更新”机制，在偏好发生局部漂移时只让出错的维度丧失置信度，从而实现选择性遗忘，理论上regret只随漂移用户数N_s增长，PRISM基准上显著优于贪心与神经网络bandit基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户偏好会随时间与情境发生漂移，导致个性化推荐、LLM回复生成及RLHF奖励模型出现失准。现有非平稳bandit方法对所有维度统一遗忘，而真实偏好漂移往往是局部的（部分维度失效、其余仍有效）；神经上下文bandit依赖Gram矩阵单调累积置信度，无法把不确定性重新定向到预测出错的维度。

Method: 提出PEARL（Prediction Error-Adaptive Regularized Learning）：当预测出现“意外错误”时，沿对应的特征方向反向执行Gram更新（削减该方向置信度），其余方向保持置信度不变，实现非均匀、按需的选择性遗忘。理论层面证明三条结论：(i) 任何非自适应策略在偏好漂移后都会产生线性regret；(ii) 不存在固定探索率能同时在平稳与非平稳区间取得低regret；(iii) PEARL的累积regret随漂移用户数N_s而非总人数N增长。实现上采用约100K参数的选择策略MLP，CPU毫秒级推理，LLM权重冻结。

Result: 在PRISM（1,500用户、21个LLM的个性化响应选择基准）上，PEARL较贪心提升4.0分、较最佳神经bandit基线提升2.5分（p<0.001）；偏好稳定时与贪心持平；较滑动窗口与折扣方法提升3–10分，表明选择性遗忘优于均匀遗忘。

Conclusion: 偏好漂移具有局部性，按预测误差定向地“有选择地遗忘”比全局统一遗忘更有效；PEARL在理论上获得与漂移用户规模挂钩的更优regret界，并在实践中以轻量、模型无关的方式显著提升个性化LLM响应选择效果。

Abstract: User preferences shift as needs, context, and tastes evolve. This matters for personalized recommendation, LLM response generation, and RLHF-trained reward models, which can become miscalibrated as preferences drift. Existing non-stationary bandit methods forget uniformly across all dimensions, but real preference shifts are typically partial: some dimensions go stale while others remain valid. Neural contextual bandits accumulate confidence monotonically via the Gram matrix, with no mechanism to direct uncertainty where predictions have gone wrong. We propose PEARL (Prediction Error-Adaptive Regularized Learning), which reverses the Gram update along feature directions where predictions are surprisingly wrong, while leaving confidence intact elsewhere. We prove that (i) any non-adaptive policy suffers linear regret after a preference shift, (ii) no fixed exploration rate achieves low regret in both stationary and non-stationary regimes, and (iii) PEARL's cumulative regret scales with the number of shifted users N_s, not the total population N. On PRISM, a personalized LLM response selection benchmark (1,500 users, 21 LLMs), PEARL beats greedy by +4.0 points and the best neural bandit baseline by +2.5 points (p<0.001), while performing on par with greedy when preferences are stable. It beats sliding-window and discounted approaches by 3 to 10 points, showing that selective forgetting outperforms uniform forgetting. The selection policy is a ~100K-parameter MLP that runs in milliseconds on CPU, leaves LLM weights frozen, and works with any API-based model.

</details>


### [44] [A low-rank decoder bottleneck bounds reliability in foundation-model perturbation prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05849)
*Zheyu Zhang,Yuanhao Huang,Fan Feng,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对STATE模型中状态转移(ST)解码器的预测可靠性进行审计，提出以"预测响应到最近训练响应的余弦距离"(predicted-NN-distance)作为逐预测置信度分数，其与PDC的Spearman相关均值达0.81，且校准阈值可跨编码器与细胞系迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型的扰动响应预测主要按聚合准确率评估，但用于实验优先级排序时需评估单个预测的可靠性，现有评估无法满足逐预测置信度需求。

Method: 审计STATE中ST解码器（SE-600M、scGPT、Tahoe-x1三种编码器，四种CRISPRi细胞系）；发现其首层注意力输出为低秩，将其投影到由训练激活估计的4–32个固定方向可保持平均PDC；据此分析可靠性随"观测响应到最近训练响应的余弦距离"变化的规律，并提出predicted-NN-distance作为置信度分数，并以该分数校准阈值。

Result: 低秩注意力瓶颈将解码器可靠性限制在与训练响应的距离上：PDC随最近邻余弦距离增大而下降；predicted-NN-distance与PDC的平均绝对Spearman相关为0.81（基于观测响应为0.88）；基于该分数校准的阈值可跨编码器与细胞系迁移，且对药物扰动仍具预测力。

Conclusion: 逐预测可靠性可由低秩瓶颈与训练响应距离刻画，predicted-NN-distance是有效、可迁移的置信度分数，可用于扰动实验的优先级排序与决策。

Abstract: Foundation-model predictors of perturbation response are evaluated primarily by aggregate accuracy. Using their predictions to prioritize experiments requires assessing each prediction's reliability. We audit the state-transition (ST) decoder used in STATE across three encoders (SE-600M, scGPT, and Tahoe-x1) and four cell lines under CRISPRi. The decoder's first attention layer has a low-rank output. Projecting this output onto 4-32 fixed directions estimated from training activations preserves mean per-perturbation Pearson delta correlation (PDC). This low-rank attention bottleneck bounds the decoder's reliability by distance to training responses: PDC declines as the cosine distance from a perturbation's observed response to its nearest training response increases. We propose predicted-NN-distance, the cosine distance from a predicted response to its nearest training response, as a per-prediction confidence score. The score has a mean absolute Spearman correlation of 0.81 with PDC, close to the 0.88 obtained with the observed response. Thresholds calibrated on this score transfer across encoders and cell lines, and the score remains predictive of PDC for drug perturbations.

</details>


### [45] [On Convergence of Gradient Temporal-Difference Learning with Regularized Corrections and Eligibility Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05869)
*Jingyi Jin,Martha White,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立约束TDRC(λ)在递减步长、非i.i.d.离策略数据（含状态依赖与历史依赖λ）下的收敛性，并提出保持同一定点的TDRC(λ)-Huber，给出基于ODE弱收敛方法与积分小增益条件的可计算收敛充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TD学习在离策略、自举与函数逼近结合时可能发散。TDRC理论可靠且实现简单，但现有收敛理论仅覆盖i.i.d.采样下的一步情形，且Huber Bellman误差形式尚未扩展到TDRC或资格迹，因此需要更一般的收敛分析与稳健变体。

Method: 采用随机逼近理论中的ODE弱收敛方法分析约束TDRC(λ)；对TDRC(λ)-Huber的非线性均值动力学给出积分小增益条件以证明目标平衡点的唯一性与全局渐近稳定性；进而建立约束算法收敛性并推导可计算充分条件。

Result: 在递减步长与非i.i.d.离策略数据下证明了约束TDRC(λ)（含状态依赖和历史依赖λ）的收敛；TDRC(λ)-Huber在保持TDRC(λ)不动点的同时降低对大Bellman误差的敏感性；识别出线性TDRC(λ)与TDRC(λ)-Huber共有的基于正则化的收敛机制；当均值TD矩阵正稳定时，两算法在足够强正则化下无需正定性即可收敛。

Conclusion: 将TDRC的收敛理论推广至资格迹与非i.i.d.离策略设定，并把Huber型Bellman误差稳健化扩展到TDRC；提供可计算的收敛判据与基于正则化的收敛机制解释，说明正则化可替代正定性假设。

Abstract: Temporal-difference (TD) learning underlies many prediction and control algorithms, but can diverge when combined with off-policy learning, bootstrapping, and function approximation. TD with Regularized Corrections (TDRC) is theoretically sound and practically simple, but existing convergence theory covers only the one-step case under i.i.d. sampling, while the Huber Bellman-error formulation has not been extended to TDRC or eligibility traces. In this work, we establish convergence of constrained TDRC($λ$) with diminishing stepsizes and non-i.i.d. off-policy data, for state-dependent and history-dependent $λ$-parameters. We also propose TDRC($λ$)-Huber, which preserves the TDRC($λ$) fixed point while reducing sensitivity to large Bellman errors. For both algorithms, we apply an ODE-based weak-convergence method from stochastic approximation theory. For the nonlinear mean dynamics of TDRC($λ$)-Huber, we give an integral small-gain condition that guarantees uniqueness and global asymptotic stability of the target equilibrium. We then establish convergence of the constrained algorithm, derive computable sufficient conditions for convergence, and identify a regularization-based convergence mechanism shared by linear TDRC($λ$) and TDRC($λ$)-Huber. When the mean TD matrix is positive stable, both constrained algorithms converge under sufficiently strong regularization without requiring positive definiteness.

</details>

*Muhammad Hasnain,N. K. Anand,Arun Srinivasa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MIGONet多重输入图神经算子与离散制造解方法(MMS)，无需解PDE即可低成本生成满足离散控制方程的稳态传热训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输运代理建模需要同时刻画空间分布源项与Dirichlet/Neumann边界条件的影响，且训练数据生成成本需足够低。

Method: MIGONet在局部消息传递前向每个节点提供完整源场与边界数据的表示；离散MMS预设温度场，用与参考有限差分法相同的离散算子反算源项与边界数据，使样本按构造满足离散方程，且边界条件类型作为算子输入。

Result: 曲导线与高斯热斑算例平均相对温度误差为10.31%与10.64%，MIONet为45.97%与48.34%，MIGONet-local为72.25%与67.82%；10000样本数据生成由486.65 s降至3.15 s；并在L形、圆盘形、环形域上验证。

Conclusion: 完整源场编码显著提升精度，离散MMS在保证物理一致性的同时大幅降低数据生成开销，模型具备跨几何域泛化能力。

Abstract: Surrogate modeling of transport requires operators that capture the combined influence of spatially distributed sources and Dirichlet and Neumann boundary conditions, together with training datasets that can be generated at low computational cost. This work presents MIGONet, a multiple-input graph neural operator, and a discrete method of manufactured solutions (MMS) that trains it without solving a partial differential equation, using steady heat conduction as the model problem. In the discrete MMS procedure, temperature fields are prescribed a priori, and the source terms and Dirichlet or Neumann boundary data are evaluated with the same discrete operators as the reference finite-difference method (FDM), so every training sample satisfies the discrete governing equations by construction. Because the boundary data are derived from each field, a Dirichlet or Neumann condition can be assigned to every boundary segment of every sample, and the boundary specification becomes an operator input. MIGONet supplies representations of the complete source field and boundary data to every node before local message passing. On the rectangular domain, it is compared with a version without the complete-source encoder (MIGONet-local) and with the multiple-input operator network MIONet, using the same training data. MIGONet achieves mean relative temperature errors of 10.31% and 10.64% for curved-wire and Gaussian-spot heating, compared with 45.97% and 48.34% for MIONet and 72.25% and 67.82% for MIGONet-local. For 10,000 samples, discrete MMS reduces data-generation time from 486.65 s to 3.15 s relative to matched iterative FDM labeling. Separately trained MIGONet models are also evaluated on L-shaped, disc-shaped, and ring-shaped domains.

</details>


### [47] [Deep Meets Classical: A CPU-Efficient Decision-Fusion Model for Automatic Modulation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05937)
*Zhiyi Yin,Zhizhao Wen,Ruoxin Zhang,Chao Wang,Puyang Xu,Yifan Jiang,Yusi Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FuseAMC将残差CNN与梯度提升树累积量分类器的后验拼接后用逻辑回归融合，在RadioML 2016.10a上以约0.2M参数、单CPU核实现55.8%总体准确率，优于两单独基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMC中两类CPU可行的标准方法——原始I/Q上的紧凑CNN与基于高阶累积量特征的经典分类器——鲜有在SNR条件下相互比较。作者指出二者互补：CNN在低SNR更稳健，累积量分类器在高SNR更准确，故值得显式融合。

Method: 先以相同条件对比两种方法在各SNR段的表现，确认互补性；再构建FuseAMC：约198k参数残差CNN与梯度提升树累积量分类器，将其类别后验拼接，在独立于测试集的验证集上训练逻辑回归元组合器进行决策级融合。

Result: RadioML 2016.10a，5个随机种子，44k帧留出测试集：总体准确率55.8%，较最佳累积量基线高5.1个点、较单独CNN高3.2个点；配对bootstrap 95%置信区间下界分别为5.0与3.1，均为正。在高SNR段匹配经典基线最佳表现，在低SNR段匹配CNN表现。

Conclusion: CNN与累积量分类器可互补，轻量决策级融合在不需硬件加速的单CPU核上即可获一致增益；模型约0.2M参数、数MB占用、约3 MFLOPs/帧，适合通用及嵌入式处理器。

Abstract: Automatic modulation classification (AMC) underpins spectrum sensing and non-cooperative signal identification in cognitive radio, yet the two standard CPU-feasible approaches to it, a compact convolutional neural network (CNN) on raw in-phase and quadrature (I/Q) samples and a classical higher-order-cumulant feature classifier, are rarely compared against each other conditioned on signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). We show that they are, in fact, complementary: the CNN is markedly more robust at low SNR, where hand-crafted statistics collapse toward chance, while the cumulant classifier is more accurate at high SNR, where higher-order statistics cleanly separate dense constellations. We exploit this complementarity with FuseAMC, a lightweight decision-fusion model that trains a logistic-regression meta-combiner over the concatenated class-posteriors of an approximately 198k-parameter residual CNN and a gradient-boosted-tree cumulant classifier, on a validation split disjoint from test. On RadioML 2016.10a (5 seeds, 44k-frame held-out test set), FuseAMC reaches 55.8% overall accuracy, improving on the best cumulant-feature baseline by 5.1 points and on the CNN alone by 3.2 points (paired-bootstrap 95% confidence-interval lower bounds of 5.0 and 3.1 points, both positive), while matching the best classical baseline in its own strong high-SNR regime and the CNN in its strong low-SNR regime. With about 0.2 million parameters, a model footprint of a few megabytes, and roughly 3 MFLOPs per frame, the whole pipeline trains and runs on one CPU core, targeting general-purpose and embedded processors without hardware acceleration.

</details>


### [48] [BAM: Bivariate Additive Models for Interpretable Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05940)
*Atsushi Koike*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Bivariate Additive Models（BAMs），一种广义可加模型与成对交互（GA²Ms）的子类，将每个成对形状函数约束为不对称双变量高斯形式的简单参数化模型，使每个组件呈单峰等高线图，从而恢复可审计性，同时通过自动选择特征对维持预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GA²Ms 虽被视为"玻璃盒"模型（每个成对形状函数可作等高线图可视化），但 EBM、NODE-GAM 等方法学到的形状函数过于复杂（不规则等高线、尖锐局部波动），实践者难以真正解释或对照领域知识检验。若要求更强意义的可审计性，需要显著更简单的形状函数；作者主张只要捕获正确的特征对，这种简化仍可保持标准 GAM 的精度。

Method: 提出 BAMs 一类 GA²Ms：每个成对形状函数被约束为简单参数形式，具体实例化为不对称双变量高斯，使每个组件可视化为单峰等高线图；特征对在训练过程中自动选择。

Result: 在六个表格基准数据集上的实验表明，BAM 的预测准确率与标准 GAM 相当，并接近 GA²Ms 的水平；同时展示了精度与需检查组件数量之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 通过将成对形状函数限制为可解释的单峰参数形式并自动选择特征对，BAM 在保持接近 GA²Ms 预测性能的同时实现了更强意义的可审计性，为玻璃盒模型提供了可解释性与精度之间的可控折中。

Abstract: Generalized Additive Models with pairwise interactions (GA$^2$Ms) are regarded as glass-box models, since each pairwise shape function can be visualized as a contour plot. In practice, the shape functions learned by methods such as EBM or NODE-GAM are so complex (irregular contours and sharp local fluctuations) that a practitioner cannot meaningfully interpret them or check them against domain knowledge. Auditability in this stronger sense requires substantially simpler shape functions. We argue that such simplification can match the accuracy of standard GAMs if the right feature pairs are captured. We propose Bivariate Additive Models (BAMs), a class of GA$^2$Ms in which every pairwise shape function is constrained to a simple parametric form, instantiated as an asymmetric bivariate Gaussian. Each component is visualizable as a unimodal contour map, and its feature pair is selected automatically during training. Experiments on six tabular benchmarks show that BAM attains predictive accuracy comparable to standard GAMs and approaches that of GA$^2$Ms. We also show the trade-off between accuracy and the number of components to inspect.

</details>


### [49] [Reward Budgeting Reduces Premature Convergence in Reinforcement Learning for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05942)
*Mengni Jia,Mengyu Zhou,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从RL的隐式目标策略出发，提出锐度因子κ及其有效版本κ_eff来刻画奖励/优势差距向概率比差距的转换强度，并据此提出"奖励预算"（reward budgeting）方法：为每个prompt分配固定总奖励预算。理论表明其在二元奖励下等价于精确有限G凹目标的梯度，实验上在多个基线上一致提升，AMC23最高+12.50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练LLM推理时，策略分布趋于尖锐导致性能过早进入平台期。已有工作将策略熵作为诊断与控制信号，但实验发现仅提高熵并不能可靠改变最终平台，因此需要从更新动力学角度重新解释早熟收敛机制。

Method: 通过隐式目标策略分析RL，推导锐度因子κ与有效锐度κ_eff，描述优势差距如何映射为概率比差距；提出reward budgeting——一种即插即用式优势构造，为每个prompt设定固定总奖励预算。对二元奖励与独立on-policy rollout，其未裁剪score-function估计器对应一个精确有限G凹目标（边际收益递减）的梯度，且当更多rollout正确时优势差距缩小；一般奖励下在一定正则条件下具有平移对数均值解释。裁剪版本仅作动机性论证而非严格等价。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B与Llama3.2-3B-Instruct上，reward budgeting一致优于多种RL基线，AMC23最高提升+12.50，AIME24与AIME25各提升+10.00。

Conclusion: 结果支持以更新动力学视角理解早熟收敛，并表明无需显式保持熵也能获得显著性能提升。

Abstract: RL for LLM reasoning often reaches an early performance plateau as the policy distribution becomes increasingly sharp. Recent work has studied policy entropy as both a diagnostic and a control signal for this behavior. In our experiments, however, increasing entropy alone does not reliably change the eventual plateau. We analyze RL through its implicit target policy and derive a sharpness factor $κ$, together with an effective counterpart $κ_{\mathrm{eff}}$, that describes how strongly reward or advantage gaps are translated into probability-ratio gaps. Motivated by this view, we introduce \emph{reward budgeting}, a plug-in advantage construction that assigns a fixed total reward budget to each prompt. For binary rewards and independent on-policy rollouts, its unclipped score-function estimator follows the gradient of an exact finite-$G$ concave objective with diminishing returns, while its advantage gap decreases as more rollouts become correct. For general rewards, a shifted log-mean interpretation holds asymptotically under the stated regularity conditions. These results motivate, rather than establish exact objective equivalence for, the clipped implementations. On Qwen3-8B and Llama3.2-3B-Instruct, reward budgeting consistently improves a range of RL baselines, with gains up to +12.50 on AMC23 and +10.00 on AIME24 and AIME25. Overall, our results support an update-dynamics view of premature convergence and show that strong gains can be obtained without explicitly preserving entropy.

</details>


### [50] [On the Design of Hyper-Connections: Simplification, Stability, and Scalability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05984)
*Xiangdong Zhang,Xiaohan Qin,Sunan Zou,Tuo Dai,Bizhao Shi,Zhuo Xia,Yebin Yang,Yizhou Chen,Yuliang Liu,Yu Cheng,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统剖析 Hyper-Connections 架构，指出"无混合的学习门控"与"固定门控的混合"是两组等效有效交互，并据此提出 SimpleHC：去除残差混合、以共享内存访问与延迟写入实现，最大规模下仅增加 2.4% 端到端训练时间并保持与 mHC 相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HC 架构在前沿语言模型中被广泛采用，但其各组件作用机制理解不足，且带来不可忽视的训练开销。作者希望厘清各组件功能，并寻找更简单、更高效的等价设计。

Method: 通过系统性消融分析识别有效交互组：仅保留学习式读/写门控（无混合）或仅保留固定门控的混合均可达到 mHC 水平；定位重复访问扩展残差状态为训练开销主因。据此设计 SimpleHC：移除残差混合，利用加法结构与共享门控输入实现共享内存访问；层特异门控共享组入口状态以批量读取与延迟写入；用标量修正精确保持前一子层输出贡献。

Result: 最大测试规模下端到端训练时间仅比原生残差基线多 2.4%；15B→30B MoE 规模下性能与 mHC 相当；标准训练中梯度动态更平滑，且在压力条件下保持稳定。

Conclusion: HC 的有效性可由更简单的结构承载；SimpleHC 在几乎无额外开销下取得可比性能与更好稳定性，倡导基础模型设计中以简洁性换取可扩展、可落地的性能收益。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) architectures are increasingly adopted in frontier language models, yet their components remain insufficiently understood and introduce non-negligible training overhead. In this work, we systematically study the HC architecture to understand its components and identify simpler, more efficient designs. We find that learned read/write gating without mixing and unconstrained mixing with fixed gates form two effective interaction groups: either alone achieves performance comparable to mHC. We also identify repeated access to the expanded residual state as a major source of training overhead, even without mixing. These findings lead to SimpleHC, which simplifies the architecture by removing residual mixing and simplifies execution through shared memory access, using the resulting additive structure and shared gate inputs. Layer-specific gates share a group-entry state, enabling batched reads and deferred writes; scalar corrections exactly preserve preceding sublayer output contributions. Our analyses support both the retained residual structure and the design of shared memory access. SimpleHC incurs only 2.4% extra end-to-end training time over a vanilla residual baseline at the largest tested scale. It maintains performance comparable to mHC when scaling from 15B to 30B MoE models, exhibits smoother gradient dynamics during standard training, and remains stable under stress. We hope SimpleHC will encourage a broader pursuit of simplicity in foundation model design, making performance gains more accessible and practical at scale.

</details>


### [51] [A Comparative Study of Importance Attribution to Columns in Deep Learning over Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05986)
*Hila Levi-Yosefi,Shunit Agmon,Ester Livshits,Agapi Rissaki,Benny Kimelfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套系统比较关系型深度学习（RDL）模型中属性重要性归因方法的框架，从属性排序与分数分布两个维度刻画解释差异，并考察其与保真度（fidelity）的关系；在异构GraphSAGE与关系图Transformer上实证比较Monte Carlo Shapley、Kernel SHAP、单属性影响与mask learning四种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据库上的深度学习预测模型日益有效，解释其行为的需求不断增长；但归因方法的选择究竟在多大程度上影响解释结论尚不清楚，缺乏系统的比较方法学与实证依据。

Method: 构建比较重要性归因方法的方法学，从两方面度量解释差异：属性重要性排序差异与重要性分数分布差异，并检验这些差异是否反映在传统解释质量指标fidelity中。实证上在多个数据集与任务、两种RDL架构（异构GraphSAGE、关系图Transformer）上比较四种方法：Monte Carlo Shapley、Kernel SHAP、单属性影响（single-attribute impact）与mask learning。

Result: 解释方法的行为随预测类型而异：回归任务中多数方法在属性排序上高度一致，分类任务中一致性显著下降；mask learning的重要性分布常出现偏离，而基于Shapley的方法与单属性影响往往收敛到相似解释。这表明在许多场景下单属性影响可以以显著更低的计算成本替代Shapley。

Conclusion: 研究提供了比较重要性归因方法的方法学工具，并给出实证证据表明归因方法的选择对解释关系型深度学习模型具有重要影响。

Abstract: Deep learning over relational databases has led to increasingly effective prediction models, motivating a growing need to explain their behavior. We study explanation methods that assign importance scores to individual database attributes, focusing on a fundamental question: to what extent does the choice of importance-attribution method matter? We develop a methodology to systematically compare importance-attribution methods and analyze their similarities and differences. In particular, we study two aspects of the explanations: which attributes they deem important, captured by differences in attribute rankings, and how importance scores vary across attributes, captured by differences in score distributions. We further investigate whether these differences are reflected in the conventional measure of explanation quality, fidelity.
  We apply our methodology in an extensive empirical study of four methods: Monte Carlo Shapley, Kernel SHAP, single-attribute impact, and mask learning. We experiment with multiple datasets and tasks using two RDL architectures: heterogeneous GraphSAGE and Relational Graph Transformers. Our experiments show that explanation methods behave differently depending on the prediction type: regression or classification. In regression, most methods exhibit high agreement in attribute rankings, whereas classification yields considerably less consensus. Notably, while mask learning frequently diverges in its importance distributions, Shapley-based methods and single-attribute impact often converge to similar explanations. This suggests that, in many settings, single-attribute impact can replace Shapley at substantially lower computational cost. Overall, our study provides methodological tools for comparing importance-attribution methods, and empirical evidence that their choice has important consequences for interpreting relational deep learning models.

</details>


### [52] [Decouple to Reuse: Fine-Grained Knowledge Augmentation for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05993)
*Yuqian Wu,Qingxiang Liu,Yuxin Guo,Weifeng Su,Jian Li,Pengfei Xu,Xi Chen,Yutao Yue,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段细粒度知识增强框架（\method），将预训练知识解耦并固化为任务共享的残差码本，用于增强冻结的时序基础模型，在八个真实基准与四个骨干网络上验证了效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练时序基础模型（TSFM）迁移到下游任务存在域偏移；现有检索增强方法通常检索粗粒度窗口上下文，易引入上下文特定噪声，且未考虑趋势与季节动态可能源自不同上下文。

Method: 主张在分解层面复用时序模式。Stage I：为解耦的趋势与季节信号分别构建双分支残差码本，将预训练知识压缩为紧凑、任务共享的码本；Stage II：通过任务自适应聚合与门控融合，动态检索并合并细粒度模式到目标表示中，骨干网络保持冻结。

Result: 在八个真实世界基准与四种代表性 TSFM 骨干上实验，方法与计算效率均优于基线；残差码本在不同骨干间具有较强可迁移性。

Conclusion: 细粒度、分解级别的知识复用优于粗粒度窗口检索，是一种高效且可迁移的冻结 TSFM 增强方案；代码已开源。

Abstract: Time series forecasting is critical for demand prediction and resource management in modern Web services, yet applying pretrained Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) to downstream tasks remains challenging due to domain shifts. Existing retrieval-augmented approaches typically retrieve coarse-grained window contexts and may introduce context-specific noise. To address this problem, we argue that temporal patterns should be reused at a decomposition level, as effective trend and seasonal dynamics may originate from different contexts. We propose \method, a two-stage \underline{F}ine-grained \underline{K}nowledge \underline{A}ugmentation framework that decouples and consolidates pre-training knowledge into compact, task-shared residual codebooks to enhance frozen TSFMs. Specifically, Stage I constructs dual-branch residual codebooks for decoupled trend and seasonal signals, while Stage II dynamically retrieves and merges these fine-grained patterns into target representations via task-adaptive aggregation and gate fusion. Extensive experiments across eight real-world benchmarks with four representative TSFM backbones demonstrate the superior effectiveness and computational efficiency of \method, while further validating the strong transferability of the residual codebooks across different backbones. The source code is available at the anonymous repository: https://github.com/yuppielqx/TSFM-Component-Token-Adapter

</details>


### [53] [How Far Can You Steer? Behavior-Capability Frontiers in Contrastive Activation Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06001)
*Deepanshu Raj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过"行为—能力前沿"框架研究CAA激活引导：以能力损失预算为约束最大化行为增益，在4个指令微调模型、7种行为和1%/3%/5%预算下评估，发现可用的引导范围高度依赖于模型、行为、方向、层与评估预算，而非通用控制旋钮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导（如CAA）无需更新参数即可改变语言模型行为，但仅凭行为有效性无法判断干预是否可用；CAA基于对比样本得到的激活方向可能同时损害无关能力。因此需明确刻画行为收益与能力退化之间的权衡关系。

Method: 提出行为—能力前沿（behavior-capability frontier）框架：选择在显式能力退化上界内最大化行为增益的干预。实验覆盖4个指令微调模型、7种行为，并设置1%、3%、5%（绝对百分点）的能力损失预算；采用验证集选择并检验对留出数据的泛化，辅以对照实验（区分学习到的行为方向与通用激活扰动）、表征几何预测性分析，以及次级能力、OOD与开放式生成评估。

Result: 验证集选出的行为效应在留出数据上泛化良好（ρ=0.945）；非零工作点下行为—能力联合存活率从1%预算的37.5%升至3%的72.7%，5%预算仅有有限额外提升。对照实验表明效果依赖于学习到的行为方向而非一般激活扰动；表征几何对因果性跨行为迁移有显著但仅中等程度的预测力。次级能力、OOD与开放式评估显示，满足受保护基准的干预仍可能损害未测试能力，或在自由生成中迁移不均衡。

Conclusion: 激活引导应被视为一种受约束的干预：其可用范围取决于模型、行为、方向、层与评估预算，而不是通用控制旋钮；仅有行为有效性不足以判断可用性，须结合显式能力预算与多维度评估。

Abstract: Activation steering can modify language-model behavior without parameter updates, but behavioral efficacy alone does not determine whether an intervention is usable. Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA) steers targeted behaviors using activation directions derived from contrasting examples, potentially degrading unrelated model capabilities in the process. We therefore study CAA through a behavior-capability frontier, selecting interventions that maximize behavioral gain subject to an explicit bound on capability degradation. We evaluate this framework across four instruction-tuned models, seven behaviors, and capability-loss budgets of 1%, 3%, and 5% (absolute percentage points). Validation-selected behavioral effects generalize strongly to held-out data ($ρ= 0.945$), while joint behavior-capability survival among nonzero operating points rises from 37.5% at the 1% budget to 72.7% at 3%, with limited additional improvement at 5%. Control experiments indicate that these effects depend on learned behavior directions rather than generic activation perturbations. Representational geometry is significantly but only modestly predictive of causal cross-behavior transfer. Finally, secondary capability, out-of-distribution, and open-ended evaluations show that interventions satisfying protected benchmarks can still degrade untested capabilities or transfer unevenly to free-form generation. These results characterize activation steering as a constrained intervention whose usable range depends on model, behavior, direction, layer, and evaluation budget, rather than a universal control knob.

</details>


### [54] [Attraction-Ranking Mismatch in Out-of-Distribution Detection: Theory and Evidence from Hamiltonian Feature Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06021)
*Zilin Wang,Lianghai Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以类别条件哈密顿动力学为受控试验台，检验"将特征吸引到ID区域能否提升OOD检测"这一直觉，指出存在"吸引—排序错配"，AUROC变化主要由ID-OOD排序边界处的相对分数变化决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OOD检测中普遍采用将特征吸引到学习的ID区域（类吸引/特征精炼）以提升检测性能，但这一策略的有效性缺乏机制性论证，需要回答类亲和分数提升是否能真正改善ID与OOD样本的相对排序。

Method: 以class-conditioned Hamiltonian dynamics作为可控测试床；区分驱动场与检测分数之间的失配，与二者对齐后仍存在的排序失败；提出精确AUROC分解与局部边界分析，在正则条件下定位一阶AUROC变化的来源为排序边界处的相对分数变化；设计分数对齐规则的跨域迁移实验与近边界扰动研究；并通过质心近似与共享尺度将零步分数联系到包含CTM检测器的静态质心族。

Result: 分数对齐规则可在留出的源域验证划分上提升性能，但迁移后一致性地退化，且全程无任何获胜类别改变；扰动研究表明近边界相对分数变化与小幅AUROC变化方向一致；质心近似与共享尺度表明零步分数属于一类静态质心检测器族，CTM为其特例。

Conclusion: 类吸引不足以作为特征精炼的合理性依据，应评估其对ID-OOD排序边界的影响而非仅看类亲和分数；检测改进应聚焦边界处的相对分数变化。

Abstract: Attracting features toward learned in-distribution (ID) regions is an intuitive strategy for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection, but does such attraction improve detection? We investigate this question using class-conditioned Hamiltonian dynamics as a controlled testbed and identify an attraction-ranking mismatch: improving individual class-affinity scores need not improve the relative ordering of ID and OOD samples. Our analysis separates misalignment between the driving field and the detection score from ranking failure that persists even when they are aligned. An exact AUROC decomposition and a local boundary analysis identify relative score changes at the ID-OOD ranking boundary as the source of first-order AUROC change under regularity conditions. Experiments demonstrate that a score-aligned rule can improve performance on a held-out source validation split yet consistently degrade it after transfer, even without a single winning-class change. A separate perturbation study finds that near-boundary relative score changes track the direction of small AUROC changes in the tested settings. Finally, centroid approximation and shared scales connect the zero-step score to a static centroid family that includes the existing CTM detector. Together, these results explain why class attraction is insufficient to justify feature refinement and motivate evaluating its effect on the ranking boundary.

</details>


### [55] [Who Pays for the Knowledge Models Copy? Model Collapse as Rogers' Paradox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06022)
*Nam H. Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将"模型崩溃"重构为文化演化中的罗杰斯悖论：问题不在循环本身，而在于"外部信息供给"这一被现有理论视为给定的量；只有当人们持续自主学习（而非仅复制）时，知识才能维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型崩溃理论把外部数据供给当作固定档案、固定分布给定，只研究循环内部退化；作者认为供给才是真正的症结，而文化演化理论正好提供了关于"学习—复制"平衡如何决定知识存量的形式化框架，因此需要将模型崩溃与罗杰斯悖论对应起来并加以检验。

Method: 三步识别检验：(1) 仅用完全合成训练数据拟合罗杰斯模型，再预测语言模型（从词三元组到预训练Transformer）在其他新鲜数据混合比例下对稀有知识的保留率；(2) 在目标分布随世界变化的情形下，比较"复制自身过去"的谱系与"每代从固定预训练权重重启"的谱系所需新鲜数据量，检验罗杰斯模型"变化越快需越多新鲜数据"的预测；(3) 闭合回路：让训练数据写作者可转而询问模型而非自行探究，使新鲜数据供给内生化，求其均衡性质。

Result: (1) 仅凭合成数据拟合的罗杰斯模型，对多种混合条件下稀有知识保留率的预测误差仅为几个百分点；(2) 世界变化越快，复制型谱系维持性能所需的新鲜数据越多，与罗杰斯模型一致，而固定目标假设的分析无法解释；从固定预训练权重重启的谱系基本免疫，因为这些权重是"从未被复制的知识"；(3) 均衡时模型可靠度恰等于人们自行探究的成本所对应的水平，总体福利不优于无模型情形，模型生成文本份额的每一次提高都必须由更多人自主学习来补偿；核查并纠错重学可打破悖论，而只保留"看似合理"文本的过滤属于从众式筛选、会加速崩溃，只有携带新信息的核查才起关键学习作用。

Conclusion: 模型崩溃的根源是复制与自主学习的失衡而非生成模型本身；理论应把数据供给视为内生的文化演化过程。可行的缓解路径是引入带新信息的核查（critical learning）与固定的"未复制知识"锚点，而非仅做合理性过滤；否则任何模型文本占比的提升都必须由人类自主学习来付费。

Abstract: Generative models trained on the output of earlier models lose knowledge across generations, rare knowledge first. The remedies that work all bring in information from outside the loop, and the theory of model collapse treats that supply as given: a fixed archive, drawn from a fixed distribution. We argue that the supply is the problem, and that cultural evolution has the theory of it. We read model collapse as Rogers' paradox: a population that comes to rely on copying ends up no better off than one that never copied, because copies go stale unless someone keeps learning for themselves. We test the identification in three steps. First, Rogers' model, fitted only to fully synthetic training, predicts to within a few percentage points how much rare knowledge language models, from word trigrams to a pretrained transformer, retain under other mixtures of fresh data. Second, when the world itself changes, a lineage that copies its own past needs more fresh data to keep up the faster the world changes, as Rogers' model says it must and as analyses that assume a fixed target cannot; a lineage that restarts every generation from fixed pretrained weights is largely insulated, because those weights are knowledge that is never copied. Third, we close the loop: when the people who write a model's training data can ask the model instead of finding things out, the supply of fresh data becomes endogenous. At equilibrium, the model is exactly as reliable as it is costly for people to find out for themselves, the population is no better off than without the model, and every increase in the share of model-written text must be paid for by more people learning for themselves. Users who check what they are told and relearn when it is wrong break the paradox. Filters that select plausible text are conformist and accelerate collapse; only checks that carry new information act as critical learning.

</details>


### [56] [Practical Estimation of the Bayes Optimal Fairness-Accuracy Tradeoff with Soft Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06027)
*Mohit Sharma,Okan Koç,Amit Deshpande,Takashi Ishida,Gang Niu,Rajiv Ratn Shah,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将贝叶斯误差的概念推广到公平性约束下的分类问题，给出"公平贝叶斯误差"（给定公平约束下可达的最优误差率）的显式表达，并构造仅依赖软标签与群体信息、无需输入特征的相合估计器，证明其偏差与样本复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类任务存在最佳预测误差的根本极限，贝叶斯误差是其度量，可作为模型基准与过拟合检测准则；现有二分类贝叶斯误差估计仅用软标签即可，但缺乏对约束/多目标分类（如公平分类）情形下极限的理论刻画。公平分类需在准确率与群体间真阳率均衡之间折衷，形成Pareto前沿，因此需要一个能界定该前沿根本极限的量。

Method: 把贝叶斯误差推广至带公平约束的分类问题，推导"公平贝叶斯误差率"（在给定公平约束下的最优可达误差率）的显式表达式；基于该表达式构造仅使用软标签（正类概率）与群体标签、instance-free（不需输入特征、不需辅助训练）的估计器，并证明其为相合估计，同时给出偏差界与样本复杂度界。

Result: 理论上获得公平贝叶斯误差的显式刻画及估计器的相合性与偏差/样本复杂度界；实验上，该估计器能给出稳健、低方差的最优公平性-准确率折衷曲线估计，即便软标签含噪或由基于硬标签训练的分类模型导出仍有效。

Conclusion: 公平分类的公平性-准确率折衷存在可由软标签与群体信息直接估计的根本极限，所提instance-free估计器在理论与实证上均可靠，可作为评估公平分类器Pareto前沿的基准工具。

Abstract: There is a fundamental limit to the best prediction error of any model for a given data distribution. In classification, the Bayes error quantifies this limit and serves as both a benchmark for trained models and a criterion to detect overfitting. The state-of-the-art Bayes error estimation techniques for binary classification use only soft labels (positive class probabilities) and require no input features or auxiliary training. We extend such notions to constrained classification problems, such as fairness. Fair classification is an important case of constrained or multi-objective classification. It requires a model not only to be accurate but also to yield fair outcomes (equal or near-equal true positive rates) across different demographic groups (e.g., race and gender). This leads to an inherent fairness-accuracy tradeoff or a Pareto frontier that is used to compare fair classifiers against each other. The focus of our work is to estimate the fundamental limit for this fairness-accuracy tradeoff. We derive an explicit expression for the fair Bayes error rate (i.e., best achievable error rate subject to given fairness constraints), and construct consistent, instance-free estimators for it using only soft labels and group information. We provably bound the bias and the sample complexity of our estimators. Empirically, our method produces a robust, low-variance estimate of the optimal fairness-accuracy tradeoff curve, even when the soft labels are noisy and derived from a classification model on hard labels.

</details>


### [57] [TRACK: Multiple Instance Learning for Anti-Money Laundering in Transaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06035)
*Seonkyu Lim,Inwoo Tae,Gwangui Hong,Jaehoon Lee,Jeongwhan Choi,Taehoon Kim,Min-Soo Kim,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出账户级反洗钱检测存在"聚合错配"问题，将账户级检测重构为多示例学习（账户窗口=包，交易=实例），提出TRACK：用共享编码器编码每笔交易并选择性地池化为账户决策；仅用账户级弱监督即可在多个数据集上取得最佳AP，并接近甚至超过交易级监督的边分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反洗钱调查以账户为中心组织，但可疑证据往往只存在于账户的少数几笔交易中。现有账户级检测器在建模前先对交易做汇总，消息传递模型还会在邻居账户间再次聚合，导致重要交易级证据被稀释——作者称之为"聚合错配"。

Method: 将账户级AML检测转化为多示例学习：账户窗口为bag，交易为instance。TRACK以共享编码器编码每笔交易，再通过选择性池化（selective pooling）聚合为账户级决策。为模拟粗粒度监督，训练时不使用五个AML数据集的交易标签，仅用账户窗口标签（定义为至少含一笔被标记交易）。采用时间顺序的评估协议，与表格/图基线对比。

Result: 在所有五个数据集上，TRACK的平均精度（AP）均高于以账户标签训练的表格与图基线中的最高值；尽管仅使用账户窗口监督，其性能仍可与直接用交易标签训练的边分类器相当。此外，其实例级分数对标记交易的排序显著优于随机水平，并在多个数据集上接近或超过直接监督的排序表现。

Conclusion: 通过缓解聚合错配、以多示例学习对齐账户级监督与交易级证据，TRACK在弱监督条件下实现了强账户级检测与可解释的实例级定位，说明细粒度交易建模对AML账户风险识别具有关键价值。

Abstract: Anti-money laundering (AML) investigations are organized around accounts, while the suspicious evidence for an account may reside in only a few of its transactions. Yet many account-level detectors summarize transactions before learning, and message-passing models may aggregate once more over neighboring accounts. We name this discrepancy the aggregation mismatch and study it under an account-window benchmark label defined by the presence of at least one flagged transaction. We recast account-level AML detection as multiple instance learning, in which an account-window is a bag and its transactions are instances. We propose TRACK, which encodes every transaction with a shared encoder and selectively pools the encodings into an account decision. To emulate coarse supervision, we withhold the transaction labels of five AML datasets from TRACK throughout training. Under a time-ordered protocol, TRACK achieves the highest average precision among the tabular and graph baselines trained from account labels on every dataset. Despite using only account-window supervision, it remains competitive with edge classifiers trained directly from transaction labels. Its instance scores also rank flagged transactions well above chance and approach or exceed directly supervised ranking performance across datasets.

</details>


### [58] [Act-HiMotif: Hierarchical Daily Activity Schedule Generation via Learned Activity-Structure Motifs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06038)
*Yushi Bai,Kang Liu,Ling Yin,Wei Wang,Yilin Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分层motif生成模型Act-HiMotif，从个体属性生成日活动日程，在分布内与空间泛化上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从年龄、收入等个体属性生成日活动日程对基于活动的出行建模与人类行为仿真有价值，但精确活动结构呈高度不平衡的长尾分布，且同一结构在全天预算下可有多种时长分配，直接生成需从极不均衡观测中学习大量精确结构并建模其时间实现。

Method: 观察到许多精确结构共享重复出现的活动组织方式，据此引入可学习的可复用活动结构motif作为个体画像与具体活动结构之间的中间表示。模型先区分纯居家日与活动日；再从画像条件先验中采样motif以引导活动结构生成；最后用预算约束的时长分配器生成恰好等于全天时间窗的时长。三层分别建模纯居家路由、活动结构生成与时长分配。

Result: 在大规模出行调查数据上，Act-HiMotif在分布内与空间泛化两种设置下均优于强基线。

Conclusion: 分层生成与可复用活动结构motif能有效产生真实且由画像条件化的日活动日程；motif使相关结构共享统计支撑而不丢失各自独特序列。

Abstract: Generating daily activity schedules from individual attributes (e.g., age and income) is valuable for activity-based travel modeling and human behavior simulation. The task is challenging because exact activity structures follow a highly imbalanced, long-tailed distribution, while the same structure can admit multiple duration allocations under a fixed full-day budget. Direct generation must therefore learn many exact activity structures from highly uneven observations while modeling their possible temporal realizations. However, many exact structures share recurring activity organization. Based on this observation, we propose Act-HiMotif, a hierarchical motif-based generative model that introduces learned, reusable activity-structure motifs as an intermediate representation between individual profiles and specific activity structures. Act-HiMotif first distinguishes home-only days from active days, then samples a motif from a profile-conditioned prior to guide activity-structure generation, and finally applies a budget-constrained duration allocator to generate activity durations that sum exactly to the full-day horizon. This hierarchy models home-only routing, activity-structure generation, and duration allocation at their corresponding levels, while motifs allow related structures to share statistical support without losing their distinct sequences. Experiments on large-scale travel survey data show that Act-HiMotif outperforms strong baselines in both in-distribution and spatial generalization settings, demonstrating the effectiveness of hierarchical generation and reusable activity-structure motifs for producing realistic profile-conditioned daily activity schedules.

</details>


### [59] [Alleviating Limited Effectiveness and Multi-Class Collapse in Deep Graph-level Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06052)
*Xiaoyang Lin,Renchi Yang,Yuqi Chen,Xin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 S$^3$GLC 框架，融合全局拓扑特征（谱签名）与局部邻域特征，并结合最优传输平衡聚类目标，解决 GIN 忽视全局拓扑与深度聚类"聚类坍缩"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图级聚类中 GIN 作为事实编码器忽略全局拓扑，导致无法区分部分图；同时现有深度聚类框架在类别不平衡的多类场景下存在严重的聚类坍缩问题。

Method: 1) 基于 Hoffman-Wielandt 不等式构造 Spectral Signature，作为图编辑距离（GED）的严格下界，为全局拓扑特征提供高效代理；2) 提出联合流形保持稀疏回归模块，将谱签名与空间表示融合，去噪并保留互补信息；3) 基于最优传输构建平衡聚类目标，利用 Sinkhorn-Knopp 归一化对簇分配施加均分约束以缓解坍缩。

Result: 在 10 个真实数据集上显著优于 15 个 SOTA 基线，在 REDDIT-12K、OGBG-PPA 等基准上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 全局拓扑与局部特征的融合加上平衡聚类目标可有效提升图级聚类性能并抑制聚类坍缩。

Abstract: Given a collection of $N$ graphs, graph-level clustering (GLC) seeks to partition the $N$ graphs into semantically meaningful groups, which serves as a fundamental task in analyzing graph-structured data. While Graph Isomorphism Networks (GINs) have become the de facto encoders for GLC, they still fail to distinguish graphs due to their neglect of global topology. On top of that, existing deep clustering frameworks suffer from severe "clustering collapse" issues, particularly in imbalanced multi-class scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose S$^3$GLC, a novel framework that integrates global topological characteristics with local neighborhood features for balanced GLC. Theoretically, we leverage the Hoffman-Wielandt inequality to construct Spectral Signature as a rigorous lower bound for graph edit distance (GED), providing a computationally efficient proxy for global topological features. We additionally propose a joint manifold-preserving sparse regression module to fuse Spectral Signature with Spatial Representation, eliminating noise while preserving the complementary information. Furthermore, to mitigate clustering collapse, we introduce a balanced clustering objective based on optimal transport, harnessing the Sinkhorn-Knopp normalization to enforce equipartition constraints on cluster assignments. Extensive experiments on 10 real-world datasets demonstrate that S$^3$GLC significantly outperforms 15 state-of-the-art baselines, achieving remarkable improvements on benchmark datasets such as REDDIT-12K and OGBG-PPA.

</details>


### [60] [Learning From Static Context in Federated Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06064)
*Viktor Walter,Andreas Wagner,Fabian Stricker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 联邦学习下TFT的静态条件输入在per-batch FedAdam中几乎与集中训练等效，而带本地epoch的通信高效方案几乎无法利用该信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测正从单序列本地模型转向跨异质序列的全局模型，需要学习序列间的区分信息；在数据不能集中的场景下用联邦学习训练，而TFT依赖描述各序列且不随时间变化的静态输入。该文追问：在每客户端仅持有一条序列的联邦设定中，联邦TFT能否学到并使用这一静态输入。

Method: 在建筑能耗电表数据、风电数据集和受控合成数据集上，比较不同FL聚合方案的联邦TFT对静态通路的利用情况：per-batch FedAdam（每批次后聚合梯度）、FedAvg、FedAdam+Prox（含本地epoch的通信高效方案），以及兼容Adam的SCAFFOLD；并在合成与风电数据上用另外两种具有不同静态条件机制的架构验证该现象是否TFT特有。

Result: per-batch FedAdam对静态输入的利用接近集中训练；FedAvg与FedAdam+Prox提取的静态信息远少，在两个应用数据集上无显著收益；同等本地计算下兼容Adam的SCAFFOLD在风电上保持静态通路活跃并显著改善，在建筑能耗上误差虽下降但静态通路“沉默”，故漂移与通信频率的影响无法完全分离；仅per-batch FedAdam在所有数据集上达到或超过各客户端本地模型。该损失并非TFT特有。

Conclusion: 联邦时间序列全局模型对静态序列标识信息的利用高度依赖聚合方案：批次级聚合（per-batch FedAdam）最有效，本地epoch与通信频率是关键制约因素；该效应在不同架构与数据集上普遍存在。

Abstract: Time series forecasting is shifting from locally trained, per-series models to global models trained across many heterogeneous series, which requires learning what sets the series apart. When the raw data cannot be centralized, such a global model is trained with federated learning (FL), one client per data owner. State-of-the-art forecasters such as the Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) tell series apart through a static input that describes each series and does not change over time. In the special case of a federated setting where each client holds a single series, this static input is constant within each client and varies only across clients. We ask whether a federated TFT can learn from it. Per-batch FedAdam, which aggregates gradients after every batch, uses the static input almost as well as central training, whereas communication-efficient schemes with local epochs (FedAvg, FedAdam+Prox) extract far less from it and gain nothing significant on either application dataset. At the same local computation, an Adam-compatible SCAFFOLD keeps the static pathway responsive on wind power and gains significantly there. On building energy its error still drops, but the static pathway falls silent, so drift and communication frequency cannot be fully separated as the cause. Only per-batch FedAdam matches or beats the per-client local models across all datasets. We confirm the effect on building-energy meter data, a wind-power dataset, and a controlled synthetic dataset. The loss is not specific to the TFT: on the synthetic and the wind data two further architectures with different static-conditioning mechanisms show it as well.

</details>


### [61] [PRISM-AMC: Replay-Free Continual Learning for Automatic Modulation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06066)
*Usman Akram,Matthew Graham,Haris Vikalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无需回放的持续学习框架PRISM-AMC，通过梯度投影记忆保留中间表示中与任务相关的子空间以缓解灾难性遗忘，在类增量与域增量调制识别中显著降低遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的自动调制分类（AMC）多假设调制类别固定且可持续访问历史训练数据；实际中类别与信道条件随时间演化，且存储/数据留存约束可能禁止重放原始基带I/Q样本。

Method: PRISM-AMC：无回放持续学习框架，利用梯度投影记忆（gradient projection memory）保护中间表示中任务相关子空间；并在SNR相关梯度噪声模型下分析投影随机梯度更新；在类增量与域增量两种设置下评估。

Result: 在MIGOU与DeepSig 2018.01A数据集上，相较顺序微调与弹性权重固化（EWC）显著降低遗忘；在类增量设置下与基于回放的RSD-AMC相当或更优，且不存储原始I/Q样本。

Conclusion: 无需存储原始样本的梯度投影型持续学习可有效用于演化条件下的AMC，兼顾抗遗忘性能与数据留存约束。

Abstract: Automatic modulation classification (AMC) is a key component of cognitive wireless systems, enabling spectrum awareness and adaptive communication. Most deep learning approaches to AMC assume a fixed set of modulation classes and access to previously observed training data. In practice, modulation classes and channel conditions may evolve over time, while storage or data-retention constraints may preclude replay of raw baseband I/Q samples. We propose PRISM-AMC, a replay-free continual learning framework that mitigates catastrophic forgetting by preserving task-relevant subspaces of intermediate representations via gradient projection memory. We further analyze projected stochastic-gradient updates under an SNR-dependent gradient-noise model. PRISM-AMC is evaluated in both class-incremental and domain-incremental settings. Experiments on the MIGOU and DeepSig 2018.01A datasets show substantially reduced forgetting relative to sequential fine-tuning and elastic weight consolidation, while remaining competitive with or outperforming replay-based RSD-AMC in class-incremental settings, without storing raw I/Q samples.

</details>


### [62] [Catastrophic Overfitting is Weight Drift the Training Loss Cannot See](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06082)
*Chao Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出快速对抗训练(FAT)中的灾难性过拟合(CO)本质上是权重的"静默逃逸"：训练目标对导致CO的权重位移几乎不敏感，而权重EMA不累积该漂移，因此提出在验证鲁棒性下降时将权重重置为EMA，并构建在耦合式基方法之上的守卫机制，在三个数据集上消除CO并取得最优AutoAttack精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAT通过用单步攻击替代多步攻击降低训练成本，但存在灾难性过拟合：训练中途对多步攻击的鲁棒性骤降至近零，而训练所用单步攻击仍被有效抵御。现有方法缺乏对CO机理的清晰解释，作者希望从权重位移与训练目标的视角阐明其成因，并据此给出几乎零额外开销的解决方案。

Method: 机理分析：将CO刻画为权重的"静默逃逸"——CO事件前后干净精度悄然提升，而训练目标值仅有微小变化，说明目标对破坏鲁棒性的位移几乎"失明"，不产生恢复力。观察：FAT协议中已维护的权重指数移动平均(EMA)不累积该漂移，在整个事件期间保持鲁棒。方案：在耦合(coupling)式基方法（其设计使权重平均落在鲁棒区域内）之上构建守卫，当验证鲁棒性下降时把权重重置为EMA。

Result: 在所有实验运行中均消除了CO；在三个基准数据集上，快速方法中取得最高AutoAttack精度；在CIFAR-100上优势明显，且以约一半的计算成本超过多步参考方法。

Conclusion: CO并非训练目标所"看见"的失败模式，而是目标近乎盲区的权重漂移；利用FAT中已有EMA的稳定性作为重置锚点，可几乎无代价地消除CO，并与耦合式基方法结合实现快速方法中的最优鲁棒精度。

Abstract: Fast adversarial training (FAT) makes robust training affordable by replacing the multi-step attack with a single-step one, but it suffers from catastrophic overfitting (CO): partway through training, robustness to multi-step attacks collapses to near zero while the single-step attack used for training keeps being repelled. We show that CO is a quiet escape of the weights. Across the event the network's clean behavior quietly improves, and moving from the weights just before CO to those just after it changes the training objective only marginally while destroying most of the robustness. The training objective is therefore nearly blind to the displacement it causes, so it exerts no force to undo it. The exponential moving average (EMA) of the weights, which FAT protocols already maintain, does not accumulate this drift and stays robust through the entire event. This observation suggests a nearly free fix: when validation robustness drops, reset the weights to their EMA. We build this guard on top of a coupling-based base method designed so that its average stays inside the robust region. The resulting training removes CO in every one of our runs and attains the strongest AutoAttack accuracy among fast methods on all three benchmark datasets, including a clear margin on CIFAR-100, where it also clears the multi-step reference at roughly half its cost. Code is available at https://github.com/fzjcdt/co-weight-drift.

</details>


### [63] [Shallow Encoding Emits Deep Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06084)
*Mingyuan Ma,Yangmin Li,Harris Nover,Zheyu Fu,Po-Han Huang,Alex Settle,Ashwin Nanjappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEED：利用混合语言模型的"写易读难"与"状态汇"两大性质，使预填充更浅（只跑部分层）、解码状态更浅（低秩表示），近乎无损地提升服务吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合模型（少量softmax注意力+大量线性注意力/状态空间层）每层都维护对历史token的记忆（KV缓存或固定大小递归状态）。服务时，预填充要对每个prompt token跑所有层，每个解码步要读写整个递归状态——在Qwen3.8-Flash-Next上，每请求递归状态字节数相当于数千token的注意力缓存，计算与HBM开销远超输出所需。

Method: 观察到两个性质：(1)写比读容易——深层对某token记忆的写入只需少量深层计算，据此训练深层写路径副本"emitters"，用较浅锚点层的残差流写入prompt深层记忆，使除最后一个外所有prompt token只跑至多63%的层；(2)递归状态存在"state sink"——能量主要集中在每头固定方向的秩一项，其余为低秩工作记忆。SEED将每个递归状态存为sink加低秩因子，Gated DeltaNet、Kimi Delta Attention、Mamba-2更新可精确扩展；并证明周期压缩回低秩的开环误差与压缩次数无关地有界。权值冻结、不压缩注意力缓存、每个生成token仍跑所有层。

Result: 在5个生产级混合模型（30B至300B+参数）的6个checkpoint上，11项官方考试近乎无损；在NVIDIA GB300上用SGLang在agentic-coding trace上服务，Qwen3.8-Flash-Next的每GPU吞吐提升1.2–1.4倍。

Conclusion: 混合模型的记忆冗余可从深度（预填充层数）与表示（递归状态秩）两个维度压缩，且无需改权重、不动注意力缓存即可获得接近无损的吞吐收益。

Abstract: Hybrid language models interleave a few softmax-attention layers with many linear-attention or state-space layers, so every layer keeps a memory of earlier tokens: a key-value cache or a fixed-size recurrent state. Serving them spends more compute and high-bandwidth memory on these memories than the output requires. The prefill runs every layer for every prompt token, and every decode step loads and stores whole recurrent states, which on Qwen3.8-Flash-Next take as many bytes per request as the attention cache of thousands of tokens. We find two properties of trained hybrids that locate this excess. First, writes are easier than reads: writing a deep layer's memory of a token needs little of the deep computation that predicting the next token needs. Trained copies of the deep layers' write paths, which we call emitters, therefore write a prompt's deep memories from the residual stream of a shallower anchor layer, and all prompt tokens but the last run as few as 63% of the layers. Second, recurrent states have a state sink: most of their energy lies in a rank-one term along a fixed direction of each head, and the rest is a low-rank working memory. SEED builds on both: it makes the prefill shallow in depth and the decode state shallow in representation. Each recurrent state is stored as its sink plus low-rank factors, which the Gated DeltaNet, Kimi Delta Attention and Mamba-2 updates extend exactly; we prove that the open-loop error of the periodic compressions back to low rank is bounded independently of their number. SEED keeps the original weights frozen, compresses no attention cache, and runs all layers for every generated token. On six checkpoints of five production hybrids of 30B to over 300B parameters, it is nearly lossless on eleven official exams. Served with SGLang on NVIDIA GB300 GPUs on an agentic-coding trace, it raises the per-GPU throughput of Qwen3.8-Flash-Next 1.2-1.4x.

</details>


### [64] [Learning When to Think Aloud in Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06108)
*Zhehao Zhou,Tongrui Su,Shengyu Zhu,Da Li,Zeyu Liu,Jun Zhu,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VITAL：一种重要性引导的混合式隐式推理框架，动态决定"显式生成token"还是"隐状态直接反馈"，在保持推理精简的同时提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全隐式CoT（Latent CoT）虽高效，但在数学等精度敏感任务上表现不佳；现有混合方法又保留了大量token生成开销。

Method: 引入轻量gating head动态在显式token生成与隐状态直接反馈间切换；基于token重要性估计从显式CoT构建混合轨迹；采用课程式监督训练，再用平衡正确性与简洁性的奖励进行强化学习。

Result: 在相近推理长度下，相比全隐式方法相对平均准确率提升GPT-2上9.6%、Llama-3.2-1B上5.1%；相比混合基线，在Qwen3-1.7B上以显著更短的推理轨迹取得更高准确率。

Conclusion: 选择性"显式化"关键锚点token、其余保持隐式计算，可在精度与效率间取得更优折中，VITAL在多个模型上一致优于现有方法。

Abstract: Latent Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning improves efficiency by shifting intermediate computation into continuous hidden states. However, fully latent methods often struggle on precision-critical tasks such as mathematical reasoning, while existing hybrid approaches retain substantial token-generation overhead. We introduce VITAL, an importance-guided hybrid reasoning framework that selectively verbalizes informative tokens as reasoning anchors while keeping most computation latent. A lightweight gating head dynamically selects between explicit token generation and direct hidden-state feedback. VITAL constructs hybrid trajectories from explicit CoT using token- importance estimation and trains the model through curriculum-based supervised training, followed by reinforcement learning with a reward that balances correctness and conciseness. Experiments show that VITAL consistently out- performs existing fully latent reasoning methods, achieving relative average accuracy gains of 9.6% on GPT-2 and 5.1% on Llama-3.2-1B at comparable reasoning lengths. Compared with hybrid baselines, VITAL achieves higher reasoning accuracy with substantially shorter reasoning trajectories on Qwen3-1.7B.

</details>


### [65] [Short-Term Forecasting of Heterogeneous Wind Power Fleets: Global Temporal Fusion Transformer Leveraging Static Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06130)
*Viktor Walter,Andreas Wagner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用德国多风电场联合训练的全局Temporal Fusion Transformer（TFT）做48小时功率预测，证明其在多数站点优于单站局部模型，且优势依赖静态协变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界短期风电功率预测仍以单资产局部模型为主，而Transformer类深度学习已被证明为先进方法；作者希望检验跨异构风电场联合训练的全局模型能否在真实多站点组合中同时提升精度并降低运维复杂度。

Method: 以ICON-D2区域与ECMWF HRES全球NWP数据为动态输入，构建在德国多风电场异构组合上联合训练的全局TFT，并与各站点局部模型及线性回归基线对比。全局模型引入风电场年龄、海拔、轮毂高度、风轮直径与功率曲线参数等静态协变量以学习跨站点共享表征。评估对象为100个合成风场剖面与11个真实德国风电场组合，采用两个季节测试期并进行中间重训练；结合Wilcoxon符号秩检验与TFT变量选择网络（VSN）分析特征重要性。

Result: 全局TFT在87%的合成风场和77%的真实风场上优于局部模型，并明显超过线性基线；Wilcoxon符号秩检验显示两季节、两数据集上的增益均显著。消融表明该优势依赖静态协变量，缺失资产特定上下文时全局模型无法泛化。VSN揭示：海拔在两数据集上均为有效静态区分变量，轮毂高度在真实组合中起决定作用；动态输入方面编码器优先使用历史功率，解码器融合两个NWP模型的上层风速，符合平均化模型特定误差的机制。

Conclusion: 结果表明应从小规模单站预测转向可扩展的多站预测：全局TFT在大规模风电场组合中兼顾更高精度与更低运维复杂度，但其收益以静态协变量提供站点上下文为前提。

Abstract: Accurate short-term wind power forecasting is critical for grid stability and electricity trading. While transformer-based deep learning is state of the art, industry practice relies mostly on local models trained for individual assets. This study investigates whether a global temporal fusion transformer (TFT), trained jointly on a heterogeneous portfolio of wind farms across Germany, outperforms site-specific local models in 48-hour-ahead forecasting. Using numerical weather prediction (NWP) data from the regional ICON-D2 and the global ECMWF HRES models, we compare the global TFT against local models and a linear regression baseline. The global model leverages static covariates, such as farm age, altitude above sea level, hub height, rotor diameter and power curve parameters, to learn shared representations across sites. We evaluate on 100 synthetic wind farm profiles and a real portfolio of 11 German wind farms, across two seasonal test periods with intermediate retraining. The global TFT outperforms the local models for 87% of the synthetic and 77% of the real wind farms and clearly surpasses the linear baseline. Wilcoxon signed-rank tests confirm that these gains are significant across both seasons and datasets. Crucially, this superiority depends on static covariates: without asset-specific context, the global model fails to generalize. The TFT's variable selection network (VSN) reveals the learned feature selection. Altitude emerges as a relevant static discriminator on both datasets, while hub height is decisive on the real portfolio. For dynamic inputs, the encoder prioritizes historical power, whereas the decoder combines upper-level wind speeds from both NWP models, consistent with averaging out model-specific errors. These findings support a shift from single-site to scalable multi-site forecasting, reducing operational complexity while improving accuracy in large portfolios.

</details>


### [66] [Neural Stochastic Forecasting for Lateral Molecular Diffusion Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06157)
*Jacob Ede Levine,Mario Hidalgo-Soria,Chen Yun Wen,Yun Lyna Luo,Sai Chandra Kosaraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经随机(NS)模型，由粗粒化MD轨迹预测膜蛋白集合扩散，可生成长达4万帧的轨迹并保持关键统计特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膜蛋白长时扩散预测困难：MD模拟计算代价高，实验单粒子追踪受有限观测时间限制。

Method: NS模型结合：基于Transformer的蛋白构型表征、多时间尺度循环网络、带可学习记忆的随机隐过程；并由交叉注意力解码器在考虑瞬时系统几何与周期性边界条件下生成相关蛋白位移。

Result: 在七个PIEZO通道体系中复现MD测得的异常扩散指数α~1，优于先前呈超扩散的变分自回归模型；生成轨迹达40,000帧；在七个PIEZO与十个TREK混合体系中捕获两类群体的差异性扩散；时间平均均方位移、Van Hove位移分布与非高斯参数对比表明模型保留了模拟动力学的关键统计特征。

Conclusion: 神经随机建模是生成多蛋白膜扩散长轨迹、扩展MD统计分析的有前景方法。

Abstract: Predicting the long-time diffusion of membrane proteins is challenging because molecular dynamics (MD) simulations are computationally expensive, while experimental single-particle tracking is often limited by finite observation times. Here, we introduce a neural stochastic (NS) model for forecasting the collective diffusion of membrane proteins from coarse-grained MD trajectories. The model combines a transformer-based representation of protein configurations, recurrent networks operating at multiple temporal scales, and a stochastic latent process with learned memory. A cross-attention decoder then generates correlated protein displacements while accounting for the instantaneous system geometry and periodic boundary conditions. We evaluate the model in two membrane systems: seven interacting PIEZO channels and a mixed system containing seven PIEZO and ten TREK channels. For the seven PIEZO system, the NS model reproduces the anomalous diffusion exponent measured in MD simulations $α\sim 1$, substantially improving on a previously developed variational autoregressive model that exhibits superdiffusive behavior. The model also generates trajectories extending to 40,000 frames and captures the distinct diffusion characteristics of PIEZO and TREK populations in the mixed system. Comparisons of time-averaged mean-square displacements, Van Hove displacement distributions, and non-Gaussian parameters show that the model preserves key statistical features of the simulated dynamics. These results establish neural stochastic modeling as a promising approach for generating long trajectories of multi-protein membrane diffusion and extending the statistical analysis of molecular dynamics simulations.

</details>


### [67] [Selective Prediction Intervals: Jointly Learning to Predict, Bound, and Abstain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06158)
*Lidor Rubi,Gilad Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SelPI，在单一损失和显式接受率预算下联合训练区间头、值预测头与弃权头，用双门控（区间损失门控+值损失门控，由Fr'echet惩罚耦合预算）实现"预测+区间+弃权"三合一的选择性回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险回归决策需同时提供点估计、风险区间与弃权选项，但深度学习现有方法割裂：区间预测方法对每个输入都给区间（至多附一个区间内值估计），选择性预测只处理点预测且不说明真值可能偏离多远。作者进一步指出"区间宽度即可判断是否弃权"这一自然反驳不成立——宽度善于预测大点误差，却与区间失效呈反相关，按宽度弃权会恰好丢弃区间正确的样本。

Method: SelPI：在统一损失、带接受率预算约束下联合训练三个头（区间、值、弃权）。弃权采用双门控选择器，一个门控以区间损失训练、一个以值损失训练，二者通过Fr'echet惩罚将其各自预算与联合接受的接受率挂钩。对比基线包括宽度阈值法、深度集成分歧选择法，并保持基础预测器固定以单独评估门控。

Result: 18个表格基准上，宽度对点误差预测AUROC 0.72，但对区间失效AUROC仅0.38（反相关）。固定基础预测器时，学习门控在全部18个数据集上以proper interval score优于宽度阈值，中位缩小87%的与oracle选择器的差距；在全部数据集上亦优于深度集成分歧选择。优势随任务异方差性增强，并报告其消失的设定。

Conclusion: 区间宽度不能替代弃权判断，需专门学习的双门控选择机制；SelPI通过联合训练与预算耦合，在选择性区间回归中显著优于宽度阈值与集成分歧基线，效果依赖任务异方差程度。

Abstract: Many high-stakes regression decisions ask three things of a model at once: a point estimate to act on, an interval that bounds the risk of acting, and the option to abstain when neither can be trusted. Deep learning provides these in isolation: prediction-interval methods return an interval, and at best a value estimate inside it, for every input, while selective-prediction methods learn when to abstain, but only for point predictions and with no statement of how far the truth may lie from the estimate. We introduce SelPI, which trains interval, value and abstention heads jointly under a single loss with an explicit acceptance-rate budget. A natural objection is that interval width already tells us when to abstain.
