We show that it does not: across 18 tabular benchmarks, width predicts large point errors well (AUROC 0.72) but is anti-correlated with interval misses (AUROC 0.38), so abstaining on wide intervals discards precisely the inputs the interval got right. SelPI therefore selects with two gates, one trained on the interval loss and one on the value loss, coupled by a Fr'echet penalty that ties their separate budgets to the joint acceptance rate they deliver. With the base predictor held fixed, the learned gate beats width thresholding on a proper interval score on all 18 tabular datasets, closing a median 87% of the gap to an oracle selector, and it also beats selection by deep-ensemble disagreement on every dataset. The advantage grows with the heteroscedasticity of the task, and we report the settings in which it disappears.

</details>


### [68] [FaCoDiff: Factor-Correlated Diffusion for Multivariate Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06166)
*Jiaxuan Li,Yao Liu,Yuewei Xue,Shijie Dong,Zixiang Jin,Xiaoyu Guo,Qiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FaCoDiff，将紧凑的条件统计估计器与结构化去噪耦合，用低秩因子协方差刻画跨变量依赖，避免稠密协方差开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量概率预测需同时建模跨变量依赖与边际不确定性，难点在于学习依赖的同时控制边际噪声尺度并规避稠密协方差的计算成本。

Method: 以历史为条件的因子载荷分别用于预测中心校正、尺度调整与噪声依赖；对角补偿保持估计的边际噪声方差；低秩算子实现残差高斯似然评估与噪声传输；因子派生特征调制显式条件化去噪器；几何感知目标考虑噪声传输后误差；同一条件几何统一前向扰动与逆向采样。

Result: 在九个数据集上，FaCoDiff在八个数据集取得最低平均variogram分数，并在54个数据集-指标组合中43个位列前二；Solar与Weather上的匹配消融显示，在中心与尺度校正的四种组合下因子协方差均改善Energy与Variogram分数；打乱跨变量样本配对而保留边际轨迹会恶化两个数据集上的联合与聚合分数。

Conclusion: 低秩因子协方差在控制边际噪声的同时有效建模跨变量依赖，生成的跨变量依赖具有实际预测价值。

Abstract: Multivariate probabilistic forecasting requires modeling cross-variable dependence alongside marginal uncertainty. The challenge is to learn this dependence while controlling marginal noise scales and avoiding dense covariance costs. We propose Factor-Correlated Diffusion (FaCoDiff), which couples a compact conditional statistical estimator with structured denoising. History-conditioned factor loadings use distinct coefficients for forecast-center correction, scale adjustment and noise dependence. Diagonal compensation preserves the estimated marginal noise variances, while low-rank operators enable residual Gaussian likelihood evaluation and noise transport without dense covariance storage or factorization. Factor-derived feature modulation explicitly conditions the denoiser, and a geometry-aware objective accounts for errors after noise transport. The same conditional geometry governs forward perturbations and reverse sampling. Across nine datasets, FaCoDiff achieves the lowest mean variogram score on eight datasets and ranks among the top two on 43 of 54 dataset-metric combinations in the reported comparisons. Matched ablations on Solar and Weather show that factor covariance improves mean Energy and Variogram scores under all four combinations of center and scale correction. Disrupting cross-variable sample pairings while preserving marginal trajectories worsens joint and aggregate scores on both datasets, demonstrating the predictive value of generated cross-variable dependence.

</details>


### [69] [GPUMAP: GPU-Accelerated Progressive Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06191)
*Myeongwon Jung,Jaemin Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GPU加速的渐进式UMAP（GPUMAP），将UMAP重构为渐进流水线并在商品级GPU上实现百万规模数据的交互式降维分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降维技术是高维数据分析的基础，但其高昂的计算开销限制了在交互场景中的使用。

Method: 识别UMAP中的三种原子操作并重组为渐进式流水线；引入操作队列、实例级局部连接（instance-wise local join）与调度策略等对UMAP的修改；利用GPU并行加速计算。

Result: 在商品级GPU硬件上实现百万规模数据集的交互式分析；并系统研究渐进降维的行为，包括投影相对于真实投影的演化过程，以及有偏数据流对投影质量与稳定性的影响。

Conclusion: GPUMAP使渐进式降维具备交互可用性，并为渐进降维的动态行为与数据流偏差影响提供了实证分析框架。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction (DR) techniques are fundamental to the analysis of high-dimensional data, yet their substantial computational cost limits their use in interactive settings. To address this limitation, we present GPU-accelerated Progressive Uniform Manifold Approximation and Projection (GPUMAP), an efficient progressive DR algorithm designed for interactive use. We identify three atomic operations within UMAP and restructure them into a progressive pipeline. As part of this adaptation, we introduce modifications to UMAP, including operation queues, instance-wise local join, and scheduling strategies. We further accelerate computation by leveraging GPU parallelism, enabling interactive analysis of million-scale datasets on commodity GPU hardware. Finally, we employ our implementation to systematically study the behavior of progressive DR, investigating how projections evolve over time relative to the ground-truth projection and how biased data streams influence the quality and stability of the resulting projections.

</details>


### [70] [Cross-Asset Knowledge Transfer for Early Fault Detection of Data-Scarce Assets Without Target Fault Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06194)
*Zekai Wang,Yuxuan Li,Zhangyue Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将跨资产早期故障检测建模为"零样本域适应"问题（目标资产无故障样本，仅有正常数据），提出融合EWMA统计过程控制自动标注、改进的域对抗网络ADANN与提前预测标注的框架。在公开轴承全寿命数据集上，ADANN在四个迁移基线中取得最高平均F1，且相对最强基线的精确率提升具有名义显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业车队可靠性管理依赖早期故障预警，但同类资产的全寿命数据高度不平衡：少数资产有完整run-to-failure记录，新装或极少失效资产仅提供正常工况数据。目标资产上无任何故障观测，导致传统诊断模型无法直接迁移。

Method: 1）用指数加权移动平均（EWMA）控制图对源资产run-to-failure数据自动打标，将人工标注简化为核验被标记片段；2）提出Adapted Domain-Adversarial Neural Network（ADANN），结合域对抗训练、最大均值差异（MMD）惩罚与Adapted Classification-Domain（ACD）损失——将分类器正常区域锚定在目标自身正常数据上；3）设计带预测视野的提前标注方案以针对早期/初期故障。并在"精确分类"假设下进行理论分析，刻画仅有正常数据目标域时域对抗项的作用及源故障特征与目标正常特征保持分离的对齐状态。

Result: 在公开run-to-failure轴承数据集上，ADANN在四个迁移基线中取得最高平均F1-score，相对最强基线的精确率增益具名义显著性；并在合成噪声条件下检验了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在目标资产无故障样本的零样本域适应设定下，仅用目标正常数据即可实现有效的跨资产早期故障检测；统计过程控制自动标注降低了标注成本，ACD损失保障了正常区域锚定，理论分析给出了域对抗项与特征分离对齐状态的可解释刻画。

Abstract: Reliability management of industrial fleets depends on early fault warnings, yet lifecycle data across similar assets are highly imbalanced. A few assets accumulate run-to-failure histories, while newly installed or rarely failing assets provide only normal-condition data. This paper asks how diagnostic knowledge can be transferred from a data-rich source asset to a data-scarce target asset when no fault data have been observed on the target. We formulate cross-asset early fault detection as a zero-shot domain adaptation problem, in which zero-shot refers to the absence of target fault samples, and propose a framework that requires only normal data from the target. The framework comprises (1) a statistical process control step that labels the source asset run-to-failure data with an exponentially weighted moving average chart, reducing manual annotation to verifying flagged segments, (2) an Adapted Domain-Adversarial Neural Network (ADANN) that combines domain-adversarial training and a Maximum Mean Discrepancy penalty with an Adapted Classification-Domain (ACD) loss, which anchors the classifier normal region to the target own normal data, and (3) an ahead-of-time labeling scheme with a prediction horizon that targets incipient faults. Under an exact-classification assumption, a theoretical analysis characterizes the domain-adversarial term for a normal-only target and the alignment state in which source fault features remain separated from target normal features. On a public run-to-failure bearing dataset, ADANN attains the highest mean F1-score among four transfer baselines, with a nominally significant precision gain over the strongest baseline, and its robustness is examined under synthetic noise.

</details>


### [71] [Separation Is a Riesz Energy: How Many Environments Does ICA Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06228)
*Yuda Bi,Yubo Guo,Jiaxiao Cai,Xiaonai Li,Zhuolin Li,Vince D. Calhoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究所有成分均为高斯时独立成分是否可恢复，证明通过多环境方差变化可实现联合对角化分离，并给出成分数 d 增大时的环境数与样本量需求：分离第 j 个成分的代价为其签名（跨环境方差）的 Riesz 2-能量 E_j，最优速率 cE_j/(n+E_j)，达到速率的样本量服从维度律 d^2/n、d log d/n、d/n（分别对应 K=2、3、K≥4 良好散布环境）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 ICA 在高斯源上不可辨识，作者要回答：若所有成分都是高斯但方差随环境变化，能否恢复各独立成分；进一步在 d 增长时究竟需要多少环境、多少样本。

Method: 把问题形式化为 K 个协方差矩阵的联合对角化；为每个成分定义签名（其在各环境下的方差），用签名的 Riesz 2-能量 E_j 度量分离难度（等于与其他成分成对 Fisher 信息倒数之和）；给出 O(d) 估计器的下界与谱估计器的上界分析，并按签名所张成的维度分析速率。

Result: 任何 O(d) 估计器在任何 n、d 下无法优于 cE_j/(n+E_j)；超过显式样本量后，谱估计器对任意混合矩阵、无需已知签名（仅需成对信息下界）即可把 E_j/n 达到常数倍；速率服从维度律：K=2 为 d^2/n，K=3 为 d log d/n，K≥4 良好散布环境为 d/n；关键在签名张成的维度而非环境数量，log d 个精心设计的环境即可使 d log d 样本足够；把协方差投影到单一线性组合则一阶速率退化为 d^2/n。仿真至 d=1000 验证预测，K≥4 联合对角化在 n/d 达数百时达到理论速率；任务态 fMRI 上能量可指示哪些成分难以分离。

Conclusion: 高斯成分在多环境方差变化下可辨识；分离速率由签名的 Riesz 2-能量与签名张成维度共同决定，给出样本量-成分数的显式相变规律，并为联合对角化实践（环境设计、样本预算）提供理论指导。

Abstract: Can independent components be recovered when all of them are Gaussian? Yes, if their variances change across environments; but how many environments, and how many samples as the number of components $d$ grows? We answer both for the joint diagonalization of $K$ covariances. Each component has a signature, its variances across the environments. Separating component $j$ costs the Riesz 2-energy $E_j$ of this signature, a sum, over the other components, of inverse pairwise Fisher informations. For Gaussian sources no estimator in $O(d)$ beats $cE_j/(n+E_j)$ at any $n$ and $d$, and above an explicit sample size a spectral estimator attains $E_j/n$ up to a constant, for every mixing and without the signatures (given a lower bound on the pairwise information). Since signatures live in $K-1$ dimensions, the rate obeys a dimension law: $d^2/n$, $d\log d/n$ and $d/n$ for $K=2$, $3$ and $K\ge4$ well-spread environments. What counts is the dimension the signatures span, not their number, and $\log d$ well-designed environments make $d\log d$ samples suffice. Projecting the covariances onto one linear combination pays $d^2/n$ at first order instead. In simulations up to $d=1000$ the predictions hold and joint diagonalization with $K\ge4$ attains the rate once $n/d$ is in the hundreds; on task fMRI the energy says which components are hard to separate.

</details>


### [72] [Rubric Curriculum RL: Exploiting the Generation-Verification Gap in Non-Verifiable Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06244)
*Sumeet Ramesh Motwani,Tejas Krishnan,Alistair Letcher,Charles London,Suhaas Bhat,Huitian Jiao,Philip Torr,Riashat Islam,Christopher Summerfield,Jakob Foerster,Christian Schroeder de Witt,Qilong Gu,Shital Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Rubric Curriculum RL（RcRL）：在无可验证奖励的开放式任务中，将"成对偏好比较"与"评分标准课程"结合，构造更具判别力且非平稳的奖励信号，无需额外数据、人工标注或更强教师模型，即可把 RLVR 的增益扩展到开放式领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在数学与代码生成上效果显著，但开放式领域缺乏真值验证，人工标注昂贵，学习型奖励模型易被 reward hacking。生成-验证差距（判断好坏比生成更容易）提供改进空间，但基于评分标准（rubric）的评审通常不精确且静态，策略易过拟合或钻空子。

Method: RcRL 利用生成-验证差距而不引入新数据、人工标注或更强教师模型：对 rollout 采用成对偏好比较（而非绝对打分）以获得更强判别信号；同时在 rubric 准则上施加课程（curriculum），使优化目标随训练非平稳演化，抑制过拟合与 reward hacking。

Result: 基线方法在数百步内即进入平台期，而 RcRL 能保持输出熵并在 1000 步之后持续提升。在创意写作上对基座模型取得 70.5% 的人类胜率，且在不同基准与评审器上均有增益。在 HealthBench 上相对基座提升 14.6%（全集）与 25.4%（共识子集），优于包括实例级 rubric 在内的所有训练基线。

Conclusion: 成对偏好 + rubric 课程是一种无需外部监督即可构建可扩展、抗钻空子奖励信号的可行范式，可将 RLVR 式强化学习有效推广到开放式生成任务，并在创意写作与健康问答等场景获得稳定、可延续的训练收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has led to significant gains in math and code generation. Extending these gains to open-ended domains remains challenging: ground-truth verification is unavailable, human annotation is expensive, and learned reward models are prone to reward hacking. The generation-verification gap, where judging good outputs is easier than producing them, offers a potential path to improvement. However, rubric-based judges are often imprecise and stationary, so policies can overfit or reward hack them. We introduce Rubric Curriculum RL (RcRL), which exploits this gap without new data, human annotations, or stronger teacher models. We combine pairwise preferences over rollouts with a curriculum over rubric criteria, yielding a more discriminative signal than absolute scoring and a non-stationary objective. Whereas baselines plateau within a few hundred steps, RcRL preserves output entropy and keeps improving past 1000 steps. On creative writing, RcRL achieves a 70.5% human win rate against the base model, with gains across benchmarks and judges. On HealthBench, RcRL improves over the base model by 14.6% (full set) and 25.4% (consensus subset), outperforming all training baselines, including instance-specific rubrics.

</details>


### [73] [Learning Noise Optimization for Reward Alignment in Few-Step Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06255)
*Hongeun Kim,Jeongsol Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeNO 将奖励引导的初始噪声优化摊销为一个学习到的残差传输，在冻结预训练生成器的前提下用 Local GRPO 学习以锚点为条件的噪声修正分布，推理时单次修正即可提升奖励，避免迭代式测试时搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散与流模型的奖励对齐通常在随机轨迹上建模，不适用于少步确定性生成器；无梯度的初始噪声优化虽无需轨迹，却需在推理时逐样本进行昂贵搜索。

Method: LeNO 学习以锚点噪声为条件的修正分布，用 Local GRPO 结合标量奖励训练，生成器保持冻结；对高斯初始噪声采用保范投影，并推导修正后噪声分布与原始先验之间 KL 散度的可计算上界，作为有原则的正则目标。

Result: 在少步文本到图像生成与蛋白质结构生成（含昂贵的黑盒可设计性奖励）上，LeNO 在推理时仅需一次噪声修正即提升目标奖励，同时基本保持生成质量与多样性。

Conclusion: LeNO 以摊销方式实现免轨迹的奖励对齐，兼顾少步确定性生成器的适配性与推理效率，在保持预训练生成器不变的情况下获得可观的奖励提升。

Abstract: Reward alignment for diffusion- and flow-based generative models is often formulated over stochastic trajectories, making it less suitable for few-step deterministic generators. Initial noise optimization provides a trajectory-free alternative, but existing gradient-free methods require costly per-sample search at inference. We introduce LeNO (Learning Noise Optimization), which amortizes reward-guided initial noise optimization into a learned residual transport while keeping the pretrained generator frozen. LeNO learns an anchor-conditioned distribution over noise corrections using Local GRPO and scalar rewards, eliminating iterative test-time optimization. For Gaussian initial noise with norm-preserving projection, we derive a tractable upper bound on the KL divergence between the corrected noise distribution and the original prior, providing a principled regularization objective. We evaluate LeNO on few-step text-to-image and protein structure generation, including expensive black-box designability rewards. Across both domains, LeNO improves target rewards with a single noise correction at inference while largely preserving generation quality and diversity.

</details>


### [74] [Learning Pseudo-Riemannian Manifolds for Heterophilic Graphs via Graph Signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06265)
*Yun Young Choi,Asung Kil,Sun Woo Park,Minho Lee,Seokhwan Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出主流GNN隐含正定几何先验，在异配图上失配，提出用类别兼容矩阵的惯性(Graph Signature Index)诊断并构建Pseudo-Riemannian Attention Network (PRAT)，在15个节点分类基准上达到或超过最强异配适配模型，并通过消融证明不定号结构本身带来性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热扩散式图卷积需半正定算子、双曲模型将特征置于正定黎曼流形、点积/GAT式注意力使用正定内积，这些家族都携带正定几何先验。在异配图上边常表示相异或结构排斥，正定形式无法为标签可分的某个方向赋予负的平方距离，先验与任务不匹配。

Method: 引入Graph Signature Index (s_G)，即仅由标签拓扑构建的类别兼容矩阵的惯性；证明边/节点同配性指数在(0,1)内任何取值都不能排除负特征值。据此提出PRAT：注意力logit为可学习对角度量下的有符号平方伪距离，该度量以s_G初始化，采用先诊断后学习策略——签名提供符号结构，训练中不受约束地保持在近零方向之外，投影学习其余度量参数。

Result: 在15个节点分类基准上，PRAT优于或匹配最强的异配适配模型。控制消融显示：固定架构与协议，禁止度量为不定时会降低被s_G标记为不定的图上的准确率，而其余图保持不变。

Conclusion: 正定几何先验与异配图存在结构性失配，可用s_G进行诊断；伪黎曼注意力的不定符号结构是可测且有效的归纳偏置，性能增益可归因于符号结构本身。

Abstract: Several widely used families of Graph Neural Networks carry a positive-definite geometric prior. Graph convolution read as heat diffusion needs a positive semi-definite operator, hyperbolic models place features on positive-definite Riemannian manifolds, and dot-product and GAT-style attention scores use positive-definite inner products. On heterophilic graphs, where edges often signify dissimilarity or structural repulsion, that prior is a mismatch: no positive-definite form can assign a negative squared distance to a direction the labels separate. To measure the mismatch, we introduce the Graph Signature Index ($s_{\mathcal{G}}$), the inertia of a class-compatibility matrix built from label topology alone, and we prove that no value of the edge or node homophily index in $(0,1)$ rules out a negative eigenvalue. We then propose the Pseudo-Riemannian Attention Network (PRAT), whose attention logit is a signed squared pseudo-distance under a learnable diagonal metric initialized from $s_{\mathcal{G}}$. The design is diagnose-then-learn: the signature supplies a sign structure that unconstrained training keeps outside near-zero directions, and the projection learns the rest of the metric around it. Across fifteen node-classification benchmarks PRAT outperforms or matches the strongest heterophily-adapted models. A controlled ablation ties that performance to the sign structure itself: with architecture and protocol fixed, forbidding the metric to be indefinite costs accuracy on the graphs $s_{\mathcal{G}}$ marks as indefinite and leaves the others unchanged.

</details>


### [75] [Consistency Learning and Distillation for One-Step MIMO-OFDM Channel Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06271)
*Fredrik Nguyen,Qiwen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 observation-anchored consistency 与 Consistency Mid-Training (CMT) 估计器，实现 MIMO-OFDM 单步信道估计，在显著降参降算力下降低误符号率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代式神经信道估计器可缓解导频污染下的恢复问题，但重复网络前向推理带来高昂推理成本，需要以单步估计替代迭代。

Method: 两类估计器均对导频导出的 LS 信道估计在 delay-beam 域进行精化；一致性学习沿 noisy-to-clean 路径耦合端点预测；CMT 进一步将紧凑的、经一致性训练的 student 蒸馏，使其能从中途轨迹状态直接预测 flow-matching teacher 的端点。

Result: 在所评估 SNR 范围内，紧凑 CMT student 相较单步 flow-matching 基线将未编码 SER 降低 9.6–20.2%（对数 SER 降低 0.44–0.98 dB），同时参数量减少 74.5%、网络乘加次数减少 73.0%。

Conclusion: 一致性建模与中程训练可在不牺牲（并提升）估计精度的前提下，把多步迭代推理压缩为单步，实现精度与推理效率的良好折中。

Abstract: Iterative neural channel estimators can improve recovery under pilot contamination, but repeated network evaluations increase inference cost. This paper develops observation-anchored consistency and Consistency Mid-Training (CMT) estimators for one-step channel estimation in multiple-input multiple-output orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (MIMO-OFDM) systems. Both estimators refine pilot-derived least-squares channel estimates in the delay-beam domain. Consistency learning couples endpoint predictions along noisy-to-clean paths. CMT then refines a compact consistency-trained student to predict a flow-matching teacher's endpoint from intermediate trajectory states. Across the evaluated SNR range, the compact CMT student reduces uncoded symbol error rate (SER) by 9.6-20.2% relative to the one-step flow-matching baseline (0.44-0.98 dB in logarithmic SER reduction), while using 74.5% fewer parameters and 73.0% fewer network multiply-accumulates.

</details>


### [76] [The Split Protocol Decides the Result: Re-Evaluating Encrypted Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06290)
*Mayan Sharma,Kriti Saini,Devansh Behl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出加密流量分类器报告的 95–99% 高准确率主要源于评估协议的泄漏（同一抓包内的流被分到训练/测试两侧）；作者提出"单标识符探针"诊断法，在训练前即可识别真正的泛化轴，并据此重新评估模型，发现多项预注册结论失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中加密流量分类普遍报告 95–99% 的准确率，但该数字可能来自评估协议本身的数据泄漏，而非模型真实泛化能力。作者希望厘清"随机划分"是否被误当作泛化性证据，并给出可复用、无需训练即可使用的评估诊断方法。

Method: 1) 对比同一模型与代码在两种协议下的表现：文献标准的随机划分 vs. 保持同一抓包流量不跨划分（会话/捕获级隔离）；2) 在一个月骨干网 QUIC 流量上，以 8 个随机种子评测五种划分协议：随机、时间、客户端隔离、服务器隔离、会话隔离；3) 提出单标识符探针（single-identifier probe）：仅用抓包标识符训练决策树，在训练任何模型之前判断哪条轴是真正的泛化轴；4) 在更严格评估下对比度量学习流嵌入与四个深度基线及梯度提升树（20 种包长）；5) 对五项预注册架构假设进行检验与消融（含度量目标消融、基于距离的未知应用拒识 vs. softmax 阈值、零样本与少样本注册）。

Result: 同一模型在文献标准协议下 macro-F1 为 0.992，在捕获级隔离协议下仅 0.567，差距 0.426（8 种子，Holm-Bonferroni 校正后 p = 0.008）。随机、时间、客户端隔离与会话隔离在统计上不可区分，服务器隔离则损失 0.192 macro-F1（d = 17.0，p = 0.008）；关键轴取决于语料采集方式，且从标签空间不可见。单标识符探针在随机划分下达 0.983，在会话隔离划分下恰为 0.000。度量学习流嵌入优于四个深度基线，但在 20 种包长上不及梯度提升树。五项预注册结论均未成立：距离拒识优于 softmax 阈值，但消融全部度量目标后拒识反而更好，类分离度与拒识质量相关系数 r = -0.88；少样本注册在单一语料内几乎无增益，跨语料时每类 5 条标注流可弥补零样本迁移失败所留缺口的 91%。

Conclusion: 加密流量分类的高分很大程度上是评估协议造成的假象，泛化性必须按下游部署语义（会话/客户端/服务器轴）划分数据集；单标识符探针可在训练前低成本识别正确的划分轴。作者开源全部划分、结果、代码与负面发现，强调应把零结果视为领域知识的一部分。

Abstract: Encrypted traffic classifiers are routinely reported at 95-99% accuracy. We show that on one of two public corpora, the same model and code score macro-F1 0.992 under the evaluation protocol standard in the literature and 0.567 under a protocol that keeps flows from one packet capture on one side of the split. Nothing changes but the partition (gap 0.426 over eight seeds, p = 0.008 after Holm-Bonferroni correction).
  The inflation is not a property of random splitting as such. On a month of backbone QUIC traffic, we evaluate five split protocols at eight seeds each: random, temporal and client-disjoint splitting are statistically indistinguishable from session-disjoint, while server-disjoint splitting costs 0.192 macro-F1 (d = 17.0, p = 0.008). Which axis matters depends on how the corpus was collected, in a way invisible from its label space.
  We give a diagnostic that identifies the right axis before any model is trained: single-identifier probes. A decision tree on the capture identifier alone reaches 0.983 under a random split and exactly 0.000 under session-disjoint splitting.
  Under this evaluation, a metric-trained flow embedding leads four deep baselines but does not beat gradient-boosted trees on twenty packet sizes. Five of our own pre-registered architectural claims fail: distance-based rejection of unknown applications beats softmax thresholding, but ablating every metric objective makes rejection better, because class separation correlates at r = -0.88 with rejection quality. Few-shot enrollment is flat within a corpus but, across corpora, five labelled flows per class recover 91% of the deficit left by failed zero-shot transfer.
  All splits, results, code and negative findings are released.

</details>


### [77] [When Does Risk-Controlled Throughput Forecasting Actually Control Risk? A Reproduction Study on LEO Satellite Link](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06307)
*Mayan Sharma,Kriti Saini,Devansh Behl,Suganya R,S. Vijayaragavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 复现BG-CFQS的实证研究：风险保证可复现但依赖校准划分；低风险预算(≤0.15)不可服务；静态保形校准不能改善排序只平移操作点；在线重校准有效而群条件化部分失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险预算吞吐预测需将LEO卫星容量的点预测转为过估计率受控的保守上界以支撑安全准入控制。作者意在检验现有SOTA方法BG-CFQS的风险保证在部署场景下是否稳健、能否服务低风险预算，并厘清"在线重校准"与"群条件化"两部分各自的真实收益来源。

Method: 在原论文3条Starlink轨迹外加独立的第4个数据集上复现BG-CFQS；对比随机划分与连续时间划分；在0.05/0.10/0.15等预算下测量实际过估计率；对静态保形校准与在线校准在延迟尖峰检测任务上比较AUPRC；将群条件在线重校准拆解为在线部分与群条件部分分别评估，并测试以服务卫星几何等协变量做条件化，以及预测哪些数据集会获益。

Result: ①随机划分下复现全部已发表量（3/3数据集风险通过），连续时间划分下仅1/3通过；②任何划分下均无法服务低于0.15的预算，0.05/0.10/0.15三档达成率相同，最紧处超出1.4–3.7倍；③静态校准无法改善排序，在线校准在延迟尖峰检测上AUPRC最高提升51%；④在线部分可复现，0.05–0.35区间内追踪预算误差<1%，静态校准偏差16–21%；群条件部分不可复现，4个数据集中3个在任一协变量条件化后均变差，且预测获益数据集的尝试失败。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于实证结论而非新算法：BG-CFQS的风险保证高度依赖校准划分且对低预算失效；静态保形校准只能平移操作点而非改进排序；在线重校准是有效成分，群条件化部分缺乏普适收益。作者明确主张"结论而非方法"。

Abstract: Risk-budgeted throughput forecasting converts a point forecast of LEO satellite capacity into a conservative bound whose overestimation rate is held at a user-specified budget, so that admission control can act on it safely. We reproduce the state of the art, BG-CFQS, on the three Starlink traces its authors used and on an independent fourth dataset, and report four findings. First, its risk guarantee is reproducible but conditional on the calibration split: under a random split we recover every published quantity, including risk-pass on 3 of 3 datasets, but under a contiguous temporal split, which is what a deployed terminal faces, it holds on 1 of 3. Second, independent of any split, the method cannot serve a risk budget below 0.15, the low end of its candidate quantile set. On all four datasets the achieved rate is identical at budgets of 0.05, 0.10 and 0.15, overshooting by 1.4x to 3.7x at the tightest. Third, static conformal calibration cannot improve a ranking, only shift an operating point; we confirm this exactly on latency spike detection, where online calibration improves AUPRC by up to 51%. Fourth, group-conditional online recalibration splits into two mechanisms. The online part replicates: it tracks the budget within 1% from 0.05 to 0.35, where static calibration is 16 to 21% off. The group-conditional part does not: on three of four datasets, conditioning on any available covariate, including measured serving-satellite geometry, is worse than not conditioning, and our attempt to predict which datasets benefit failed. What is new here is empirical, not algorithmic: we claim the findings, not the methods.

</details>


### [78] [Conformal Prediction for Distribution-to-Distribution Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06320)
*Trung-Khang Tran,Tuan A. Hoang,Viet-Hoang Tran,Tan M. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向"分布到分布回归"(D2D)的保形预测框架，在正交基空间中构造可解释且具覆盖保证的预测集，并给出渐近条件覆盖的自适应变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Split Conformal Prediction已扩展到多标签、高维与函数型输出，但直接套用于分布到分布回归时，所得预测集仅边缘有效，缺乏明确体积定义或可行采样流程，难以解释与实践使用。

Method: 在正交基（基函数系数）空间中执行Split CP，使预测集具有可解释结构与可计算体积；并设计自适应变体，在温和假设下获得渐近条件覆盖。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上的实验验证了所提方法相对朴素Split CP在可解释性与实用性上的有效性，同时保持覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 正交基空间中的保形框架可为D2D回归提供既统计有效又可解释的不确定性量化，自适应变体进一步改善条件覆盖；代码已公开。

Abstract: Conformal Prediction (CP) provides a model-agnostic framework for constructing finite-sample prediction sets with coverage guarantees and has become a tool for reliable decision-making in high-risk settings. Recent advances have extended Split Conformal Prediction to increasingly complex learning problems, including multilabel, high-dimensional, and functional outputs. In this work, we study CP for Distribution-to-Distribution Regression, a setting of growing interest across many application domains. We show that a naive application of Split CP yields prediction sets that, while marginally valid, are non-interpretable, e.g., lacking a well-defined volume or a tractable sampling procedure, and of limited practical value. To overcome this, we propose a new conformal framework that operates in an orthogonal basis space, producing interpretable prediction sets while preserving coverage guarantees. We further develop an adaptive variant with asymptotic conditional coverage under mild assumptions. Empirical results on synthetic and real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of the proposed approach. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/tuanhacs/conformal_d2d.

</details>


### [79] [Learning to Tune with Biased Gradients: Iterative Feedback Tuning of Model-Free Control on Throttle Valves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06338)
*Félix Martinez-Jiménez,Sander Miller-Murphy,Tobie Ondoa-Essomba,Emmanuel Witrant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究IFT在非线性（摩擦/粘滑）电子节气门上的有偏梯度问题，揭示控制器状态失配导致梯度误差、IFT梯度常非下降方向、各更新规则平台化后漂移，硬件上最优更新规则依赖梯度噪声结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IFT每次迭代需两次闭环实验估计代价梯度，该估计仅对线性对象无偏；实际电子节气门存在阀间与运行间变化的摩擦和粘滑，使梯度有偏，且现有更新规则（梯度下降、Gauss–Newton、Adam）在该有偏情形下的表现缺乏对比研究。

Method: 对象为智能比例（无模型、增量PI）控制器控制的车用电子节气门；分析两次实验起点控制器状态失配（粘滑所致）引入的误差，证明其与第二次实验幅值成反比；在10个阀的数字孪生上评估IFT梯度的下降方向性与各更新规则行为；在2个硬件阀上比较不同更新规则。

Result: 孪生实验中，仅在低成本区69%的迭代中IFT梯度为下降方向（摩擦为成因），所有更新规则均先平台化再漂移；硬件上最优规则取决于梯度噪声结构：第一阀某梯度分量淹没于噪声，Adam优于Gauss–Newton，其归一化与动量各改善一个参数；第二阀两分量一致，同一归一化放大了Gauss–Newton本应抑制的方向，Adam漂移至劣于初值的控制器。孪生复现了机制但未复现该排序。

Conclusion: IFT在含摩擦/粘滑的非线性对象上梯度有偏，起点状态失配是误差来源之一；更新规则选择应依据梯度噪声结构，而非固定使用Adam；数字孪生可复现机理但不足以预测硬件上的规则排序，需硬件验证。

Abstract: Iterative feedback tuning (IFT) learns the parameters of a fixed-structure controller by stochastic gradient descent. At each iteration, two closed-loop experiments are run on the plant: the first one measures the tracking error, and the second one, driven by this error, provides the sensitivities from which the gradient of the cost is estimated. This estimate is unbiased for linear plants only. We study the biased case on automotive electronic throttle valves, whose friction and stiction vary between valves and runs, controlled by an intelligent proportional (model-free) controller, which is an incremental PI controller. We show that the mismatch of the controller state at the start of the two experiments, which stiction makes unavoidable, adds an error inversely proportional to the amplitude of the second experiment. On digital twins of ten valves, the IFT gradient is a descent direction in only 69\% of the low-cost region, friction being the cause, and all update rules plateau and then drift. On two hardware valves, the best update rule depends on the noise structure of the gradient. On the first valve, where one gradient component is buried in noise, Adam outperforms Gauss--Newton, its normalisation and its momentum each improving one parameter. On the second valve, where both components are consistent, the same normalisation amplifies a direction that Gauss--Newton rightly discounts, and Adam drifts to a controller worse than the initial one. The twins reproduce the mechanism but not this ranking.

</details>


### [80] [Flexible Intensities Matter: A Comprehensive Re-evaluation of Hawkes Processes and Neural Temporal Point Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06348)
*Hendrik Mehrtens,Florian Buettner,Oliver Stegle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统重评了经典（Poisson/Hawkes）与神经时序点过程（TPP）模型，发现此前"神经TPP大幅超越Hawkes"的结论主要源于基线对比不公平；配备灵活可学习基线与核函数后，Hawkes几乎追平神经TPP，且神经模型的优势主要来自事件发生时间密度而非类型分布，仅在大规模MIMIC-IV ICU数据上保留类型项优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究宣称神经TPP显著优于Hawkes等经典TPP，但这些比较往往将灵活的神经模型与最受限形式的Hawkes对比，且未涉及大规模医疗数据，导致结论可能存在偏差，需要更公平、系统的评估以指导方法开发与模型选择。

Method: 在5个标准基准数据集及2个大规模医疗任务（UK Biobank疾病史、MIMIC-IV ICU记录）上，于自回归下一事件预测设定下，对灵活度递增的Poisson、Hawkes过程与神经TPP进行系统对比；将Hawkes配备灵活可学习基线与核函数；提出带样条强度头的Transformer；并将对数似然分解为时间项log p(t)与类型项log p(d|t)分别分析；同时开源统一实现框架TorchPP。

Result: 1）Hawkes配备灵活基线与核函数后，与神经TPP的差距基本消失；2）强度参数化的灵活性同样限制神经TPP，样条强度头Transformer优于此前基于强度的神经TPP，与无强度模型相当；3）该灵活性优势几乎完全来自时间密度项，而非类型项；4）仅在MIMIC-IV ICU数据上神经TPP在类型项上保持优势。

Conclusion: 神经TPP相对经典TPP的优势被此前不公平对比所高估，其收益主要在"事件何时发生"而非"发生何种事件"；复杂/大规模数据对区分模型能力至关重要；研究结果可为TPP方法开发与模型选择提供依据，并发布开源框架TorchPP以支持可复现的公平评估。

Abstract: Temporal point processes (TPPs) are the canonical framework for event sequence data, with applications spanning seismology, social media, and electronic health records. Recently, neural TPPs have been reported to substantially outperform classical TPPs, such as Hawkes processes, but these comparisons pitted flexible neural models against Hawkes processes in their most restricted form, and did not consider large-scale medical data. Here, we revisit this comparison through a systematic evaluation of Poisson and Hawkes processes of increasing flexibility against neural TPPs, on five established benchmark datasets and on two large-scale medical evaluation tasks derived from UK Biobank disease histories and MIMIC-IV ICU records, under autoregressive next-event prediction. First, the previously reported gap between Hawkes processes and neural TPPs largely disappears once Hawkes processes are equipped with flexible, learnable baselines and kernels. Second, the flexibility of the intensity parametrization also limits prior neural TPPs: a Transformer with a spline-based intensity head outperforms previous intensity-based neural TPPs and is on par with the intensity-free model. Decomposing the log-likelihood into a time term $\log p(t)$ and a type term $\log p(d \mid t)$, we find that this flexibility advantage lies almost entirely in the time density: more flexibility does not improve the type term. On these benchmarks, the gains of neural TPPs thus lie mostly in modeling when events occur, not which. Third, our results underscore the value of complex datasets for evaluating TPPs: only on the MIMIC-IV ICU records do neural TPPs retain an advantage in the type term. Our results inform both future method development and model choice. We release our framework, TorchPP, as an open-source package with consistent implementations of classical and neural TPPs.

</details>


### [81] [EEGDash Corpus: A Training-Ready Public Corpus for EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06356)
*Kuber Shahi,Bruno Aristimunha,Arnaud Delorme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布 eegdash-corpus-v0：一个可直接用于训练的公开 EEG 语料库，含 77 个数据集、14,075 小时、7,272 名受试者、每 epoch 62.1 亿 token；并验证基于该语料可从头复现 REVE-Base 的预训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG 基础模型依赖海量预训练数据，但数据难以汇集：最大来源为不可再分发的临床档案，公开数据则分散于多个仓库、文件格式与许可协议。以 REVE（已发表最大的 EEG 基础模型）为例，其宣称的 89 个预训练数据集中有 12 个不可再分发（4 个临床档案占其小时数的 82%，8 个为禁止衍生作品许可），因此需要一个合规、可复现、训练就绪的公开语料。

Method: 构建 eegdash-corpus-v0：经 EEGDash 抓取 REVE 采样率所允许的 76 个数据集，另加 REVE 发布说明中的 1 个，共 77 个数据集；统一按 REVE 的预处理配方转换一次，存为分块 Zarr 存储，每个数据集独立分桶并在旁记录其许可证；每个文件附 SHA-256 校验；以门控方式发布于 Hugging Face（EEGDash/eegdash-corpus-v0）。验证实验：使用作者原代码从头重训 REVE-Base 两次，仅改动数据读取方式，在 OpenEEGBench 的 7 个公开任务上线性探测与微调评估，并以已发布模型和未训练编码器为对照。

Result: 语料规模为 77 个数据集、14,075 小时、7,272 名受试者、500 万个 10 秒样本、每 epoch 62.1 亿 token。复现模型线性探测得分 0.331、微调后 0.273；已发布模型对应为 0.333 与 0.325；未训练编码器为 0.200 与 0.205。

Conclusion: 该语料可在无原始临床数据、无禁止衍生许可数据的条件下支撑 EEG 基础模型预训练：线性探测性能与官方发布模型基本持平，微调后存在可感知差距（0.273 vs 0.325），说明本语料可作为透明、可再分发的预训练替代方案。转换器、转换报告与加载器改动随语料一同发布，支持整体或任意子集训练。

Abstract: Foundation models for electroencephalography (EEG) are pretrained on tens of thousands of hours of recordings, and their accuracy grows with that amount. Such data is hard to assemble. The largest sources are clinical archives that cannot be redistributed, and the public sources are spread over several archives, file formats, and licences. We release eegdash-corpus-v0, a training-ready corpus of 77 public EEG datasets, as the gated Hugging Face dataset EEGDash/eegdash-corpus-v0 with the SHA-256 of every file. REVE, the largest published EEG foundation model, names 89 datasets as its pretraining corpus. Twelve cannot be redistributed: four clinical archives that hold 82% of its hours, and eight under no-derivatives licences. The corpus holds the 76 others that REVE's sampling rate allows and one more from REVE's release card: 77 datasets, 14,075 hours from 7,272 subjects, 5.0 million 10-second samples and 6.21 billion tokens per epoch. Every dataset is fetched through EEGDash, converted once with REVE's recipe, and stored in its own buckets of a chunked Zarr store with its licence recorded beside it, so a model can train on the whole store or on any subset of datasets. To test that the store pretrains a model, we retrain REVE-Base from scratch twice with the authors' code, changed only in how it reads data, and score it on seven public OpenEEGBench tasks. It reaches 0.331 under linear probing and 0.273 after fine-tuning, against 0.333 and 0.325 for the released model and 0.200 and 0.205 for an untrained encoder. The converter, the conversion reports, and the loader changes are released with the corpus.

</details>


### [82] [Give Up to Get More: Priors, Not Policies, Govern Test-Time Compute Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06372)
*Jinhyung Bae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在多次尝试的预算分配中，对"较难输入多分配"的比例规则在难任务上收益有限；真正决定收益的是"哪些输入可解"的信息而非分配策略本身，Thompson Sampling配合可靠的先验与验证器可将最难工作负载的解决率从14%提升到27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中普遍采用按难度比例分配重复尝试预算的规则，但该规则在模型难以解决的高难度工作负载上表现不佳；作者希望厘清难度比例分配的失效条件，并明确提升分配收益的真正驱动因素。

Method: 从理论上分析单次额外尝试价值的难度分布（在中等难度处达到峰值，故最优静态分配呈倒U形）；对221篇LLM预算分配论文进行严格综述；在公开数学、代码与形式化证明样本上评估现有规则；引入验证器与来自相关模型历史运行的先验，使用Thompson Sampling进行动态分配；并考察先验秩相关性与验证器误接受率的影响。

Result: 最佳已发表的难度比例规则在模型基本能解决的基准上可捕获一半以上的oracle增益，但在硬工作负载上至多13%（即便给定真实通过率）。综述发现至少13篇论文给最难输入分配最多尝试次数，其在较易设置下近最优，代价体现在难工作负载。Thompson Sampling将最难工作负载的解决率从14%提升到27%；无先验时无策略优于轮询。当先验秩相关低于约0.3–0.55时增益消失，而廉价文本先验在最弱模型上难以达到该水平，且需要误接受率相对通过率极低的验证器。该结论在Qwen2.5新样本上复现，对先验做退火可避免在简单工作负载上的损失。

Conclusion: 分配策略的收益主要由"可解性信息"而非策略形式驱动：高难度场景下难度比例规则低效，只有在具备可靠验证器与足够准确先验时，基于Thompson Sampling的分配才显著获益；据此作者给出了一套决策指南。

Abstract: When many inputs share a budget of repeated attempts by a large language model (LLM), a common rule gives harder inputs more. On public math, code and formal-proof samples, the best published difficulty-proportional rule captures half or more of the oracle gain on benchmarks the model mostly solves, but at most 13% on hard workloads, even given true pass rates. Because the value of one more attempt peaks at intermediate difficulty, the optimal static allocation is inverted-U and gives little to inputs that are hopeless at the current budget. In a strict survey of 221 LLM allocation papers, at least 13 give the hardest inputs the most attempts; their easier settings sit at budgets where this is near-optimal, so the cost appears on harder workloads. Acting on it requires knowing which inputs are solvable, so information, not the policy, drives the gain. With a verifier and a prior from a related model's past runs on the same inputs, Thompson Sampling raises the solved share on the hardest workload from 14% to 27%; without such a prior, no policy we tested beats round-robin. For the policies we tested, the gain vanishes between prior rank correlations of about 0.3 and 0.55, which cheap text-based priors barely reach on the weakest models, and it requires a verifier whose false acceptances are rare relative to the pass rate. On new Qwen2.5 samples the pattern holds, and tempering the prior avoids losses on easy workloads. We turn these results into a decision guide.

</details>


### [83] [From hydrological laws to catchment models: A process-based model matches deep-learning accuracy on three continents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06382)
*Xilin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于三条物理定律（质量守恒、耗散、聚合）构建的低维集总式流域模型CaRT/CaRT+，其精度可媲美甚至部分超越LSTM，表明流域尺度上存在"形式定律"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应Dooge关于水文是否存在流域尺度定律的问题：检验在物理定律约束下构建的低维系统能否达到超越过程式模型的神经网络同等精度。

Method: 三条定律约束集总模型：守恒使其成为通量连通的储水网络；耗散固定每个通量符号；聚合决定未解析成分如何通过阈值分布上的有效定律及对强迫的记忆进入通量。定律允许四类细化。以简约模型CaRT（每流域11参数）为基础，由AI代理辅助迭代细化，每次针对观测到的缺陷提出文献假设，得到CaRT+（14个状态、35参数、无日尺度神经网络）。

Result: 在英国602个和美国593个有测站流域的留出年份上，单一CaRT+的NSE中位数分别为0.863和0.765；有状态LSTM为0.869和0.751，实体感知LSTM为0.862和0.754；三种子LSTM集成在中位配对NSE上仍领先CaRT+集成0.003–0.012。在未参与开发的505个较干旱澳大利亚流域，单一CaRT+达0.778，高于两LSTM的0.762和0.753。

Conclusion: 流域尺度定律可能以"形式定律"存在：本构族共享，仅其参数需学习。低维物理约束模型可与LSTM精度相当，在干旱流域体现出更好泛化性。

Abstract: Dooge asked whether hydrology has laws at the catchment scale. We ask whether a low-dimensional system built under physical laws can match the accuracy of neural networks that outperform process-based models on the same inputs. Three laws constrain a lumped catchment model. Conservation makes it a flux-connected storage network, dissipation fixes the sign of every flux and aggregation determines how the unresolved composition enters the fluxes, through effective laws over threshold distributions and a memory of the forcing. These laws admit four kinds of refinement. A parsimonious model under them, CaRT (11 parameters per catchment), is refined in a loop assisted by AI agents. Each refinement addresses an observed deficit through a hypothesis from the literature. The result, CaRT+, has fourteen states, 35 parameters per catchment and no daily neural network. Most refinements follow from aggregation over thresholds or from variables that carry no water but modulate fluxes. On 602 British and 593 US gauged catchments, single CaRT+ models reach median Nash-Sutcliffe efficiencies of 0.863 and 0.765 in held-out years. A stateful LSTM reaches 0.869 and 0.751 and an entity-aware LSTM 0.862 and 0.754, while three-seed LSTM ensembles remain ahead of the CaRT+ ensemble by 0.003-0.012 in median paired NSE. On 505 drier Australian gauged catchments unused in development, single CaRT+ models reach 0.778 against 0.762 and 0.753 for the two LSTMs. The results suggest that catchment-scale laws exist as laws of form, with the constitutive families shared and only their parameters learned.

</details>


### [84] [KolTa: A Physics-Informed Active Learning Framework for Formulation Discovery with Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06394)
*Boyong Tang,Tao Wang,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出主动学习框架KolTa，在有限实验预算下以批选择方式发现目标温度下性能排名前5%的配方；融合三阶段预测器与物理残差校准UCB采集规则，在四个任务上AULC排名第一或第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本、实验预算受限的场景下，配方的高性能组合难以通过大量测量筛选；需要在固定查询预算内高效定位目标温度下的最优配方子集。

Method: KolTa由两部分组成：(1) 三阶段预测器，依次建模组分贡献、角色条件交互与锚定温度响应，并附加任务相关的软物理约束；(2) 物理残差校准上置信界（PRC-UCB）采集规则，融合预测效用、集成不确定度与目标温度处的物理残差，探索权重由折外有符号误差拟合且非负约束。以批选择策略迭代推荐配方。

Result: 在三个实测任务（二元混合物黏度、药物溶解度、离子液体黏度）与一个仿真任务（电解质电导率）上评估：学习曲线下面积（AULC）均值在每个任务上均列第一或第二；配对检验中优于八种对照策略中的七种，跨任务与随机种子共78次配对比较中胜出75次；三个实测任务上其与最佳均值AULC的最大差距为0.032，而所有对照策略至少为0.056。药物任务中，实现跨药物80%平均目标回收所需额外配方查询的中位估计为348次，比最快对照少约18%。

Conclusion: KolTa在有限预算下显著提升高性能配方发现的效率与稳定性；其基于组分角色与物理约束的可迁移设计，便于研究者适配到多样化的配方问题。

Abstract: Finding high-performing formulations is difficult when few have been measured and experimental budgets are limited. We propose KolTa, an active learning framework that selects batches to discover the top 5% of formulations at a target temperature under a fixed query budget. KolTa combines a three-stage predictor of component contributions, role-conditioned interactions, and anchored temperature response with task-specific soft physics constraints. Its Physics-Residual-Calibrated Upper Confidence Bound (PRC-UCB) acquisition rule combines predicted utility, ensemble disagreement, and a target-temperature physics residual, with nonnegative exploration weights fitted to out-of-fold signed errors. We evaluate the framework on three measured tasks and one simulated task covering binary-mixture viscosity, drug solubility, ionic-liquid viscosity, and electrolyte conductivity. KolTa ranks first or second in mean area under the learning curve (AULC) on every task and outperforms seven of the eight comparator strategies in paired tests, winning 75 of 78 paired comparisons across tasks and seeds against those seven strategies. Across the three measured tasks, its largest gap to the best mean AULC is 0.032, compared with at least 0.056 for every comparator. On Drug, reaching 80% average recovery of the remaining targets across drugs requires an estimated median of 348 additional formulation queries, about 18% fewer than the fastest comparator. Researchers can adapt the framework to diverse formulation problems through component roles and physical constraints.

</details>


### [85] [Zero-Shot Coordination among LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06409)
*Adrian Hayler,Shashank Reddy Chirra,Andrei Lupu,Johannes Forkel,Bidipta Sarkar,Siheng Feng,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM智能体的零样本协调（ZSC），在最小化环境中隔离协调挑战，发现前沿LLM仍难以协调，并提出"协调友好定义"（CFDs）作为改善方案且可自动发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZSC在RL中研究充分，但LLM智能体的ZSC表现尚不清楚；现有LLM协调工作依赖独立开发的智能体难以共享的专用脚手架，且在Hanabi等复杂环境中难以定位协调失败的来源。

Method: 使用简单、通用的脚手架和最小化环境，隔离特定协调挑战；提出Coordination-friendly definitions（CFDs）作为实现LLM智能体鲁棒协调的原则性方法，并证明CFDs可被自动发现，无需人工设计。

Result: 即便在受控的简单环境中，前沿LLM智能体仍难以协调，主要源于对协调问题理解有限以及对伙伴信念的推理薄弱；LLM引入的语义信息虽构成额外协调轴，但未能有效利用。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM在零样本协调上存在根本性不足；CFDs提供了一种有原则的改进路径，且可自动发现，减少人工工程需求，代码已开源。

Abstract: We study zero-shot coordination (ZSC), where independently developed agents must coordinate at test time. While ZSC has been well studied in the RL literature, far less is known about the performance of LLM agents despite their increasing deployment in such settings. Existing work on LLM coordination often relies on specialised scaffolding that independently developed agents are unlikely to share in practice. Moreover, evaluations on complex environments (e.g., Hanabi) make it difficult to pinpoint the sources of coordination failure. In contrast, we focus on simple, general-purpose scaffolds in minimal environments designed to isolate specific coordination challenges. Our results show that even in these controlled settings, frontier LLM agents struggle to coordinate, largely due to limited understanding of the coordination problem and weak reasoning about their partner's beliefs. While LLMs introduce semantic information as an additional axis for coordination, they nonetheless fail to exploit this structure effectively. Towards this, we propose Coordination-friendly definitions (CFDs) as a principled approach for enabling robust coordination among LLM agents. Finally, we show that CFDs can be discovered automatically, removing the need for manual engineering. Code is available at https://github.com/ahayler/zsc-llm-agents.

</details>


### [86] [Partially Interpretable Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06428)
*Ludovic Schwartz,Federico Berton,Tim van Erven*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出三种决策树式"部分可解释模型"（可把复杂输入延迟给黑箱）：精确搜索的 Branches 扩展、对标准 CART 的后处理转换、以及将延迟选项纳入分裂准则的广义 CART；并给出理论界解释三者性能相近的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可解释模型通过"延迟"机制在可解释性与精度间权衡，但现有训练方法仅基于线性模型或决策规则，缺少针对决策树的系统方法；同时需要理解智能树构建究竟能带来多少额外收益。

Method: 提出三种算法：(1) 现有 Branches 算法的部分可解释扩展，做精确搜索优化树；(2) 在标准 CART 之上运行的转换算法，计算更高效；(3) 广义 CART，其分裂准则在生长过程中直接考虑延迟选项。另推导了在黑箱精度足够同质时智能树构建潜在增益的形式化上界，并构造黑箱精度刻意非同质的合成数据集进行验证；最后将方法应用于概念瓶颈模型。

Result: 在众多真实数据集上三种算法性能相近；形式化上界在"黑箱精度在同域内足够同质"的条件下解释了这一现象。在人造非同质合成数据上，延迟感知的广义 CART 优于普通 CART 并达到精确搜索的水平。在概念瓶颈模型中，部分可解释决策树可替代瓶颈后的不可解释神经网络，未延迟输入上完全可解释，延迟则保持精度。

Conclusion: 当黑箱精度同质时，复杂的精确搜索收益有限，简单高效的转换/广义 CART 已足够；当精度异质时，延迟感知的分裂准则更关键。部分可解释决策树可作为概念瓶颈模型中黑箱后端的可解释替代方案。

Abstract: Partially interpretable models extend simple interpretable models with the option to defer complicated inputs to an accurate but uninterpretable black-box model. By deferring more inputs, the model gets more accurate; by deferring fewer, it gets more interpretable. Existing procedures to train such partially interpretable models build on linear models or decision rules. We propose three algorithms for decision trees: a partially interpretable extension of the existing Branches algorithm, which performs exact search to optimize the tree; a conversion algorithm that we run on top of standard CART, which is computationally more efficient; and a generalized CART whose splitting criterion takes into account the option to defer while growing the tree. Perhaps surprisingly, the performance of the three algorithms is similar across many realworld datasets. We can explain this through a formal bound on the potential gain of smart tree construction, provided the black box accuracy is sufficiently homogeneous across the domain. We also construct a synthetic dataset where the accuracy of the black box is deliberately inhomogeneous. There, our deferral-aware generalization of CART does outperform regular CART and matches the performance of exact search. Finally, we evaluate an application to concept bottleneck models. Partially interpretable decision trees can replace uninterpretable neural networks after the bottleneck. On undeferred inputs, the model is fully interpretable, and deferring preserves accuracy.

</details>


### [87] [Understanding the Out-of-Distribution Generalization of Chain-of-Thought Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06438)
*Nuo-Jing Liang,Xiao-Tong Yuan,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将CoT视为组合式OOD泛化，从理论上刻画其风险上界：OOD任务风险由ID子任务风险之和乘以与T无关的𝒪(k)系数控制，估计项为𝒪(√(T/n))而非线性；并给出下界Ω(√(T/n))，实验（Atom-Task、GSM8K）支持短到长与组合泛化结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT显著提升LLM推理能力，但其OOD泛化机制缺乏理论解释。作者希望回答：当目标任务缺少完整推理轨迹或直接输入-输出对、训练仅有ID原子步骤数据时，CoT为何以及如何实现组合式OOD泛化。

Method: 将CoT建模为对共享单步预测器的反复应用，以组合可复用ID转移；在目标任务步数T、每子任务样本数n、每步输出token数k的设定下推导OOD风险界，并结合Rademacher复杂度分析估计项；进一步针对自回归NTP给出终任务风险界，并构造parity-coupled实例给出下界。

Result: OOD风险受ID子任务风险之和控制，缩放系数为𝒪(k)且与T无关（但求和风险本身可随T增长）；估计项为𝒪(√(T/n))而非线性于T，源于共享类一致容量约束下的预测器共享；NTP下终任务风险≤类内最优步级风险×𝒪(k)加Õ(k√(T/n))估计项；parity-coupled构造表明T≤n时子任务ERM的期望终任务风险至少为类内最优风险加Ω(√(T/n))。Atom-Task与GSM8K实验验证短到长及组合泛化，且显式状态追踪至关重要。

Conclusion: CoT的组合式OOD泛化可由单步预测器共享的重复组合来解释，理论界在T上呈√增长而非线性，并配以匹配的下界；共享容量约束是关键，显式状态追踪对实际泛化不可或缺。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning substantially improves Large Language Models (LLMs), yet its out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization mechanism remains theoretically underexplored. We study CoT as compositional OOD generalization: the target task lacks complete reasoning trajectories or direct input-output pairs, while training provides only in-distribution (ID) atomic step data. We show that CoT generalizes by repeatedly applying a shared single-step predictor to compose reusable ID transitions. Theoretically, let $T$ denote the number of reasoning steps in the target task, $n$ the number of training samples per sub-task, and $k$ the number of output tokens per step; then the OOD task risk is controlled by the sum of the ID sub-task risks, scaled by a coefficient of order $\mathcal{O}(k)$ that is independent of $T$, although the summed risk itself may still grow with $T$. A Rademacher-complexity analysis further shows that the estimation term is of order $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T/n})$, rather than linear in $T$, due to predictor sharing under a uniform capacity control on the shared class. For autoregressive Next-Token Prediction, the final-task risk is bounded by the best-in-class step-level risk scaled by the same coefficient of order $\mathcal{O}(k)$, plus an estimation term of order $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\big(k\sqrt{T/n}\big)$. A parity-coupled construction further shows that, for the sub-task empirical risk minimizer, the expected final-task risk is at least the best in-class risk plus $Ω(\sqrt{T/n})$ when $T\le n$. Experiments on Atom-Task and GSM8K support the theory: CoT enables short-to-long and compositional generalization, while explicit state tracking is crucial.
  Our code is available at https://github.com/maxliang114514/CoT-OOD.

</details>


### [88] [Graph Foundation Models Can Become Effective Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06441)
*Zhuangyan Hu,Zhuoxuan Liang,Wei Li,Xiaohua Jia,Moustafa Youssef,Sumi Helal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Gfm4Mtsad框架，将预训练图基础模型（GFM）适配到多变量时间序列异常检测（MTSAD），通过SICA、MAPS、TGEC三个模块解决跨模态对齐、伪异常监督与节点-窗口聚合失真问题，在两个GFM骨干上取得优于基线的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图检测器需在有限目标域数据上从零训练任务专用网络，易受虚假相关影响且无法复用可迁移先验；预训练GFM虽具备可迁移拓扑先验与异常判别能力，但直接适配MTSAD存在三重障碍：跨模态特征-拓扑失配、不可靠的伪异常监督、节点到窗口聚合过程中的决策失真。

Method: 1) 结构-信息契约对齐（SICA）：统一主成分子空间token投影、正常先验引导的图构建与残差恢复，抑制虚假边并保留细粒度时序细节，桥接连续时序与预训练GFM空间；2) 机制对齐伪异常筛选（MAPS）：施加局部依赖断裂同时保留经验残差，选取靠近正常边界的难负样本进行定向监督；3) 尾部门控证据校准（TGEC）：以正常运行参考校准多粒度图响应，保持良性分数排序不变，仅选择性细化尾部分布中的极端异常候选。

Result: 在两个GFM骨干上的广泛实验表明，Gfm4Mtsad的检测性能优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 通过契约对齐、机制对齐伪异常筛选与尾部证据校准，可有效释放预训练图基础模型在MTSAD中的能力，实现可靠的窗口级检测与可解释的传感器证据。

Abstract: Multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTSAD) relies on modeling both temporal dynamics and inter-sensor dependencies to identify complex system faults. Although graph-based detectors explicitly model sensor topologies, they train task-specific networks from scratch on limited target-domain data, remaining vulnerable to spurious correlations and unable to reuse transferable priors. While pretrained Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) offer transferable topological priors and anomaly discriminability, directly adapting them to MTSAD is hindered by cross-modal feature-topology mismatch, unreliable pseudo-anomaly supervision, and decision distortion during node-to-window aggregation. To fill these gaps, we propose a novel adaptation framework, Gfm4Mtsad, which unleashes pretrained GFMs for MTSAD. We introduce the Structure-and-Information Contract Alignment (SICA) module to bridge continuous time series and the pretrained GFM space. This module harmonizes principal subspace token projection, normal-prior-guided graph construction, and residual recovery to suppress spurious edges while retaining fine-grained temporal details. We propose a Mechanism-Aligned Pseudo-Anomaly Screening strategy (MAPS) to steer unsupervised learning. It applies localized dependency breaks while preserving empirical residuals, selecting hard negatives near the normal boundary for targeted supervision. We develop a Tail-Gated Evidence Calibration mechanism (TGEC) to eliminate node-to-window aggregation distortion. It calibrates multi-granular graph responses against normal operational references, strictly preserving benign score rankings while selectively refining extreme anomaly candidates in the tail distribution for reliable window detection and interpretable sensor evidence. Extensive evaluations with two GFM backbones demonstrate that Gfm4Mtsad achieves superior detection performance over existing baselines.

</details>


### [89] [The Alignment Tax Is Graded: Task-Selective Capability Loss Under Length-Normalized DPO with SFT Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06476)
*Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一项实证表征研究，考察在结构化金融文件任务上用长度归一化DPO加SFT保留项（RPO目标）微调Qwen3.5-4B时，随着隐式奖励边际增长，下游能力是均匀退化还是选择性退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐税（alignment tax）通常被当作整体退化来讨论，但其是否在任务间均匀分布、在何种边际区间集中、由何机制驱动尚缺系统刻画。作者希望在无新方法、无对比实验的前提下，精确表征边际增长与能力退化的关系。

Method: 在结构化金融文件任务上，用稀疏LoRA（8/32层）、长度归一化DPO加SFT保留项微调Qwen3.5-4B，将隐式奖励边际推至约7个β单位（nf4训练；fp16测量约3）。采用预注册设计（8个种子，冻结时6个未见）、产物感知评分与TOST等价检验。

Result: 对齐税呈分级且任务选择性：extraction基本保持（Δ=-0.55 pts，±5 pts内等价），json损失有限（Δ=-3.52 pts，在聚类条目级区间上等价性不成立），router严重退化（Δ=-30 pts）。router损失集中在边际1–3的"损伤窗口"并伴随短暂的格式破坏尖峰。边际约≥96%由对拒绝补全的抑制解释，选中奖励近似持平。gold-logp通缩也具任务选择性（extraction -0.15、json -1.04、router -1.47 nats/token，fp16下），且extraction上gold相对错误对照被选择性保护（8/8种子）。QLoRA奖励边际在nf4训练与无量化fp16测量间差异达3.4倍，且存在轴依赖差距。

Conclusion: 对齐税是分级且任务选择性的，集中于特定边际窗口；边际主要由拒绝补全抑制贡献而非选中奖励提升；量化训练与测量间存在显著边际差异。结论限于长度归一化奖励+SFT保留、稀疏LoRA、单一4B模型、易分离合成偏好与三类结构化任务；无保留控制组与格式对齐度量留待未来工作。

Abstract: Setting. This is an empirical characterization study; we propose no new method and run no comparisons against alternative objectives. We fine-tune Qwen3.5-4B with sparse LoRA and length-normalized DPO plus an SFT retention term (the RPO objective) on structured financial-filing tasks, and track downstream capability as the implicit reward margin grows to $\sim 7$ $β$-scaled units (nf4 training; $\sim 3$ under fp16 measurement on the unquantized base). Question. Does margin growth degrade capability uniformly across tasks, or selectively? Findings. In a pre-registered study (8 seeds, 6 unseen at freeze) with artifact-aware scoring and TOST equivalence testing, the alignment tax is graded and task-selective: extraction is preserved ($Δ= -0.55$ pts, equivalent at $\pm5$ pts), json loses little ($Δ= -3.52$ pts, equivalence fails on a clustered item-level interval), and router degrades severely ($Δ= -30$ pts). Router's loss concentrates in a margin 1-3 damage window containing a transient format-break spike. The margin is accounted for almost entirely ($\geq 96\%$) by suppression of rejected completions, with chosen reward approximately flat, consistent with the retention term anchoring it; we do not attribute the behavioral damage to any single gradient channel. Gold-logp deflation is task-selective (extraction $-0.15$, json $-1.04$, router $-1.47$ nats/token under fp16), with gold selectively protected relative to incorrect controls on extraction (8/8 seeds). QLoRA reward margins differ up to $3.4\times$ between nf4 training and unquantized fp16 measurement, with an axis-dependent gap. Scope. Length-normalized rewards with SFT retention, sparse LoRA on 8/32 layers, one 4B model, easily-separable synthetic preferences, three structured tasks. A no-retention control and a measured format-alignment metric are left to future work.

</details>


### [90] [What Does Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning Really Improve in LLM Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06483)
*Kejian Zhu,Zhuoran Jin,Shangqing Tu,Tianyi Men,Yupu Hao,Jiachun Li,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多轮智能体强化学习的收益高度依赖交互预算；作者提出BudgetMix RL，通过在训练中变化交互预算来缓解预算依赖，实现跨执行设置的稳健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮智能体RL在长程任务上表现优异，但其性能增益对交互约束（采样广度K与交互深度T）高度敏感。作者希望系统刻画这种预算依赖性，并缓解由此带来的部署风险。

Method: 以Pass@(K,T)为评估框架，系统分析采样广度K与交互深度T对性能的影响；分析轨迹级RL优势与回合预算的关系，以及智能体RL对单步决策质量与错误恢复能力的差异化作用；初步讨论过程监督信号的利弊，并提出BudgetMix RL——训练时动态变化交互预算。

Result: 轨迹级RL优势需要充足的回合预算，预算收紧时收益显著衰减；智能体RL并不普遍提升单步决策质量，其优势集中于错误恢复与长程韧性；BudgetMix RL在多种执行设置下均取得稳健性能。

Conclusion: 交互预算约束是多轮智能体RL性能的关键影响因素，存在部署风险；过程监督信号有一定价值但存在局限，而训练期预算混合（BudgetMix RL）是提升跨预算鲁棒性的有效替代方案。

Abstract: While multi-turn agentic reinforcement learning (RL) excels on long-horizon tasks, its performance gains remain highly sensitive to interaction constraints. Evaluating across sampling breadth $K$ and interaction depth $T$ via $Pass@(K,T)$, we show that trajectory-level RL advantages depend on sufficient turn budgets, diminishing significantly when budgets tighten. Besides, agentic RL does not uniformly elevate single-step decision quality; rather, its strength concentrates in error recovery and long-horizon resilience. To mitigate the deployment risks caused by this budget dependence, we provide a preliminary discussion on the benefits and limitations of process supervision signals and introduce BudgetMix RL as an alternative that varies interaction budgets during training. Experiments demonstrate that BudgetMix RL achieves robust performance across diverse execution settings. Project Page: https://rethinking-agentic-rl.github.io/

</details>


### [91] [Model Merging Does Not Require Uniform LoRA Ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06486)
*Zirong Xie,Zainab Afolabi,Nikita Kozodoi,Jack Butler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型合并中"所有专家用相同LoRA秩"的耦合并非合并本身的属性；低秩专家被丢弃的根因是合并算子按奇异值大小选分量。改用对损失的效应选分量后，秩可按专家独立选择，多域精度不降甚至提升最多1.8pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有做法在合并前对每个专家都用相同LoRA秩微调，把各专家本可不同的最优秩强行耦合。作者要检验这种耦合是否来自合并方法而非合并机制本身，并解释舍入/冲突消解类方法在多域合并中掉点的原因。

Method: 在数学、指令遵循、多语言三个域上，以4种秩、3种模型规模训练36个专家；用6种合并方法分别在统一秩与各域自身最优秩下合并；对比平均、效用选择与冲突消解类方法的表现；并从谱分量选择准则（按幅度vs按对损失的影响）角度分析失败机理（如instruction adapter奇异值最小、被近乎完全丢弃导致IFEval下降16–25pp）。

Result: 在统一秩与逐域最优秩下对比：平均法与效用选择法几乎无代价，多域精度最高提升1.8pp；冲突消解类方法表现不一致，最多损失4pp。按损失效应选分量可保留全部三个专家，且在8倍秩范围内精度波动仅0.7pp（4B规模下TIES为6.7pp）。

Conclusion: 秩耦合源于合并算子的分量选择准则而非合并本身；只要合并算子不按幅度选择，即可为每个专家独立选择秩，从而实现更灵活、更鲁棒的多专家合并。

Abstract: Model merging combines separately fine-tuned experts into a single model without retraining. Standard practice trains every expert at the same LoRA rank, which couples the rank choice across experts even though each domain has its own best rank. We show this coupling is a property of the merge method, not of merging. We train 36 experts on three domains (mathematics, instruction following, multilingual) at four ranks and three model scales, then merge with six methods at uniform ranks and at each domain's own best rank. Giving experts different ranks costs averaging and utility-based selection nothing, and gains them up to 1.8 pp of multi-domain accuracy, while the conflict-resolution methods are inconsistent and can lose up to 4 pp. The split has a simple cause. Merging keeps only a fixed share of the pooled spectral components, and methods that choose them by size discard many that would have reduced loss. They also tend to discard the same expert: the instruction adapter has the smallest singular values, so it is dropped almost entirely and IFEval falls by 16 to 25 pp. Choosing components by their effect on loss instead keeps all three experts, and leaves accuracy nearly flat across an 8$\times$ rank range (0.7 pp, against 6.7 pp for TIES at 4B scale). Rank can therefore be chosen per expert whenever the merge operator does not select by magnitude.

</details>


### [92] [Nowcasting Future Performance for Population Pruning in Survival-of-the-Fittest Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06512)
*Rico Städeli,Léo Meynent,Boris Knyazev,Damian Borth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"nowcaster"预测候选模型未来准确率，并结合SANE表示用于Survival-of-the-Fittest(SOTF)剪枝，在性能差异大的模型库中可接近最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练大量候选模型（超参数、架构、随机种子不同）成本高，但提前停止可能误删后期反超的模型，因学习曲线会交叉，需要更可靠的中途预测与剪枝依据。

Method: 训练nowcaster预测五轮后或训练结束时的测试准确率，输入包括准确率/损失历史、权重统计量和SANE表示（将检查点编码为潜在token序列的Transformer自编码器）；在SOTF训练中按预测准确率排序并停止排名最低的run，与按当前准确率剪枝对比。

Result: 五轮预测中权重统计量效果最佳，最终准确率预测中SANE表示最佳；同质CNN zoos中按当前准确率剪枝已接近最优，而在性能分布更分散的ResNet-18与ViT zoos中，SOTF结合nowcasted最终准确率几乎追平最佳模型。

Conclusion: 中途检查点信息可有效预测未来准确率，SOTF剪枝在性能差异大的模型库中显著优于仅按当前准确率剪枝，可降低训练开销而不牺牲最终模型质量。

Abstract: Training high-performing neural networks often involves evaluating populations of candidate models with varying hyperparameters, architectures, or random seeds. Training every candidate to completion is computationally expensive, yet stopping seemingly weaker runs early risks discarding models that would ultimately perform better, as learning curves can cross. To address this challenge, we investigate whether intermediate training checkpoints can predict future model accuracy and enable more informed pruning decisions. Our nowcasters predict test accuracy five epochs ahead or at the end of training. They are trained with accuracy and loss histories, weight statistics, and SANE representations as inputs. SANE is a transformer autoencoder that encodes a checkpoint as a sequence of latent tokens. We then use the nowcasters in Survival-of-the-Fittest (SOTF) training. SOTF repeatedly ranks runs during training by predicted accuracy and stops the lowest-ranked ones. Our results show that weight statistics work best for five-epoch forecasts, and SANE representations work best for final accuracy prediction. For evaluation, we compare SOTF with pruning the population by current accuracy. In homogeneous model zoos such as the CNN zoos, pruning by current accuracy already retains a final model only slightly worse than the best. Model zoos with a larger spread in performance, such as the ResNet-18 and ViT zoos, are more difficult to prune by current accuracy alone. In these zoos, SOTF with nowcasted final accuracy almost closes the gap to the best model.

</details>


### [93] [An Interaction-Aware Decision Framework for FDIC-Assisted Distressed Bank Acquisitions: Empirical Evidence from the FDIC Failed-Bank Panel and the SVB-First Citizens Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06514)
*Yousef Nejatbakhsh,Malihe Aliasgari,Parsa Safaee,Sina Shokoohyar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出"蜘蛛网络过滤框架"，用四条可解释的交互规则刻画五个维度间的依赖关系，为收购失败银行的决策建模并做实证检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失败银行收购中，收购价值、监管保护、风险、买方实力与执行难度彼此依赖（如政府损失分担会削弱高不良贷款的影响），单一信号无法独立解读，因此需要能编码信号间交互的建模方式。

Method: 将四类可解释交互规则编码为图启发的特征，输入逻辑回归，并给出"推进/谨慎/回避"决策规则；样本为2008—2024年由美国上市母公司参与的55起FDIC协助收购，6项财务输入取自失败前的监管报表，2项交易条款取自监管新闻稿；以公告前后三日异常收益为正作为结果变量；采用分组嵌套交叉验证与重复全流程模型选择的置换检验。

Result: Spider增强模型平均AUC为0.594，低于同输入逻辑回归的0.615与岭回归的0.632；置换检验至多提供任一模型优于随机的弱证据；直接使用Spider分数不优于随机；目标困境与更高收益的表观关联在以银行板块为基准后消失；按F1调优的阈值会批准多数负收益收购，并在单笔成本为正向收益三倍时产生负效用。

Conclusion: 交互规则驱动的框架在小样本下未展现样本外预测优势，其信号排序能力不超过随机，实用阈值下的经济效用为负；交互依赖的价值需更充分的样本与更稳健的评估设计来检验。

Abstract: Acquirers of failed banks weigh acquisition value, regulatory protection, risk, buyer strength, and execution difficulty jointly, because each signal's meaning depends on the others: high non-performing loans matter less when government loss sharing absorbs the losses. The proposed Spider Network Filtering Framework encodes such dependencies as four interpretable interaction rules among five dimensions, yielding graph-inspired features for a logistic model and a Pursue/Caution/Avoid decision rule. We evaluate it on 55 acquisitions assisted by the Federal Deposit Insurance Corporation in 2008-2024 by acquirers with a parent listed in the United States. Six financial inputs come from regulatory reports published before each failure; two deal terms, which the winning bidder knows when it bids but investors learn at the announcement, come from the regulator's press releases. The outcome, a positive three-day abnormal return around the announcement, records the market's initial reception, not realized success. Under grouped, nested cross-validation, the Spider-enhanced model reaches a mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.594, against 0.615 for logistic regression on the same inputs and 0.632 for ridge regression; permutation tests that repeat the full model selection give at most weak evidence that any of them ranks better than chance. The Spider score used directly does not rank better than chance, and an apparent link between target distress and higher returns disappears against a bank-sector benchmark. Thresholds tuned for the F1 score approve most acquisitions with negative returns and realize negative utility when each costs three times what a positively received acquisition gains.

</details>


### [94] [IDSISA: Influence-Distilled Sharded Training for Efficient Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06519)
*Deeraj SK,Sadhana Devarajan,Tanmoy Hazra,Anupam Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IDSISA 利用影响函数将易遗忘样本集中到少数分片，并用未受影响分片的蒸馏恢复孤立重训损失的效用，从而在保持 SISA 精确删除保证的同时提升遗忘效果与效用保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SISA 通过分片与独立重训实现可认证删除，但随机分片会把遗忘请求分散到大多数分片，且孤立重训导致效用下降，删除成本与精度损失难以兼顾。

Method: 以影响函数识别并对易遗忘样本做分片重排以集中遗忘请求；对被影响分片重训后，用未受影响分片对其做知识蒸馏以恢复效用；并针对重复删除序列下跨分片蒸馏引入的依赖，提出基于闭包的级联重训流程。

Result: 删除保证继承 SISA 重训步骤而保持精确，蒸馏效用恢复作为单独量化的有界近似（期望 ε_cert≤0.015）；理论上证明级联重训在任意删除序列下可将删除保证恢复到完整聚合模型。ResNet-18/CIFAR-10、ResNet-18/CIFAR-100、BiLSTM/IMDB、TCN/IMDB 及 Fashion-MNIST 全连接网络上，相对 SISA 选择性遗忘提升 14–91%，效用保持相对提升最高 50%，遗忘耗时变化 −6.7% 至 +6.4%。

Conclusion: IDSISA 在保持精确可认证删除的前提下，显著改善选择性遗忘与效用保持，并给出重复删除场景下的认证扩展，是比较有吸引力的 SISA 改进方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning removes the influence of specific training examples to comply with privacy regulations. Full retraining gives strong guarantees but is computationally prohibitive at scale; approximate methods are efficient but lack formal guarantees. SISA (Sharded, Isolated, Sliced, and Aggregated training) achieves certified deletion through data sharding and independent retraining, but random partitioning scatters forget requests across most shards, and isolated retraining degrades utility. We introduce IDSISA (Influence-Distilled SISA), which uses influence functions to concentrate forget-prone samples into few shards and distillation from unaffected shards to recover utility lost to isolation. The deletion guarantee inherited from SISA's retraining step remains exact and requires no probabilistic qualification; the distillation-based utility recovery is a separately quantified, empirically bounded approximation ($ε_{\text{cert}} \leq 0.015$ in expectation). We further formalize certification under sequences of repeated deletion requests, where cross-shard distillation can create dependencies between shards; we prove that a closure-based cascading retraining procedure restores the deletion guarantee to the full aggregated model under any deletion sequence. Experiments on ResNet-18/CIFAR-10, ResNet-18/CIFAR-100, BiLSTM/IMDB, TCN/IMDB, and a fully connected network on Fashion-MNIST show $14-91\%$ better selective forgetting, up to $50\%$ better utility retention in relative terms (with consistent percentage-point gains on the other architectures, where a relative baseline is undefined), and unlearning-time changes ranging from $-6.7\%$ to $+6.4\%$ depending on architecture, relative to SISA.

</details>


### [95] [EvoOPD: Evolving Privileged Information Strategies for Black-Box On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06542)
*Ximo Zhu,Ruiqi Liu,Yi Han,Zhengan Wu,Gengsheng Li,ZiAn Wang,Zhu Yuantian,Jin Jiang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EvoOPD，将黑盒教师反馈的获取方式当作优化问题，维护随学生状态演化的PI策略库，以修复效用动态调整反馈焦点、信息类型与粒度，在六种模型-任务设置上均取得最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有/黑盒模型输出分布不可得，依赖教师logits的细粒度蒸馏难以直接应用；虽有工作将教师文本反馈作为特权信息（PI）转化为学生轨迹上的稠密监督，但"应向教师索取何种PI"尚不明确——不同关注点、信息类型与粒度的PI策略效果差异显著，且其相对有效性会随训练进程变化。

Method: EvoOPD把PI获取本身形式化为优化问题：构建PI策略库（覆盖焦点、信息类型、粒度三个维度），由PI条件化的特权教师将采样的文本反馈转成学生自身on-policy轨迹上的稠密监督；再利用该PI对当前学生的实测修复效用（repair utility）驱动的策略更新，随学生状态演化调整反馈策略。

Result: 在数学推理与代码生成任务、Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B学生模型上共六个设置中均取得最佳整体性能，相较各设置最强基线平均提升数学2.14个百分点、代码1.30个百分点；分析表明PI策略的相对有效性随学生状态变化，固定策略难以全程保持最优。

Conclusion: 黑盒蒸馏中PI的"获取方式"应被视为可自适应优化的对象而非固定超参；基于修复效用的演化式策略选择能持续匹配学生阶段性学习需求，优于任何静态PI策略。

Abstract: Proprietary language models exhibit strong reasoning and coding capabilities, but their output distributions are inaccessible, making fine-grained distillation methods that rely on teacher logits difficult to apply directly to black-box models. We observe that rich textual feedback from a black-box teacher can be treated as privileged information (PI) and converted, through a PI-conditioned privileged teacher, into dense supervision signals on the student's own trajectories. However, what PI should be acquired from the teacher to most effectively support student learning remains unclear. PI strategies that differ in where they focus, what information they request, and how much detail they provide can have substantially different effects on the current student, and their relative effectiveness can further change as training progresses. Based on this observation, we propose Evolving Privileged Information Strategies for Black-Box On-Policy Distillation (EvoOPD), which treats PI acquisition itself as an optimization problem and maintains a PI Strategy Bank that evolves with the student state. EvoOPD uses the measured repair utility of PI on the current student to adapt the focus, information type, and granularity of teacher feedback, allowing the acquired information to continually match the student's learning needs. We systematically evaluate EvoOPD on mathematical reasoning and code generation with Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B students. EvoOPD achieves the best overall performance in all six model-task settings, improving over the strongest baseline in each setting by an average of 2.14 percentage points in mathematics and 1.30 points in code. Further analysis shows that the relative effectiveness of PI acquisition strategies changes with student state, making a fixed strategy difficult to sustain as the best choice throughout distillation.

</details>


### [96] [From Fixed Defaults to Calibrated Activation Policies for Long-Term Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06559)
*Shijie Xu,Boyan Tang,Jianghua Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种校准的激活选择策略，从历史实验中学习可靠默认配置，并按任务自适应调整激活函数及其位置，在证据不足时保留默认；基于1225次Informer训练实验（17个数据集变体、51个预测任务），平均MSE降低7.86%，72.55%任务得到改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期时间序列预测中，现有研究多聚焦模型架构，而固定激活配置无法说明激活选择应如何随数据集与预测步长变化；同时缺乏利用既有实验证据指导新任务配置选择的方法。

Method: 构建校准的激活选择策略（calibrated selection policy）：从先验实验中学习可靠默认配置，针对每个任务自适应选择激活函数及其在网络中的放置位置，并在证据不足时回退到默认配置，避免逐任务穷举搜索。

Result: 在17个数据集变体、51个预测任务上的1,225次Informer训练运行评估中，该策略相较固定激活基线平均降低均方误差7.86%，并在72.55%的任务上取得提升。

Conclusion: 激活函数的选择与位置是改进现有预测模型的实用方向，且累积的实验证据可在不重复穷举搜索的情况下有效指导新任务的配置决策。

Abstract: Long-term time-series forecasting supports decisions in energy, transportation, and other applications where reliable predictions are needed beyond the observed data. Although forecasting research has advanced model architectures, fixed activation configurations provide limited guidance on how activation choices should vary across datasets and prediction horizons. This study aims to establish when task-specific activation choices improve forecasting accuracy and how previous experiments can guide the selection of suitable configurations for new tasks. We develop a calibrated selection policy that learns a reliable default from prior experiments, adapts the activation function and its location to each task, and retains the default when evidence for a change is insufficient. In an evaluation supported by 1,225 Informer training runs across 17 dataset variants and 51 forecasting tasks, the policy reduces mean squared error by 7.86% on average relative to the conventional fixed-activation baseline and improves 72.55% of tasks. These findings establish activation choice and placement as practical opportunities for improving existing forecasting models and show how accumulated experimental evidence can guide configuration decisions without repeating an exhaustive search for every task.

</details>


### [97] [Thermodynamics-Informed Learning Captures Hierarchical Hydrogen-Bond Networks in Deep Eutectic Solvents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06590)
*Yanpeng Ye,Chaoran Duan,Mian Xu,S. Mani Sarathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出热力学信息神经网络TiNN，分层建模HBA-HBD缔合与溶质容纳，预测DES中木质纤维素组分溶解度（R²=0.917），实验验证误差由COSMO-RS的>23倍降至约1.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DES的溶剂化行为取决于多组分氢键网络而非单一组分性质，传统方法（如COSMO-RS）对溶解度预测误差大，亟需能刻画氢键网络且具备外推能力的预测模型以支持木质纤维素精炼的溶剂筛选。

Method: 构建TiNN：以学习到的自由能路径分层表示HBA-HBD缔合与溶质容纳，基于938条记录训练；通过消融实验分析各模块贡献；并用葡萄糖、木聚糖、硫酸盐木质素在酸性/碱性/中性DES中的实验数据及玉米秸秆分离数据验证。

Result: 测试集R²=0.917，可泛化至新HBA-HBD组合与稀疏条件；消融显示溶质特异性稳定化贡献最大，氢键兼容性重要性依赖溶解机制；实验溶解度几何平均误差由COSMO-RS的>23倍降至约1.5倍；未经分离数据训练即可区分高/低木质素与半纤维素脱除的DES配方。

Conclusion: TiNN为具有化学可解释性的DES溶解度预测与溶剂筛选工具，可迁移至木质纤维素分离性能判别，支撑基于机理的溶剂设计。

Abstract: Deep eutectic solvents (DESs) offer a tunable platform for lignocellulose refining, but their solvation behavior depends on multicomponent hydrogen-bond networks rather than individual constituent properties. We develop TiNN, a thermodynamics-informed neural network that hierarchically represents HBA-HBD association and solute accommodation through a learned free-energy pathway. Trained on 938 records, TiNN achieves a test-set $R^2$ of 0.917 and generalizes to new HBA-HBD combinations and sparse conditions. Ablation identifies solute-specific stabilization as the largest contributor to prediction accuracy, while the importance of hydrogen-bond compatibility depends on the dissolution mechanism. Experimental measurements of glucose, xylan, and Kraft lignin in acidic, basic, and neutral DESs show that TiNN reduces the geometric mean solubility error from more than 23-fold with COSMO-RS to approximately 1.5-fold. Without training on fractionation data, TiNN also distinguishes DES formulations with high and low lignin and hemicellulose removal from corn stover. These results support chemically grounded solvent screening for lignocellulose fractionation.

</details>


### [98] [A trust layer for fast pedestal-stability surrogates: calibrated error bars, out-of-distribution alarms and few-shot recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06597)
*Amin Vakhshouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个模型无关的"信任层"，为剥离-气球模稳定性机器学习代理模型提供保形误差棒、分布外告警与少量新设计算例的重校准，并以前置注册假设进行检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稳定性代理模型仅作点估计，不给出误差范围，也无法判断查询是否超出训练分布；而聚变公司设计新装置时，几乎所有感兴趣的平衡都落在既有代理模型的训练数据之外，这正是最关键的情形。

Method: 构建模型无关的信任层：保形预测给出误差棒；分布外检测器发出告警并将可疑算例路由到物理代码；用新设计的少量仿真做重校准。在JET类平衡（理想MHD增长率）大规模公开数据集、电阻性BOUT++代理、SPARC库与DIII-D线圈集平衡上进行前置注册假设检验，并自建神经算子代理以验证该框架的诊断能力。

Result: 误差棒在分布内达到标称覆盖率，在等离子体位形偏移下覆盖不足，用数十个新位形仿真或路由告警算例可恢复；BOUT++代理在留出运行上保持校准，告警能标记训练中未见过的托卡马克，场景扫描由数小时缩短至数秒；基于自标注BOUT++分级标签训练的代理在未见平衡上优于基线，而外部训练的代理排序反向并被告警拒答；自建神经算子代理经该框架暴露两处输入缺陷，修复后与简单网络的差距消失。

Conclusion: 对所有测试的代理模型，信任层均将覆盖率维持在目标附近，误差棒宽度由该代理自身误差决定；部分前置注册假设未获支持，文中如实报告了这些结果及其局限。

Abstract: Machine-learning surrogates of linear peeling-ballooning stability now run in milliseconds, but they are mostly deployed as point estimators: they do not say how wrong they may be, or when a query lies outside what they were trained on. For a fusion company designing a new device, every equilibrium of interest is outside the training data of existing surrogates, so this is the case that matters. We describe a model-agnostic trust layer for stability surrogates, made of conformal error bars, out-of-distribution alarms that route doubtful cases to the physics code, and recalibration from a few simulations of a new design, and we test it with pre-registered hypotheses. On a large public dataset of JET-like equilibria with ideal-MHD growth rates, the error bars hold their nominal coverage in distribution and fall short under a shift in plasma shape; a few dozen simulations of the new shape, or routing the cases the alarms flag, restore it. On resistive BOUT++ surrogates, the bars stay calibrated on held-out runs, the alarm flags tokamaks absent from training, and a scenario scan is screened in seconds instead of hours. Across equilibria from the SPARC library and the DIII-D coil set, a surrogate trained on our own graded BOUT++ labels beats the baseline on unseen equilibria, while a surrogate trained elsewhere ranks the same cases backwards and its alarm declines them. Building our own neural-operator surrogate, the harness exposed two input defects whose fix closed its gap to a simple network. For every surrogate tested, the trust layer keeps coverage near target, with a bar width set by that surrogate's own error. Not every pre-registered hypothesis held, and we report those results and the limits of all of them.

</details>


### [99] [The Behavioral Rate of Fine-Tuning: Compression as a Measure of Useful Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06598)
*Joey David,Paul Caillon,Alexandre Allauzen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出操作性的“行为码率”R*，定义为压缩后适配器保留固定比例微调增益所需的最小序列化大小，并揭示其由数据集–接收方对共同决定，且压缩能过滤损坏适配器的领先奇异方向、显著恢复甚至提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 适配器秩、参数量或数值重建误差并不能直接回答“微调需要写入多少比特才能保留所获得行为”这一问题，因此需要一个以行为保持为准则的信息量度量。

Method: 定义R*为重新载入冻结基座模型后仍保留固定比例留出微调增益的压缩适配器最小序列化尺寸；在不同训练内容、优化预算、基座模型上测量R*，并在数据上测量校正谱以做接收方内排序；同时对人为损坏数据集上训练的适配器进行压缩，分析奇异方向与跨任务泛化。

Result: R*随固定预算下训练内容多样性增加而上升，但不由源描述长度、微调增益或权重空间重建误差决定；同一数据集在不同冻结基座模型上R*差异显著甚至次序反转；校正谱可在接收方内对R*排序；压缩使损坏适配器在GSM8K上从23.7%提升至82.9%（冻结Qwen2.5-7B为77.6%），且损坏集中于领先奇异方向；在更难数学任务上压缩后的损坏适配器保留97%基座精度，而干净适配器仅44%。

Conclusion: 微调可压缩性是数据集–接收方对的属性而非数据集单独属性；基于数据的校正谱可预测接收方内的行为码率；压缩具有显著的降噪与泛化保护效应，可作为理解与改进LoRA微调的信息论视角。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapts a frozen language model through a compact learned update, making it a natural setting for asking how much information fine-tuning needs to write. Yet adapter rank, parameter count, or numerical reconstruction error do not directly tell us how many bits are required to preserve the acquired behavior. We therefore define an operational behavioral rate $R^\star$ as the smallest serialized size of a compressed adapter that, once reloaded into the frozen base model, retains a fixed fraction of its held-out fine-tuning gain. Across LoRA fine-tuning experiments, this rate increases with distinct training content at fixed optimization budgets, but is not determined by intuitive metrics like source description length, fine-tuning gain, or weight-space reconstruction error. Most importantly, the same datasets exhibit substantially different, and even reordered, rates across frozen base models, showing that fine-tuning compressibility is a property of the dataset--receiver pair rather than of the dataset alone. However, we show that correction spectra measured on the data are able to rank these rates within a receiver. Finally, we study a striking noise-reducing effect of compression: adapters trained on deliberately corrupted datasets fall far below the frozen model, yet after compression score above it (23.7% to 82.9% on GSM8K, against 77.6% for frozen Qwen2.5-7B), consistently across tested families and sizes. The damage is unevenly concentrated in the adapter's leading singular directions, which compression appears to filter. Away from its training task, the compressed corrupted adapter also generalizes better than a clean fine-tune: on harder math it retains 97% of base accuracy, versus 44% for the clean adapter.

</details>


### [100] [Verifier-Guided Self-Consistency: Test-Time Reasoning with Imperfect Generators and Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06606)
*Ekaterina Fadeeva,Xueyan Li,Mrinmaya Sachan,Maxim Panov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Verifier-Guided Self-Consistency（VGSC）随机解码框架，在生成过程中结合步骤级正确性与未来结果估计，并保留多条轨迹做答案级自一致性投票，在多基准上平均准确率优于代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 多步推理脆弱：看似合理的中间步骤常导致错误答案。测试时提升推理需平衡两个不完美信号——生成器（提供多样性）与验证器（引导正确性）。现有方法偏向一端：验证器加权投票只用生成器采样的轨迹，无法把生成导向正确路径；束搜索式验证器引导搜索虽能引导生成，却易过度集中于狭窄轨迹集并放大验证器错误。

Method: VGSC 为随机解码框架：每一步由基础语言模型反复提议候选下一步，在接受/重采样（acceptance/resampling）过程中同时使用步骤级正确性与未来结果估计两个信号进行筛选；同时维护多条轨迹，最终在答案层面执行自一致性投票。作者并给出概率解释：VGSC 近似从"验证器倾斜"（verifier-tilted）的轨迹分布中采样。

Result: 在多个推理基准、多种生成器模型与多类验证器家族上，VGSC 的平均准确率均高于代表性基线。

Conclusion: VGSC 通过将验证器引导嵌入逐步随机采样、并以多轨迹自一致性收尾，在"保持多样性"与"引导正确性"之间取得更好折中，缓解束搜索式方法的轨迹过度集中与验证器误差放大问题。

Abstract: Large language models can solve many multi-step reasoning problems, but their reasoning remains brittle: plausible intermediate steps often lead to incorrect answers. Improving reasoning at test time requires balancing two imperfect signals: the generator, which provides diversity, and the verifier, which guides correctness.
