Existing approaches largely favor one side of this tradeoff. Verifier-weighted voting relies on trajectories sampled from the generator and cannot steer generation toward correct ones. By contrast, beam-style verifier-guided search can steer generation, but may over-concentrate on narrow trajectory sets and amplify verifier errors.
  We propose Verifier-Guided Self-Consistency (VGSC), a stochastic decoding framework that combines step-level correctness and future-outcome estimates to guide reasoning during generation. VGSC repeatedly proposes candidate next steps from the base language model and uses both signals in an acceptance/resampling procedure, while maintaining multiple trajectories for final answer-level self-consistency. We provide a probabilistic interpretation of VGSC as approximate sampling from a verifier-tilted trajectory distribution. Across multiple reasoning benchmarks, generator models, and verifier families, VGSC achieves higher average accuracy than representative baselines.

</details>


### [101] [From Reconstruction to Imputation: Test-Time Optimization for Missing Time-Series Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06612)
*Björn Ludwig,Alexander Windmann,René Heesch,Oliver Niggemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出测试时自一致性精调：将冻结的序列重构模型中缺失条目视为优化变量，仅用观测坐标的重构误差反向传播进行推理时填补，无需掩码增强训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整传感器通道失效导致无法局部插值，且为每种失效模式单独训练填补器不现实。作者追问：未经人工掩码增强训练、随后冻结的重构器能否在推理时转化为填补器。

Method: 对每个测试窗口的缺失条目设为优化变量，固定所有观测值，通过冻结模型反向传播，最小化仅观测坐标上的重构误差（观测自一致性目标）；用从缺失输入到观测残差的交叉雅可比刻画该精调何时有信息量，耦合弱时可选引入时间平滑先验。实验：5个真实数据集、每窗口掩蔽恰一个通道、4种重构架构。

Result: 无先验的测试时精调在19/20个数据集-骨干组合中相对单次重构降低MAE。与掩码感知填补器（标准结构化掩码或匹配的全通道dropout训练）相比，最佳精调重构器在2个数据集上MAE最低，3个上落后于最强基线；增益需显著更多推理时间且对跨通道耦合敏感。

Conclusion: 测试时自一致性可作为冻结重构模型的条件式推理时适配器，补充掩码感知填补而无需重训重构器，但其效果依赖跨通道耦合且计算开销大。

Abstract: Complete sensor-channel failures create a difficult form of time-series missingness: local interpolation is unavailable, and deploying a separately trained imputer for every failure pattern may be impractical. We ask whether a sequence reconstructor trained without artificial mask augmentation and then frozen can be converted into an imputer at inference time. Our method treats the missing entries of each test window as optimization variables, clamps all observations, and backpropagates through the frozen model to minimize reconstruction error only on observed coordinates. The resulting observed-self-consistency objective requires neither artificial mask augmentation nor full-channel dropout during reconstructor training. We characterize when this refinement is informative through the cross-Jacobian from missing inputs to observed residuals, and use an optional temporal-smoothness prior when that coupling is weak. Across five real-world datasets with exactly one channel synthetically masked per test window and four reconstruction architectures, no-prior test-time refinement reduces MAE relative to one-pass reconstruction in 19 of 20 dataset-backbone combinations. Compared with mask-aware imputers trained under either standard structured masks or matched full-channel dropout, the best refined reconstructor achieves the lowest MAE on two datasets and trails the strongest baseline on three. These gains require substantially more inference time and are sensitive to cross-channel coupling. Our results establish test-time self-consistency as a conditional inference-time adapter for frozen reconstruction models that complements mask-aware imputation without retraining the reconstructor.

</details>


### [102] [Dimension-Free Private Mean Estimation is Impossible Without the Covariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06615)
*Renaud Gaucher,Ephraim Linder,Adam Smith,Jonathan Ullman,Lydia Zakynthinou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究协方差矩阵未知时差分隐私高斯均值估计的极限，建立了新的欧氏误差下界，表明未知协方差下隐私估计器无法获得与已知协方差时仅依赖谱（而非环境维度d）相当的保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明，当协方差Σ已知时，最优样本复杂度仅依赖其谱而非环境维度d。作者希望探究：在协方差未知的情形下，是否仍能利用各向异性结构在差分隐私下获得维度无关的保证。

Method: 针对高斯均值估计（欧氏误差），构造并证明新的差分隐私下界，对比已知协方差与未知协方差两种设定下的最优误差，按样本量n与有效维度trace(Σ)/‖Σ‖_op的相对大小分两种情形分析。

Result: 情形一：当n小于有效维度时，未知协方差的最优误差可比已知协方差时大√(d/n)倍；情形二：当n大于有效维度时，即使对有效维度被log d界定的对角协方差矩阵，未知协方差的最优误差仍可比已知协方差时大√d/n^{3/2}倍。

Conclusion: 在未知协方差设定下，差分隐私下利用各向异性结构可能不可避免地引入依赖维度的误差或样本复杂度，这与协方差已知时仅依赖谱的结论形成鲜明对比。

Abstract: We study the limits of differentially private Gaussian mean estimation when the covariance matrix is unknown. Prior work shows that when the covariance $Σ$ is known, the optimal sample complexity can depend only on its spectrum, rather than on the ambient dimension $d$. We establish new lower bounds for Gaussian mean estimation with Euclidean error which show that private estimators cannot achieve similar guarantees when the covariance is unknown. First, when the sample size $n$ is smaller than the effective dimension $\mathrm{trace}(Σ)/\|Σ\|_{\text{op}}$, the optimal error when the covariance is unknown can be larger than the optimal error when it is known by a factor of $\sqrt{d/n}$. Second, when the sample size is larger than the effective dimension, even for diagonal covariance matrices with effective dimension bounded by $\log d$, the optimal error when the covariance is unknown can be larger than the optimal error when it is known by a factor of $\sqrt{d}/n^{3/2}$. Together, these results show that in the unknown covariance setting, exploiting anisotropic structure under differential privacy can require a dimension-dependent error or sample complexity.

</details>


### [103] [Fingerprinting the Dispatchers behind Distributed Model Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06661)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用请求时间戳（到达时间与流完成时间）对API蒸馏活动进行取证式账号排序，即使内容过滤失效也能揭示共享基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API蒸馏攻击通过把请求拆分到数千个负载均衡账号来隐藏模型窃取流量，标准内容分类器常常失效，因此需要一种不依赖内容的新检测信号。

Method: 基于请求时间戳（到达时间与流完成时间）分析调度策略特征（如轮询计数器、键混洗池），并仅用20%种子账号集对剩余活动账号做取证式排序与检测评估。

Result: 20分钟内即可在数千真实生产账号中实现0.92–1.00 AUC的账号排序；自适应攻击者用抖动难以隐藏轮换调度器，但键固定（pinning keys）能成功规避检测，代价是在配额受限时严重降低吞吐。

Conclusion: 时间特征能在内容过滤失效时暴露共享基础设施模式；检测与规避之间存在吞吐—隐蔽性权衡。

Abstract: API distillation campaigns try to hide model-stealing traffic by splitting requests across thousands of load-balanced accounts, which standard content classifiers often miss. However, request timestamps, specifically arrival time and stream completion time, reveal the underlying dispatching strategy, such as round-robin counters or key-shuffling pools. Using a 20 percent seed set, this timing analysis can forensically rank the remaining accounts of a campaign with 0.92 to 1.00 AUC within 20 minutes, even across thousands of real production accounts. While adaptive attackers struggle to hide rotating dispatchers using jitter, pinning keys successfully evades detection, though it can severely throttle throughput when quotas bind. Ultimately, timing exposes shared infrastructure patterns even when content filters fail.

</details>


### [104] [Self-Directed Apple Tasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06677)
*Tanmay Devale,Reed Phillips,Arvind Ramaswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"苹果品尝"部分反馈模型中学习器可自适应选择点的"自主导向"设定，在可实现情形下给出期望极小极大错误数的四分类刻画，在不可知情形下给出极小极大遗憾的二分刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典苹果品尝设定（仅当预测为1时才观测真实标签）的在线对抗设定的速率已有组合刻画（Raman et al., 2024），但学习器可自适应选择预测顺序的自主导向设定尚无系统分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 引入"自主导向维度"这一新组合参数，结合VC维与∅-中心星数，对可实现与不可知两种情形分别进行极小极大速率分析。

Result: 可实现设定下，依VC维、∅-中心星数与自主导向维度的取值，期望极小极大错误数呈现四分类：Θ(1)、O(log n)∩ω(1)、Õ(√n) 或 Θ(n)。不可知设定下，极小极大遗憾依VC维为 Õ(√n) 或 Θ(n)。

Conclusion: 自主导向设定下苹果品尝问题的最优速率由VC维、∅-中心星数与自主导向维度共同完全决定，呈现清晰的分段相变结构；不可知情形下速率仅由VC维决定。

Abstract: We study the classical apple-tasting setting, a partial feedback model in which the learner observes the true label only when it predicts $1$. A combinatorial characterization for the rates of this problem in the online adversarial setting has been studied by Raman et al. (2024). We study the self-directed setting, in which the learner can adaptively choose the order of the points it predicts on. In the realizable setting, we establish a tetrachotomy on the expected minimax number of mistakes made by any learner. Depending on the VC dimension, the $\emptyset$-centered star number, and the self-directed dimension, we show that the rates could possibly be $Θ(1)$, $O(\log n) \cap ω(1)$, $\tilde Θ(\sqrt{n})$, or $Θ(n)$. In the agnostic setting, the minimax regret is $\tilde Θ(\sqrt{n})$ or $Θ(n)$, depending on the VC dimension.

</details>


### [105] [Do unlearning algorithms learn the right features?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06681)
*Polina Dolgova,Amartya Sanyal,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出标准的"快照式"遗忘评测（仅比较输出）会掩盖特征层面的差异：输出一致但特征不同的遗忘模型与重训模型，单步梯度即可打破一致；并据此提出区分缺失特征与多余特征的特征恢复度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘评测主要采用后删除指标，将遗忘模型与在剩余数据上重训的模型在输出层面比较。这种快照式评估可能掩盖两模型所编码特征的重要差异，从而误判遗忘方法是否真正"等价于重训"。

Method: 1) 理论证明：若遗忘模型与重训模型输出一致但特征不同，对其预测头施加单步梯度即可破坏输出一致性；并证明删除任意小比例数据即可使重训选择定性不同的特征，故匹配重训可能要求学习完整模型从未具备的特征。2) 提出特征恢复度量，将缺失特征（missing）与多余特征（extraneous）分开量化。3) 在八种遗忘方法上评估该度量，并与后续再学习、下游适配下的偏差进行关联分析，同时通过对齐特征直接修正该失配。

Result: 在八种遗忘方法中，标准指标表现良好的模型常常无法恢复重训模型的特征；该特征失配可预测模型在再学习与下游适配阶段相对重训的偏差，而直接修正该失配能系统性降低这些偏差。

Conclusion: 仅凭输出层面的快照指标不足以评估机器遗忘，特征层面的恢复程度是更可靠、更具预测力的判据；将缺失/多余特征分离度量并加以对齐，可显著提升遗忘模型与重训模型的行为等价性。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is commonly evaluated with standard post-deletion metrics that compare an unlearned model to one retrained on the remaining data. Such snapshot evaluations can conceal consequential differences in the features the two models encode. We prove that if an unlearned and a retrained model agree on their outputs but encode different features, a single gradient step on their prediction heads can break this agreement. We further show that deleting an arbitrarily small fraction of the data can cause retraining to select qualitatively different features, so matching retraining can require learning features that the full model never had. We then introduce a measure of feature recovery that separates missing from extraneous features. Across eight unlearning methods, models that perform well on standard metrics often fail to recover the retrained features. This mismatch predicts deviations from retraining under subsequent relearning and downstream adaptation, and directly correcting it systematically reduces these deviations.

</details>


### [106] [Hallucination Is Linearly Decodable from Mid-Layer Hidden States in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02628)
*Aizierjiang Aiersilan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究开源指令微调LLM隐藏状态中是否存在线性可分的真实性信号及其最强网络深度。在Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B、Qwen2.5-7B（4-bit NF4）上，于四个幻觉基准提取逐层隐藏状态，比较线性/MLP探针、INSIDE EigenScore、自一致性与注意力熵。单层线性探针达0.904–1.000 AUROC，采样类检测器不超过0.541。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究开源LLM内部是否编码可线性分离的真实性（truthfulness）信号，并定位该信号最强的网络层深度，为低成本的幻觉检测提供依据。

Method: 在三个7B–8B指令微调模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B、Qwen2.5-7B，4-bit NF4量化）上，于TruthfulQA、HaluEval-QA、FEVER及受控合成集四个基准提取逐层隐藏状态；对比线性探针、MLP探针、INSIDE EigenScore、自一致性、注意力熵五类检测方法。

Result: 单中层线性探针在留出划分上达0.904–1.000 AUROC，而采样类检测器在同一协议下不超过0.541；MLP探针较线性探针提升极少（>0.01 AUROC罕见），说明信号近似线性；峰值探针层呈一致带段：Llama与Mistral为32层中的13–18层，Qwen为28层中的19–25层；首块注意力熵在知识支撑场景提供互补信号（HaluEval-QA上0.866–0.941 AUROC）且无额外推理开销。

Conclusion: LLM隐藏状态中存在近似线性的真实性信号，集中于中深层，可用单层线性探针高效检测幻觉；采样方法在此协议下表现低并非其固有缺陷，而是配对标签评估与其所访问信息之间的结构性错配。代码与数据已公开，可在单张8GB GPU上复现。

Abstract: We investigate whether open-source LLMs encode a linearly separable truthfulness signal in their hidden states, and at which network depth this signal is strongest. Across three $7$B--$8$B instruction-tuned models (Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, Qwen2.5-7B) loaded in $4$-bit NF4 quantization, we extract per-layer hidden states on four hallucination benchmarks (TruthfulQA, HaluEval-QA, FEVER, and a controlled synthetic set) and compare four detection approaches: linear and MLP probes, INSIDE EigenScore, self-consistency, and attention entropy. A linear probe on a single mid-network layer achieves $0.904$--$1.000$ AUROC on held-out splits, while sampling-based detectors do not exceed $0.541$ AUROC under the same protocol. The truthfulness signal is approximately linear: MLP probes rarely surpass linear probes by more than $0.01$ AUROC. Peak probing layers fall in a consistent band across model families on natural-language benchmarks -- blocks~$13$--$18$ of~$32$ for Llama and Mistral, and blocks~$19$--$25$ of~$28$ for Qwen. First-block attention entropy provides a complementary signal in knowledge-grounded settings ($0.866$--$0.941$ AUROC on HaluEval-QA) at no additional inference cost. The low discriminability of sampling methods under this protocol reflects a structural mismatch between paired-label evaluation and the information these methods access, rather than an inherent limitation of those methods. Code and data are released for full reproducibility on a single $8$\,GB GPU.

</details>


### [107] [SecureGARD: Dynamic Ontology-Guided Governance for Secure Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06682)
*Guoqin Xiong,Shengtao Wen,Xiang Chen,Xinyi Zeng,Zhaoxin Fan,Wei Ye,Xiaofeng Cao,Sheng-Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出\\ours{}——一个动态本体引导的治理框架，将异构工具调用统一到共享语义表示，结合可执行本体与回合级任务状态做出ready/waiting/forbidden的确定性决策，从而在不扩大授权边界的前提下抵御间接提示注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型LLM智能体将模型输出转化为具有实际后果的动作，使间接提示注入成为执行安全问题。现有防御（约束工具调用、轨迹或信息流）隐含处理动作语义、状态依赖与证据关系，导致跨异构工具泛化受限、安全更新复杂。

Method: \\ours{}将异构调用落地为共享语义表示，用可执行本体加回合级任务状态推导三种确定性决策；waiting决策识别可恢复的未满足义务，支持有界重规划而不扩张授权边界。本体通过离线方式从经验证的训练执行中演化：离线LLM提出实例无关的动作—状态公理，仅当公理针对训练失败暴露的本体错误、保持逻辑一致性与受保护蕴含、并通过留出验证时才被接受，之后本体冻结。

Result: 在AgentDojo、ASB、AgentDyn三个基准的良性及间接提示注入设置下评估：\\ours{}在三个基准上ASR均为0%，且相比现有基线在攻击下任务效用更高。

Conclusion: 显式的语义治理可在不进行无差别拦截的情况下强化执行安全，实现安全性与可用性的兼顾。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents translate model outputs into consequential actions, turning indirect prompt injection into an execution-security problem. Existing defenses constrain tool calls, trajectories, or information flows, but often leave action semantics, state dependencies, and evidence relations implicit, limiting generalization across heterogeneous tools and complicating safe updates. We present \ours{}, a dynamic ontology-guided governance framework. \ours{} grounds heterogeneous calls in a shared semantic representation and combines an executable ontology with episode-level task state to derive deterministic ready, waiting, and forbidden decisions. A waiting decision identifies an unmet but recoverable obligation, enabling bounded replanning without expanding the agent's authorization boundary. \ours{} evolves its ontology offline from verified training executions. An offline LLM proposes instance-independent action--state axioms, which are accepted only if they address ontology errors exposed by training failures, preserve logical consistency and protected entailments, and pass held-out validation before the ontology is frozen. We evaluate \ours{} on AgentDojo, ASB, and AgentDyn under benign and indirect prompt-injection settings. \ours{} achieves a 0\% attack success rate (ASR) across all three benchmarks while improving task utility under attack compared with existing baselines. These results show that explicit semantic governance can strengthen execution security without indiscriminate blocking.

</details>


### [108] [Don't Replace, Complement: A Detector-Agnostic Subspace Signal for Near-OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06688)
*Wonseok Shin,Songkuk Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PLUS（Per-class Low-dimensional sUbspace Signal）信号，通过稀疏自编码器选取每类最活跃神经元的解码方向构建低维子空间，以白化马氏距离 z 作为近 OOD 专属补充分数，与任意校准基分数以固定权重加性融合，在 18 种检测器 × 7 种骨干的 126 种组合上均提升 near-OOD AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后验 OOD 检测在 far-OOD 上已成熟，但在 near-OOD 上仍较弱。二者关键差异在于对类别特征特征方向的使用：far-OOD 输入基本无法激活其预测类的方向，而 near-OOD 输入像 ID 样本一样能激活这些方向。区分 far-OOD 的轴几乎无法区分 near-OOD 与 ID，因此针对单一设置优化的分数与另一设置不匹配；在 far-OOD 已饱和处，提升 near-OOD 敏感性往往以牺牲 far-OOD 为代价。这一分歧促使设计一个与现有分数互补的、针对性的 near-OOD 信号。

Method: 1）对每个类从稀疏自编码器中选取 m 个最类活跃神经元；2）将其对应的解码方向正交归一化为低维子空间；3）以输入在预测类子空间内的逐类白化平方马氏距离 z 作为评分。PLUS 定位为专用补充项而非独立检测器：z 与任意已校准基分数在单一固定权重下加性融合，该权重为全局常量，不随骨干、OOD 数据集或部署调整；所有校准统计量仅由 ID 验证数据估计。

Result: 在覆盖多种方法族的 18 种现有检测器上应用，z 在全部 126 个 detector–backbone 组合中均一致提升 near-OOD AUROC。Far-OOD 性能总体保持或提升，仅在基分数已接近最优时有轻微下降。

Conclusion: z 是一种稳健、与检测器无关的互补信号，可跨骨干泛化，无需逐场景调参，为 near-OOD 检测提供了低成本的增益，同时基本不损害 far-OOD 性能。

Abstract: Post-hoc out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is mature on far-OOD yet remains weak on near-OOD. These two settings differ in their use of class-characteristic feature directions: far-OOD inputs largely fail to activate those of their predicted class, whereas near-OOD inputs activate them much as in-distribution samples do. Because the axis separating far-OOD leaves near-OOD almost indistinguishable from ID, a score optimized for one setting is misaligned with the other; where a detector already saturates far-OOD, added near-OOD sensitivity is bought partly against it. This divergence motivates a targeted near-OOD signal designed to complement existing scores. We propose PLUS (Per-class Low-dimensional sUbspace Signal). For each class, PLUS selects the $m$ most class-active neurons from a sparse autoencoder, orthonormalizes the corresponding decoder directions to form a low-dimensional subspace, and scores an input by its per-class whitened squared Mahalanobis distance $z$ within the subspace of its predicted class. Designed as a dedicated complement rather than a standalone detector, the signal $z$ is fused additively with any calibrated base score at a single, fixed weight. This weight is a single global constant: no value is adjusted per backbone, per OOD dataset, or per deployment, and all calibration statistics are estimated from ID validation data alone. When applied to 18 existing detectors spanning diverse method families, $z$ consistently improves near-OOD AUROC in all 126 detector--backbone combinations. Far-OOD performance is broadly maintained or improved, with only marginal declines where the base score is near-optimal, making $z$ a robust, detector-agnostic complement across backbones.

</details>


### [109] [PINR-ESI: Physics-Informed Self-Supervised Implicit Neural Representation Framework for EEG Extended-Source Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06700)
*Zhiyong Zhou,Sheng Liao,Youshen Xiao,Xiaolin Li,Yakang Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息自监督隐式神经表示（INR）框架，无需配对源标签或仿真监督预训练即可完成EEG扩展源空间重建，并在仿真与真实癫痫/颅内刺激数据上验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG扩展源成像需定位活跃皮层区域并刻画其空间范围，但现有监督逆问题网络依赖合成的EEG-源配对数据，易受合成-真实域失配影响。

Method: 以导联场矩阵编码源-传感器容积传导模型，用预测与实测EEG的一致性约束INR；将皮层电流密度建模为连续坐标条件场；以经典逆解作为暖启动并提供源活动空间约束；通过patch-integrated projection将连续场映射为离散源系数用于前向建模。

Result: 不同噪声水平、源数量与源范围的仿真中，在识别活跃皮层区域上优势最明显，尤其对多源与大范围源；真实癫痫患者EEG与颅内刺激实验的定量与定性评估支持其源成像可行性。

Conclusion: 物理信息自监督INR在扩展源成像中具有潜力。

Abstract: EEG extended-source imaging aims to localize active cortical regions and characterize their spatial extent, yet supervised inverse networks typically depend on synthetic EEG-source pairs and may be sensitive to synthetic-to-real mismatch. We propose a physics-informed self-supervised implicit neural representation (INR) framework for spatial source reconstruction without paired source labels or simulation-based supervised pretraining. The lead field matrix encodes the source-to-sensor volume-conduction model and constrains the INR through agreement between predicted and measured EEG. Cortical current density is modeled as a continuous coordinate-conditioned field. A classical inverse estimate provides warm initialization and spatial constraints on source activity, while patch-integrated projection maps the field to discrete source coefficients for forward modeling. Simulations with varying noise levels, source counts, and source extents showed the clearest advantage in identifying active cortical regions, particularly for multiple and larger sources. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations using real EEG recordings from epilepsy patients and intracranial stimulation experiments further supported the feasibility of the framework for source imaging. These findings suggest the potential of physics-informed self-supervised INR for extended-source imaging.

</details>


### [110] [Where It Breaks: Diagnosing Perception, Memory, and Decision Failures of a Vision-Language Agent in Pac-Man](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06704)
*Xinglu Wang,Edward Wang,Yong Zhang,Zhenan Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文通过分离"感知检查"与"原始策略强化学习"，诊断基于Qwen3.5-9B的Pac-Man智能体的失败来源，并据此提出后训练评估的具体检查项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅用上/下/左/右四个动作玩Pac-Man时，性能不佳的根因不明：可能来自读屏（感知），也可能来自长序列动作选择（策略）。因此需要把问题拆开分别诊断。

Method: （1）独立的感知检查；（2）原始策略强化学习研究，用文本化棋盘替代图像转写，从而把图像识别从决策接口中剥离。

Result: 策略研究中，八个留出迷宫的完成率从优化前0%提升至选定检查点80.2%（额外三条命）。感知检查显示：静态截图读取得准确，交互过程中仍会出现错误，两者可共存。另一策略对比显示：进一步优化可能伴随更差的动作选择。

Conclusion: 后训练应做三项检查：在使用场景中评估感知；区分训练期成功与整局验证；考察决策过程与后续表现，而非依赖单一选定分数。

Abstract: Training an agent to play Pac-Man with only up, down, left and right actions raises a basic question: when performance is poor, what should be improved? Difficulty can arise from reading the screen or from choosing actions over a long sequence. We examine these problems in a Qwen3.5-9B-based agent through separate perception checks and primitive-policy reinforcement-learning studies. In the policy study, which uses textual boards to remove image transcription from the decision interface, completion on eight held-out mazes increased from 0% before this optimization to 80.2% at a selected checkpoint, with three extra lives. The perception checks show that accurate static screenshot reading can coexist with errors during interaction. A separate policy comparison also shows that further optimization can accompany worse action choices. Together, these findings motivate concrete checks for post-training: evaluate perception in use, distinguish training success from full-game validation, and examine decisions and later performance rather than relying on one selected score. The study provides empirical lessons for improving future agent training and evaluation.

</details>


### [111] [Energy-Guided Transport for Projection-Free Physics-Informed Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06711)
*Giuliano Galadini,Aurelio Uncini,Danilo Comminiello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Energy-Guided Transport 训练-free 框架，将非线性物理约束建模为光滑势能，以连续耗散力直接作用于生成中间态，在保持 Flow Matching 自然动力学与 O(N) 复杂度的前提下引导样本趋于物理流形，避免刚性代数投影的破坏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型（尤其 Flow Matching）在合成复杂物理系统时纯统计驱动，推理中不了解控制方程，常违反基本物理约束。现有零样本约束方法依赖刚性代数投影，对稀疏边界条件有效，但对稠密全场 PDE 的离散几何修正会破坏概率路径、偏离学习流形并带来高昂计算开销。

Method: 将非线性能量约束表述为光滑势能景观，在 ODE 积分过程中不再中断求解，而是对中间生成状态施加连续耗散力，将其引导至物理流形；该力通过反向模式自动微分隐式求得，从而保留流的自然动力学并维持 O(N) 算法复杂度。

Result: 在多种 1D、2D 物理系统（含混沌 Navier-Stokes 方程）上广泛评测：实现无结构发散的稳定生成，相比 SOTA 方法显著改善稳定性-精度权衡，且相较精确投影策略大幅加速推理。

Conclusion: 以连续能量引导力替代刚性投影，可在训练-free 下高效满足稠密 PDE 约束，兼顾稳定性、精度与计算效率，为物理感知生成建模提供可行路径。

Abstract: Deep generative models, particularly Flow Matching frameworks, have emerged as powerful tools for synthesizing complex physical systems. However, standard architectures remain purely statistically driven and agnostic to the underlying governing equations, often violating fundamental physical constraints during inference. Existing zero-shot approaches designed to enforce these constraints typically rely on rigid algebraic projections. While effective for sparse boundary conditions, applying such discrete geometric corrections to dense, full-domain PDEs may disrupt the probability path, \rev{potentially deviating from the learned manifold and introducing substantial computational overhead}. To address these challenges, we introduce Energy-Guided Transport, a novel training-free framework that formulates non-linear physical constraints as a smooth potential energy landscape. Rather than interrupting the ODE integration, our method applies a continuous dissipative force directly to the intermediate generative states, steering them toward the physical manifold. By evaluating this force implicitly via reverse-mode automatic differentiation, our framework preserves the natural dynamics of the flow while maintaining an efficient $\mathcal{O}(N)$ algorithmic complexity. We extensively evaluate our framework across various 1D and 2D physical systems, including the chaotic Navier-Stokes equations. Our method achieves stable generation without structural divergence, significantly improves the stability-accuracy trade-off compared to state-of-the-art methods, and substantially accelerates inference with respect to exact projection strategies. Code available at: https://github.com/ispamm/Projection-free-energy-guided-flow-matching.

</details>


### [112] [Symmetry Breaking at the Edge of Stability via Central Flow Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06718)
*Naima Elosegui Borras,Georgios Arvanitidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究在边缘稳定（EOS）机制下，用中心流（central flow）描述有限步梯度下降时，梯度流守恒律（如层平衡性）如何演化，并刻画由曲率修正引起的沿临界参数方向的有限维漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续对称性带来梯度流下的守恒量（线性/ReLU网络的层平衡性），但有限步梯度下降不保证守恒，尤其在EOS处迭代围绕更平滑轨迹振荡，需理解中心流下这些守恒律的保持与破缺。

Method: 在中心流框架下分析守恒量演化；证明曲率修正诱导由振荡协方差沿临界参数方向决定的有限维漂移；在标量两层模型、两层矩阵分解与两层ReLU网络（其已知守恒律已被证明完备）上做分析。

Result: 曲率修正产生沿临界参数方向的有限维漂移，其大小由振荡协方差确定；据此可判定EOS下哪些守恒律仍被保持、哪些被破坏。

Conclusion: 首次针对EOS中心流动力学给出守恒律保持/破缺的初步分析，为理解有限步梯度下降与梯度流之间的差异提供框架。

Abstract: Continuous symmetries in neural networks lead to conserved quantities that are invariant under gradient flow, such as layer balancedness in linear and ReLU networks. Finite-step gradient descent need not preserve these laws, particularly at the edge of stability (EOS), where the iterates oscillate around a smoother trajectory, which can be described by the central flow dynamics (Cohen et al., 2025). We study how gradient flow conservation laws evolve under the central flow description of this regime. We show that the curvature correction induces a finite-dimensional drift determined by the oscillation covariance along critical parameter directions. We thus take the first steps to analyse which conservation laws remain and are broken at the EOS according to the central flow dynamics by analysing a scalar two-layer model, two-layer matrix factorisation, and a two-layer ReLU network, where the known conservation laws are shown to be complete (Marcotte et al., 2023).

</details>


### [113] [Template Gap Reconstruction for Shell Output Simulation with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06731)
*Jarrod Ragsdale,Rajendra Boppana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模板间隙重建（TGR）：用可复用输出模板字面量+哨兵占位，交由自回归生成间隙填充，从而在小型语言模型上提升Shell命令输出仿真保真度并缩短生成长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型可做不执行真实命令的Shell输出仿真，但全输出解码（FOD）需同时预测跨执行不变的工具输出结构与每次变化的内容，负担重、生成目标长。

Method: 从18.7M条、跨异构执行环境收集的输出构建模板库；用模板选择器选取待重建模板（空模板退化为FOD），模板提供可复用字面量并以哨兵标记需自回归生成的间隙；在排除于模板库构建之外的目标环境上，训练选择器及五个由不同小型模型checkpoint初始化的重建模型。

Result: 当最优模板覆盖率>25%时TGR的O-ES优于FOD，低覆盖率时相当；短间隙序列O-ES为0.964 vs 0.812，长序列相当；跨所用分词器族生成token数减少26.3–38.3%。

Conclusion: 可复用输出结构是小型Shell仿真器的实用设计选择：提升响应保真度、缩短生成目标；模板选择与残差预测仍是决定响应质量的核心。

Abstract: Small language models offer a compact basis for shell simulation without executing submitted commands, but full-output decoding requires them to predict recurring utility structure alongside content that varies across executions. We introduce template gap reconstruction (TGR), which supplies reusable output literals interspersed with sentinels to be interleaved with autoregressively generated gaps. A template selector chooses which template to be reconstructed, with the empty template retaining full-output decoding. We construct a template bank from 18.7 million outputs collected across heterogeneous execution environments and train a template selector and five reconstruction models initialized from various small language model checkpoints on a target environment excluded from bank construction. Evaluation demonstrates that TGR improves output edit similarity (O-ES) over full-output decoding (FOD) when the optimal template covers more than 25\% of the template, while performing similarly for lower-coverage outputs. Additionally, TGR outperforms FOD for shorter gap sequences, with O-ES values of 0.964 and 0.812, respectively, while maintaining similar performance for longer sequences. TGR reduces the number of tokens generated by 26.3--38.3\% across used tokenizer families. These results support using a reusable output structure as a practical design choice for small shell simulators, improving response fidelity and shortening generation targets while keeping template selection and residual prediction central to response quality.

</details>


### [114] [Continual Classification Beyond the Kernel Regime: Harnessing the Sparsity Bias of L2 Regularization in Diagonal Linear Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06759)
*Gilad Karpel,Itay Evron,Ron Meir,Daniel Soudry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究高维统计设定下线性预测器的持续学习逻辑回归，推导持续线性分类的最优L2正则强度并分析深层模型（两层对角线性网络）中L2正则的稀疏偏置问题，提出一种加权L2正则变体，将单任务中的稀疏偏向性扩展到持续学习，在稀疏教师假设下理论证明其收敛显著快于常规持续L2/L1正则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有持续学习理论研究多集中于持续线性回归及其L2变体，因其解析简便且与神经网络核机制相关联，但持续线性分类的理论刻画仍不充分；同时，两层网络上的L2正则本可诱导稀疏特征学习、超出核机制，然而在持续学习中以先前任务权重为中心的L2正则会破坏这一稀疏偏向性，因此需要设计能保留稀疏偏置的正则方案。

Method: 先在高维统计框架下推导持续线性分类的最优L2正则强度并刻画其泛化性能；再引入最小化的两层对角线性网络（其单任务情形下权重L2正则可诱导预测器稀疏），分析持续学习中该稀疏偏置的丧失机制；据此提出一种加权L2正则变体，使稀疏偏置在持续学习中得以延续，并通过理论与实验进行验证。

Result: 持续线性分类的最优L2强度与泛化行为与既有持续线性回归结论一致；在两层对角线性网络中，以先前任务权重为中心的常规L2正则无法保持稀疏偏向性；对于足够稀疏的教师模型，加权L2变体收敛速度显著快于直接学习线性预测器并配合常规持续L2或L1正则的方法。

Conclusion: 持续学习中的正则化设计需考虑其对特征学习的结构性影响；通过加权L2正则可恢复并利用稀疏偏置，在中等任务跨度下大幅提升性能，但需以一定的长时程偏置为代价。

Abstract: We study logistic regression for continual learning with linear predictors in a high-dimensional statistical setting. Existing theoretical work has largely focused on continual linear regression with variants of L2 regularization, motivated by their analytical simplicity and connection to the kernel regime of neural networks. We first derive the optimal L2 regularization strength for continual linear classification and characterize its generalization performance, recovering behavior analogous to prior results for continual linear regression. Next, we examine the effect of L2 regularization in deeper models using a minimal two-layer diagonal linear network. In the single-task setting, L2 regularization on the weights of such models is known to promote sparsity in the resulting predictor, yielding a rich, sparse feature-learning regime beyond the kernel regime (a similar effect arises in two-layer ReLU networks). However, in continual learning, where L2 regularization is typically centered on previous-task weights, this sparsity bias is no longer preserved. We therefore propose a weighted variant of L2 regularization that extends this beneficial sparsity bias to continual learning. We show theoretically and empirically that, for a sufficiently sparse teacher, our variant converges substantially faster than directly learning a linear predictor with conventional continual L2 or L1 regularization, trading some long-horizon bias for markedly improved performance over moderate task horizons.

</details>


### [115] [FedFiOT: Fisher-Weighted Optimal Transport for Model Merging in One-Shot and Few-Round Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06783)
*Quan Minh Nguyen,Hoang M. Ngo,Bhakti Chowkwale,Yasaman Keshtkarjahromi,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedFiOT：用Fisher加权最优传输联合对齐神经元并聚合模型，提升单轮/少轮联邦学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮/少轮FL通信开销小，但因缺少频繁同步，客户端模型发生神经元位置漂移；同时数据异构使对应参数对各客户端的重要性不同。直接平均会把不匹配的神经元合并，并忽略参数重要性差异，导致性能低于多轮FL。

Method: 提出FedFiOT框架：用Fisher信息量化参数重要性，据此引导神经元对齐与模型聚合；通过交替最小化共享的Fisher加权目标，联合优化神经元对应关系与全局模型参数（逐层合并）。

Result: 理论上证明该优化过程单调降低逐层合并目标，并给出平稳性界，刻画客户端异构性与非均匀Fisher权重的影响；在多个数据集的单轮与少轮FL设定下均稳定优于对比基线。

Conclusion: 将Fisher加权与最优传输结合可有效缓解神经元错位与参数重要性差异问题，为低通信轮次的联邦学习提供有理论保证的对齐聚合方案。

Abstract: One-shot and few-round federated learning (FL) reduce communication burden by constructing a global model within one or a few communication rounds, but often achieve lower performance than conventional multi-round FL. Without frequent synchronization, client models may drift apart, causing neurons that learn similar features to occupy different positions across clients. At the same time, local data heterogeneity can cause corresponding parameters to differ in their importance to each client's predictions. Together, these challenges cause direct averaging to combine mismatched neurons and assign equal weight to parameters despite differences in their importance. We propose FedFiOT, a Fisher-weighted optimal transport framework for one-shot and few-round FL that leverages Fisher information to quantify parameter importance and guide neuron alignment and model aggregation. FedFiOT jointly optimizes neuron correspondences and global model parameters through alternating minimization of a shared Fisher-weighted objective. Theoretically, we show that FedFiOT's optimization procedure monotonically decreases the layer-wise merging objective and establish a stationarity bound that quantifies the effects of client heterogeneity and nonuniform Fisher weighting.Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over competing baselines in both one-shot and few-round federated learning settings.

</details>


### [116] [Transforming Integer Grids to Spatial Intuition: Perception, Not Planning, Governs ARC-AGI-3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06793)
*Harsidak Singh Banwait*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SARBLOH 提出以“空间感知而非规划能力”为核心的开放权重智能体，用零参数 O(HW) 感知引擎将每帧（35 ms）解析为物体清单、二分时序跟踪、几何简报与标注画布，配合 code-as-action 运行时直接书写算法假设，而非低层动作。在 ARC-AGI-3 上把评分从 3/28 提升到 25/28，token 消耗降低 89.4%，公开集 183 关中解决 143 关，隐藏 Kaggle 评测得分 16.53，为首个 Milestone 冠军的 13.7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型直接读取原始整数网格时，序列化破坏了空间拓扑，导致需要数百个动作重新发现边界与对称性；同时 ARC-AGI-3 以 Relative Human Action Efficiency 评分并对探索性浪费做二次惩罚，使得基于原始网格的探索极其低效。作者据此主张交互式推理的瓶颈在于空间感知而非规划容量。

Method: 零参数 O(HW) 感知引擎：每帧 35 ms 生成物体清单 + 二分图时序跟踪 + 几何简报（区分已证实不变量与可供性假设）+ 标注画布；结合 code-as-action 运行时，让智能体编写算法级假设替代低层动作；并设计主动验证探针以校验单帧可供性猜测。

Result: 八种表示下，grounded 感知将评分由 3/28 提升至 25/28，token 使用下降 89.4%；23 个留出游戏上几何不变量泛化准确率 100%，而单帧可供性猜测仅 6–50%；公开集 Run A（简报关闭）8.2 小时解决 183 关中的 143 关，单遍效率 59.4%，Run B（简报开启）达 63.5%；单张 Kaggle RTX PRO 6000、9 小时预算内隐藏评测得分 16.53，为首个 Milestone 冠军的 13.7 倍。

Conclusion: 空间感知是 ARC-AGI-3 交互推理的主导因素；几何不变量可高精度泛化，但可供性假设仍需主动验证探针，说明探索不可完全消除而应被结构化压缩。感知前端 + 代码级动作是提升样本效率与动作效率的有效架构。

Abstract: Sample-efficient fluid intelligence requires agents that rapidly form working models of unfamiliar interactive environments. ARC-AGI-3 tests this across uninstructed visual games scored by Relative Human Action Efficiency, which penalizes exploratory waste quadratically. When frontier models read raw integer grids, serialization destroys spatial topology, and they spend hundreds of actions rediscovering basic boundaries and symmetries. We introduce SARBLOH, an open-weight agent built on the premise that spatial perception, not planning capacity, governs interactive reasoning on ARC-AGI-3. A zero-parameter O(HW) perception engine turns each frame, in 35 ms, into an object inventory with bipartite temporal tracking, a geometric briefing that separates proven invariants from affordance hypotheses, and an annotated canvas. Paired with a code-as-action runtime, the agent writes algorithmic hypotheses instead of low-level moves. Across eight presentations, grounded perception raises the rubric score from 3/28 to 25/28 points while cutting token use by 89.4%. On 23 held-out games, geometric invariants generalize with 100% accuracy, while single-frame affordance guesses fall to 6-50%, so active verification probes remain necessary. On the public set, Run A (briefing off) solved 143 of 183 levels in 8.2 hours at 59.4% single-pass efficiency; Run B (briefing on) reached 63.5%. Within the nine-hour budget on one Kaggle RTX PRO 6000 GPU, SARBLOH scored 16.53 on the hidden Kaggle evaluation, 13.7x the first Milestone winner.

</details>


### [117] [Message-passing graph persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06801)
*Jorge Luiz Franco,Arthur Queiroz Moura,Moacir Ponti,Amauri Souza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Message-Passing Graph Persistence (MGP)，将消息传递融入图过滤过程，使节点嵌入与持续同调（PH）的持久事件相结合，从而获得比现有PH描述子更强的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递GNN受限于1-WL测试，表达能力有限；持续同调（PH）可提供拓扑描述子补充GNN表示，但现有方法通常将持久性的计算与表示学习过程分离，两者缺乏耦合。

Method: 提出MGP：在过滤的每一步使用当前子图更新节点嵌入，并将其与持久事件关联；这些嵌入同时决定持续同调计算中连通分量的代表元，从而实现消息传递与PH计算的融合。

Result: 理论证明MGP在图同构下不变，且严格强于其所扩展的PH描述子；在表达能力基准测试及多个图分类、回归数据集上，MGP在多数数据集上优于其他基于PH的描述子。

Conclusion: 将消息传递与持续同调计算耦合，可在保持同构不变性的同时提升拓扑描述子的表达能力，是增强GNN拓扑表示的有效途径。

Abstract: Message-passing graph neural networks (GNNs) are limited in expressive power by the one-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman test. Persistent homology (PH) provides topological descriptors that can complement GNN representations, but existing approaches typically compute persistence separately from the representation learning process. In this paper, we introduce Message-Passing Graph Persistence (MGP), a family of topological descriptors that incorporates message passing into graph filtrations. At each filtration step, node embeddings are updated using the current subgraph and associated with persistence events. These embeddings also determine the representatives of connected components during persistence computation. We prove that MGP is invariant under graph isomorphism and that it is strictly more expressive than the PH-based descriptors it extends. We evaluate MGP on an expressivity benchmark and on several graph classification and regression datasets. Experiments show that MGP outperforms other PH-based descriptors on most of these datasets.

</details>


### [118] [Systolic Linear Attention: Co-Designing Tile-Grouped Gated Delta Attention for TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06828)
*Gagik Amirkhanyan,Nuojin Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Systolic Linear Attention (SLA)，针对TPU脉动矩阵单元重塑gated delta线性注意力结构，在TPU v7x上显著加速并保持/改善困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有gated-delta线性注意力层（GDN、KDA、GDN-2）为GPU优化（小tensor-core tile、快速向量寄存器），直接移植到TPU会导致每个256×256脉动矩阵单元半空、并受向量单元瓶颈限制。

Method: 围绕矩阵单元重塑：用分组value heads将key宽度扩至256，将衰减门分组为四个64通道tile，并用块矩阵乘法在单一融合kernel中完成chunk求解。

Result: TPU v7x上完整SLA-R4层比开源Tokamax KDA kernel快1.6×–2.5×（精确chunk求逆时1.4×–2.4×），主要来自head sharing与kernel融合；2048芯片、6.5B–63.1B稀疏MoE预训练中训练速度提升11%–24%，困惑度相近，小规模匹配种子实验困惑度略低。

Conclusion: 通过硬件对齐的算子重塑，可显著提升TPU上线性注意力层效率而不牺牲模型质量。

Abstract: Gated-delta linear attention layers such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN), Kimi Delta Attention (KDA), and GDN-2 are tuned for GPUs, where small tensor-core tiles and fast vector registers make per-channel decay rescaling and sequential in-block solves cheap. On Google TPUs, which concentrate compute in wide $256 \times 256$ systolic matrix units backed by a slower vector unit, porting these layers directly leaves half of each matrix tile empty and bottlenecks on vector operations. We present \textbf{Systolic Linear Attention (SLA)}, which reshapes gated delta attention around the matrix unit by widening keys to $256$ with grouped value heads, grouping decay gates into four $64$-channel tiles, and computing the chunk solve entirely with block matrix products in a single fused kernel. On TPU v7x, a full \texttt{SLA-R4} layer runs $1.6\times$--$2.5\times$ faster than the open-source Tokamax KDA kernel ($1.4\times$--$2.4\times$ with exact chunk inversion), driven primarily by head sharing and kernel fusion. Across a $2{,}048$-chip TPU v7x pre-training study of sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models from $6.5\text{B}$ to $63.1\text{B}$ parameters, SLA trains $11\%$--$24\%$ faster than KDA at similar perplexity, and reaches slightly lower perplexity than KDA in smaller matched-seed runs.

</details>


### [119] [Context--Answer Metamodels: Forecasting LLM Activations Before the First Answer Token](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06837)
*Tommy He,Thomas Jiralerspong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"上下文—答案元模型"，用读取上下文时的激活序列直接预测随机解码下答案激活的均值/分布，从而在生成首个token前低成本刻画回答多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机解码使同一上下文产生多种回答，刻画这种变异性通常需大量昂贵的自回归rollout，且每次仅揭示一个实现，成本高、方差大。作者希望验证：仅凭读取上下文阶段的激活能否前瞻性地预测rollout激活。

Method: 训练上下文—答案元模型，将上下文激活序列映射为对答案激活（mean-pooled）的点预测或分布预测，并比较四类元模型族；用规模扩展实验（至50万prompt）、域外零样本测试、行为预测性检验及跨模型迁移实验（Qwen2.5→Qwen3.5、Gemma 4）评估。

Result: (i) 四类元模型均能高保真预测均值池化的答案激活，分布型元模型还能刻画rollout变异性；(ii) 训练集扩至50万prompt时性能持续提升，并可零样本泛化到域外数据集；(iii) 预测结果保留行为预测信号；(iv) Qwen2.5的上下文激活可预测Qwen3.5与Gemma 4的答案激活，显示跨模型可迁移。

Conclusion: 上下文激活轨迹可在首个答案token生成前支持对LLM回答行为的可扩展预测，为替代大量rollout、低成本刻画随机解码下的输出分布提供可行路径。

Abstract: Under stochastic decoding, a single context can elicit many different answers from a language model. Characterizing this variation typically requires many expensive autoregressive rollouts, each revealing only one realization. We ask whether the activations produced while reading the context can directly forecast the model's rollout activations. We train context--answer metamodels that map a context activation sequence to point or distribution forecasts of mean-pooled answer activations. We find that (i) the four metamodel families we study forecast mean-pooled answer activations with high fidelity, and the distribution metamodels also capture rollout variability; (ii) performance improves as the training set grows to 500,000 prompts, and the metamodels generalize zero-shot to out-of-domain datasets; (iii) their forecasts retain behavior-predictive signal; and (iv) context activations from Qwen2.5 predict mean-pooled answer activations from Qwen3.5 and Gemma 4, suggesting that activation forecasts can transfer across models. Together, these results suggest that context activation traces can support scalable forecasts of behaviors in LLM answers before the first answer token is generated.

</details>


### [120] [Adaptive Multi Mode: Budget-Aware Routing of Optimizers By Online Landscape Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06879)
*Benjamin Jacob Bodner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMM 是一种面向连续黑箱优化的自适应路由框架，通过在线探针按预算在五种优化引擎间切换；在嵌套 bbob 上于600个单元格中382个取得最高目标份额，但并非全面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优经典优化器取决于预算与地形，而现有路由器仅利用维度、预算、历史改进或单一基准拟合特征，且很少在线检验优化器的隐含假设。

Method: AMM 在已知预算下，用针对曲率、平台、可分性、折点、盆地与分形结构的在线探针，在准牛顿、信赖域、坐标、多起点与进化策略引擎间分配预算；每个探针设有默认值、维度-预算有效区间及计入预算的开销；采用预注册开发循环与描述性后验规则评估。

Result: bbob 开发循环36项预测中32项在最近16次确认中成立；留出 bbob(101-110, D=2-40, 10D-1000D) 单次测试中对至多七个对手在600个单元格中382个目标份额最高（236独胜、146并列），可信落后35个（多在10D、30D）；各预算下均优于自建控制（单引擎、分维度预算最优引擎、贪心调度）与 NgIohTuned；对IPOP-CMA-ES的领先在仿射与箱变换、箱约束及旋转f2-f4上成立，但旋转可分f3、f4后大部分领先消失；MA-BBOB 2000D 处最终份额持平、anytime面积更低。

Conclusion: 在线假设探测的路由可稳定超越单引擎与调参基线，但优势依赖地形假设成立（如可分性），并非普适；作者开源发布为 Python 包 amm-optimizer。

Abstract: The best classical optimizer for a continuous black-box problem depends on its budget and landscape. Existing routers decide from the dimension and budget, past improvement or features fitted to one benchmark, and few test an optimizer's implicit assumptions online. AMM (Adaptive Multi Mode) routes a known budget among quasi-Newton, trust-region, coordinate, multistart and evolution-strategy optimization engines with online probes of curvature, plateaus, separability, kinks, basins and fractal structure. Each probe has a default, a dimension-by-budget validity range and a cost charged to the budget. In its pre-registered development loop on bbob, 32 of 36 predictions held over the last 16 confirmations. AMM was then tested once on held-out bbob instances 101-110 (D=2-40, 10*D-1000*D) against up to seven competitors. It has the highest target share in 382 of 600 function-dimension-budget cells (236 outright, 146 tied) and, by a descriptive posterior rule, is credibly behind in 35, mostly at 10*D and 30*D. At every budget it also leads the controls built from its own engines (the best engine alone, the best engine per dimension and budget, and a greedy schedule), narrowly at 10*D and 30*D, and nevergrad's NgIohTuned. In pre-registered runs its lead over IPOP-CMA-ES holds under affine and box transformations, on box-constrained bbob and, pooled, on rotated f2-f4, but rotating the separable f3 and f4 removes most of the lead there. On held-out MA-BBOB its final share at 2000*D is level with IPOP-CMA-ES's and its anytime area lower. We published AMM as an open-source Python package (pip install amm-optimizer; https://github.com/BjBodner/amm-optimizer).

</details>


### [121] [Reinforcement Learning Enables Scaling Generalist Robot Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06905)
*Michal Korniak,Marco Bagatella,Ralf Römer,Michal Nauman,Yarden As,Pranav Vignesh,Manan Agarwal,Leander Diaz-Bone,Piotr Zalewski,Mikołaj Czarnecki,Lenart Treven,Shashwat Saxena,Sreyas Venkataraman,Sarvesh Patil,Zhiyuan Zhou,Daniel Marta,Oier Mees,Nur Muhammad Mahi Shafiullah,Carmelo Sferrazza,Bhavya Sukhija,Angela P. Schoellig,Andreas Krause,Max Simchowitz,Andrew Wagenmaker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究通用机器人策略能否在部署阶段实现多任务在线自提升，并系统评估算法与设计选择，证明基于价值的RL配方可实现跨多样任务的在线改进，甚至泛化到未参与在线训练的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用机器人策略虽零样本泛化强，但部署仍需适配阶段；已有的迭代式自提升RL几乎只在单任务设定下探索，与基座策略的多任务能力不匹配，缺乏对"多任务通用提升"是否可行及所需算法配方的研究。

Method: 构建结构化评测套件以检验通用提升能力；系统评估价值估计、策略提取（policy extraction）与资源分配等算法及实践设计选择；分析训练任务分布等关键因素的影响，刻画泛化发生的条件。

Result: 通用策略可在多样任务上在线提升；正确的配方还能提升在线训练中未涉及任务的性能；训练任务分布与基于价值的RL是产生该泛化的两个关键条件。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是机器人基础模型在线通用自提升的早期证据之一，表明多任务通用改进可行，且价值型RL与合理的任务分布设计至关重要。

Abstract: The introduction of generalist robot policies has enabled a single, large model to learn to perform a variety of tasks from a vast pre-training corpus. While these models often exhibit powerful zero-shot generalization capabilities, they typically require an adaptation phase to achieve sufficient performance in deployment. Iterated self-improvement via reinforcement learning (RL) has demonstrated promise in enabling this adaptation, yet such approaches have been largely explored in single-task settings - where the goal is to simply improve on a narrow deployment task - which lies in stark contrast to the multi-task capabilities of the base policy. In this work we ask whether generalist robot policies are capable of multi-task, generalist improvement in deployment - targeting many diverse tasks simultaneously - and what algorithmic recipe enables this. To answer this question, we first develop a set of structured evaluations to test generalist improvement capabilities, and systematically evaluate algorithmic and practical design choices regarding value estimation, policy extraction and resource allocation. We show that not only can generalist policies be improved online across diverse tasks, but that the right recipe can enable generalization and increase performance on tasks not considered during online training. We characterize when this generalization occurs and show that both the training task distribution and the use of value-based RL are critical. To the best of our knowledge, this is among the first evidence for online generalist improvement for robot foundation models.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [What Do We Mean When We Say 'F*ck AI'?: Moral Panic, Semantic Indeterminacy, and the Reification of Techno-Capitalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05574)
*Sasha Luccioni,Travis LaCroix*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文指出"AI"这一语义含混的伞形术语与当前"道德恐慌"相结合，使善意批评反而复刻并放大企业话术中的"自主威胁"叙事，并主张以提升AI素养、强化问责和再利用现有监管来打破这一悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界与公众对AI的抵制常把对技术资本主义剥削的合理批判同（被夸大的）生存风险混为一谈，话语呈现出寻找单一"替罪羊"的道德恐慌特征，掩盖了真实的系统性焦虑根源；作者希望厘清这种话语混乱所带来的环境、政治与社会技术后果。

Method: 概念与话语分析：围绕"AI"术语的语义不确定性（无统一、单一指称对象）与道德恐慌框架展开论证，分析其如何与环境、政治和社会技术后果相互作用。

Result: 由于"AI"缺乏连贯的单一指称，围绕它的道德恐慌无意中把AI物化为一个自主、单一、具有内在倾向的行动者，而这恰与企业叙事相吻合；结果是善意的批评、行动者反弹与公众抗议悖论性地放大了基于炒作的AI讨论，为科技领袖夸大产品能力与市场价值的"威胁"叙事提供了合法性，进一步集中其政治与社会经济权力；同时，若"AI"不指向统一的技术类别，针对"AI"的治理政策必然系统性失效。

Conclusion: 需通过提升AI素养、落实问责机制、重新利用现有监管来推进，以构想更积极的AI未来。

Abstract: Academic and (increasingly) public resistance to artificial intelligence (AI) often blurs the line between legitimate critique of techno-capitalist exploitation and (inflated) existential risk. Moreover, discourse around AI can sometimes resemble a "moral panic" where an apparently clear and identifiable scapegoat is targeted in lieu of the complex systemic problems that are causing (genuine and justified) social anxieties. However, AI is not a monolith. In this paper, we explore the environmental, political, and socio-technical consequences of the semantic indeterminacy of discourse about "AI" in conjunction with the increasing moral panic encompassing these technologies. We argue that since the umbrella term "AI" lacks a coherent, singular referent, the moral panic about AI inadvertently reifies the concept of an autonomous, singular agent with inherent and often autonomous tendencies, which aligns with corporate narratives. Hence, well-intentioned critique, activist pushback, and public protest paradoxically amplifies hype-based discussions of AI, thus validating the "threat" narrative that tech leaders deploy to exaggerate their products' capabilities and market value, further consolidating and centralising their political and socio-economic power. Moreover, if "AI" does not refer to a unified class of technologies, then policy intended to govern "AI" will systematically (and necessarily) fail. We conclude by proposing ways forward to improve AI literacy, enforce accountability, and repurpose existing regulation in order to imagine a more positive AI future.

</details>


### [123] [Cibersapiens: Gamifying Cybersecurity Education to Engage Secondary Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05587)
*Soraya Frías-Guadalupe,Pedro Paredes,Carlos Castaneda-Marroquín*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"新提案"形式，介绍面向中学生的交互式游戏化网络安全教育环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全教育与意识培训多面向企业，普通公民尤其是青少年未获平等教育；现有资源缺乏参与度与行为改变动机，青少年在网络风险中仍显脆弱。

Method: 设计并开发"游戏化"教育环境（交互式），采用主动学习（active learning）方法论，面向中等教育学生提供游戏化教学资源。

Result: 摘要未给出量化评估结果，仅声称该设计提升学生参与度、加深对网络风险的理解并促进数字安全最佳实践。

Conclusion: 游戏化+主动学习可将网络安全教育转化为有吸引力的有意义体验，弥补青少年群体教育缺口；后续需实证评估其行为改变效果。

Abstract: Cybersecurity education and awareness are often tailored for businesses, leaving general citizens, especially young people, unprepared. Despite the availability of educational resources, cybersecurity training is not provided equally to all individuals, particularly children and teenagers. As digital risks such as sexting, grooming, and cyberbullying, among others, increase, young users remain vulnerable. However, existing awareness efforts fail to engage them effectively and current content lacks the motivation needed to drive meaningful behavioral change. This paper introduces "Cibersapiens: Digital Well-being for a Connected Generation," an interactive, gamified environment designed to teach cybersecurity to secondary school students. The project focuses on developing and implementing gamified educational resources that actively engage students, improve their understanding of cyber risks, and promote best practices in digital security. By leveraging active learning methodologies, this approach transforms cybersecurity education into an engaging and meaningful experience.

</details>


### [124] [The Male Abstraction Bias in Internet Culture and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05730)
*Douglas Guilbeault,Bhargav Srinivasa Desikan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究发现在整个概念空间中，抽象性与男性关联存在系统性联系：越抽象的概念越与男性关联，该模式跨语言、跨测量手段、跨人类与AI均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象性与性别通常被作为两个独立的人认知维度分别研究，作者希望检验二者是否存在系统性耦合关系。

Method: 构建约4万个单词概念与6.7万个多词表达的概念空间；基于互联网数十亿词文本与数百万张图像，采用多种测量技术评估概念的抽象度与性别关联；并进行跨语言复制检验，考察其与词性结构（动词、副词）的对应关系，以及人类和AI在自由联想任务中的表现。

Result: 更抽象的概念更强烈地与男性关联，该效应在全球多种语言中均可复制；模式跨概念领域成立，并映射到语法结构上——动词和副词更抽象且更男性编码，体现了强调男性能动性特质的刻板印象系统性痕迹；该偏差同样出现在人类与AI的自由联想反应中。

Conclusion: "男性抽象偏差"可能统合并解释多种刻板印象，表明性别是概念空间的核心组织维度。

Abstract: Two foundational aspects of human cognition, abstractness and gender, are typically examined as separable. Here, we find they are systematically linked throughout the entire space of concepts spanning single (N = 40,000) and multi-word expressions (N = 67,000): across billions of words and millions of images on the internet -- and across numerous measurement techniques -- more abstract concepts are more associated with men, an effect which replicates in languages around the world. This pattern not only holds across conceptual domains, but it also maps onto grammatical structure: verbs and adverbs are more abstract and more male-coded, a system-wide trace of stereotypes accentuating agentic qualities in men. Furthermore, this bias emerges in human and AI responses in free association tasks, indicating its internalization and its algorithmic reproduction. The male abstraction bias may underlie and unify a range of stereotypes, evidencing gender as a core organizing dimension in conceptual space.

</details>


### [125] [Thick Alignment: A Social Framework for Values and AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05857)
*Alondra Nelson,Sola Kim*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批评主流AI"对齐"研究将人类价值当作可被指定与优化的内容，提出"薄对齐"（thin alignment）与"厚对齐"（thick alignment）的区分，并阐述厚对齐的三大支柱及其对AI研究与政策的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学的主流对齐技术通过偏好标注、奖励建模、消费者评分等"通约"装置，将情境化的道德/价值判断压缩为一个或少数指标，忽视了数十年社会科学所强调的价值是"在实践中被践行、在社会情境中被解释、在多元评价秩序中被争辩"的这一特性。作者希望引入哲学与人类学的"厚"概念，以弥补这一简化。

Method: 概念分析与理论建构：界定"薄对齐"；借鉴哲学与人类学关于"厚"（thickness）的论述提出"厚对齐"；提出两类错位概念——"Halcyon misalignment"（预设技术与价值曾处于公正均衡并已偏离）与"Mirrored misalignment"（薄对齐系统准确复制了不平等社会秩序）；梳理2023年以来研究者对厚对齐概念的采纳与改造，据此提炼三大支柱。

Result: 识别出薄对齐掩盖的两种错位形式；总结厚对齐的三大支柱：(1) 既反映也构成价值的情境化描述；(2) 在系统全生命周期中对价值进行审视与追问；(3) 通过共享、可问责的决策过程，让受影响公众参与决定系统所遵循的价值。

Conclusion: 厚对齐将看似"已对齐"却复制不平等的系统重新识别为错位系统；作者最后讨论了该视角对AI研究方法与政策的启示，主张价值对齐应是情境化、全生命周期与公共参与式的过程，而非单一指标优化。

Abstract: Computer science research on the "alignment" of artificial intelligence (AI) systems treats human values as content to be specified and optimized. Decades of social science scholarship describe values differently, as enacted in ordinary practice, interpreted in social context, and contested across plural orders of evaluation and worth. We argue that the dominant alignment techniques, which we call "thin alignment," convert situated moral or value judgments into one or a few metrics via devices of commensuration such as preference labeling, reward modeling, and consumer ratings. Drawing on philosophical and anthropological accounts of thickness, "thick alignment" instead examines how values take shape, inform the development of AI systems, and become subject to contestation over whose interests they serve. We describe two forms of misalignment in seemingly aligned algorithmic systems that highlight thin alignment: "Halcyon misalignment," which presumes a prior, just equilibrium of technology and values from which a system has departed, and "Mirrored misalignment," which names the risks and harms produced by systems that are aligned in a thin sense yet accurately reproduce an unequal social order. A thick account registers such systems as misaligned. We then trace how researchers have taken up and adapted the concept of thick alignment since it was first proposed in 2023, using these adaptations to refine the concept, and to distill three of its pillars: (1) contextual description that both reflects and constitutes values; (2) interrogation of values across the life cycle of a system; and (3) shared, accountable decision-making through which affected publics help determine which values guide the system. We close by considering implications for AI research and policy.

</details>


### [126] [FireOPS: A Human-Centric Emergency Operations Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06176)
*Alexander Wallner,Michael Riegler,Philipp Neuhauser,Helmut Haider,Lukas Neuhauser,Marco Stadler,Eduard Paireder*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: FireOPS是一个面向志愿消防队的以人为中心决策支持平台，整合手机告警、导航、实时地理态势图（COP）、辖区资源负荷总览与场景手册，采用边缘+云双通道架构，并以2024年洪灾日志与2025年公开上线经验验证其差异化价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 志愿消防队现有告警渠道碎片化，态势感知依赖无线电与纸质材料，难以形成共享的共同作战图景（COP），指挥员与救援人员的决策支持不足。

Method: 采用经验教训报告（lessons-learned）形式：阐述FireOPS设计理由，将其定位到应急响应信息系统研究中；基于2024年重大区域性洪灾的主日志抽取与2025年公开发布数据进行运行分析；并与DIVERA 24/7、blaulichtSMS及LifeRescue原型做特征矩阵对比。

Result: 特征矩阵显示COP、决策支持与双通道摄取冗余为主要差异化点；边缘+云双通道在无线电台或省级服务器任一失效时提供源级冗余，但手机端投递仍依赖云核心与移动网络；洪灾期间观察到的运行缺口被映射为已部署或在研的具体工程改进。

Conclusion: FireOPS可作为志愿消防队向共享COP与决策支持转型的可行方案，其双通道冗余缓解部分单点故障，但端到端可用性仍受云与移动网络制约，后续需针对实战缺口持续工程迭代。

Abstract: Volunteer fire brigades rely on fragmented alerting channels and radio plus paper for situational awareness, hindering a shared common operational picture (COP). FireOPS integrates smartphone alerting, navigation, a live geospatial COP, a district resource-load overview and contextual manuals into a human-centric decision-support surface for commanders and responders, running on an edge + cloud architecture whose dual-channel provides source-level redundancy against failure of either the radio or the provincial server feed; delivery to smartphones still depends on the cloud core and mobile networks. This lessons-learned report presents the design rationale, positions FireOPS within emergency-response information systems research, and reports operational findings from a primary log extract of a major regional flood in 2024 and from the 2025 public app release. A feature-matrix comparison with DIVERA 24/7, blaulichtSMS and the LifeRescue prototype identifies COP, decision support and dual-ingestion redundancy as the main differentiators, and we map the operational gaps observed during the flood to concrete engineering responses, deployed or in active development.

</details>


### [127] [Building a Method: Challenges and Lessons from Developing an Impact Evaluation Framework for Public Investment in Open Source Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06281)
*Laia Domenech Burin*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文描述主权技术机构（Sovereign Tech Agency）从定性报告与受资助方自评，转向纳入定量、目标导向评估的混合方法框架；指出评估开源软件投资面临指标异质性、数据基础设施碎片化脆弱、以及因果归因不足三大挑战，主张评估既是方法学任务也是治理实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件已成为支撑关键系统的开放数字基础设施，随着其被日益作为基础设施来资助与治理，政策制定者面临问责、透明度与可信影响力证据的需求；但开源投资的公共评估方法尚不成熟，缺乏广泛接受的方法学标准。

Method: 基于框架开发实践，描述该机构评估框架的初始设计、实施中遇到的实际与结构性挑战及应对措施，采用定性自评与定量目标导向评估相结合的混合方法路径。

Result: 识别出三项核心挑战：（1）评估指标须反映异质的项目目标与情境；（2）定量评估依赖碎片化且脆弱的数据基础设施；（3）投资前中后的变化观测在缺乏额外研究设计时无法确立因果归因。

Conclusion: 评估开源软件投资不仅是方法学任务，更是治理实践，依赖可持续的开放研究基础设施、社区知识与持续的方法学改进。

Abstract: Modern societies and institutions depend on an invisible layer of open digital infrastructure: foundational, openly accessible digital technologies that support a broad range of social and economic activities. Open source software is a core component of this infrastructure, underpinning everyday technologies and critical systems in various critical sectors. As open source software becomes increasingly recognised, funded, and governed as open digital infrastructure, policymakers face growing demands for accountability, transparency, and credible evidence of impact. However, methods for evaluating public investment in open source software remain underdeveloped, with no widely accepted methodological standard. This paper describes the Sovereign Tech Agency's transition from qualitative reporting and funding-recipient self-assessment towards a mixed-methods approach that incorporates quantitative, goal-oriented evaluation to inform funding decisions. Drawing on the development of this framework, it examines its initial design, the practical and structural challenges encountered, and the responses adopted. The work identifies three central challenges: evaluation indicators must reflect heterogeneous project goals and contexts; quantitative assessment relies on fragmented and fragile data infrastructure; and observed changes before, during, and after investment do not establish causal attribution without additional research designs. The paper argues that evaluating investment in open source software is not only a methodological task but also a governance practice, dependent on sustainable open research infrastructure, community knowledge, and ongoing methodological refinement.

</details>


### [128] [Who Gets Counted? Large Language Models, Informative Refusal, and Downstream Bias in Name-Based Demographic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06550)
*Christian Pipal,Morgan Wack,Eva-Maria Vogel*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文比较3个LLM与7个传统工具从姓名推断性别/种族/族裔，指出LLM的表观准确率优势部分源于选择性标注，个体标签普遍低估White–Black民主党登记差距，而基于BISG后验的BIRDiE估计误差仅0.03。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多样性研究常用姓名推断种族与族裔，但标准工具恰在最需测量的群体上误分类严重；LLM因标注更准而被视为解决方案，然而"标注准确率"是否等价于"研究者所报告聚合估计的准确性"尚不清楚。

Method: 在三个数据集上比较3个LLM与7个传统工具：10,000个中文姓名、19,998名自报种族的北卡州选民、300名政治学作者（带人工参照编码）；将选民样本与党派登记及年龄关联；分析缺失标签的选择性（如Haiku仅标注半数选民，与限制在最高置信半区的传统工具对比）；以BIRDiE估计（基于BISG后验）与硬标签作对比。

Result: 选择性标注可解释LLM部分准确率优势：Claude Haiku仅标注一半选民，而限制在其最高置信半区的传统工具准确率更高。所有工具的硬标签均将民主党登记率中White–Black差距低估36%–65%；BIRDiE估计误差在0.03以内。Haiku的缺失标签在年龄上存在选择偏差，使年龄差距被夸大约6年。LLM答案结合BISG可细化个体标签，但未显示BIRDiE精度上的提升。

Conclusion: 评价姓名分类器应以其所产生的统计估计为准，并纳入缺失标签的后果，而非仅看标注准确率；LLM+选择性标注在聚合层面并不优于传统方法，BIRDiE等估计框架更可靠。

Abstract: Researchers routinely infer gender, race, and ethnicity from names, yet the standard tools used for this task misclassify the groups that diversity research most needs to measure. Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as a remedy because they label diverse names accurately. We ask whether that accuracy carries over to the estimates researchers report. Across 10,000 Chinese names, 19,998 North Carolina voters with self-reported race, and 300 political science authors with human reference codes, we compare three LLMs with seven traditional tools and link the voter sample to party registration and age. Selective labeling explains part of the LLMs' accuracy advantage. Claude Haiku labels half of the voters, and a traditional tool limited to its most confident half is more accurate. Hard labels from every tool understate the White--Black gap in Democratic registration by 36% to 65%, while BIRDiE estimation on BISG posteriors comes within 0.03 of it. Haiku's missing labels select on age, inflating the age gap by six years. Combined with BISG, LLM answers sharpen individual labels without a demonstrated gain in BIRDiE accuracy. Classifiers should be judged by the estimates they produce, including the consequences of missing labels

</details>


### [129] [Algorithmic Authority and the Clinical Standard of Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00044)
*Aizierjiang Aiersilan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文借用Lessig"代码即法律"框架，论证临床AI的系统架构已构成事实上的医疗监管，并以"幻觉"与人类认知偏差的结构类比提出将AI-医师共同体作为单一责任主体的辩证注意义务标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医学引入AI后，算法概率推理与专家医师经验直觉之间产生根本性张力；现有讨论缺乏对AI架构如何实质重塑责任分配与注意义务标准的治理层面分析，需要统一的失败模式治理框架。

Method: 概念与规范分析：运用Lawrence Lessig的"代码即法律"（Code is Law）框架解析临床AI系统架构的规制效应；将AI"幻觉"重构为与确认偏误、过早诊断闭合等人类认知失败结构同源的失效模式，据此推导统一治理回应；提出"辩证注意义务标准"（dialectical standard of care）。

Result: 论证AI架构已作为事实上的医疗监管发挥作用，重塑责任归属与注意义务标准；表明AI幻觉与人类认知偏差在结构上同源，因而需统一的治理与问责路径；提出以整合的AI-医师二元体（dyad）为单一负责任诊断实体的辩证注意义务标准，要求在稳健的数据治理与患者隐私框架内实现算法精确性与人类解释权威的融合。

Conclusion: 临床AI不应被视为纯工具，而应被理解为具备规制效力的架构；责任归因应从"人/机二分"转向AI-医师共同体的整体问责，并以辩证注意义务标准制度化算法精确性与人类判断的互补综合。

Abstract: The integration of artificial intelligence into clinical medicine creates a fundamental tension between algorithmic probabilistic reasoning and the experiential intuition of expert physicians; applying Lawrence Lessig's \enquote{Code is Law} framework, I argue that the architecture of clinical AI systems already functions as de facto medical regulation, reshaping liability and the standard of care. Reframing AI \enquote{hallucination} as structurally analogous to well-documented human cognitive failures such as confirmation bias and premature diagnostic closure, I show that both failure modes demand a unified governance response. I therefore propose a dialectical standard of care that treats the integrated AI-physician dyad as the singular responsible diagnostic entity, mandating the synthesis of algorithmic precision with human interpretive authority within robust data governance and patient privacy frameworks.

</details>


### [130] [Does Personality Persuade? Investigating Personality Trait Prompting and Persuasion in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06572)
*Ruben Boone,Gilles Eerlings,Sebe Vanbrabant,Davy Vanacken,Kris Luyten*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 大模型可被提示表达特定人格，但人格提示的可控性有限，且未观察到人格匹配对说服效果有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM被提示表达人格画像时的可靠性，以及人格是否影响其说服力，区分"技术层面的提示控制"与"用户层面的可观察影响"。

Method: 两项评估：(1) 用IPIP-NEO-120量表检验大五人格提示的可复现性、特质可控性、跨特质效应及无条件下模型人格画像；(2) 被试内用户研究（N=32），参与者与人格"对齐/错配/中性/无条件"的LLM代理讨论道德困境。

Result: 人格画像可复现且对请求特质有响应，但可控性因模型而异且存在跨特质溢出；无条件下模型也呈现系统性人格而非中性基线。用户研究中人格对齐对感知说服力、立场转变、信心变化均无显著影响；128段对话仅8次立场转变，信心变化更常见；参与者大五特质与易受说服无显著关联，但自评AI说服素养越高，信心变化越少；探索性分析显示代理讯息以逻辑诉求为主。

Conclusion: 提示层面的LLM人格控制不等同于对用户的可观察影响；应超越显性决策改变的指标来评估说服性交互。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be prompted to express different personality profiles, but it remains unclear how reliably they do so or whether personality affects their persuasiveness. We investigate these questions in two evaluations. First, we assess Big Five personality prompting using the IPIP-NEO-120, examining reproducibility, trait controllability, cross-trait effects, and unconditioned model profiles. Second, we conduct a within-subjects user study (N=32) in which participants discuss moral dilemmas with LLM agents whose personalities are aligned, mismatched, neutral, or unconditioned. We find that prompted profiles can be reproducible and responsive to requested traits, although controllability varies across models and changes to one trait can affect others. Unconditioned models also exhibit systematic personality profiles rather than neutral baselines. In the user study, personality alignment did not significantly affect perceived persuasiveness, stance switching, or confidence changes. Participants switched their initial stance in only 8 of 128 conversations, while confidence changes were more common. Participants' Big Five traits were not significantly associated with susceptibility to persuasion, but higher self-reported AI persuasion literacy was associated with less frequent confidence changes. Exploratory analysis showed that logical appeals dominated agent messages. These findings distinguish technical control over prompted LLM personality from observable influence on users and motivate evaluating persuasive interactions beyond explicit changes in decisions.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond accuracy: what AI assurance needs to establish in public services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06655)
*Louise Yorke*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文结合技术评测与对英欧AI治理文件的分析，考察公共部门使用GPT-4o生成的政策议题分类证据是否可靠，指出高准确率下答案仍不稳定、配置间一致不等于正确，现行报告要求未充分覆盖此类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共机构日益依赖AI生成的分类、摘要与评估作为决策证据，其可靠性关涉公共问责；但仅凭聚合准确率难以判断能否信赖，组织如何判定"测试足够"也缺乏系统考察。

Method: 将技术评测与AI治理要求分析结合，以测量理论和管理型监管为理论依据：用8种GPT-4o配置对1200条英国首相质询按21个政策议题分类，与人工编码标签比对并在不改提示的重复运行间比较；同时分析37份英国与欧盟政策文件。

Result: 准确率为68.2%–70.3%，但24.3%的问题在不同运行中获不同标签，议题占比与排序随之变化；配置间一致性高于与人工编码的一致性，说明一致性不保证正确性。评分提取程序下被标记需人工复核的比例为0.9%–41.0%。政策文件均要求测试与监督，但报告要求未一致覆盖答案漂移、共享错误、选择性评测、实质性结论与复核分流；英国部分指南明确涉及稳定性。

Conclusion: 治理取向存在差异：英国侧重公共机构自查自身使用，欧盟则将大量测试义务置于提供方，但留给其度量与接受阈值的自由裁量。公共政策应要求组织通过超越聚合准确率的检验、明确责任、获取专业能力并接受结果审查来证明其依赖的合理性，否则可能误述政策议题、错误配置注意力与资源并掩盖错误。

Abstract: Public bodies increasingly rely on AI-produced evidence, including classifications, summaries and assessments. These outputs can inform policy decisions and service delivery, making their reliability a matter of public accountability. Establishing whether this evidence can be relied upon requires testing its outputs and examining how organisations decide whether those tests are sufficient. We combine technical evaluation with analysis of AI governance requirements, informed by measurement theory and management-based regulation. We compare eight GPT-4o configurations classifying 1,200 UK Prime Minister's Questions into 21 policy topics, against human-coded labels and across unchanged repeats. Accuracy ranges from 68.2% to 70.3%, yet 24.3% of questions receive different labels, with changes in topic shares and rankings. Configurations agree more with one another than with human coding: agreement does not guarantee correctness. Under the implemented score-extraction procedure, the proportion flagged for human review ranges from 0.9% to 41.0%. We analyse 37 UK and EU policy documents. Both sets expect testing and oversight, but reporting expectations do not consistently cover changing answers, shared errors, selective evaluation, substantive conclusions or review routing. Some UK guidance explicitly addresses stability. The UK sample emphasises public bodies checking their own use; the EU places substantial testing duties on providers while leaving them important choices about measures and acceptance thresholds. Without appropriate checks, evidence could misrepresent policy issues, redirect attention or resources, and leave errors undetected. Public policy should require organisations to justify reliance through checks beyond aggregate accuracy, clear responsibility, access to expertise and scrutiny of results.

</details>


### [132] [Beyond the principle-practice gap: Responsible AI as situated epistemic and ethical practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06709)
*Thao Do Ngoc,Emma Carmel*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文提出对AI"原则—实践缺口"的替代性解释：缺口并非单纯实施/意识/合规缺陷，而是行动者识别伦理问题时所处的制度与专业条件差异所致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有"负责任AI"研究将伦理原则未落地归结为实施、意识或合规失败，隐含地将问题定为系统或个体行为缺陷；作者希望提出替代解释，说明缺口如何从制度与专业条件中生成。

Method: 对执法、商业、研究及幸存者支持等场景中设计与治理AI反贩运系统的行动者进行14次半结构化访谈，并提出"感知—判断—情境化行动—社会技术物质化"四阶段分析框架，考察认知选择与伦理判断的交织。

Result: 行动者常具真实伦理承诺，但承诺受制度逻辑与职业"道德经济"塑造；部分关切被物质化，另一些则被窄化、延迟或未实现。伦理能动性在职业、角色、既往经验与制度环境的竞争性约束中展开。

Conclusion: 负责任AI治理需要目的明确、发展充分的"认识论基础设施"，跨越上述约束，建立使受影响社群知识与潜在危害在设计中"可被接收、可被行动"的制度条件。

Abstract: Responsible AI scholarship has found that ethical principles are frequently not reflected in AI design and development. Conceptualised as a `gap' between principle and practice, this is framed as a problem of implementation, awareness or compliance, and by implication, a deficit of systems or individual behaviour. This paper sets out an alternative explanation. Drawing on fourteen semi-structured interviews with actors designing and governing AI-enabled anti-trafficking systems across law enforcement, commercial, research, and survivor-support contexts, it argues that such apparent gaps emerge from the varied and complex institutional and professional conditions through which actors come to recognise some issues, but not others, as ethical problems requiring action. We show that epistemic choices are intertwined with ethical judgements, and trace four interconnected moments---perception, judgement, situated action and socio-technical materialisation---through which concerns become intelligible, are weighed and acted upon, and are embedded in technical artefacts and governance arrangements. Actors often hold genuine ethical commitments, but these commitments are shaped by institutional logics and professional moral economies: some concerns are materialised, while others are narrowed, deferred or left unrealised. We show that ethical agency is exercised within and between competing constraints of profession, role, prior experience, and institutional settings. The paper argues that responsible AI governance requires purposeful and well-developed epistemic infrastructures that can cut across these constraints to establish institutional conditions through which the knowledge of affected communities and potential harms becomes receivable and actionable in design.

</details>


### [133] [Using AI Predictions to Augment Rather Than Replace Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06743)
*Lai Wei,Yifei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 结合预测驱动推断（PPI）框架与普查级辅助信息，AI预测虽不能替代调查，但可提升社会调查的统计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预测人类调查响应的能力引发"合成受访者"乐观派与"脆弱替代品"怀疑派的争论，本文欲厘清AI预测与调查的关系定位。

Method: 采用源于调查方法学、在社会学中称为"混合受试者设计"的预测驱动推断（PPI）框架，将AI预测与普查规模辅助信息结合；以GSS和NHIS的83项社会结果为目标，ACS为辅助文件。

Result: 在估计均值时，监督式预测器带来约7%–12%的有效样本量增益；LLM的增益较小但为正。

Conclusion: AI预测无法替代调查，但可作为增强手段提升统计效率。

Abstract: LLMs can predict human survey responses with nontrivial accuracy. What this means for the future of social surveys remains hotly contested. Optimists anticipate a coming era of synthetic respondents; skeptics see a fragile and overhyped substitute. This article advocates a position: AI predictions cannot replace surveys, but they can augment them. Using prediction-powered inference (PPI), a statistical framework rooted in survey methodology and known in sociology as "mixed subjects design", the study tests whether AI predictions can improve the statistical efficiency of social surveys when combined with census-scale auxiliary information. Applying the framework to 83 social outcomes in the General Social Survey (GSS) and National Health Interview Survey (NHIS), with American Community Survey (ACS) being the auxiliary file, we find that supervised predictors yield effective-sample-size gains of about 7-12 percent when estimating the mean, while LLM gains are smaller but positive.

</details>


### [134] [U.S. State Guidance on AI in K-12 Education: A Comparative Content and Citation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06762)
*Shahan Ali Memon,Anna-Maria Gueorguieva,Anukriti Kumar,Lucy Lu Wang,Jevin D. West,Nic Weber*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文系统比较了美国35个州及波多黎各的K-12 AI使用指南，发现各州政策优先级高度趋同，集中于数据隐私与学术诚信等法律锚定议题，而非与州人口特征或行政能力相关；政策文本呈现对非营利组织（TeachAI、Code.org）的显著依赖与州际相互引用，但普遍缺乏可操作性与问责机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ChatGPT发布后，美国过半州独立出台了K-12 AI使用规范，形成了"共同技术冲击、各自分散响应"的天然政策实验样本。作者希望系统刻画这批指南在 pedagogy、受众、可操作性、职业准备、负责任使用与公平等维度上的异同，并揭示这些政策主张背后的"知识基础"（作者构成与引用来源），以判断州级AI教育治理的实际质量与驱动力。

Method: 对35个州及波多黎各公开发布的AI使用指南文档进行内容分析：在多维度编码比较（教学法、受众、可操作性、职业准备、负责任使用与公平、与州人口与行政能力的匹配度）基础上，进一步分析文档作者身份与引用来源，构建州际引用网络以识别知识来源的相互依赖关系。

Result: 政策重点高度收敛：数据隐私与学术诚信各在97%的文档中涉及；86%提及公平的获取机会、97%提及算法偏见，但较少讨论具体边缘群体。86%强调"为就业准备而紧迫整合AI"，86%推广定义模糊的"个性化学习"，却缺乏能力建设、可操作性与问责方面的具体指导。州级优先级与州人口特征、行政能力及党派倾向均无关联。州际存在显著的知识依赖：共同引用TeachAI、Code.org等非营利组织，部分州近乎逐字复制TeachAI的示范指南，同行引用集中于俄勒冈、华盛顿等少数早期州。无任何州将家长列为文档贡献者，仅5个州纳入学生。

Conclusion: 州级K-12 AI指南表面上多样，实质上是围绕少数法律议题与非营利组织模板的高度同质化产物，缺乏可操作的落地机制与真实利益相关者（尤其家长、学生）的参与，其政策优先级并非由本地情境塑造。这提示AI教育治理存在"象征性政策"风险，亟需加强能力建设、问责设计与多元化知识来源。

Abstract: In the years following the public release of ChatGPT in late 2022, more than half of U.S. states have published formal guidelines around AI usage in K-12 education, creating a record of largely independent state education departments responding to a shared technological disruption. In this work, we analyze publicly available AI usage guidance documents from 35 states in the U.S. and Puerto Rico. We compare the guidance in these documents across various dimensions, including pedagogy and instruction, audience, actionability, workforce and career readiness, responsible use and equity, and alignment with state-level demographics and administrative capacity. Finally, we investigate who authored these documents and their cited sources, identifying epistemic foundations underlying their claims. We find that guidance priorities converge on topics anchored in laws such as data privacy and academic integrity, each addressed in 97% of documents. While most documents mention equity of access (86%) and algorithmic bias (97%), fewer discuss specific marginalized populations. Across documents, there is a common framing of urgency around AI integration to prepare students for workforce readiness (86%) and promote loosely-defined personalized learning (86%), which is not matched by specific guidance for capacity-building, actionability, or accountability. State priorities show no relationship to state demographics, capacity, or partisanship. Instead, we identify notable interdependencies between states with shared citations to non-profit organizations like TeachAI and Code.org, with some states reproducing TeachAI's model guidance near-verbatim, and peer citations concentrating on a few early states such as Oregon and Washington. No state includes parents as contributors on these documents, and only five include students.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Who May Overrule the Agent? Evidence-Gated Authority in LLM Review Institutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05390)
*Fuad Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将"评审者(reviewer)是否被授权推翻上游提案"建模为"机构管辖权(jurisdictional)"问题，提出"证据门控(evidence gate)"机制：仅当评审者给出机械可校验的违规记录与合规修复方案时才允许替换。实验显示门控能显著降低对正确提案的破坏，同时保留大部分对违规的修复，但该权衡的方向由规则本身部分决定，幅度则依赖评审者转移模式与上游提案状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体语言模型系统常引入 reviewer 提升上游决策质量，但 reviewer 既能修复错误，也会错误替换本已正确的决策。作者希望回答一个制度设计问题：在给定相同提案、任务信息、模型与评审输出的条件下，reviewer 何时才有权让建议具有约束力？即把"权威边界"从能力问题转化为管辖权问题。

Method: (1) 形式化设定：以"机构管辖权"框架定义条件性授权；(2) 对比两种接口：宽泛覆盖权威(broad override authority) vs. 证据门控(evidence gate，要求reviewer产出机械可校验的"约束违反记录+合规修复")；(3) 实验规模：2个本地运行的开源权重模型(Qwen、Mistral)、96个空间政策任务，以及96任务的组合迁移(portfolio-transfer)基准；(4) 前瞻性验证(prospective validation)：为reviewer设置显式三选一接口——保留(retain)、替换(replace)、上报(escalate)。

Result: 证据门控在正确提案被破坏的比例上大幅下降，同时保留了多数针对违规的修复；权衡方向部分由规则逻辑蕴含，幅度取决于reviewer的转移矩阵与上游提案状态。前瞻性验证中，三选一接口对Qwen完全消除了对正确提案的破坏，但Mistral仍替换了全部48个正确提案；Qwen拒绝所有仅涉及福利(welfare-only)的修复，而Mistral则常尝试此类修复。

Conclusion: 结论并非"受限评审普遍更优"，而是reviewer能力、动作接口与约束性管辖权三者共同决定纠错域(correction domain)与破坏面(corruption surface)；接口设计可改变评审行为，但不构成与模型无关的安全保障。

Abstract: Multi-agent language-model systems often add a reviewer to improve an upstream agent's decision. Yet a reviewer can both repair errors and replace decisions that were already correct. We study this as a problem of institutional jurisdiction: conditional on the same proposal, task information, model, and review output, when is the reviewer authorized to make its recommendation binding? We compare broad override authority with an evidence gate that permits replacement only after the reviewer produces a mechanically valid record of a binding-constraint violation and a compliant repair. Across two locally run open-weight models, 96 spatial-policy tasks, and a 96-task portfolio-transfer benchmark, the gate sharply reduces corruption of correct proposals while retaining most repairs of violations. The directions of this tradeoff are partly implied by the rule; its magnitude depends on reviewer transitions and upstream proposal states. A prospective validation then gives the reviewer an explicit choice to retain, replace, or escalate. This eliminates correct-proposal corruption for Qwen, but Mistral still replaces all 48 correct proposals; Qwen also declines every welfare-only repair that Mistral frequently attempts. Thus interface design changes reviewer behavior but is not a model-independent safeguard. The result is not that restricted review is universally better. It is that reviewer competence, action interface, and binding jurisdiction jointly determine both the correction domain and the corruption surface.

</details>


### [136] [Agent Harness Awareness: Turning Verified Telemetry into Operational Control for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05391)
*Manu Agrawal,Shrey Nagpal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Agent Harness Awareness"（信任分离设计），在关键动作前由宿主向模型提供小型、新鲜、经验证的状态快照，以消除"操作盲区"（operational blindness）；在工具-网络实例ToolRoute上，验证的工具健康状态使平均可观测策略遗憾降低96.9%，单次派发完成率由69.4%升至95.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具描述只说明动作"能做什么"，却不揭示该动作当下是否健康或合适。执行宿主通过监控、追踪与近期失败记录掌握这些信息，而选择下一动作的模型却没有，形成操作盲区（operational blindness），导致选错工具或错误派发。

Method: 采用信任分离（trust-separated）架构：宿主保留测量、执行、评估与最终派发权，仅在关键动作前向模型注入小规模、实时、经验证的状态快照。构建ToolRoute受控隔离基准，保持任务与候选语义不变，仅变化模型可得的操作信息；进行216次决策的消融实验（GPT-5.6 Sol、Claude Sonnet 5、Gemini 3.7 Flash）与27次决策的端到端复现（真实本地TCP/HTTP执行、OpenTelemetry客户端span、Toxiproxy注入故障）。
