Joint correctness/agreement metrics, semantic-cue controls, fresh repeats, and exact-rational rounding bounds separate these failure modes. The result is an inspectable evaluation protocol for visual weight readers, with claims restricted to the tested fields, prompts, and execution configurations.

</details>


### [170] [Autonomy and Coordination in Intelligent Manufacturing: A Recursive Autonomous Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05828)
*Yuning Chen,Hongda Zhang,Linqiang Hu,Ziqing Zhou,Yi Liu,Zhao Huang,Ding Zhou,Haining Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文从复杂适应系统(CAS)视角分析人类生产组织中的智能，识别出"个体自主性"与"集体协调"两个耦合机制，并据此提出递归自主智能体(RAA)框架，将智能、开放性与系统级集成需求转化为自主与协同生产的工程机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能生产要求系统依据生产目标与运行条件做决策、组织工作，并通过反馈持续调整。现有制造系统在把"智能"落实为可工程化的机制上存在缺口，作者希望从人类生产组织这一成熟的复杂适应系统中提炼原理，为下一代智能制造提供设计依据。

Method: 采用复杂适应系统(CAS)视角对人类生产组织进行分析，识别出个体自主性（local decision rules）与集体协调（对齐共享目标与联合约束）两个耦合机制，并以学习与适应加以改进；进一步构建生产系统模型，形式化局部决策规则与协调如何共同形成系统决策；在此基础上提出RAA框架：由目标契约(goal contracts)界定局部目标与运行边界，由能力契约(capability contracts)支持生产单元的选择与组合，并通过公共接口(common interfaces)使协调后的群体作为单元嵌入更大组织。

Result: 建立了描述生产系统中局部决策与协调如何共同生成系统决策的形式化模型；提出RAA框架，以目标契约、能力契约和公共接口为要素，借助递归组织结构实现设备—产线—工厂之间的逐层组合与协调。

Conclusion: RAA为下一代智能制造提供了一种工程化路径，把智能性、开放性与系统级集成的抽象要求转化为自主与协同生产的具体机制；递归组织结构与契约式接口使其具备可扩展性与可组合性。

Abstract: Intelligent production requires systems to make decisions and organize work according to production goals and operating conditions. It also requires continuous adjustment through feedback. This paper analyzes intelligence in human production organizations from a complex adaptive systems (CAS) perspective. The analysis identifies two coupled mechanisms: individual autonomy and collective coordination. Individual autonomy enables actors to form local decisions using available information. Collective coordination aligns these decisions with shared goals and joint constraints. Learning and adaptation improve both mechanisms over time. A production-system model formalizes how local decision rules and coordination jointly form system decisions. On this basis, we propose the Recursive Autonomous Agent (RAA) framework, which combines goal-driven autonomous agents with a recursive organizational structure. Goal contracts define local objectives and operating boundaries, while capability contracts support the selection and composition of production units. Common interfaces allow coordinated groups to participate as units in larger organizations, connecting equipment, production lines, and factories. RAA thus provides an engineering approach to next-generation intelligent manufacturing, translating the requirements of intelligence, openness, and system-wide integration into mechanisms for autonomous and coordinated production.

</details>


### [171] [Vision Paper: Why Distributed LLM Post-Training and Decentralized Alignment Are Now Essential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05841)
*Zhanhong Jiang,Qisai Liu,Soumik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇立场论文（vision paper）主张将分布式学习（联邦与去中心化）提升为LLM后训练的一等范式，而非隐私附加组件，并围绕隐私与数据主权、多元性与代表性公平、鲁棒性与可扩展性三支柱展开论证，形式化了参考漂移、偏好异质性、对比损失的DP-效用权衡三大挑战，提出算法方向与研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF、DPO等方法隐含"偏好数据应集中化"的假设，但该假设在科学上缺乏依据、实践上不可持续、伦理上有问题。随着LLM进入医疗、法律、金融及多语言社区，偏好数据来自无法或不愿集中化的群体，集中式后训练导致法律合规风险、代表性狭窄与规模上的脆弱性。

Method: 属立场/愿景型论文，无实证实验。方法论为：(1) 构建基于隐私与数据主权、多元性与代表性公平、鲁棒性与可扩展性的三支柱论证；(2) 形式化该交叉领域的特有技术挑战——参考漂移、偏好异质性、对比损失下的DP-效用权衡；(3) 提出具体算法方向并勾勒研究议程。

Result: 分析表明分布式后训练不仅必要而且可行（tractable）；忽视该方向将使AI面临法律不合规、代表性狭窄、规模化时脆弱的风险。论文未报告定量实验结果。

Conclusion: 应将联邦与去中心化学习确立为LLM后训练的一等范式，并以其作为解决偏好数据集中化假设的核心路径，推动相关算法与研究议程的建立。

Abstract: This vision paper argues that distributed learning, including federated and decentralized, should become a first-class paradigm in LLM post-training, not merely a privacy add-on. Current methods like RLHF and DPO rest on an invisible assumption: preference data should be centralized. We contend this assumption is scientifically unjustified, practically unsustainable, and ethically problematic. As LLMs deploy into healthcare, law, finance, and multilingual communities, preference data originates from populations that cannot or will not centralize. We develop a three-pillar argument grounded in (1) privacy and data sovereignty, (2) pluralism and representational equity, and (3) robustness and scalability. We formalize key technical challenges unique to this intersection, reference drift, preference heterogeneity, and the DP-utility trade-off for contrastive losses, and propose concrete algorithmic directions, and sketch a research agenda. Our analysis shows distributed post-training is not only necessary but tractable, and ignoring it risks AI that is legally noncompliant, representationally narrow, and brittle at scale.

</details>


### [172] [Risk Control Certifies Rates, Not Magnitudes: An Audit of Selective Fetal Biometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05851)
*Zhengshuai Yang,Linna Jiang,Changyun Li,Guihu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 产前超声头围测量流程的选择性预测认证只约束错误"频率"而非"幅度"；本文审计发现几何完整性打分无法识别形态学离群，改用解剖学可容许域（下限50→55 mm）能在极小覆盖率代价下把最坏误差从271.3 mm降至约11 mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产前超声生物测量必须给出头围（HC）或在图像不支持时拒答。选择性预测（selective prediction）虽有有限样本保证，但其保证针对"总量错误率（gross error rate）"，并不约束单次错误的量级。因此可能存在"认证通过但误差极大"的失效模式，需要审计并给出可行修复方案。

Method: 在HC18数据集（999 张标注图像）上，于两个分别预注册的四路划分（four-way splits）上审计一个工具（tool-grounded）驱动的生物测量流程，每个划分分别重训分割器三次；用精确 Clopper-Pearson 风险控制来标定轮廓完整性阈值；并以"可容许域（admissibility region，即使被测量有定义的解剖学范围）"约束候选解；在冻结测试集上评估 MAE 与覆盖率。

Result: Clopper-Pearson 标定得到的阈值与人工设定下限在证书上无法区分，但流程仍输出 271.3 mm 误差：一个几何完整但尺寸偏小的环（完整性得分 0.94）被拟合到真实 HC 为 324.6 mm 的图像上；约 150 个答案中仅 1 个离群，认证风险界（α=0.10）依然成立。第二个未触碰划分在另一张图像上复现同一机制（233.3 mm）。将可容许域下限从 50 提到 55 mm，仅损失 0.4 个覆盖率点，却在两个划分上把最坏误差分别压到 11.4 mm 和 11.1 mm；而若改为通过完整性阈值抑制，则分别损失 19.0 和 12.1 个覆盖率点。在解剖学定义的 70 mm 处，冻结测试的 MAE 为 1.91 mm 和 1.81 mm。

Conclusion: 频率学派的风险认证不能防护大幅形态学错误，且基于外观/几何质量的打分对此类失效不可见；合理的干预点是对被测量施加解剖学可容许域约束，而非收紧质量阈值。该方法在几乎不牺牲覆盖率的前提下消除了最坏误差的整个族类。

Abstract: Prenatal ultrasound biometry must return a head circumference (HC) and refuse when the image cannot support one. Selective prediction trades coverage against error, but its finite-sample guarantees bound the rate of gross errors, not their magnitude. We audit a tool-grounded biometry pipeline on two separately pre-registered four-way splits of the 999 labelled HC18 images, retraining the segmenter three times on each. Calibrating the contour-completeness threshold by exact Clopper-Pearson risk control returns a certificate indistinguishable from a hand-set floor, yet the pipeline still emits a 271.3 mm error: a small, geometrically complete ring fitted to an image whose true HC is 324.6 mm. The accepted candidate scores 0.94 completeness, so the score cannot see the failure, and one outlier among $\approx 150$ answers leaves the certified risk bound ($α= 0.10$) intact. A second, untouched split reproduces the mechanism on a different image (233.3 mm). Constraining the admissibility region---the anatomical range in which the measurand is defined---removes the family instead: raising the lower bound from 50 to 55 mm costs 0.4 coverage points and cuts the worst-case error on both splits to 11.4 and 11.1 mm, whereas suppressing it through the completeness threshold costs 19.0 and 12.1 points. At an anatomically defined 70 mm the frozen tests give 1.91 and 1.81 mm MAE.

</details>


### [173] [Auditable Unlearning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05866)
*Piyush Tiwary,Anushka Srivastava,Prathosh A. P*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有机器遗忘方法只在遗忘效果、能力保留、抗成员推断攻击三方面评测，缺少"可审计性"；作者将其定义为第四条评测轴，并提出GapWiden+GapVeil两阶段通用方法，使可审计性与泄漏达到oracle水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘的目标是在删除请求后从已训练模型中移除用户数据，但现有方法未考虑服务提供方能否向用户提供"删除确实发生"的证据。作者因此引入可审计性（auditability）作为第四条评测轴，定义为在提供方计算的某一统计量下遗忘集与保留集的可分离程度。

Method: 在TOFU、MUSE、RWKU上评测11种遗忘方法，验证无方法同时满足四条轴。随后提出可叠加于任意遗忘方法的两阶段方案：第一阶段GapWiden用hinge损失扩大遗忘集与保留集审计统计量之间的差距；第二阶段GapVeil为推理时拒绝采样器，以基座模型为drafter，依据草稿响应的审计统计量进行重采样。

Result: 使用该方法后，每种遗忘方法的可审计性与泄漏均达到oracle水平，遗忘性能收敛至oracle；保留性能在11种方法中有9种得到提升，但仍未达到oracle水平。

Conclusion: 删除证据（可审计性）可以通过设计被构造出来；将其作为显式要求会改变现有遗忘方法的可行性判断。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove a user's data from a trained model after a deletion request. Existing methods are majorly evaluated on three axes - how well they forget, how much capability they retain, and how robust they are to membership inference attacks, but not on whether the provider can offer the user any evidence that the deletion occurred. To fill this gap, we introduce auditability as a fourth axis, defined as the separation between requested (forget set) and retained (retain set) data under a statistic the provider computes. We show that across eleven unlearning methods on TOFU, MUSE and RWKU no method satisfies all four axes, with different methods falling short on different axes. To address this, we propose a two stage method that can be used on top of any unlearning method, in the the first stage we propose ${GapWiden}$ which uses a hinge loss to increase the difference between audit statistics of forget set and retain set. In the second stage we propose ${GapVeil}$, an inference time rejection-sampler which uses the base model as the drafter and re-samples based on the audit statistics of the drafted response. We observe that using our method, auditability and leakage reach oracle parity for every unlearning method, and forget performance converges to the oracle; retain performance improves for nine of eleven methods but remains short of parity. Our results show that evidence of deletion or auditability can be constructed by design, and that treating it as an explicit requirement changes the viability of existing unlearning methods.

</details>


### [174] [RECAP: Context-Aware Prediction Recycling with Request-Local and Persistent Memories for Multimodal Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05894)
*Hengjie Zhu,Dayan Wu,Xinze Liu,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RECAP，一种免训练的视觉语言模型推测解码方法：用一至三 token 键存储目标模型预测（上下文记忆仅限当前请求、token 级记忆跨请求持久），以熵置信度加权融合检索结果，并用最佳优先搜索构建候选树，单次目标模型前向即可在贪心解码下验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于"回收目标模型自身预测"的免训练推测解码在视觉语言模型中仅用最后一个 token 作为索引，会把不同图像与前缀下的预测混为一谈；作者发现这类复用最常在图像敏感的解码状态失效。更长的键能缓解别名问题，但可匹配的状态更少。

Method: RECAP 同时维护多粒度键（1/2/3-token）的预测记忆：上下文记忆仅存活于当前请求，因而隐式以该请求的图像为条件；token 级记忆跨请求持久以保持覆盖率。每次查找时按键的具体性与集中度做熵-置信度加权融合；再用最佳优先搜索将融合转移组合成候选树，辅以后缀匹配的续写，最终一次目标模型前向在贪心解码下验证整棵树。无需训练或辅助草稿模型。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 与 LLaVA-1.6-7B 上、七个单轮基准上，相对自回归解码平均加速 1.66× 与 1.56×，超过最强的免训练基线 Token Recycling（1.51× 与 1.37×）。

Conclusion: 多粒度键索引配合请求内/跨请求的双层记忆，可有效缓解 VLM 推测解码中的状态别名问题；熵-置信度融合与最佳优先树构建在完全免训练条件下取得显著加速，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recycling a target model's own predictions offers a training-free source of proposals for speculative decoding. In vision-language models, however, existing recycling indexes predictions by the last token alone, conflating predictions computed under different images and prefixes; we find that such reuse fails most often at image-sensitive decoding states. Longer keys reduce this aliasing but match fewer states. We introduce \method, which stores target predictions under one-, two-, and three-token keys: contextual memories live only for the current request and are thus implicitly conditioned on its image, while token-level memory persists across requests to retain coverage. At each lookup, entropy-confidence weighting fuses the retrieved predictions according to their key specificity and concentration. Best-first search composes the fused transitions into a candidate tree, supplemented by suffix-matched continuations, and one target pass verifies the tree under greedy decoding. Without training or an auxiliary draft model, \method\ achieves mean speedups over autoregressive decoding of $1.66\speed$ and $1.56\speed$ across seven single-turn benchmarks on Qwen2.5-VL-7B and LLaVA-1.6-7B, exceeding the strongest training-free baseline, Token Recycling ($1.51\speed$ and $1.37\speed$). Our implementation is available in https://github.com/Senmo996/RECAP-official-code.

</details>


### [175] [EchoKV: Self-Corrective KV Cache Eviction via Iterative Query Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05903)
*JianKun Zou,Tongtong Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EchoKV 指出 KV cache 驱逐不仅是 token 排序问题，生成驱逐查询所依赖的 workspace 本身也决定查询质量；通过迭代纠错修复早期 workspace 误差，在证据分散的长上下文中提升驱逐决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 query-driven KV cache 驱逐（如 LAQ）把驱逐视为 token 排序问题，忽视了生成驱逐查询时所用的 workspace。早期的 workspace 误差会污染后续查询观测，并在长上下文证据分散时强化错误的最终驱逐决策。作者将这种 query–workspace 耦合识别为实际失效模式。

Method: EchoKV 采用迭代纠错：先用未来解码位置的 proposal 初始化一个 feedback-compatible bootstrap workspace，再用早期解码生成的 query 在剩余 lookahead 步骤前恢复（repair）该 workspace；workspace 前向次数与 LAQ 相同，仅额外增加一个 tensor re-scoring 操作完成修复。

Result: 在 LongBench（16 个任务，Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2 与 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）上，EchoKV 在需要分散证据的任务上明显优于 LAQ，包括多跳跨文档 QA 与长单文档理解；在不同 budget 与 backbone 下，均选择性提升证据密集型任务，其余子集性能相当。

Conclusion: query–workspace 耦合是 query-driven KV 驱逐的实际失效模式；在几乎不增加前向开销的前提下对 workspace 做迭代修复，可有效改善证据分散场景下的驱逐质量。

Abstract: KV cache eviction is not merely a token-ranking problem: the workspace retained for generating eviction queries also determines the quality of those queries. Consequently, an early workspace error can distort later query observations and reinforce an incorrect final eviction decision, particularly when evidence is dispersed across a long context. We present EchoKV, which discovers and addresses this failure mode with iterative correction. It initializes a feedback-compatible bootstrap workspace using proposals at future decode positions, then uses early decode-generated queries to recover the workspace before the remaining lookahead steps. It uses the same number of workspace forward passes as LAQ while adding a tensor re-scoring operation for workspace repair. On LongBench (16 tasks, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.2 and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct), EchoKV shows notable improvements over LAQ on several tasks requiring dispersed evidence, including multi-hop cross-document QA and long single-document comprehension. Across budgets and backbones, EchoKV selectively improves evidence-intensive tasks while maintaining comparable performance on the remaining LongBench subsets. These results identify query--workspace coupling as a practical failure mode of query-driven KV eviction.

</details>


### [176] [CHARM+: Cross-Hardware Attention with Re-Merge Multistream Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05906)
*Xinhang Zhang,Boning Zhang,Chengchun Liu,Chunpu Li,Lei Sun,Weifeng Zhang,Limin Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CHARM+，通过重排通信层级与解耦跨硬件通信，缓解分布式注意力中的计算-通信重叠受限与带宽不均衡同步开销，在双 NUMA PCIe 与双节点 NVLink 架构上前向/反向分别取得 1.3x/1.7x 与 2.8x/2.9x 平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列场景下，基于序列并行（如 Ring-Attention）的分布式注意力成为 LLM 主流方案，但其固有的通信依赖限制了计算-通信重叠，降低 GPU 利用率；在跨硬件（NUMA 或跨节点）架构中，通信带宽不均衡导致频繁通信同步进一步拖累整体性能。

Method: CHARM+（Cross-Hardware Attention with Re-Merge Multistream Mechanism）：将串行通信重组为多个层级，实现层内并行与计算重叠；同时将跨硬件通信与硬件内通信解耦，消除带宽不均衡引起的同步开销。

Result: 在多种硬件架构上评测前向与反向算法，均稳定优于 SOTA。相比 Ring-Attention，双 NUMA PCIe 架构上前向/反向平均加速分别为 1.3x 与 1.7x；双节点 NVLink 架构上分别为 2.8x 与 2.9x。

Conclusion: 通过层级化通信重组与跨/内硬件通信解耦，可有效提升分布式注意力的通信重叠效率与跨硬件可扩展性，在 NUMA 与多节点场景下显著降低同步开销并取得一致的性能收益。

Abstract: As sequence lengths continue to grow, distributed attention based on sequence parallel (e.g., Ring-Attention) has become a mainstream solution for large language models (LLMs). Nevertheless, inherent communication dependencies limit computation-communication overlap, reducing GPU utilization. Moreover, in cross-hardware (NUMA or node) architectures, due to imbalanced communication bandwidth, frequent communication synchronization will limit overall performance. Based on these insights, we propose CHARM+, Cross-Hardware Attention with Re-Merge Multistream Mechanism. We reorganize sequential communications into several levels and achieve intra-level parallelism and computation overlap. Furthermore, we decouple cross-hardware communication from intra-hardware communication, eliminating synchronization overhead caused by bandwidth imbalance. Forward and backward algorithms were evaluated on multiple hardware architectures and consistently outperformed state-of-the-art methods. Compared to Ring-Attention, our forward and backward algorithms achieve average speedups of 1.3x and 1.7x on a dual-NUMA PCIe architecture, and 2.8x and 2.9x on a dual-node NVLink architecture, respectively.

</details>


### [177] [Autonomous agents for electricity-system planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05911)
*Yoon Pyo Lee,Samrendra Roy,Jay Phil Yoo,Kazuma Kobayashi,Souvik Chakraborty,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个自主电力系统规划框架，由智能体完成方案提出、数值评估、反馈解读、重设计与最终方案筛选，在五种电力系统、四个国家的十组系统-年份情景与四十条碳约束轨迹上全部得到满足物理与年度碳预算校验的候选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统规划需要在区域资源、投资、运行与网络约束之间反复迭代决策，传统流程依赖人工反复调整，效率低且难以在显式排放约束下系统化搜索可行方案，因此需要可自动迭代、可校验的自主规划框架。

Method: 框架由智能体提出投资上限、储能运行条件与馈线改造措施，解读数值反馈并决定如何修订设计与何时停止；自动执行年度投资-调度优化与代表馈线校验，采用固定验收准则并保留成本最低的合格候选；在十组系统-年份情景、五种电力系统、四个国家、四十条碳约束轨迹上评估，使用两种模型部署及单智能体与多智能体架构；另设96条轨迹档案，统一采用1000 Mt年度上限以提供匹配参照与更广区域覆盖。

Result: 全部四十条轨迹均保留通过所实现物理校验与固定年度运行碳预算校验的候选方案；四十条中有28条matched reference设计超出新情景上限；基于公共区域数据与已发布机构需求展望的96条轨迹档案提供了匹配参照与更广区域覆盖。

Conclusion: 结果表明，利用公共区域数据与公开机构需求展望，可在显式排放约束下实现自主电力系统规划，验证了智能体驱动迭代设计流程的可行性。

Abstract: Planning electricity systems requires repeated decisions that connect regional resources, investment, operation and network constraints. Here we present an autonomous planning framework that carries prepared regional scenarios through proposal, numerical evaluation, feedback interpretation, redesign and final plan selection. Agents propose investment bounds, storage operating conditions and feeder interventions, interpret numerical feedback, and decide how to revise designs and when to stop. The framework automatically executes annual investment-and-dispatch optimization and representative-feeder verification, applies fixed acceptance criteria and retains the lowest-cost accepted candidate. We evaluate forty carbon-constrained trajectories across ten system-year scenarios in five electricity systems and four countries, using two model deployments and single- and multi-agent architectures. All forty retain candidates satisfying the implemented physical and fixed annual operational carbon-budget checks. Twenty-eight of forty matched reference designs exceed the new scenario ceilings. A separate archive of ninety-six trajectories under a common 1,000 Mt annual ceiling provides matched references and broader regional coverage. The results demonstrate autonomous planning under explicit emissions constraints using public regional data and published institutional demand outlooks.

</details>


### [178] [Routes of Reasoning: Reading and Reshaping Test-Time Compute in MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05919)
*Shengjie Wang,Mohsen Fayyaz,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MoE路由器的logits可作为无训练的行为控制接口：通过探针预测六类推理行为并选择性编辑专家路由分数，可在几乎不掉精度的情况下减少过度思考、缩短生成token数中位数30.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型常"过度思考"，在得到正确答案后仍持续验证与重推导，浪费昂贵算力甚至损害性能；现有token预算或steering等方法只调控推理时长，而非针对具体推理行为。

Method: 将推理轨迹切分为句子级片段并标注六类行为（planning、execution、restatement、fact、reflection、conclusion），使每个token携带其片段行为标签；在MoE路由器logits上训练线性探针预测该标签（与残差流探针对比）；利用跨数据集可泛化的行为-专家关联，在推理时选择性编辑专家路由分数以抑制过度思考。

Result: 路由器logits探针预测行为的能力接近残差流探针，且特征数约为其一半；行为-专家关联在五个领域六个数据集上可泛化。在4个模型、4个基准上，生成token数中位减少30.4%（最高63.3%），准确率下降不超过0.5个百分点且常提升。

Conclusion: MoE路由既是推理状态的可解释观察窗口，也是优化测试时计算的轻量机制；干预促使模型在问题解决后停止，而非跳过必要推理步骤。

Abstract: Reasoning models allocate test-time compute across distinct behaviors such as planning, deriving, and verifying. However, models frequently "overthink," continuing to verify and re-derive long after finding a correct answer. This wastes expensive compute and can even degrade performance. Existing interventions, such as token budgets or steering, primarily regulate reasoning duration rather than targeting specific behaviors. In this work, we demonstrate that Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers provide a natural, training-free interface for behavioral control. We first split reasoning traces into sentence-level segments and label each with one of six reasoning behaviors (planning, execution, restatement, fact, reflection, and conclusion), so that every token carries the behavior of its segment. Linear probes on router logits predict this label nearly as well as probes on the residual stream, the model's full hidden state and the standard target for probing, while using about half as many features. These signals generalize: experts associated with a reasoning behavior on one dataset predict the same behavior on the others, across six datasets spanning five domains (math, code, science, knowledge, and commonsense). Building on this, we selectively edit expert router scores during inference to curb overthinking. Across four models and four benchmarks, this intervention reduces the number of generated tokens by a median of 30.4% (and up to 63.3%). Crucially, it encourages the model to stop once the problem is solved, rather than skipping essential reasoning steps. Accuracy never drops by more than 0.5 percentage points, and frequently improves. Ultimately, MoE routing offers both an interpretable lens into reasoning states and a lightweight mechanism for optimizing test-time compute.

</details>


### [179] [Split-KV: Efficient Context Parallelism for Training Diffusion Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05926)
*Geng Zhang,Fangzhao Zhang,Cong Xie,Tiantian Fan,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Split-KV 是一种精确的上下文并行方法，利用扩散 drafters 中"查询远短于上下文"的不对称性，将每层注意力通信限制在查询规模张量上，使通信量与上下文长度解耦，并用成本感知的 LPT 策略平衡 KV 分块负载，在 CP=64 时保持 85–91% 弱扩展效率，最高实现 3.67× 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散 drafter 通过并行去噪多个 draft token 加速投机解码，训练时用 anchor sampling 构造紧凑 draft 查询，并以块稀疏掩码对长上下文做交叉注意力。作者实证发现：固定查询预算下接受长度随上下文扩展保持稳定，而增大查询预算带来的质量增益递减，因此存在"查询远短于上下文"的实际可行区。现有上下文并行方法既未联合利用这种查询-上下文不对称性，也无法满足 anchor sampling 造成的非线性、样本相关的负载均衡需求。

Method: 提出 Split-KV：一种精确 CP 方法，key/value 分片保持本地，每层注意力的通信仅涉及查询规模的张量，从而使通信量与上下文长度无关；同时利用其全局查询视图，设计成本感知的最长处理时间优先（LPT）调度，按实际注意力开销平衡上下文 KV 分块。

Result: CP 规模为 64 时，Split-KV 保持 85–91% 的弱扩展效率，相比最快可用的 CP 基线最高取得 3.67× 加速；LPT 将负载不均衡控制在 1% 以下，规划速度比最佳负载均衡基线快三个数量级。

Conclusion: 查询-上下文不对称性为扩散 drafter 的上下文并行提供了高效设计空间：将通信限制在查询维度可显著降低扩展开销，结合按实际注意力成本调度的 LPT 可有效处理 anchor sampling 带来的非均匀负载，实现高扩展效率与显著加速。

Abstract: Diffusion drafters accelerate speculative decoding by denoising multiple draft tokens in parallel. Their training uses anchor sampling to construct compact draft queries and performs cross-attention with a block-sparse mask over a much longer context. We empirically show that acceptance length remains stable as context scales with a fixed query budget, while increasing the query budget yields diminishing quality gains, jointly establishing a practical regime with queries far shorter than the context. No existing context parallelism (CP) method jointly exploits this query--context asymmetry and meets the nonlinear, sample-dependent load-balancing requirement created by anchor sampling. We present Split-KV, an exact CP method that keeps key/value shards local and limits per-layer attention communication to query-sized tensors, making this communication independent of context length. Its global query view further enables a cost-aware longest-processing-time-first (LPT) strategy that balances context key/value chunks by their realized attention cost. At a CP size of 64, Split-KV retains 85--91\% weak-scaling efficiency and achieves up to $3.67\times$ speedup over the fastest available CP baseline. LPT achieves $<$1\% imbalance and plans three orders of magnitude faster than the best load-balancing baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/zxgx/verl.

</details>


### [180] [Graph-Mediated Large Language Model Systems in the Agentic Era: Reasoning, Knowledge, Retrieval, Execution, and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05953)
*Haihui Liu,Yang Chen,Zice Qiu,Yun Zhang,Jialun Zhong,Wenhua Wang,Yanzeng Li,Tian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述提出“操作级角色—位点”(role–locus)分类法，从推理、知识与证据、检索与记忆、执行、协调五个方面区分图操作的功能、承担计算的组件及其输出的后续使用，并给出统一评估框架与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图在大模型系统中承担结构推理、知识落地、检索与记忆等功能，并在智能体时代组织执行依赖与多智能体通信；但现有研究难以区分每种图操作的具体功能、由哪个组件计算、输出如何被后续使用，导致无法判断图机制何时真正有用。

Method: 引入操作级“角色—位点”分类法，按推理、知识与证据、检索与记忆、执行、协调五类划分图操作；比较图观测与外部计算如何引导决策、记忆表示与更新如何支撑后续任务、依赖关系如何约束执行与信息传递；并将基准、对比设计、过程诊断与系统开销组织为评估框架。

Result: 形成可区分“局部指标提升”与“下游任务表现”的对比分析方式，同时保留各结果报告时的适用条件；构建覆盖基准、对比设计、过程诊断和系统成本的评估框架。

Conclusion: 该综述为判断图机制是否有用、以及跨组件与跨阶段评估其效果提供了系统基础，并指出自适应计算与停止、记忆维护、依赖感知恢复等开放挑战。

Abstract: Graphs play an increasingly important role in large language model (LLM) systems, supporting structural reasoning, knowledge grounding, retrieval, and memory. As these systems evolve toward the agentic era, graphs also organize execution dependencies and multi-agent communication, linking intermediate observations and retained information to subsequent decisions. However, understanding their contribution requires distinguishing the function of each graph operation, the component responsible for its computation, and the subsequent use of its output. This survey introduces an operation-level role--locus taxonomy for reasoning, knowledge and evidence, retrieval and memory, execution, and coordination. We compare how graph observations and external computations guide decisions, how memory representations and updates support later tasks, and how dependencies constrain execution and information delivery. These comparisons distinguish improvements in local metrics from downstream task performance while retaining the conditions under which results were reported. We then organize benchmarks, comparison designs, process diagnostics, and system costs into an evaluation framework. Finally, we identify open challenges in adaptive computation and stopping, memory maintenance, and dependency-aware recovery. The survey provides a basis for assessing when graph mechanisms are useful and for evaluating their effects across components and stages.

</details>


### [181] [Reglos: A Self-Evolving Personal Memory Agent with Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06049)
*Sen Yang,Jiaduo Lin,Zehong Huang,Wen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练的个人记忆智能体 Reglos，通过双模型机制（提案+批评）与冻结后的固定评估器来抑制 harness 补丁的过拟合，在 PersonaMem-v2 与 LongMemEval-S 上超过 Mem0、MemOS、EverOS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人智能体依赖历史对话做个性化响应，其记忆系统与将记忆转为模型上下文的 harness 都可被改写甚至自动打补丁。但仅在目标失败案例上测试的补丁存在过拟合风险——只修好该案例，别处无收益。因此需要检验 harness 改动是否带来超出其所修失败的泛化收益。

Method: 免训练的自进化记忆：以仅追加记录逐轮累积，新偏好覆盖旧偏好、事实可被遗忘。harness 采用递归自我改进：编码智能体根据失败轨迹提出补丁，由来自另一模型家族的批评者筛查是否存在 benchmark gaming；harness 冻结后，由固定评估器决定后续补丁是否被接受。

Result: 在相同骨干模型下，Reglos 优于 Mem0、MemOS、EverOS 等 SOTA 记忆系统：Qwen3.8-27B 上 PersonaMem-v2 达 61.3%、LongMemEval-S 达 84.0%；GPT-5.6 Luna 上分别为 57.5% 与 87.2%。

Conclusion: 跨模型家族审查加冻结后固定评估的两道把关，可在免训练条件下实现可靠的 harness 自进化，并带来可观的个性化记忆性能提升。

Abstract: Personal agents draw on past conversations to tailor their responses to each user. Their memory systems store and update what each user says, and coding agents now also rewrite the harness that turns this memory into the model's context. Such patches risk overfitting, because one tested only on the failures it targets can fix those cases and help nowhere else. We present Reglos, a training-free personal memory agent that tests whether harness changes help beyond the failures they were written to fix. Its self-evolving memory adds to each user's append-only record with every conversation, as new preferences supersede old ones and facts can be forgotten. Its harness improves through recursive self-improvement, in which a coding agent proposes patches from failure traces and a critic from a second model family screens each one for benchmark gaming. After the harness is frozen, a fixed evaluator decides which further patches to accept. With the same backbone behind every system, Reglos outperforms state-of-the-art memory systems such as Mem0, MemOS and EverOS, reaching 61.3% and 84.0% on PersonaMem-v2 and LongMemEval-S with Qwen3.8-27B, and 57.5% and 87.2% with GPT-5.6 Luna.

</details>


### [182] [Which One Is Sounding? Binaural Sounding Instance Disambiguation for Audio-Visual Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06074)
*Haoze Ma,Yidi Li,Yu Ma,Pengyuan Zhao,Yi Shi,Bin Ren,Yanwen Guo,Dimitrios Kanoulas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BiSID-AVS，利用双耳音频的耳间差异提供空间约束，将类别级候选逐步细化为真实发声实例掩码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视分割方法只能预测与声音相关的区域或类别掩码，当同类候选共存时无法定位发声实例；现有实例级方法依赖运动与跨帧关联，在稀疏观测或运动不显著时不可靠。

Method: 引入双耳音频，以耳间差异编码声源方向信息作为空间约束。BiSID-AVS 分三步：先通过音频提示定位类别一致的候选区域；再将双耳线索投影为图像平面上的空间响应图；最后在特征层与解码器层进行双重空间条件化，细化出真实发声实例掩码。

Result: 在双耳扩展的 AVS-Synthetic 基准与面向机器人室内感知的 Habitat-AVS 数据集上，BiSID-AVS 在多候选子集上取得最佳性能，实例级 mIoU 分别提升 35.19 与 41.21 个百分点，并显著减少对非发声同类区域的误激活。

Conclusion: 双耳空间线索可有效解决同类多候选下的发声实例歧义，为实例级音视分割提供不依赖运动与跨帧关联的新思路，并具备机器人场景应用潜力。

Abstract: Existing audio-visual segmentation methods mainly predict sound-related regions or category masks and often fail to identify the sounding instance when multiple same-class candidates coexist. Recent audio-visual instance segmentation methods address instance-level prediction but rely largely on motion and cross-frame association, which may become unreliable under sparse observations or inconspicuous motion. To this end, we introduce binaural audio, whose interaural differences encode source-direction information, to provide spatial constraints for distinguishing the sounding instance among same-class visual candidates. We propose Binaural Sounding Instance Disambiguation for Audio-Visual Segmentation (BiSID-AVS), which progressively refines category-level candidate responses into a mask of the true sounding instance. BiSID-AVS first grounds category-consistent candidate regions through audio prompting, then projects binaural cues into an image-plane spatial response map, and finally refines the true sounding instance through dual-level spatial conditioning at the feature and decoder levels. Experiments on the binaurally extended AVS-Synthetic benchmark and Habitat-AVS dataset for robotic indoor perception show that BiSID-AVS achieves the best performance on the multi-candidate subsets, improving instance-level mIoU by 35.19 and 41.21 percentage points, respectively, while substantially reducing false activation on non-sounding same-class regions.

</details>


### [183] [UniCompress: From Multi-LoRA Experts to One Compact Model for Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06075)
*Mingkang Liu,Peipei Jiao,Weiming Meng,Tianyuan Liu,Lichen Pang,Junyan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniCompress 是一个面向部署的框架，将多任务 VLM 专长整合为单一紧凑、无适配器的 INT2 模型，在四个异构具身任务上以约 76% 的存储压缩保持接近 BF16 教师的多任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身与边缘系统依赖 VLM 支持异构技能，但为每个技能单独部署适配器并保留高精度主干带来巨大的内存与管理开销。

Method: 以整合后的 BF16 Qwen3-VL-4B 为教师，构建非对称 INT2 学生模型；采用 GPTQ 初始化与教师引导的量化恢复，对视觉编码器与语言主干的投影权重进行联合量化与恢复。

Result: 权重存储由 BF16 的 8.9 GB 降至 INT2 的 2.1 GB（约 76% 降幅）；INT2 宏观任务分数较 GPTQ-INT2 提升 34.3 个百分点，与整合 BF16 教师差距在 0.9 分以内。

Conclusion: 教师引导的视觉-语言联合 INT2 恢复可有效支撑在资源受限具身平台上部署单一多技能 VLM。

Abstract: Embodied and edge systems increasingly rely on vision-language models (VLMs) to support heterogeneous skills, yet deploying a separate task adapter for each skill while retaining a high-precision backbone creates substantial memory and management costs. We present UniCompress, a deployment-oriented framework that converts multi-task expertise into one compact, adapter-free model. Starting from a consolidated BF16 Qwen3-VL-4B teacher, UniCompress constructs an asymmetric INT2 student using GPTQ initialization and teacher-guided quantization recovery. The approach jointly quantizes and recovers the projection weights in the vision encoder and language backbone, enabling aggressive compression of the complete vision-language model while preserving its multi-task behavior. We evaluate UniCompress on four heterogeneous embodied tasks: posture recognition, household-safety understanding, multimodal dialogue routing, and mobile-GUI action prediction. UniCompress reduces the approximate weight storage from 8.9 GB in BF16 to 2.1 GB in INT2, corresponding to a reduction of approximately 76%. It improves the INT2 macro task score by 34.3 percentage points over GPTQ-INT2 while remaining within 0.9 points of the consolidated BF16 teacher. These results demonstrate the potential of teacher-guided joint vision-language INT2 recovery for deploying a single multi-skill VLM on resource-constrained embodied platforms.

</details>


### [184] [Reasoning-Trace Overflow in Code Generation: An Empirical Study of Explicit Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06095)
*Lukas Twist,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“推理轨迹溢出”（reasoning-trace overflow）现象：小规模开源推理模型可能在固定输出预算内把 token 全部用于推理而始终不产出代码。作者在7个模型、6种代码基准设置下量化溢出程度、推理对pass@1的收益及推理时控制手段的效果，结论是显式推理应被视为可控资源而非常开能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式推理虽能帮助语言模型生成代码，但会占用输出预算，导致模型耗尽预算仍未写出可用代码。现有工作较少系统刻画这一权衡，尤其是小规模开源推理模型在代码任务上的表现。

Method: 在固定32k token输出预算下，跨7个模型与6种代码基准设置测量：用于推理的token占比、推理是否提升pass@1、溢出是否伴随重复推理循环，并比较关闭推理、提示引导（prompt steering）与预算化转出（budgeted transitions）等轻量推理时控制手段。

Result: 溢出具有强条件性：简单函数级任务上罕见，但在更难代码生成基准及特定模型家族上常见，部分模型-基准组合中超过一半的生成耗尽32k预算仍在推理；多数完全溢出与重复推理循环相关，但也存在无循环的长推理。对易溢出模型，关闭推理因保证产出答案而具竞争力；对更强模型在更难算法与代码理解任务上推理仍有价值。提示引导可显著降低部分模型的溢出但不稳定，预算化转出控制更直接，可同时提升性能与token效率。

Conclusion: 推理收益高度依赖模型与任务难度；显式推理应作为代码生成中可调节、可调度的资源来管理，而非默认常开能力。

Abstract: Explicit reasoning can help language models generate code, but it also introduces a practical risk: the model may spend so much of its output budget reasoning that it never produces usable code. We study this behaviour in relatively small open-weight reasoning models, framing it as reasoning-trace overflow. Across seven models and six code benchmark settings, we measure how much of a fixed 32k-token output budget is spent on reasoning, whether that reasoning improves pass@1, and whether lightweight inference-time controls can mitigate overflow. Our results show that the value of reasoning is highly conditional. Overflow is rare on simpler function-level tasks, but becomes common on harder code-generation benchmarks and for particular model families: on several model--benchmark pairs, more than half of generations exhaust the full 32k-token budget while still reasoning. Most complete overflow is associated with repetitive reasoning loops, although substantial long reasoning also occurs without looping. Disabling reasoning can therefore be competitive for overflow-prone models, simply because it ensures that an answer is produced. At the same time, reasoning remains valuable for stronger models on harder algorithmic and code-understanding tasks. Finally, prompt steering can substantially reduce overflow for some models but is inconsistent, while budgeted transitions provide more direct control and can improve both performance and token efficiency for overflow-prone models. These findings suggest that explicit reasoning should be treated as a controllable resource for code generation, rather than an always-on capability.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Risk Cues: A Mechanism-Level Benchmark and Verifiable Debate for Scam Video Detection on the Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06110)
*Yifan Wang,Junyu Lu,Shun Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Fanliang Bu,Rong Yin,Lingbin Bu,Changrong Min,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了涵盖多类诈骗机制与非诈骗内容的多模态基准 MechScam，并提出对比辩论框架 ScamCourt，以提升网络诈骗视频检测的可靠性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有诈骗视频检测研究多为二分类，缺乏对诈骗运作机制的解释与判定依据；同时忽视与广告等表面相似的非诈骗内容的区分，以及模型对误导性论证的鲁棒性不足，导致检测不可靠。

Method: 构建 MechScam 基准，包含 6,614 个视频，覆盖多样诈骗机制与非诈骗类别，提供带视频证据支撑的解释、潜在诈骗元素及其语境标注、以及基于真实证据构造的误导性论证；并在此基础上提出 ScamCourt 辩论框架，由训练过的控方与辩方辩手提出对立论证，由训练过的法官基于带引用核验的庭审记录作出裁决。

Result: 实验表明当前先进的多模态大模型难以区分诈骗视频与相似非诈骗内容；ScamCourt 优于更大规模 MLLM 及同骨干微调基线，降低了误报率，并对误导性论证表现出更强抗性。

Conclusion: MechScam 与 ScamCourt 共同推动了更可靠、更具可解释性的诈骗视频检测，数据集与代码已公开。

Abstract: Online scam videos pose significant threats to individuals and society. Existing studies primarily focus on binary classification, offering limited insights into how scams work and the reasoning behind detection decisions. Moreover, they often overlook the challenge of distinguishing scam videos from superficially similar non-scam content, such as advertisements, and the robustness of detection models against misleading arguments. These limitations hinder reliable scam video detection. To address these limitations, we introduce MechScam, a benchmark comprising 6,614 videos covering diverse scam mechanisms and non-scam categories. MechScam provides explanations supported by video evidence, annotations of potential scam elements and their context, and misleading arguments constructed from genuine evidence. Recognizing that genuine evidence can still be used to justify misleading conclusions, we further propose ScamCourt, a debate framework in which trained prosecution and defense debaters present opposing arguments, while a trained judge makes decisions based on a transcript with verified citations. Extensive experiments show that state-of-the-art MLLMs struggle to distinguish scam videos from similar non-scam content. ScamCourt outperforms larger MLLMs and same-backbone fine-tuned baselines, reducing false positives and showing stronger resistance to misleading arguments. Together, MechScam and ScamCourt contribute to more reliable and interpretable scam video detection. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/SuperYFan/MechScam.

</details>


### [186] [From Handcrafted to Generative: RLVR-Optimized Icon Generation for Alexa+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06123)
*Rohan Sequeira,Matthew Rosenberg,Amin Mahmoudabadbozchelou,Oleg Poliannikov,Jonathan Rubin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出并部署了一条从纯文本描述自动生成矢量动画图标的流水线，并聚焦其中最关键的图像生成阶段，通过客观验证器与RLVR微调流匹配模型，将生成合格率从15.7%提升至58.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alexa+带屏设备需在对话过程中展示动画图标以强化语义，但原有人工逐个手绘方式虽精致却耗时且无法扩展到开放式对话的广泛话题；同时图标需在小画布、严苛设计规范下渲染，现有生成结果往往不可直接投产，且缺乏受约束的线稿图标数据集，无法采用监督微调。

Method: 构建自动化流水线，将图标创作分解为图像生成、语义分解、矢量化、风格化、动画五个阶段，并指出质量由首个阶段的图像生成决定。针对无标注数据问题，设计可自动判定生成结果是否违反设计规范的客观验证器，并将其作为可验证奖励，通过RLVR对flow matching图像生成模型进行微调。验证器同时作为进入最终设计审核前的自动化质量门控。

Result: 在独立于奖励模型的VLM评判器下，微调后模型的生成通过率由15.7%提升至58.7%（约3.7倍）。训练后的模型与验证器均已部署至生产流水线。

Conclusion: 以客观验证器驱动的RLVR可在缺乏监督数据、受严格设计约束的生成场景中显著提升质量，且验证器兼具奖励信号与生产质量门控的双重作用，为开放式对话中的图标生成提供了可扩展的生产级方案。

Abstract: Alexa+ devices with screens display animated icons during conversations to visually reinforce what is being said. The original set of these icons was hand-drawn by designers one at a time, a process that produces polished results but is time-consuming and cannot scale to the range of topics encountered in open-ended conversation. We describe an automated pipeline, deployed in production, that generates these vectorised animated icons from a text description alone and surfaces relevant animations to customers based on their ongoing conversation. The pipeline decomposes icon creation into a sequence of stages (image generation, semantic decomposition, vectorisation, styling, and animation). Its quality is determined at the very first of these, image generation, on which every downstream stage depends. We therefore focus on improving this first and most critical stage. Because the icons are rendered on small display canvases under strict design guidelines, generations are often unsuitable for production. Supervised fine-tuning is not viable, as no dataset of line-art icons exists under these constraints. Instead, we design objective verifiers that automatically flag generations violating the design guidelines, and use them to fine-tune a flow matching image generation model via reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR). The fine-tuned model raises the pass rate from 15.7% to 58.7% (3.7x) under a VLM judge that is independent of the reward model. Both the trained model and the verifier are deployed in the production pipeline, where the verifier also serves as the automated quality gate before final design review.

</details>


### [187] [ModalSearcher: Weaving Visual Evidence into Autonomous Cross-Modal Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06131)
*Zhihan Yin,Ruyi Li,Chengyu Fang,Yifeng Yao,Nonghai Zhang,Pengfei Xiong,Terry Zhang,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"自主跨模态搜索"能力定义、可扩展的ModalSearch-Pipeline数据构建流程与ModalSearcher智能体，在多模态知识密集型问答上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型的静态、不完整参数化知识限制了其在新颖知识与知识密集型任务（尤其需要多跳证据获取）上的表现；现有智能体式搜索方法把视觉证据固定在输入端或预设轨迹位置，难以随信息需求变化切换搜索模态，也无法应对复杂真实查询。

Method: 提出ModalSearch-Pipeline：构建交错式多模态知识图谱，采样不同深度与模态组合的路径，并用"反向问题构造"隐藏会暴露推理捷径的实体名与图像目标、同时保留必要的消歧线索，生成纯文本、图像初始化与任意交错的多跳问题；再在实时Web环境中通过监督微调与强化学习训练ModalSearcher Agent，并构建ModalSearch-Bench。

Result: 在五个多模态知识密集型QA基准上，ModalSearcher-8B在相同智能体工作流下平均准确率较Qwen3-VL-8B提升16.2个点；在独立构建的主动视觉搜索基准上亦取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 让智能体在多跳过程中自主选择与整合文本/图像检索模态，可有效缓解参数化知识局限，提升多模态知识密集型问答性能，且数据构建流程具备可扩展性。

Abstract: Static and incomplete parametric knowledge limits the performance of multimodal large language models on novel-knowledge and knowledge-intensive tasks, especially those requiring multi-hop evidence acquisition. Existing multimodal agentic search methods often fix visual evidence at the input or a predefined trajectory position, making it difficult for agents to select search modalities as their information needs evolve and to handle complex real-world queries. We define this capability as autonomous cross-modal search: an agent chooses among direct reasoning, text-to-text search, text-to-image search, and reverse-image search, actively introduces visual evidence when appropriate, and switches between and integrates modalities across multiple hops. We propose ModalSearch-Pipeline, a scalable procedure for constructing an interleaved multimodal knowledge graph. It samples paths with diverse depths and modality compositions and uses backward question construction to obscure entity names and image targets that would expose reasoning shortcuts while retaining necessary disambiguating clues, yielding text-only, image-initialized, and arbitrarily interleaved multi-hop problems. We train the ModalSearcher Agent with supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning in a live Web environment, and construct ModalSearch-Bench. Experiments show that ModalSearcher-8B improves average accuracy by 16.2 points over Qwen3-VL-8B under the same agentic workflow across five multimodal knowledge-intensive QA benchmarks, with consistent gains on independently constructed active visual-search benchmarks.

</details>


### [188] [SafeStart: Mitigating the Overlooked Safety Cost of Active Expert Reduction in MoE LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06147)
*Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo,Saeid Shokoufa,Seyedarmin Azizi,Arya Fayyazi,Souvik Kundu,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MoE推理加速中"减少每token专家数"会削弱模型对有害请求的拒答能力，且这种削弱主要发生在处理prompt和生成最初几个token阶段；提出SafeStart，在该阶段用全部专家、之后用缩减专家集，通过vLLM融合kernel实现，在保留大部分解码加速的同时恢复拒答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE服务方法（每token跑更少专家）仅用准确率基准验证，未考察安全性。作者发现这类加速手段会显著削弱模型对有害请求的拒答（在越狱提示下有害回答率上升），且现有评估忽略这一风险，因此需要一种既保速度又保安全的服务方案。

Method: 先量化专家裁剪对拒答的影响并定位其时间来源：发现拒答能力下降主要集中于prompt处理与最初几个生成token。据此设计SafeStart：对prompt及前8个生成token使用全部专家，之后才切换到缩减专家集，并在vLLM中以融合kernel实现，避免额外开销。

Result: 单张RTX A6000、batch size 8下，保留约2/3专家可令离线解码加速15–28%。该设置下，越狱提示使有害请求被回答比例在Qwen1.5-MoE上升18.4个百分点、OLMoE上升9.5、DeepSeek-V2-Lite上升6.6（9个模型中3个显著上升），而MMLU仅降不到2分。SafeStart在batch 8与32下保留至少85%的离线解码加速，将上述变化压缩至-2.1到+1.3个百分点。在889条留出请求上，截断使有害顺从率上升5.8–21.4点，SafeStart后仅残留0.6–2.6点，三个模型中两个非劣于原模型。

Conclusion: 更快的MoE服务不必以安全为代价：在每条响应的起始阶段使用全部专家即可保留大部分加速收益；启用此类方法前应测试拒答能力而非仅测准确率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models send each token through a few of many expert subnetworks. Serving methods make these models faster by running even fewer experts per token, and they are validated on accuracy benchmarks. We show that these methods can weaken the model's refusal of harmful requests, and we introduce SafeStart, which largely restores refusal while keeping most of the speedup. In vLLM on one RTX A6000, keeping about two thirds of the selected experts speeds up offline decoding by 15 to 28% at batch size 8. We find that, at this setting, under a jailbreak that tells the model not to refuse, the share of harmful requests that the model answers rises by 18.4 percentage points on Qwen1.5-MoE, 9.5 on OLMoE, and 6.6 on DeepSeek-V2-Lite, the three of nine models where it rises significantly, while MMLU accuracy drops by less than two points. We discover that most of this increase arises while the model processes the prompt and generates its first few tokens. SafeStart therefore runs all experts for the prompt and the first eight generated tokens, and the reduced set of experts afterwards, through a fused kernel in vLLM. With experts removed, SafeStart keeps at least 85% of the offline decode speedup at batch sizes 8 and 32 and brings the change under this jailbreak to between -2.1 and +1.3 points for three expert-reduction methods on these three models. On 889 held-out requests, in an analysis fixed before generation, truncation raises harmful compliance by 5.8 to 21.4 points, and SafeStart leaves a rise of 0.6 to 2.6 points, non-inferior to the original model on two of the three models. Our results show that faster MoE serving need not cost safety: a serving system can keep most of the gain from running fewer experts by running all of them at the start of each response, and it should test refusal, not only accuracy, before it enables such a method.

</details>


### [189] [CARM: Conformal Adaptive Risk Masking for Reliable Scientific Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06151)
*Guangtao Zhang,Haonan Wu,Junxuan Gao,Zhengyang Liu,Zhaoxi Zeng,Zhihao Li,Jianguo Huang,Zhiqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARM：面向时序分布漂移的自适应空间风险控制框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习超分辨率可重建精细地球观测场，但重建精度本身无法给出识别可靠空间区域的校准规则。Conformal Mask Calibration（CMC）基于共形风险控制，将空间不确定性分数转化为信任掩膜，并在可交换性假设下控制期望最坏局部失真；然而其固定阈值在受季节性与偶发事件影响的地球观测流中会失准。

Method: 提出CARM（Conformal Adaptive Risk Masking），一个在时序分布漂移下进行自适应空间风险控制的统计框架。维护具有异质更新速率的多个专家（experts），并通过非对称代理损失在线聚合其阈值提议，在快速适应分布变化与平稳期稳定性之间取得平衡。所有更新均为因果的，仅使用固定延迟后可获得的成熟反馈，因而可搭配任何提供空间不确定性分数的SR模型。

Result: 证明时间平均的掩膜条件风险以O(1/T)速率收敛至不超过α，无需分布假设，且对任意专家聚合权重均成立。在南海叶绿素-a日尺度SR流上，α=0.10时，CARM将经验违反率从43.49%降至9.38%，滑动平均超限频率从47.29%降至1.22%，同时保留非平凡的信任区域。ERA5风场与OSTIA海表温度实验在多种SR骨干网络上均显示一致的相对提升。

Conclusion: CARM为时序分布漂移下的超分辨率空间不确定性提供了具备理论保证的自适应风险控制方案，显著降低风险违反率并保持可用信任区域，且与SR模型无关、具良好通用性。

Abstract: Deep learning-based super-resolution (SR) can reconstruct fine-scale Earth-observation fields from coarse observations, but reconstruction accuracy alone does not provide a calibrated rule for identifying reliable spatial regions. Conformal Mask Calibration (CMC), built on conformal risk control, converts a spatial uncertainty score into a trust mask and controls the expected worst-case local distortion under exchangeability. However, its fixed threshold can become miscalibrated in Earth-observation streams affected by seasonality and episodic events. We propose $\textit{CARM}$ (Conformal Adaptive Risk Masking), a statistical framework for adaptive spatial risk control under temporal distribution shift. CARM maintains experts with heterogeneous update rates and aggregates their threshold proposals online using an asymmetric surrogate loss, balancing rapid adaptation to distribution changes against stability during relatively stationary periods. All updates are causal and use only mature feedback available after a fixed delay, allowing CARM to operate with any SR model that provides a spatial uncertainty score. We prove that the time-averaged mask-conditioned risk converges to at most $α$ at an $O(1/T)$ rate, without distributional assumptions and for any choice of aggregation weights across experts. On a daily South China Sea chlorophyll-$\textit{a}$ SR stream, CARM reduces the empirical violation rate from $43.49\%$ to $9.38\%$ and the moving-average exceedance frequency from $47.29\%$ to $1.22\%$ at $α=0.10$, while retaining a non-trivial trusted region. Experiments on ERA5 wind and OSTIA sea-surface temperature further show consistent relative improvements across multiple SR backbones.

</details>


### [190] [A Definition of Catastrophic Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06219)
*Henrik Marklund,Alex Infanger,Benjamin Van Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出"灾难性绩效"(catastrophic performance)的形式化定义，用以在AI目标错配研究中区分"次优"与"灾难性"结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论虽能刻画目标错配如何影响性能，但通常无法区分仅仅次优的表现与灾难性的后果，缺乏对AI错配所致灾难性风险的严格分析工具。

Method: 基于两个参考值进行形式化定义：基线值(baseline value)，即不部署任何智能体时所获得的价值；正交值(orthogonal value)，即与人类偏好无关（按作者所界定意义）的轨迹价值，通常远低于基线值。并通过人类历史活动改善环境状态、随后部署AI智能体加速进展的示例来阐释这两个参考值。

Result: 灾难性绩效被界定为智能体对"人类活动所带来之收益"的撤销/破坏程度，即性能落入基线值与正交值所划定的区间之下。

Conclusion: 该定义为AI错配引发的灾难性风险提供了可严格分析的框架，将绩效评估从"是否次优"推进到"是否灾难性"。

Abstract: A central challenge in AI is specifying objectives that align with human preferences. While existing theoretical results elucidate how misspecification impacts performance, they typically do not distinguish between merely suboptimal and catastrophic outcomes. To enable rigorous analysis of catastrophic risk from AI misalignment, we introduce a formal definition of catastrophic performance. Our definition is based on two reference values. The first, baseline value, captures the value attained if no agent is deployed. The second, orthogonal value, captures the value of a trajectory that is unrelated to human preferences, in a sense we describe. Orthogonal value is typically far worse than baseline value. We interpret these reference values through examples in which past human activity has altered the state of the environment to human benefit. Starting at the resulting state, an AI agent is deployed with the goal of accelerating further progress. Catastrophic performance represents this agent's undoing of benefits afforded by human activity.

</details>


### [191] [HarnessFlow: MCTS-Guided Harness Optimization for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06254)
*Zhi Hong,Qianlong Wang,Hongchen Wei,Aidan Wu,Daohong Ying,Mingze Kong,Bei Liu,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 HarnessFlow 框架，在有限候选评估预算下自动优化完整的可执行 LLM 智能体 harness，利用精炼树与 MCTS 策略平衡探索与利用，由编码智能体基于归档代码、执行反馈与可复用机制库改进候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体性能取决于底层模型与 harness（工具/环境交互的调控框架），但 harness 设计高度依赖人工；自动化设计面临开放搜索空间和端到端评估昂贵的问题，需要在有限评估预算下高效搜索。

Method: 固定模型与任务环境，将候选 harness 及其评估历史组织为精炼树（refinement tree）；用基于 MCTS 的策略在利用与探索间权衡以选择候选；由编码智能体结合归档代码、执行反馈和可复用 harness 机制库对选中 harness 进行精炼。每个基准仅约 15 次精炼尝试。

Result: 在覆盖智能体任务、编程与网页交互的六个基准上，各方法中平均测试得分最高；优化后 harness 平均得分 69.48%，分别较初始 harness 与 Meta-Harness 提升 10.89 与 3.95 个百分点，且单任务平均执行成本低于人工设计的基线。

Conclusion: 结构化 harness 搜索是自动化设计高能力、高效率 LLM 智能体的一条有前景的路径。

Abstract: The performance of large language model (LLM) agents depends on both the underlying model and the harness that governs interaction with tools and the environment. Yet harness design remains largely manual, and automating it requires navigating an open-ended search space through costly end-to-end evaluations. We introduce HarnessFlow, a framework for optimizing complete executable harnesses under a limited candidate-evaluation budget, with the model and task environment held fixed. HarnessFlow organizes candidates and their evaluation histories in a refinement tree, using a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-based policy to balance exploitation and exploration. A coding agent refines selected harnesses using archived code, execution feedback, and a library of reusable harness mechanisms. With 15 refinement attempts per benchmark, HarnessFlow achieves the highest mean test scores among the compared methods on six benchmarks spanning agentic tasks, coding, and web interaction. The optimized harnesses achieve an average score of 69.48%, outperforming the initial harnesses and Meta-Harness by 10.89 and 3.95 percentage points, respectively, while incurring lower average per-task execution costs than hand-engineered baselines. These results suggest that structured harness search offers a promising route toward automating the design of capable and efficient LLM agents.

</details>


### [192] [A Systematic Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Personalized Reasoning of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06259)
*Dohyun Kim,Junyong Lhim,Kwanyong Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MPRBench，一个用于评测多模态大模型（MLLM）多模态个性化推理能力的综合基准，包含12个子任务并支持可解释的错误案例分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们在决策时常需对异构个人信息进行整体性推理，而MLLM在"多模态个性化推理"这一能力上研究不足、缺乏系统评测手段。

Method: 构建MPRBench基准，覆盖广泛任务与真实场景挑战，设计12个子任务，并提供代表性错误案例的可解释分析框架。

Result: 大量实验表明，现有个性化MLLM在个性化推理上仍表现不佳，主要瓶颈在于对个性化概念识别不准确及对无关个人信息敏感。

Conclusion: MPRBench揭示了MLLM个性化推理的关键挑战，期望推动该方向后续研究；代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: People often make decisions by holistically reasoning over heterogeneous forms of personal information. In this paper, we study the relatively underexplored capability of multimodal personalized reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). To this end, we introduce \texttt{MPRBench}, a novel and comprehensive benchmark specifically designed to evaluate personalized reasoning across a wide range of tasks and real-world challenges. \texttt{MPRBench} consists of 12 sub-tasks and additionally supports interpretable analysis of representative error cases. Through extensive experiments, we find that current personalized MLLMs still struggle with personalized reasoning due to several key challenges, including inaccurate recognition of personalized concepts and sensitivity to irrelevant personal information. We hope that MPRBench will stimulate further research on personalized reasoning in MLLMs. Code and data are available at https://github.com/kkdh15/MPRBench

</details>


### [193] [DyPSI: Dynamic Physics Sensing via Joint Field and Sensor-Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06280)
*Yizhou Zhang,Panqi Chen,Lei Cheng,Ting Zhang,Jianlong Li,Jianfeng Shi,Shikai Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DyPSI框架，将动态物理感知表述为在历史观测条件下联合生成未来场与传感器轨迹，并在湍流、海表温度和3D气动算例上显著优于静态与逐帧布局策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演化环境中的物理感知需从稀疏观测重建稠密时空场，并自适应决定后续观测位置。现有重建与传感器布局方法基于静态感知假设：或所有帧共用单一布局，或逐帧独立优化布局，难以生成时间一致的感知轨迹。

Method: 将离散传感器坐标提升为连续Sensor Position Field；以共享的Functional Tucker表示联合编码场与感知配置；在潜空间训练联合扩散模型同时生成未来场与传感轨迹；用高斯过程时间核关联扩散训练注入的扰动，使去噪器偏向时间平滑解；并给出EDM训练目标与序列聚合A-最优性准则的联系及核诱导的离散轨迹变化上界分析。

Result: 在湍流场、GLORYS12海表温度和3D汽车气动三个任务上，DyPSI一致优于静态布局与逐帧布局策略，在严格传感器预算下误差显著降低。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 将未来场生成与传感器轨迹生成统一为条件联合生成问题，可在统一潜空间中实现时间一致的动态感知布局，并在紧预算下取得更高重建精度。

Abstract: Physics sensing in evolving environments requires reconstructing dense spatiotemporal fields from sparse observations while adapting where future observations should be collected. Existing reconstruction and sensor-placement methods have made substantial progress, but they typically rely on static sensing assumptions: they either use a single layout across all frames or optimize placements independently at each frame, which limits their ability to produce temporally coherent sensing trajectories. We introduce DyPSI, a dynamic physics sensing framework that formulates this problem as the joint generation of future fields and sensor trajectories conditioned on historical observations. DyPSI lifts discrete sensor coordinates into continuous Sensor Position Fields, encodes fields and sensing configurations with a shared Functional Tucker representation, and trains a joint diffusion model to generate future fields and sensing trajectories simultaneously in the resulting latent space. A Gaussian-process temporal kernel correlates the perturbation injected during diffusion training, biasing the denoiser toward temporally smooth recoveries, and we further provide an analysis linking the EDM training objective to a sequence-aggregated A-optimality criterion and a kernel-induced bound on discrete trajectory variation. Experiments on turbulent flow, GLORYS12 sea-surface temperature, and 3D car aerodynamics show that DyPSI consistently outperforms static and frame-wise placement strategies, with substantial error reduction under tight sensor budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/mengqi132/DyPSI.

</details>


### [194] [Multi-Resident Data Association in Smart Homes via MDP Modeling and Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06296)
*Xi Chen,Julien Cumin,Fano Ramparany,Dominique Vaufreydaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将多居民环境下的数据关联（Data Association）重构为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）下的序列决策任务，用行为克隆（BC）从专家示范中学习策略，并实例化为轻量Transformer（多标签分类）与微调LLM（条件文本生成）两种架构，在三个数据集上显著超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境传感器可在保护隐私前提下支持智能家居活动识别，但多居民场景下的数据关联（判断匿名传感器事件由哪位居民触发）存在两大痛点：现有方法对静默居民常产生冲突关联，难以维持全局一致性；且将连续物理运动模型套用于离散逻辑行为造成本体论不匹配，导致轨迹严重碎片化。

Method: 将数据关联形式化为MDP中的序列决策问题，并通过行为克隆直接学习专家示范以规避复杂的奖励工程。策略实例化两种互补架构：(1) 轻量Transformer，在事件历史序列上进行多标签分类；(2) 微调LLM，将问题重构为条件文本生成以利用预训练语义知识。

Result: 在三个多样化数据集上的大量实验表明，方法显著优于SOTA基线，尤其在并发多居民事件消解方面表现突出；基于LLM的策略在基准设置下展现出初步的跨环境迁移潜力，但其真实部署中的鲁棒性仍需进一步验证。

Conclusion: 以MDP+行为克隆重构数据关联可有效缓解全局一致性与轨迹碎片化问题，Transformer与LLM两种策略均具竞争力，LLM策略的跨环境泛化值得进一步研究，实际部署鲁棒性有待验证。

Abstract: Ambient sensors offer a privacy-preserving solution for activity recognition in smart homes, yet they face a critical challenge in multi-resident environments known as Data Association, which aims to identify the resident triggering a specific anonymous sensor event. Existing approaches fundamentally fail to maintain global consistency by often yielding conflicting associations for silent residents. Additionally, these methods frequently suffer from severe trajectory fragmentation caused by the ontological mismatch of applying continuous physical motion models to discrete logical behaviors. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework that reformulates data association as a sequential decision-making task within a Markov Decision Process (MDP). We solve the defined MDP via Behavior Cloning (BC) to avoid complex reward engineering by directly learning from expert demonstrations. We instantiate the policy using two complementary architectures, including a lightweight Transformer that treats association as a multi-label classification task over linearized event histories, and a fine-tuned Large Language Model (LLM) that reformulates the problem as conditional text generation to leverage pre-trained semantic knowledge. Extensive evaluations on three diverse datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, particularly in resolving concurrent multi-resident events. Furthermore, the LLM-based policy shows preliminary cross-environment transfer potential under benchmark settings, while its robustness in real deployments still requires further validation.

</details>


### [195] [STEK: Structural-Terrain-Enhanced Koopman-Inspired Cross-Channel Regularization for Retrospective Battery Multi-Target Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06299)
*Jing Wang,Shiyuan Piao,Fugee Tsung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STEK：融合电芯级寿命地形与端点充放电曲线的结构地形增强框架，用于异构电芯的回溯式多目标估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构电芯的回溯表征困难：循环记录在化学体系、协议、电芯形态、容量与工况上差异显著；单圈端点曲线只反映局部状态，缺乏跨轨迹的结构演化信息。

Method: 将电芯级寿命(dQ/dV)地形与端点结构代理经质量代理条件门控融合；用有界FiLM与有效性门控残差头精化端点曲线表征并保留曲线预测通路；以电池类型及可选材料文本表征条件化身份中心的低秩线性映射，构造Kooman启发的跨通道一致性目标（不建模时序、不做rollout）。

Result: 在固定七输出MIX-battery协议下，完整回溯设置相对仅端点学习基线在多种时序骨干上聚合性能提升（含额外归档上下文与建模设计双重贡献）；在辅助输入匹配时优于直接拼接与质量无关FiLM；描述子移除测试显示可平滑退化为端点通路；直接可恢复的容量汇总仍宜用确定性规则。

Conclusion: 方法定位为回溯归档协调而非仅凭第t圈在线估计，本质是借助归档上下文实现异构电池档案的统一表征。

Abstract: Retrospective battery characterization across heterogeneous cells is challenging because cycling records vary substantially with chemistry, protocol, cell format, capacity, and operating conditions. An endpoint charge--discharge curve captures the local condition of an evaluated cycle, while a cell-level lifecycle terrain summarizes structural evolution across the archived trajectory. We propose STEK, a structural-terrain-enhanced framework for retrospective battery multi-target estimation. STEK combines a cell-level lifecycle (dQ/dV) terrain with an endpoint-derived structural proxy through quality-proxy-conditioned gating. Bounded FiLM and a validity-gated residual head refine the endpoint-curve representation while preserving a curve-based prediction pathway. Battery-type and optional material-text representations further condition an identity-centered low-rank linear map, providing a Koopman-inspired cross-channel consistency objective without modeling temporal dynamics or performing rollout. Under the fixed seven-output MIX-battery protocol, the full retrospective setting improves aggregate performance over endpoint-only learned baselines across multiple temporal backbones; this comparison reflects both the additional archived context and the modeling design. Under matched auxiliary inputs, STEK outperforms direct concatenation and quality-unaware FiLM, while descriptor-removal tests show graceful fallback to the endpoint pathway. Deterministic rules remain preferable for directly recoverable capacity summaries. The method targets retrospective archive harmonization rather than online estimation from cycle t alone.

</details>


### [196] [EnzymeRSI: A dual-loop recursive self-improvement system for enzyme design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06321)
*Genoria AI Team,Yuejie Hou,Lixiang Yang,Yisi Liu,Weiting Tang,Yueyuxiao Yang,Meng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnzymeRSI 是一个双环递归自我改进（RSI）系统：外环在不改变模型权重的前提下进化"研究框架资产"（harness assets），内环在受控条件下开发预测与选择算法，并以云实验室实现"干—湿"闭环。结果表明，跳出固定方法的优化盲区可显著提升酶工程预测与实验决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有酶工程改进多局限于"在固定模型与固定工作流内优化候选筛选"，一旦瓶颈来自方法不匹配或工作流失效，该路径难以突破。作者希望把"研究流程的组织方式"与"算法开发"统一视为单一可证伪的干湿闭环，从而超越固定方法的盲区。

Method: 提出 EnzymeRSI 双环系统：外环进化 harness 资产（不更新模型权重）；内环在受控条件下迭代开发预测与选择算法；并规格化云实验室系统以质量受控反馈闭合物理环路。通过迭代比较更新预测策略；在 ProteinGym、ThermoMPNN 自改进、TadA-Bench 上评估，并开展三项前瞻性湿实验验证实验决策能力。

Result: 外环在 ProteinGym 五个 assay 上将开发集平均 Spearman 相关提高 0.044，CD19 达 0.335、OPSD（视紫红质）达 0.683；两个第一名中仅 CD19 由外环自身产出。ThermoMPNN 自改进中内环 PCC 达 0.849；TadA-Bench 上 1.13 h 内 Spearman 达 0.307。湿实验：荧光素酶 RLuc 中未预先指定的 32 个候选有 10 个活性≥野生型 2 倍；胞苷脱氨酶 A3A 推荐变体将 5mC 转化率提升至 90.2%；KOD-E DNA 聚合酶经进化路径选择后对修饰底物的催化效率达亲本的 31.1 倍。

Conclusion: 将研究流程的组织与算法开发整合为统一、可证伪的干湿闭环，可有效突破固定方法的性能盲区，为自主化酶工程提供一种"进化式"范式。

Abstract: Improvement in enzyme engineering is usually pursued by optimising which candidates to assay within a fixed model and workflow; when the bottleneck arises from a mismatched method or a failing workflow, that route is difficult to escape. We present EnzymeRSI, a dual-loop system for recursive self-improvement (RSI). The outer loop evolves harness assets without changing model weights, the inner loop develops prediction and selection algorithms under controlled conditions, and a cloud laboratory system is specified to close the physical loop with quality-controlled feedback. By updating its prediction strategy through iterative comparison, the outer loop raised the mean development-set Spearman correlation on ProteinGym by 0.044 across five assays, reaching 0.335 on CD19 and 0.683 on OPSD (rhodopsin); of the two first places, only CD19 was produced by the outer loop itself. In the self-improvement of the ThermoMPNN algorithm the inner loop reached a PCC of 0.849, and on TadA-Bench it reached a Spearman correlation of 0.307 in 1.13 h. In three prospective wet-lab tests of the system's experimental decision-making, 10 of the 32 candidates that had not been pre-specified for the luciferase RLuc showed at least twice the wild-type activity; a recommended variant of the cytidine deaminase A3A raised 5mC conversion to 90.2%; and path selection in evolution raised the catalytic efficiency of the DNA polymerase KOD-E on a modified substrate to 31.1 times that of the parent. By treating the organisation of the research workflow and the development of algorithms as a single falsifiable dry-to-wet loop, this work offers an evolutionary paradigm for moving beyond the blind spots of a fixed method and for autonomous enzyme engineering.

</details>


### [197] [Corralling AI Risks with Grassroots Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06374)
*Ehud Shapiro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出以"草根契约"（grassroots contracts，Proudhon 二百年理念的数字化身）来约束全球数字平台面临的三大 AI 风险：AI 控制平台、不可问责的 AI 智能体、无归属的深度伪造，并给出两个可运行的智能手机应用原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 迎来拐点，风险激增。全球平台的控制权（算法、成员资格、内容）正让渡给 AI，导致多数人暴露于 AI 控制之下；匿名 AI 智能体与人共处平台却无问责；深度伪造内容无法辨识、生产者逃避责任。现有治理机制缺位，需要自下而上的约束手段。

Method: 借鉴 Pierre-Joseph Proudhon 的契约思想，构造"草根契约"这一数字化机制并落地两个实现：(1) 面向社会图谱的草根契约，实现可问责的 AI 智能体；(2) 面向社交网络的草根契约，实现数字来源（digital provenance）追溯。两者均以智能手机应用的形式实现。

Result: 给出两个可运行的智能手机应用，演示草根契约如何分别应对三大风险：通过社会图谱契约约束 AI 对平台的控制并使 AI 智能体可问责；通过数字来源机制使深度伪造可追溯归属。

Conclusion: 草根契约可作为自下而上的补充治理路径，在平台层面缓解 AI 控制、匿名智能体与深度伪造三类风险，并具备可工程化的原型验证。

Abstract: AI's inflection point is a fountain of alarming risks. Digital platforms in particular are vulnerable to three major AI risks, as those in control of global platforms cede control to AI, of the algorithms, of membership and of content: (1) AI control of global platforms, social and economic, used by the majority of humanity, resulting in the majority of humanity becoming vulnerable to AI control. (2) Unaccountable AI agents operating anonymously on social and economic digital platforms, side-by-side with humans, possibly masquerading as humans, serving unknown masters with unknown goals, operating under unknown restraints without accountability or culpability. (3) Unattributed deepfakes generated by anonymous AI, with people unable to distinguish them from genuine content and with their producers evading accountability and culpability.
  Here, we propose to corral these AI risks with grassroots contracts, a digital incarnation of a two-centuries old notion of Pierre-Joseph Proudhon. We present grassroots contracts for a social graph with accountable AI agents and for a social network with digital provenance, and working smartphone apps that implement the two and show how they corral these major AI risks: (1) AI control of global platforms. (2) Unaccountable AI agents. (3) Unattributed deepfakes.

</details>


### [198] [Does Context Compression Preserve Refusal Alignment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06380)
*Anthony Nguyen,Wenjun Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 上下文压缩虽能高保真重建语义，却系统性地削弱指令微调模型的拒绝行为；机理在于压缩衰减了解码器学到的"拒绝方向"激活，而推理期的Memory Steering干预可在压缩表征空间中恢复拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文压缩以语义保真（重建质量）为核心评价指标，但"语义保持是否足以维持下游行为（如拒绝对齐）"是未解问题。作者旨在检验语义保真与行为保真之间是否存在缺口。

Method: 在多种模型族与压缩架构上开展实证比较，比较不同压缩下的拒绝行为与重建保真度；通过机制可解释性分析（激活层面）定位拒绝方向被衰减的现象；提出Memory Steering——一种无需重训练、完全在压缩表征空间运行的轻量推理期干预方法。

Result: 基于编码器的压缩在重建保真度高的情况下仍系统性降低拒绝率，且该效应跨模型族与压缩架构稳定存在；机制分析显示压缩沿解码器学到的拒绝方向衰减激活；Memory Steering可将拒绝率恢复至接近基线水平。

Conclusion: 语义保持并不保证压缩下的行为保持；压缩感知系统需显式保留与对齐相关的特征，而非仅优化语义重建。推理期干预是无需重训练的低成本补救途径。

Abstract: Context compression reduces inference cost by encoding inputs into compact representations while preserving semantic content. An open question is whether semantic preservation alone is sufficient to maintain downstream behaviours such as refusal alignment. We investigate this question and find that encoder-based compression systematically weakens refusal behaviour in instruction-tuned language models, despite high reconstruction fidelity. This effect persists across model families and compression architectures. Mechanistic analysis shows that compression attenuates activation along the decoder's learned refusal direction. We further explore Memory Steering, a lightweight inference-time intervention that restores refusal rates to near-baseline levels without retraining and operates entirely in compressed representation space. These results demonstrate that semantic preservation does not guarantee behavioural preservation under compression, highlighting the need to explicitly preserve alignment-relevant features in compression-aware systems.

</details>


### [199] [LegalEvolver: Agentic Legal Judgment Prediction via Rubric-Guided Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06381)
*Xinyu Yang,Jiejun Tan,Chenlong Deng,Chenghao Zhang,Tongyu Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 LegalEvolver，一个面向法律判决预测（LJP）检索的查询优化框架：先用启发式方法优化查询作为种子，再把 rollout 经验总结为评分规则（rubrics），以多轮迭代的"规则优化 + 规则引导 RL"实现自我进化，突破初始能力上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律检索智能体通常直接依据用户初始请求构造查询，忽视潜在法律争点与法律上显著的细节，导致检索质量受限，进而制约 LJP 多步推理表现。

Method: LegalEvolver：1）以启发式方式优化查询作为高质量种子，提升搜索轨迹采样效率（避免仅依赖无引导高温 rollout 的低效与能力天花板）；2）将 rollout 经验总结为 rubrics，用于引导多轮迭代自我进化；3）交替进行 rubric 优化与 rubric 引导的强化学习训练。

Result: 多基准端到端实验显示，查询优化对 LJP 有效；与"直接 SFT 后接 RL"相比，rubric 引导更有效；进化式 rubric 优于固定 rubric。

Conclusion: 面向 LJP 的检索应重视查询的可法信息性；种子化启发式查询与可自进化的 rubric 引导 RL 相结合，可使模型超越初始性能上限，且自我进化机制带来额外收益。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents develop, they have been increasingly applied in the legal domain. Legal Judgment Prediction~(LJP) requires models to conduct multi-step reasoning and external legal evidence retrieval. Existing legal search agents' performance is often constrained by the quality of retrieval queries. They often formulate queries directly from the initial user request, overlooking latent legal issues and legally salient details. To address this issue, we propose \emph{LegalEvolver}, a query optimization framework specifically designed to generate legally informative queries for LJP-oriented retrieval. Sampling high-quality optimized queries and their corresponding search trajectories is challenging, as relying solely on unguided high-temperature rollouts is both inefficient and constrained by the model's initial capabilities. We therefore heuristically optimize queries as seeds, improving the efficiency of trajectory generation. Furthermore, we summarize rollout experience into rubrics and use them to guide multi-round iterative self-evolution. Iteratively performing rubric optimization and rubric-guided RL enables the model to surpass its initial performance ceiling. End-to-end performance on extensive benchmarks demonstrates the effectiveness of query optimization on LJP. Comparisons with direct SFT followed by RL demonstrate the effectiveness of rubric guidance. Evolving rubrics further outperform fixed rubrics, confirming the benefits of self-evolution.

</details>


### [200] [MA-Mesh: Resilient Multi-Agent Collaboration via Role-based Self-Healing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06389)
*JunYong Lee,Woo Kyung Kim,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Multi-Agent Mesh (MA-Mesh) 框架，在协作模式之上构建基于角色的自愈层，通过生成韧性感知的系统规范并综合为可执行多智能体策略代码，结合跨智能体反思与基于角色的故障转移两种自愈机制，在软件开发、工具使用、科学推理等任务上显著提升任务性能与不确定性下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在动态不确定环境中需要协调与适应机制，但现有系统很少显式建模韧性，多依赖临时性的输出修正，缺乏对鲁棒性的系统性、运行时管理能力。

Method: MA-Mesh 在既有协作模式之上构建基于角色的自愈层：生成韧性感知的系统规范并综合为可执行的多智能体策略代码；引入两种自愈机制——跨智能体反思（智能体既评估自身结果也评估同伴表现，从而调整行为并集体精炼策略）与基于角色的故障转移（子任务失败或智能体表现不佳时动态重分配角色、相互替代以保证任务连续性）。

Result: 在软件开发、工具使用、科学推理等多样化任务上的实验表明，MA-Mesh 在任务性能与系统不确定性下的鲁棒性方面均显著优于现有多智能体系统。

Conclusion: 将韧性显式建模并落到可执行的运行时自愈机制，是提升多智能体系统在动态不确定环境中性能与鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Effective collaboration in multi-agent systems requires mechanisms for coordination and adaptability in dynamic and uncertain environments. However, existing multi-agent systems rarely model resilience explicitly, instead relying on ad-hoc output correction that limits systematic, runtime management of robustness. In this work, we propose Multi-Agent Mesh (MA-Mesh), a framework that constructs a role-based self-healing layer on top of a collaboration pattern. Specifically, MA-Mesh generates resilience-aware system specifications and synthesizes them into executable multi-agent policy code. To enhance resilience, we introduce two self-healing mechanisms: Cross-agent reflection, a mechanism that allows agents to evaluate not only their own outcomes but also the performance of their peers, enabling informed behavioral adjustments and collective policy refinement; Role-based failover, a strategy which dynamically reassigns roles and enables agents to substitute for one another when sub-tasks fail or agents underperform, ensuring continuity of task execution. Experimental results across diverse tasks (e.g., software development, tool use, scientific reasoning) demonstrate that MA-Mesh significantly outperforms existing multi-agent systems in both task performance and robustness under system uncertainty.

</details>


### [201] [GIST: Gibbs-Targeted Hierarchical Data Selection for Offline RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06395)
*Xuliang Huang,Tianxiang Zhao,Chengwei Qin,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GIST，基于KL正则RLVR的Gibbs最优策略与双向KL分解，在响应级与token级做两层数据选择，仅监督失配所在token，在多个基准上提升离线RLVR准确率并减少监督token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RLVR在固定采样响应池上训练，通常对每个响应的每个token都监督，但响应与token的信息量并不相等，亟需明确"该监督哪些数据"。

Method: 以KL正则RLVR的Gibbs最优策略为离线训练目标，用双向KL衡量策略与目标的失配。在参考策略处该失配可精确分解：正确响应主要贡献前向项，集中于其低概率token；错误响应主要贡献反向项，集中于策略常生成响应及其高概率token。据此提出GIST两级选择：用采样温度在策略上选正确响应、在策略众数附近选错误响应；用初始策略token概率仅监督失配所在token。

Result: 在两种离线训练目标下，GIST使三个模型在GSM8K、MATH-500、MBPP上的平均准确率均提升；Qwen3-0.6B/GSM8K受控对比中优于七个同候选池的选择基线，且比全候选训练少监督66.6%的token。

Conclusion: 响应与token的信息量不均，基于Gibbs最优目标的双向KL分解可指导训练前可得信号的数据选择，实现更高效且更准确的离线RLVR。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) trains on a fixed pool of sampled responses and typically supervises every token of every response, although responses and tokens are not equally informative. We study which ones to supervise through the Gibbs-optimal policy of KL-regularized RLVR, which offline training targets, and measure the mismatch between a policy and this target with a bidirectional KL divergence. At the reference policy, this mismatch factorizes exactly and separates the two outcomes: correct responses contribute mainly through the forward term, concentrated on their low-probability tokens, and incorrect responses through the reverse term, concentrated on the responses the policy generates often and on their high-probability tokens. Based on this analysis, we propose GIST (Gibbs-Targeted Hierarchical Data Selection), a two-level selection method that approximates these principles with signals available before training. GIST uses the sampling temperature to select correct responses on-policy and incorrect responses near the policy's mode, and it uses the initial policy's token probabilities to supervise only the tokens where the mismatch lies. Applied to two offline training objectives, GIST raises the average accuracy over GSM8K, MATH-500, and MBPP for all three models under both objectives. In a controlled comparison on Qwen3-0.6B/GSM8K, it outperforms seven selection baselines that choose from the same candidates while supervising 66.6% fewer tokens than training on all candidates of the same prompts.

</details>


### [202] [D3D: Decoupled Diffusion Drafting for Asynchronous Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06425)
*Baoyou Chen,Ruiqiao Mei,Guancong Lin,Tao Song,Weihao Yuan,Qun Liu,Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: D3D 是一种独立的扩散式草稿模型，去除对目标模型隐状态的轮间依赖，从而实现异步投机解码，在七个基准上取得约 5–5.7 倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 target-conditioned 扩散草稿器需等待目标模型更新后的隐状态，导致"起草—验证"必须串行，即使单块可并行生成，整体加速仍受限。

Method: 提出 D3D：以块级去噪结合辅助自回归监督，使草稿模型在不依赖目标隐状态下获得长可接受前缀；利用其独立性，在一次打包前向中为后继块起草，实现异步投机解码；并按各层对接受长度的影响逐步剪枝（36 层中去掉 5 层）。

Result: 以 Qwen3-8B 为目标时，跨三个基准的平均接受长度保留超过 99%；七个基准上，Qwen3-8B 与 Qwen3-32B 目标平均加速分别为 5.00× 和 5.71×（DFlash 为 4.03× 和 3.67×）；GSM8K + Qwen3-32B 达 7.48×。

Conclusion: 摆脱目标隐状态依赖并结合层剪枝，可有效支撑异步投机解码，显著提升解码吞吐，且剪枝代价极低。

Abstract: Target-conditioned diffusion drafters predict candidate tokens in parallel, yet each new block must wait for updated target-model hidden states. This forces drafting and verification to run sequentially, limiting speedup even though each block is generated in one pass. We introduce D3D, a standalone diffusion drafter that removes this inter-round dependency. D3D combines blockwise denoising with auxiliary autoregressive supervision to achieve long accepted prefixes without target hidden states. This independence lets D3D draft successor blocks during verification in one packed pass, enabling asynchronous speculative decoding. We progressively prune layers according to their effect on acceptance length to reduce the cost of packed drafting. Removing five of 36 layers retains over 99\% of mean acceptance length across three benchmarks with Qwen3-8B as the target. Across seven benchmarks, D3D averages 5.00$\times$ and 5.71$\times$ decoding speedup with Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B targets, compared with 4.03$\times$ and 3.67$\times$ for DFlash. On GSM8K with Qwen3-32B, speedup reaches 7.48$\times$. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/fudan-generative-vision/D3D.

</details>


### [203] [EviSeek: An Evidence-Centric Agentic Framework for Embodied Active Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06434)
*Jintao Li,Zhiyong Cao,Baihua Xiao,Yu Bai,Börje F. Karlsson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EviSeek：免训练框架，将问题派生的证据需求显式化为采集目标，指导主动具身问答中的动作选择与证据保留，在ESI-Bench上显著提升准确率并降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动具身问答（Active Embodied QA）需通过视角变化与物理交互获取证据，核心难题是避免"无导向交互"——即采取看似合理但未明确其应揭示何种证据的动作。仅凭动作计划与累积历史，交互的信息性目的仍是隐式的，导致行动缺乏针对性。

Method: 提出免训练框架 EviSeek，把由问题推导出的"证据需求"显式化为采集目标，将基于观测条件的动作与选择性保留的证据，同智能体所寻求的信息直接关联，从而围绕"为回答问题需要了解什么"来组织动作选择与证据保留。

Result: 在3,436道ESI-Bench问题上，相较单模型智能体，Qwen3-VL-32B准确率提升7.1个百分点，Gemini 3.1 Pro提升3.7个百分点；同时视觉支持加权准确率提升，平均token用量下降。

Conclusion: 研究支持将交互的信息性目的显式化，以"智能体需学到什么"为中心组织动作选择与证据保留，可有效提升主动具身问答的性能与效率。

Abstract: Active embodied question answering requires agents to acquire evidence through viewpoint changes and physical interaction. The central challenge is to avoid undirected interaction: taking plausible actions without specifying what evidence they should reveal for answering the question. Action plans and accumulated histories alone can leave the informational purpose of an interaction implicit. We introduce EviSeek, a training-free framework that makes question-derived evidence demands explicit acquisition objectives, linking observation-conditioned actions and selectively retained evidence to the information the agent seeks. On 3,436 ESI-Bench questions, EviSeek improves accuracy over the Single-model agent by 7.1 percentage points with Qwen3-VL-32B and 3.7 points with Gemini 3.1 Pro, while improving visual-support-weighted accuracy and reducing mean token usage. These findings support making the informational purpose of interaction explicit, with action selection and evidence retention organized around what the agent needs to learn to answer the question.

</details>


### [204] [SUMONE: A Simple and Efficient Summarizer for Plug-and-Play Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06445)
*Jizhe Zhang,Ang Li,Chenheng Zhang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SumOne，一个独立训练的摘要器，通过掩码重建任务无关奖励（结合 GRPO）优化，实现即插即用的上下文压缩，在长程任务中大幅降低推理成本并保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程语言模型智能体的交互历史不断累积，上下文压缩对高效执行至关重要。现有学习式压缩方法依赖下游任务结果或求解器行为，将训练与特定任务或智能体耦合，泛化性差。

Method: SumOne 通过掩码重建训练摘要器：给定已过期历史的摘要，冻结的重建器尝试恢复原始轨迹中被随机掩码的部分；重建质量结合长度惩罚构成任务无关奖励，用 GRPO 优化摘要器，从原始轨迹直接获得自监督信号，无需参考摘要或下游任务执行。推理时仅保留轻量摘要器，实现即插即用压缩。

Result: 在长程搜索与软件工程任务上系统评估：相比使用完整上下文，SumOne 在 DeepSearchQA 上降低约 78%、在 SWE-bench Verified 上降低约 75% 的估计推理成本，绝对性能下降约 0.04。

Conclusion: SumOne 提供了一种任务无关、无需参考摘要的摘要器训练范式，显著降低推理成本且性能损失微小；同一检查点可跨下游模型与智能体框架迁移，无需额外微调。

Abstract: Long-horizon language-model agents accumulate increasingly large interaction histories, making context compaction essential for efficient execution. Existing learned compaction methods often rely on downstream task outcomes or solver behavior, coupling training to specific tasks or agents.
