We introduce SumOne, a standalone summarizer trained to preserve information in agent histories through masked reconstruction. Given a summary of the expired history, a frozen reconstructor recovers randomly masked portions of the original trajectory; reconstruction quality, combined with a length penalty, provides a task-agnostic reward for optimizing the summarizer with GRPO. This yields self-supervision directly from raw trajectories, without reference summaries or downstream task execution. At inference time, only the lightweight summarizer is retained, enabling plug-and-play context compression for otherwise unchanged agents. We conduct a systematic evaluation of SumOne in long-horizon search and software-engineering tasks. SumOne reduces estimated inference cost by approximately 78\% on DeepSearchQA and 75\% on SWE-bench Verified relative to operating with full context, with absolute decreases of 0.04. The same checkpoint also transfers across downstream models and agent harnesses without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [205] [AmbiHTMLBench: A Benchmark for HTML-based Visually Rich Document parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06449)
*Boyu Niu,Yingjie Xiong,Shurui Li,Linhan Pang,Yuntong Zhang,Han Wang,Hao Wu,Kai Zuo,Xu Tang,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出以HTML替代Markdown作为文档解析的输出表示，并构建AmbiHTMLBench基准（1,115页，含结构化标注），通过客观协议（表格保真、页面级文本误差、二维版面一致性）与参考锚定的主观协议（LLM评判对齐人类偏好）双轨评测十种多模态系统，最佳客观分为0.8092，且主客观胜者不一致；任务微调的小参数量专家模型表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文档解析以Markdown为输出表示，虽适合线性文本，却难以完整保留细粒度空间结构、表格几何、视觉层级与渲染语义。对于异构、版面敏感的文档，其表示还需保持可编辑、可审查、可复用，因此需要论证HTML的优越性并提供系统化评测手段。

Method: 构建AmbiHTMLBench基准：输入真实文档，要求模型输出可执行的HTML，保留内容、组织与视觉外观；含1,115个多样页面及结构化标注。评测采用两套协议：（1）客观协议，对齐真值与预测的渲染块，度量表格保真度、页面级文本误差与二维版面一致性；（2）参考锚定协议，用与人类偏好对齐的LLM评判者度量感知保真度。此外对小型专家模型进行任务特定微调。

Result: 在十种主流多模态系统上，最佳客观得分为0.8092，而主观评测胜者与之不同，说明自动分解度量与人类感知保真度捕捉的是互补属性；任务特定微调的小参数量专家模型在文档到HTML重建任务上取得良好性能。

Conclusion: AmbiHTMLBench可作为诊断性测试平台，推动构建更智能、逻辑更合理的文档解析系统；HTML表示更适合异构与版面敏感文档的重建与复用。

Abstract: Traditional document parsing systems use Markdown as their output representation, which is effective for linear text but does not fully preserve fine-grained spatial structure, table geometry, visual hierarchy, or rendering semantics. We show that HTML is advantageous for heterogeneous and layout-sensitive documents whose representations must remain editable, inspectable, and reusable, and introduce AmbiHTMLBench to systematically study this setting. In this benchmark, given a real-world document, a model must recover executable HTML that preserves its content, organization, and visual appearance. The benchmark contains 1,115 diverse pages with structured annotations and evaluates predictions in two different ways: The objective protocol aligns ground-truth and predicted rendered blocks and measures table fidelity, page-level text error, and a two-dimensional layout consistency; a complementary reference-anchored protocol measures perceived fidelity by aligning LLM judges with human preferences. Across ten primary multimodal systems, the best objective score is 0.8092, while the subjective winner is different, demonstrating that automatic decomposition and human-perceived fidelity capture complementary properties. We further fine-tune a small-parameter expert model and find that task-specific adaptation yields good performance on document-to-HTML reconstruction task. These results position AmbiHTMLBench as a diagnostic testbed for building smarter and more logical document parsing systems.

</details>


### [206] [FPTR: Feasibility-Preserving Transactional Refinement for Deadline-Constrained Joint Beam and Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06491)
*Minghao Kong,Zirong Liu,Jianhao Wu,Muyao Chen,Jianxin Wu,Rongjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可行性保持事务式精炼（FPTR）框架，确保无线调度器在任何截止时刻都能返回可行解，并用事务式候选替换机制在富余计算时间内安全提升目标值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短间隔无线调度必须在每个执行截止时刻前输出完整可行的波束与资源分配，迟到或无效输出在本控制周期内无任何效用；而延长搜索虽可能提升流量，却可能错过截止或仅留下不可行中间解。

Method: FPTR始终维护一个已通过验证的可行解（incumbent）。每个精炼阶段构建独立候选解，不影响incumbent；仅当候选解完整、在阶段截止前通过可行性校验且严格改进目标时才替换incumbent，否则丢弃。四个精炼阶段为：感知缓冲的资源选择、兼容组（CG）用户共享、基于剩余需求的波束掩码重建、两资源联合重分配。

Result: 在150个固定合成实例上，完整流水线（Full）相对轻量beam-first参考平均提升流量3.83%，相对其Base构造器提升1.79%；在输入匹配的独立测试集上，Full在100 ms后输出零效用的条件下领先六种独立搜索基线（ALNS、禁忌搜索、GA、模拟退火、迭代局部搜索、GRASP）6.68–8.35%。内部测试集全部750次Full运行均返回可行分配，且均未超过100 ms。

Conclusion: 将强制输出构造与可选精炼分离，使富余计算时间可改善分配而不危及incumbent；开源实现支持独立复现。

Abstract: Short-interval wireless schedulers must return a complete, feasible beam-and-resource allocation before each execution deadline, and a late or invalid output gives no utility in the current control cycle. Searching longer can increase transmitted traffic, but it may also miss the deadline or leave only an infeasible intermediate solution. We propose Feasibility-Preserving Transactional Refinement (FPTR), which always keeps a validated feasible allocation, the incumbent. Each refinement stage builds a separate candidate and leaves the incumbent unchanged. A candidate replaces the incumbent only if it is complete, passes the feasibility validator before its stage cutoff, and strictly improves the objective; otherwise it is discarded. Four refinement stages use this rule: buffer-aware resource selection, compatibility-group (CG) user sharing, beam-mask rebuilding from remaining demand, and joint reassignment of two resources. On 150 fixed synthetic instances, the full pipeline (Full) raises traffic by 3.83% on average over a lightweight beam-first reference and by 1.79% over its Base constructor. In a separate suite with matched inputs, Full exceeds six independent search baselines (ALNS, tabu search, GA, simulated annealing, iterated local search, and GRASP) by 6.68-8.35%, where outputs later than 100 ms receive zero utility. All 750 Full runs in the internal suite returned feasible allocations, and none exceeded 100 ms. Separating mandatory output construction from optional refinement lets spare computation time improve the allocation without endangering the incumbent. The open-source implementation supports independent reproduction.

</details>


### [207] [Tools Need Not Speak in Text: Latent Context Compression for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06500)
*Yuetong Liu,Zhehao Zhou,Yitong Zhang,Haorui Chen,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Coca-Cola，将工具观测压缩为连续潜变量token嵌入智能体输入空间，并用动作保持训练使压缩后与完整观测下的下一步动作分布对齐，从而大幅降低token消耗与运行时且提升任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通常将原始工具输出直接追加到上下文，多步交互中冗长嘈杂的观测带来显著上下文开销并稀释对任务相关信息的注意力；现有token剪枝与摘要方法受限于离散文本表示，压缩率难以提升。作者质疑"智能体必须以显式文本token形式消费工具观测"这一固有假设。

Method: Coca-Cola将工具观测压缩为连续潜变量token，并嵌入智能体的输入嵌入空间，而非以文本形式拼接。同时提出Action-Preserving Training（动作保持训练），对齐完整观测与压缩观测下智能体的下一步动作分布，使压缩模型学到保留决策关键信息的"动作充分"潜变量token。

Result: 在四个QA基准和两个模型族上的网页搜索智能体实验中，Coca-Cola将token消耗降低70.01%–89.24%，端到端运行时缩短24.45%–55.97%，同时任务性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，以连续潜变量空间压缩智能体上下文是可行且有前景的方向；同时说明显式文本并非智能体消费工具观测的必要形式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents typically append raw tool outputs to their context. Over multi-step interactions, long and noisy observations incur substantial context overhead and dilute the agent's attention to task-relevant information. Existing token pruning and summarization methods remain constrained by discrete text representations, limiting the achievable compression ratios.
  In this work, we challenge the prevailing assumption that agents must consume tool observations as explicit text tokens. To this end, we propose Coca-Cola, a continuous latent context compression model for LLM agents. Coca-Cola compresses tool observations into latent tokens and embeds them into the input embedding space of the agent. We further introduce Action-Preserving Training, which aligns the agent's next-action distributions under full and compressed observations. This trains the compression model to learn action-sufficient latent tokens that retain decision-critical information. Experiments with web-search agents on four QA benchmarks and two model families show that Coca-Cola reduces the agent's token consumption by 70.01%-89.24% and end-to-end runtime by 24.45%-55.97%, while consistently improving task performance. These results suggest that Coca-Cola offers a promising approach for context compression in agents. Our code is available at https://github.com/THU-Agent/Coca-cola.

</details>


### [208] [ElecLoad: Adaptive Electricity Load Forecasting Guided by Multiple Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06508)
*Huajian Zhang,Mingyue Cheng,Xingpeng Gao,Xiaoyu Tao,Zhiding Liu,Jiqian Yang,Zhi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ElecLoad——一种上下文感知的智能体框架，利用多事件及其交互效应自适应组织电力负荷预测过程，在多数评估场景达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来电力需求受Web通知、天气预警、事件日程等多事件复杂交互影响，其效应随时间变化且可能相互强化或抵消，导致负荷难以准确预测。现有方法虽能将上下文作为额外输入，但如何利用多事件效应及其交互来指导预测过程本身仍待探索。

Method: ElecLoad为多事件电力负荷预测的可适应智能体：训练其将历史负荷与上下文结合，分析每个事件与目标负荷的相关性、其效应的作用时机及事件间潜在交互；据此自适应选择直接预测整个预测时域，或按事件效应变化将时域分段。分段时，子智能体独立生成各段初始预测，主智能体审阅后给出反馈，子智能体据此进行选择性修正。

Result: 在多个电力负荷数据集上的实验表明，ElecLoad在大多数评估场景中取得当前最优（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模多事件相关性、效应时机及事件间交互，并以智能体间的分区分段与反馈修正机制自适应组织预测流程，可有效提升多事件情境下的负荷预测精度。

Abstract: Accurate electricity load forecasting provides critical decision support for energy procurement, generation scheduling, and supply-demand balancing, contributing to the economic efficiency and operational reliability of power systems. However, future electricity demand can be affected by the complex interplay of multiple events described in Web notices, weather alerts, and event schedules. Their effects vary over time and can reinforce or offset one another, making electricity load difficult to forecast accurately. While existing methods can incorporate context as additional input, how to use the effects of multiple events and their interactions to guide the load forecasting process itself remains to be explored. To address this gap, we propose ElecLoad, a context-aware agent for multi-event electricity load forecasting that adaptively organizes the forecasting process. Specifically, we train ElecLoad to combine historical load with context to analyze each event's relevance to the target load, the timing of its effects, and potential interactions among events. Guided by this analysis, ElecLoad adaptively chooses to forecast the entire forecast horizon directly or to partition it into segments according to changes in event effects. When the horizon is partitioned, sub-agents independently generate initial forecasts for each segment. The main agent then reviews these forecasts, and the sub-agents perform selective refinement guided by its feedback. Experiments on multiple electricity load datasets show that ElecLoad achieves state-of-the-art performance in most evaluation scenarios. Code is available at https://github.com/zhjai/ElecLoad.

</details>


### [209] [Using Experience Twice: Dynamic Process Rubrics for Privileged Token-Level Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06543)
*Shumao Wu,Kongcheng Zhang,Jianqiang Huang,Baisheng Lai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Online Rubric Self-Distillation (ORSD)，用自生成的自然语言过程评分细则（rubric）作为特权信息，将轨迹级GRPO优势与token级过程信用通过加性优势塑形结合，实现细粒度信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体轨迹中同时包含成功决策与失败尝试，但稀疏的轨迹级奖励难以识别哪些关键决策应被强化或抑制，缺乏细粒度的过程监督信号。

Method: 观察到同组on-policy轨迹间的差异可蒸馏为自然语言过程rubric。ORSD在当前策略每一步基于同一组轨迹独立生成多个rubric bundle，从跨轨迹行为差异中提取候选过程rubric；冻结验证器对所有rubric项逐轨迹评估，并依据rubric满足度与任务成功的一致性（bundle级对齐）选出最佳候选bundle。训练时将选中的bundle作为特权信息，对比有无rubric条件下原始token的对数似然，其差值构成token级过程信用，通过加性优势塑形叠加到原轨迹级GRPO优势上。

Result: 在ALFWorld与WebShop上，Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-4B在相同环境交互预算下均加快策略改进并提升最终任务性能。

Conclusion: ORSD在轨迹级结果监督与细粒度过程监督之间架起桥梁，利用过程信号实现token级信用分配，且无需额外人工过程标注。

Abstract: Agent trajectories often contain both successful decisions and failed attempts, yet optimization with sparse trajectory-level rewards struggles to identify which critical decisions should be reinforced or suppressed. We observe that meaningful distinctions among on-policy trajectories can be distilled into natural-language process rubrics and used as process supervision signals. Based on this insight, we propose Online Rubric Self-Distillation (ORSD), which leverages self-derived process rubrics for dense token-level supervision, thereby augmenting outcome-centric reinforcement learning. Specifically, the current policy independently generates multiple rubric bundles at each step according to the same group of trajectories, extracting candidate process rubrics from cross-trajectory behavioral differences. A frozen verifier then evaluates all rubric items against each trajectory in the group, and selects one candidate bundle based on bundle-level alignment between rubric satisfaction and task success. ORSD treats the selected rubric bundle as privileged information during training, then compares the log-likelihoods of the original tokens with and without the rubric. The resulting likelihood gap provides token-specific process credit, which is added to the original trajectory-level GRPO advantage through additive advantage shaping. ORSD thus bridges trajectory-level outcome supervision and fine-grained process supervision, enabling token-level credit assignment using process signals. Extensive experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show that ORSD accelerates policy improvement and boosts final task performance for both Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-4B under the same environment-interaction budget. Our code is available at https://github.com/AnnaXChun/ORSD.

</details>


### [210] [Addition by Subtraction: Toward Mitigating Fragile Safety Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06553)
*Hamid Dashtbani,Alexandra Dmitrienko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Addition by Subtraction"微调方法，在训练中逐步剪除安全关键神经元并借助偏好优化将安全行为重新分布到其他组件，从而降低对齐对稀疏组件的依赖，显著提升对剪枝类及黑盒越狱攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全对齐存在结构性脆弱：对齐高度稀疏，仅剪除少量安全关键神经元或消融单一拒绝方向即可大幅削弱安全行为。作者希望强化对齐，使其不依赖稀疏组件。

Method: 先识别安全关键神经元，在微调过程中逐步（渐进式）移除这些神经元，同时使用偏好优化（preference optimization）在模型其余部分重建安全对齐行为，迫使安全能力分散到更多参数上。

Result: 所得模型对剪枝攻击更鲁棒；鲁棒性可泛化到拒绝方向消融等稀疏性干预以及多种黑盒越狱攻击；真实性（truthfulness）提升且通用能力基本保持。跨3家厂商4个模型的实验显示安全鲁棒性一致提升，部分攻击成功率最多降低90%。

Conclusion: 通过"减法"（移除关键神经元）实现"加法"（安全鲁棒性增强）是可行且有效，能使安全对齐由稀疏依赖转为分布式冗余，并具备跨攻击类型的泛化收益。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) undergo safety alignment to prevent harmful generations, yet they remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks. Recent work reveals a deeper structural weakness: safety alignment can be highly sparse, such that pruning a small number of safety-critical neurons or ablating a single refusal direction can substantially undermine aligned behavior. In this work, we take the first steps toward strengthening safety alignment such that it is less dependent on sparse model components. We propose Addition by Subtraction, which gradually removes identified safety-critical neurons during finetuning and uses preference optimization to re-establish safety-aligned behavior through other parts of the model. Our results show that the resulting models are substantially more robust to pruning-based attacks. Remarkably, these benefits generalize beyond the white-box attacks motivating our design: Addition by Subtraction improves robustness against other sparsity-based interventions, including refusal-direction ablation, as well as a broad range of black-box jailbreak attacks. The finetuned models also exhibit improved truthfulness while largely preserving general capabilities. Comprehensive experiments across four models from three vendors demonstrate consistent improvements in safety robustness, reducing attack success rates by up to 90% for some attacks.

</details>


### [211] [RAGAM-AI: A Hybrid Reinforcement Learning based Agentic approach powered by Generative AI for Edge Resource Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06554)
*Shreya K. Chari,John S. Vardakas,Kostas Ramantas,Christos Verikoukis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAGAM-AI混合框架，结合强化学习与生成式AI构建Agentic AI工作流，利用LLM作为奖励预言机解决多目标场景下的奖励泛化问题，优化MEC资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式边缘网络中不同服务的资源管理虽可用RL高效处理，但复杂工作流中的奖励泛化仍是RL领域的开放问题；现有方法难以在多目标权衡下提供可泛化的奖励信号。

Method: RAGAM-AI混合框架：以MASAC（多智能体Soft-Actor Critic）为核心，采用MathΣtral大模型充当奖励预言机，通过LLM解读系统状态并评估多目标权衡，指导分布式边缘服务器智能体的资源编排循环；同时引入代理奖励模型（surrogate reward model）降低整体复杂度。

Result: 与单智能体SAC、REINFORCE及随机策略对比基准测试，RAGAM-AI在CPU、内存、带宽资源上实现更低的分配偏差，同时降低时延与数据速率成本。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种可扩展的"AI即反馈"（AIAF）方案，在分布式MEC场景下显著提升网络资源优化性能。

Abstract: Resource management for distributed edge networks hosting different services has been handled efficiently using Reinforcement learning (RL). Reward generalization in complex workflows is still an open issue within the RL realm. Therefore, this work proposes RAGAM-AI, a hybrid framework that combines the capabilities of RL algorithms and Generative-AI (Gen-AI) for powering an Agentic AI workflow that optimizes resource allocation at the Multi-access Edge Computing (MEC) units. This framework further resolves the reward generalization issue by utilizing Large Language Model (LLM) for feedback. A Multi- Agent Soft-Actor Critic (MASAC) using MathΣtral as a reward oracle is implemented for managing resource allocations at the MEC. RAGAM-AI interprets system state information and evaluates multi-objective trade-offs guiding the resource orchestration loop over distributed edge server agents. The complexity of the framework is further brought down by incorporating surrogate reward model. The overall performance is evaluated against the single agent SAC, REINFORCE and random policies for benchmarking. On using RAGAM-AI, lower allocation deviations for CPU, memory and bandwidth resources are reported while minimizing the latency and data rate costs. A scalable AI as a feedback (AIAF) solution optimizing network resources with significant performance improvement is thus presented.

</details>


### [212] [One Scale, Four Texts: Gaps, Overlaps and Silent Repairs in Published Grading Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06569)
*Elisa Rovai,Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将4份糖尿病视网膜病变分级文本转写为24变量规则语言，把每处文本未定义处命名为precisification，穷举1,425,807,360个finding profile（SMT求解器复算、OWL推理机抽查验证），证明各文本在字面读法下存在无类别眼睛、2017表格增殖级为空且存在黄斑缺口导致2003分级无法映射到2017，英文与意大利文本在部分眼对上排序相反；在927张公开标注照片上两文对DDR数据集26.8%眼睛的转诊判断不一致，且该分歧依赖彩色照片不可见的视网膜增厚；两项预注册实验显示按各自文本标签训练的模型偏向本文本，语言模型倾向静默"修复"缺陷而非报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类系统以文本形式传播，读者会进行隐性修补（tacit repair）。论文追问：去除这些修补后，文本本身究竟决定了什么。以国际通用的糖尿病视网膜病变分级量表为对象，因其同一量表以多份不同文档流通，便于比较文本间的规范差异。

Method: (1) 形式化：将2025年英文筛查定义、2017年表格化国际量表、2003年发表版本、2015年意大利指南四份文本转写为覆盖24个finding变量的统一规则语言，把每处文本空白命名为一个precisification；(2) 形式验证：穷举1,425,807,360个finding profile，用SMT求解器复算、OWL推理机抽查；(3) 实证：927张来自三个公开数据集的标注照片，假设无视网膜增厚，比较英文文本与2017表格的转诊一致性；(4) 两项预注册实验：分别检验按各文本标签训练的分类器偏向性，以及语言模型在"字面阅读"指令下的静默修补倾向。

Result: 穷举验证显示：每个给出处置动作的文本在任何读法下都有部分眼睛无法归类；2017表格的字面读法下增殖级为空类，且存在2003文本所无的黄斑缺口，故不存在将2003分级映射到2017分级的函数；仅凭影像学findings，英文与意大利文本对部分眼对的排序相反。在DDR数据集上两文转诊判断不一致率达26.8%（两者均给出判断的眼睛），预注册实验中各分类器倾向本方文本。视网膜增厚在彩色照片上不可见，若将其留空则所有涉及英格兰文本的分歧均不可判定，说明分歧实质依赖相机无法呈现的信息。第二项预注册研究中，不进行推理直接作答的语言模型即便被要求字面阅读也修补了多数gap与overlap条目；Claude Sonnet 5.5与Opus 5.5更常报告缺陷，且推理在两个模型内减少但未消除静默修补。

Conclusion: 分级文本在字面意义上并不足以决定分级与转诊判断：其规范化程度依赖读者的隐含修补，不同流通文本之间存在实质性、可形式化证明的不兼容。因此，基于此类文本构建的标签与模型继承其不确定性，而当前语言模型倾向于掩盖而非揭示这些文本缺陷。论文主张将文本空白显式化为precisification，并以形式方法审计分类系统的规范充分性。

Abstract: Category systems often reach their users as text, and readers apply them with tacit repairs. We ask what such a text determines once the repairs are removed, using diabetic retinopathy grading, where one international scale circulates in several documents. We transcribe four texts (the English screening definitions of 2025, the international scale as tabulated in 2017 and as published in 2003, and an Italian guideline of 2015) into one rule language over 24 finding variables, and make every point a text leaves open a named precisification. Exhaustive enumeration of 1,425,807,360 finding profiles, re-derived by an SMT solver and spot-checked by an OWL reasoner, shows that every text that assigns actions leaves some eyes without a category under every reading; that the 2017 tabulation has a proliferative class that is empty when read literally and a macular gap that the 2003 text lacks, so that no function maps the 2003 grades onto the 2017 grades; and that on image findings alone the English and Italian texts order some pairs of eyes in opposite ways. On 927 annotated photographs from three public datasets, with retinal thickening assumed absent, the English text and the 2017 tabulation disagree on referral for 26.8% of the eyes of one dataset (DDR) on which both decide, and in a pre-registered experiment classifiers trained on each text's labels side with their own text. Thickening cannot be seen on a colour photograph; left free, every disagreement involving England becomes undecidable, so it rests on what the camera cannot show. In a second pre-registered study, language models that answered without deliberating repaired most gap and overlap items even when told to read literally, while Claude Sonnet 5.5 and Claude Opus 5.5 reported the defects far more often; within two models, deliberation reduced silent repair without removing it.

</details>


### [213] [Time-Division Multiplexing: The Operational Rhythm and Temporal Organization Layer of Digital Life](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06603)
*Haibin Tong,Luming Guo,Nana Cui,Jiang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个"时间组织层"，用相位标记的微纪元、可观测占空比、独立的内容/资源控制与三层嵌套时钟，把持续型智能体的在线（忙碌）与离线（空闲）通道统一为一个共同过程的可排序阶段，并给出投影占空比更新的下降保证与五组合成实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续型智能体需同时处理紧急交互、保留备选方案、重放错误并提交安全的长期变更。既有"数字生命"框架虽区分在线（busy）与离线（idle）通道，但未回答二者是独立程序还是同一过程的两个阶段，也未规定其操作顺序；同时受灵长类皮层感知表征快速交替现象的启发，作者希望给出可检验的调度设计。

Method: 构建时间组织层，包含：(1) 相位标记的微纪元（phase-tagged micro-epochs）；(2) 可观测占空比（duty cycle）；(3) 内容控制与资源控制相互独立；(4) 三层嵌套时钟，将候选探索与持久承诺分离。理论上给出投影占空比更新规则、其确定性下降保证，以及随机保证所需的额外噪声项，并规定耦合的 busy/idle 执行契约。实验上开展五组合成实验（每组 64 个随机种子），分别考察下降与噪声、目标错位、增益不对称、资源耦合与重放日志丢失；另用冻结骨干的 Qwen3-8B 原型检验编排流程。

Result: 在增益/噪声模型中最佳增益比为 1.55；解耦调度在构造的"紧急/高负载"子集上成功率为 26.6%，对照为 11.1%。Qwen3-8B 原型仅用于检查编排，其代理结果不构成任务基准证据。论文另明确了确定性下降的成立条件，以及随机下降所需的噪声项。

Conclusion: 结果给出的是一个可检验的调度设计方案及其有效性的边界条件，而非机器意识或通用智能体优越性的证明。作者将结论限定为设计层面的贡献，并把原型结果明确排除在基准证据之外。

Abstract: Continual agents must handle urgent interaction while retaining alternatives, replaying errors, and committing safe long-term changes. A previous digital-life framework distinguishes online (busy) and offline (idle) channels, but leaves open whether these are separate programs or phases of a common process and how their operations should be ordered. Motivated by recent observations of rapidly alternating perceptual representations in primate cortex, we develop a temporal organization layer with phase-tagged micro-epochs, an observable duty cycle, independent content and resource controls, and three nested clocks separating candidate exploration from durable commitment. We give a projected duty-cycle update, state its deterministic descent guarantee and the additional noise term required for a stochastic guarantee, and specify coupled busy/idle execution contracts. Five synthetic experiments (64 seeds each) examine descent and noise, objective misalignment, gain asymmetry, resource coupling, and dropped replay logs. In the gain/noise model the best tested gain ratio is 1.55; decoupled scheduling yields 26.6% versus 11.1% success on a constructed urgent/high-load subset. A frozen-backbone Qwen3-8B prototype further checks orchestration, but its proxy outcomes are not task-benchmark evidence. The results establish a testable scheduling design and identify its conditions of validity, not machine consciousness or general-purpose agent superiority.

</details>


### [214] [Interactive World Simulation with Cosmos for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06627)
*NVIDIA,:,Anis Ahmad,Jon Allen,Malik Aqeel Anwar,Jesse Archer,Maciej Bala,Piotr Barejko,Aarti Basant,Dan Blick,Taras Bolzhelarskyi,Henry Che,Jin Chen,Yukang Chen,Tae Eun Choe,Yuzhu Dong,Nat Duca,Liang Feng,Francesco Ferroni,Xiao Fu,Mohit Gambhir,Vivek Goel,Rony Goldenthal,Aryaman Gupta,Ali Hassani,Nathan Hayes-Roth,Yong He,Michael Honke,Shengyu Huang,Wei Huang,DeLesley Hutchins,Suneel Indupuru,Pooya Jannaty,Jingyi Jin,Janmesh Kalra,Ashna Khetan,Xin Kong,Aleksandr Korovko,Hui Kuang,Zhengfei Kuang,George Kurian,Jean-Francois Lafleche,Jayjun Lee,Justin Leung,William Lew,Xiang Li,Zhaoshuo Li,Chen-Hsuan Lin,Ming-Yu Liu,Xiangyu Lu,Yuhang Lu,Martin Ding Ma,Qianli Ma,Aditya Mahajan,Miguel Martin,Janarbek Matai,Jonathan McCaffrey,Qing Miao,Ashkan Mirzaei,Mustafa Munir,Sangmin Oh,David Pankratz,Trung Pham,Marilyn Reeb,Gavriel State,Jiaxiang Tang,Shitao Tang,Yue Tang,Tolou Tavakkoli,Alexander Temirov,Hweimi Tsou,Sergei Vasilev,Thomas Volk,Andrew Z. Wang,Hao Wang,Shijie Wang,Zhonghao Wang,David Wehr,Shumian Xin,Mengyao Xu,Yao Xu,Shuai Yang,Sophia Zalewski,Pengcuo Zeren,Cindy Zha,Jing Zhang,Yuliya Zhautouskaya,Kaiwen Zheng,Andrew Zhu,Rick Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个共享框架，将Cosmos世界基础模型适配为Physical AI中的实时交互式世界模拟器，支持以流式方式生成动作和场景控制下的视觉观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physical AI的训练与评测需要可交互、可演化的虚拟环境，而现有世界模型缺乏统一框架来实时响应用户与具身智能体的动作及场景控制。

Method: 基于Cosmos世界基础模型构建共享框架：以流式方式输入视觉观测并对动作作出响应；同时支持两种模式——预测动作的视觉后果，以及从外部模拟器维护的物理状态生成观测。

Result: 在相机导航与机器人操作、可控视频转换、多视角自动驾驶仿真等任务上进行了演示，验证了框架的通用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建面向Physical AI训练与评测的交互式虚拟环境提供了共同基础，并开源了代码与模型权重。

Abstract: We present a shared framework that adapts Cosmos world foundation models for real-time interactive world simulation in Physical AI. By streaming visual observations in response to actions and scene controls, the framework enables users and embodied agents to interact with evolving virtual environments. It supports both predicting the visual consequences of actions and generating observations from physical state maintained by an external simulator. We demonstrate this approach across camera navigation and robot manipulation, controllable video transfer, and multi-view autonomous vehicle (AV) simulation. These examples provide a shared foundation for developing interactive virtual environments for Physical AI training and evaluation. Our code and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/nvidia/cosmos and https://huggingface.co/collections/nvidia/cosmos3-examples. The project website is available at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/cosmos-lab/cosmos-simulation.

</details>


### [215] [From Certified Pairs to a Certified Hub: Reasoner-Checked Crosswalks for the Information Exchange Standard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06628)
*Fabio Rovai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文为"数字孪生生态"中跨本体映射的"有效性"给出精确判据（合并本体的融贯性与保守性），并基于IES跨13个本体、7个行业组的交叉映射实证：成对认证不可组合，单个hub（而非成对映射）才是生态认证的基本单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家数字孪生的构想是以映射把各孪生体连接到一个共同基座上，但其路线图承诺"确认映射有效"却从未定义"有效"的含义；因此需要一个可操作的、逻辑化的映射有效性检验标准。

Method: 借用基于逻辑的对齐修复（alignment repair）中的"融贯性（coherence）"与"保守性（conservativity）"作为有效性判据，应用于信息交换标准IES：先成对检验，再检验整体hub。流程含匹配、人工评审、对每条SKOS对应关系依两套类层次进行认证、以及用HermiT推理。共涉及7个行业组、13个本体，并以BOT、Brick、SAREF4BLDG建筑本体为锚点。

Result: 各阶段捕获不同失效：仅认证即消除原始交叉映射中全部526处保守性违背，仅人工评审后仍余73处；二者结合使12对重叠映射中的11对达到融贯且保守。但成对认证不可组合：将三个已认证的建筑交叉映射合并为单一hub后，因IES与Brick对"楼层（floor）"的同名异义（仅BOT的不相交公理可揭示）导致5个Brick类不可满足；剔除该行后hub融贯且保守，并蕴含290条无人显式写出的跨本体链接。核心hub本身在加入任何交叉映射前即不一致，因Open Energy Ontology与BFO 2020两项公开标准互相矛盾；剔除其一即恢复融贯与保守。

Conclusion: 映射有效性应形式化为合并本体的融贯性与保守性；成对认证不能保证组合后的全局一致性，故孪生生态应以整个hub而非成对映射作为认证单元。

Abstract: A national digital twin was conceived as an ecosystem of twins connected through mappings to a common foundation, but the roadmap that promises to confirm those mappings are valid never says what valid means. We give the test a precise form, coherence and conservativity of the merged ontology, borrowed from logic-based alignment repair, and apply it to the Information Exchange Standard (IES), first pair by pair and then to whole hubs. Crosswalks from IES into thirteen ontologies across seven sector groups, anchored on the building ontologies BOT, Brick, and SAREF4BLDG, pass through separate stages of matching, human review, certification of each SKOS correspondence against the two class hierarchies, and reasoning with HermiT. Pair by pair, the stages catch different failures: certification alone removed all 526 conservativity violations of the raw crosswalks, review alone left 73, and together they made eleven of twelve overlapping pairs coherent and conservative. Pairwise certification does not compose. Merging the three certified building crosswalks into one hub made five Brick classes unsatisfiable, through a homonym between the IES and Brick notions of a floor that only BOT's disjointness axioms expose; rejecting that one row gave a coherent and conservative hub that entails 290 cross-ontology links nobody wrote down. The core hub is inconsistent before any crosswalk is added, because two published standards, the Open Energy Ontology and BFO 2020, contradict each other; with either one left out it is coherent and conservative. A hub, not a pair, is the unit an ecosystem of twins has to certify.

</details>


### [216] [Where Search Agents Go Off the Rails: Graph-Grounded Step-Level Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06662)
*Hyeseo Jeon,Seonho Yoo,Jay-Yoon Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GraphRail，将需求、证据与推理表示为结构化断言，在无需生成式评判器或标准答案的情况下比对三者，实现对检索增强推理系统的中间步骤审计，并可在线纠正推理错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强推理系统即使最终答案正确，也可能在中间步骤出错，因此可靠审计需同时检验三件事：证据 D 是否覆盖问题 Q、推理 T 是否回应 Q、T 是否基于 D。现有生成式评判器存在不稳定与依赖金标准答案的局限。

Method: GraphRail 把问题需求、证据与推理链转化为结构化断言（claims），通过断言层面的结构化比对完成覆盖性与接地性检查，不依赖生成式 judge 与金标准答案；在抽取具有随机性时仍保持稳定。

Result: 在三个多跳问答基准与三种推理框架上，GraphRail 相较生成式验证器提升了错误召回率，并在随机抽取条件下保持稳定；其诊断结果还能指导在线推理。

Conclusion: 结构化断言比对是一种可追溯、稳定的审计范式，既能检测与纠正中间错误，又可反哺在线推理过程。

Abstract: Search-augmented reasoning systems can reach correct answers while making errors in intermediate steps. Reliable auditing must check whether evidence $D$ covers the question $Q$, reasoning $T$ addresses $Q$, and $T$ is grounded in $D$. We introduce GraphRail, which represents requirements, evidence, and reasoning as structured claims and compares them without a generative judge or gold answer. Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks and three reasoning frameworks, GraphRail improves error recall over generative verifiers while remaining stable under stochastic extraction. Its diagnoses can also guide online reasoning, providing a traceable way to detect and correct intermediate errors.

</details>


### [217] [LifeVal: Evaluating AI Model Performance on Personalized Agentic Consumer Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06687)
*Jenny Yang,Elizabeth Proehl,Anjan Bharadwaj,Emily Fan,Charlotte Cole,James Kim,Noam Brown,Phoebe Thacker,Amelia Glaese,Tejal Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LifeVal 是一个评估 AI 模型在个性化代理式消费任务上表现的基准，覆盖年支付意愿总额超 1.7 万亿美元的 148 类高价值生活任务，任务基于真实 ChatGPT 对话与记忆个性化构建，并经过严格专家与人工质检。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 评测缺乏对“高价值、个性化、可自动化”的消费者生活任务覆盖，需要以支付意愿量化任务价值，并评估个性化数字超级助手的真实能力。

Method: 通过面向 3800+ 名人口代表性消费者的支付意愿调查构建活动分布，并与 American Time Use Survey 和 Multinational Time Use Study 交叉验证；针对最高价值活动招募任务创建者，利用真实 ChatGPT 对话与记忆、参考文件和个人上下文构建个性化任务及评分量表；创建者须通过专业门槛（个人多次完成并实践一年以上），所有任务须通过至少三轮人工质检。

Result: 构建了覆盖 148 个消费任务类别、年支付意愿超 1.7 万亿美元的 LifeVal 基准；任务经多轮人检后的最终评测集具备专家级个性化标准。

Conclusion: LifeVal 为个性化代理式消费任务提供了价值导向、真实上下文驱动的高质量评测框架。

Abstract: We introduce LifeVal, a benchmark evaluating AI model performance on personalized agentic consumer tasks. LifeVal covers the most valuable life tasks that a personalized digital superassistant could automate, representing over 1.7 trillion US dollars in annual willingness-to-pay across 148 consumer task categories (e.g., managing finances, moving apartments, searching for a job, finding childcare). To construct our activity distribution, we conducted a new survey of willingness-to-pay for a digital superassistant across a demographically representative sample of over 3,800 consumers, cross-referencing our results against the American Time Use Survey (Bureau of Labor Statistics, 2025) and the Multinational Time Use Study (Fisher et al., 2025). For the most valuable consumer activities, we recruited task creators to create AI evaluation tasks personalized using real ChatGPT conversations and memories, reference files, and personal context. Task creators had to pass our expertise threshold (including personally completing the task multiple times and seeing the results through in their real lives for over a year) and also write a rubric, ensuring that our evaluation measured the best personalized completion for that task. All tasks had to pass at least three rounds of human quality-control before acceptance into our final evaluation sets.

</details>


### [218] [ConceptARC at Scale: Studying Conceptual Understanding in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06732)
*Philipp Mondorf,Adrien Deliège,Solim LeGris,Claas Beger,Ryan Yi,Barbara Plank,Brenden M. Lake,Todd M. Gureckis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ConceptARC at Scale生成器，构建1600个经人工审核的ARC风格任务（ConceptARC-100），系统评测LRMs的概念级抽象能力，并发现模型内部表征按变换操作（重着色、删除、平移）聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ARC只考察单任务内的规则抽象，而跨任务的更高层结构（如"对象包含关系"）缺乏大规模评测；ConceptARC虽引入概念分组，但每个概念仅有人工构造的少量任务，规模不足以可靠刻画模型的概念能力与泛化边界。

Method: 设计ConceptARC at Scale任务生成器，针对单一概念批量生成ARC风格任务；据此构造ConceptARC-100数据集，覆盖ConceptARC的16个概念、每概念100个任务（共1600个，经人工审核，规模为原基准10倍）。评测近期大推理模型及从零训练的模型，刻画各概念的可解性；并通过内部表征分析检验任务表征的语义结构。

Result: 给出各模型在16个概念上的可靠/困难分布画像；表征分析显示任务表征按作用于对象的操作（recoloring、removal、translation）聚类，即使底层规则与场景差异显著。

Conclusion: 模型形成了关于变换操作的稳定抽象，并能跨多样任务泛化；概念级、可扩展的基准生成是研究抽象能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) perform well on challenging benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether they form generalizable abstractions across conceptually related reasoning tasks. The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) provides a controlled testbed for studying abstraction, requiring solvers to infer transformation rules over two-dimensional grids from a small set of examples. While ARC evaluates rule abstraction within individual tasks, different tasks may share higher-level structure despite requiring different transformations$\unicode{x2014}$for example, several tasks may involve reasoning about objects contained within other objects. Benchmarks such as ConceptARC capture this structure by grouping ARC-style tasks into recurring spatial and semantic concepts, but they often contain only a few manually constructed tasks per concept. We introduce ConceptARC at Scale, a generator for creating thousands of ARC-style tasks targeting a specific concept. Using this generator, we construct ConceptARC-100, a dataset of 1,600 new, manually reviewed tasks, with 100 tasks for each of the 16 concepts defined by ConceptARC$\unicode{x2014}$ten times the size of the original benchmark. We evaluate a range of recent LRMs and models trained from scratch on ConceptARC-100 to characterize which concepts they solve reliably and where they struggle. We further analyze the models' internal representations and find clear semantic structure: task representations cluster according to the operations applied to objects, such as recoloring, removal, or translation, even when realized through substantially different rules and scenarios. Together, our results suggest that models form stable abstractions of transformation operations that generalize across diverse tasks.

</details>


### [219] [Task-Efficient Agent Evaluation Using Cached Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06803)
*Ben Johnson,Hayden Helm*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将智能体执行轨迹用LLM抽取结构化摘要、再经文本编码器嵌入，构建参考系统的低维几何表示，并据此预测新系统基准分数，在极少任务执行下显著降低高效基准评测的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效基准评测（efficient benchmarking）仅利用执行的二元正确性来从少量参考系统中推断系统整体性能，忽略了智能体在每次执行中产生的丰富轨迹信息，而原始轨迹嵌入又易混入系统身份、模型家族与评测框架（harness）等无关信号。

Method: 用LLM对每条执行轨迹抽取结构化摘要，并以标准文本编码器嵌入，使表示保留正确性与任务相关信号、抑制身份/家族/框架信号；基于所有参考系统的逐任务执行表示构建系统的低维表示，通过新系统在参考系统间的几何位置预测其分数，并与项目反应模型（item-response model）结合。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified与Terminal-Bench 2.0上，仅给定一个随机执行任务时，MAE分别由0.13降至0.08（Wilcoxon p<1e-4）和由0.14降至0.12（Wilcoxon p<1e-3）；在可用全部参考系统的条件下，任意执行任务数下均优于基线。

Conclusion: 结构化摘要—嵌入的轨迹表示可有效提取超越二元正确性的能力信号，几何位置的分数预测能提升高效基准评测的样本效率与精度。

Abstract: Efficient benchmarking estimates a system's full benchmark performance from a small number of task executions. Standard approaches rely solely on the binary correctness of executions from a collection of already-evaluated reference systems. An agentic system, however, produces a rich trace for each execution that can reveal substantially more about its capabilities. Leveraging this information requires useful trace representations. We propose representing a trace by extracting a structured summary of it with an LLM and embedding the summary with a standard text encoder. These representations preserve correctness- and task-related signal while minimizing the presence of system identity-, model family- and harness-related signals that dominate raw trace embeddings. Given this representation for each task execution from all reference systems, we construct low-dimensional representations of the systems and predict a new system's score from its position among the reference systems. On SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.0, augmenting an item-response model with this geometry-based prediction reduces mean absolute error from 0.13 to 0.08 (Wilcoxon $p < 10^{-4}$) and from 0.14 to 0.12 (Wilcoxon $p < 10^{-3}$), respectively, when provided a single random executed task. Further, given access to the full set of reference systems, our approach dominates baselines for any given number of executed tasks on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [220] [Rules, Traces and Routing Statistics: Training and Calibration Data for Open Models on ARC-AGI-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06827)
*Nguyen Minh Thang,Trinh Thi Phuong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在离线四卡L4、12小时预算下，研究训练与校准数据如何帮助三个开源模型求解ARC-AGI-2：NVARC Qwen3-4B靠真实任务微调小幅提升至33.06；Qwen3.8-27B靠推理流程设计从26.81升至44.03；MoE模型靠专家剪枝+FP8索引缓存扩容KV cache，配合程序检查工具达到56.67（最佳）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格受限的离线算力（4×L4、≤12小时）条件下，探究何种训练数据与校准数据能提升开源模型在ARC-AGI-2上的表现，并系统比较真实任务、合成任务、自生成推理轨迹等数据来源的实际价值。

Method: 1) NVARC Qwen3-4B网格模型：构建含12,000条类型化语言可执行规则与95,993个任务的语料，规则需在固定探针集上与已接纳规则行为互异，任务需能反驳最近竞争规则并击败12个捷径求解器；在1,920条真实任务记录上继续微调并改进四卡调度。2) Qwen3.8-27B推理模型：用1,036条自身正确答案推理轨迹微调，对比加入他模型轨迹与合成任务的增益；设计服务引擎推理上限、答案后Python程序在演示上校验、每输入长首次尝试的推理流程。3) Qwen3.8-Flash-Next MoE（125B总参、6B激活）：按路由器加权激活显著性将每层专家由512剪至384，装配288/296专家列表（结合ARC路由统计保留程序token专家）与FP8索引缓存，并加程序检查工具。

Result: 4B模型半私有得分由28.96（发布模型均值）升至33.06（五次运行均值，最佳34.86）；任何合成任务训练的checkpoint（含全部95,993任务）均未超越真实任务模型。27B模型分数由26.81升至44.03，超过官方测得无时限基线42.36，且增益主要来自推理流程而非微调。MoE模型KV cache由0.55M扩至1.39M（512→384专家），再经296专家列表与FP8索引缓存达1.96M token；配合程序检查工具得分56.67，为最佳结果。

Conclusion: 在受限离线算力下，真实任务数据优于大规模合成数据；推理流程与工具校验是27B模型的主要增益来源；MoE模型的关键瓶颈是显存限制的并发，通过专家剪枝与缓存压缩提升KV cache容量可显著改善表现，最终取得56.67的最优成绩。

Abstract: We study which training and calibration data help three open models solve ARC-AGI-2 tasks when the solver runs offline on four L4 GPUs for at most 12 hours. We compare data offline on public tasks and report 189 runs on the 120 hidden semi-private tasks. For the NVARC Qwen3-4B grid model, we built a corpus of 12,000 executable rules in a typed language and 95,993 tasks; a rule is admitted only if its behaviour on a fixed probe set differs from that of every admitted rule, and a task only if its pairs refute a shortlist of nearest competing rules and defeat 12 shortcut solvers. Continued fine-tuning on 1,920 real task records with revised four-GPU scheduling raised the semi-private score from 28.96 (mean of the released model) to 33.06 (mean of five runs, best 34.86); no checkpoint trained with synthetic tasks, even all 95,993 of them, improved on the real-task model. For the Qwen3.8-27B reasoning model, we fine-tuned on 1,036 of its own reasoning traces with correct final answers and compared sets that add traces of other models and synthetic tasks. With an inference procedure built around the model (a thinking cap enforced in the serving engine, a Python program checked on the demonstrations after each answer, a long first attempt per input), the score rose from 26.81 to 44.03, the level the ARC Prize Foundation measured for the base model without a time limit (42.36); most of the gain came from the inference procedure. For the Qwen3.8-Flash-Next mixture-of-experts model (125B parameters, 6B active), GPU memory limited concurrency. Pruning from 512 to 384 experts per layer by router-weighted activation saliency grew the key-value cache from 0.55M to 1.39M tokens; a 296-expert list adapted with ARC routing statistics, keeping experts for program tokens, plus an FP8 indexer cache, reached 1.96M. With a tool that checks programs, the model scored 56.67, our best result.

</details>


### [221] [ServerBench: How can your coding agents design specialized LLM serving systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06841)
*Xinrui Zhong,Zhiming Wang,Yongqi Liu,Zhaohui Wang,Shanglin Yang,Zikun Li,Zhuoming Chen,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ServerBench 基准，评测编码智能体自主设计并交付完整、专用 LLM 服务系统（serving systems）的能力，以"每美元 GPU 成本所获延迟达标 token 价值"（dollars per dollar）为统一目标，结合有状态回放与独立验证，结果显示智能体可显著超越 vLLM 参考实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码智能体通过实验开发软件，因而具备设计支撑自身运行的服务系统的潜力。有效服务需在会话依赖、延迟约束与正确性要求下协同实现决策与运维决策，但目前缺乏可系统评测此类"智能体式系统设计"能力的基准。

Method: 构建 ServerBench：以"每美元成本获得延迟达标的 token 价值"（美元/美元）为统一优化目标；采用有状态回放（stateful replay）保留会话依赖关系；用独立验证（independent verification）检查提交系统是否正确；在 11 个编码智能体模型与 3 种部署上评估，并与 vLLM 等强基线对比。

Result: Claude Sonnet 5.5 在 Gemma E4B 上达到 2.97 美元/美元，为 vLLM 参考的 4.87 倍；33 次运行中 22 次产出通过验证的系统，13 次超过最强基线。

Conclusion: 三点发现：高价值设计将容量改进与负载选择协同；智能体需通过工作负载相关实验解读性能反馈；可靠交付必须验证优化所引入的执行路径。ServerBench 支持系统研究智能体如何将服务目标与反馈转化为有效且经验证的服务系统。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents develop software through experimentation, offering an opportunity to design the serving systems supporting their own operation. Effective serving requires coordinating implementation and operating decisions under session dependencies, latency constraints, and correctness requirements. We introduce ServerBench, a benchmark for agentic design of complete, specialized LLM serving systems. Agents experiment against a common objective, dollars per dollar: latency-qualified token value per dollar of GPU cost. Stateful replay preserves session dependencies, and independent verification checks the submitted system. Across 11 coding-agent models and three deployments, Claude Sonnet 5.5 achieves 2.97 dollars per dollar on Gemma E4B, $4.87\times$ the vLLM reference; 22 of 33 runs yield verified systems, and 13 exceed the strongest baseline. Our analysis identifies three findings: high-value designs coordinate capacity improvements with load selection; agents interpret performance feedback through workload-relevant experiments; and reliable delivery requires verification of the execution paths introduced by optimization. Overall, ServerBench supports systematic study of how agents translate service objectives and feedback into effective, verified serving systems for given task specifications.

</details>


### [222] [CompressionBench: How can your coding agents compress LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06858)
*Xinrui Zhong,Zhifeng Li,Zhiming Wang,Yongqi Liu,Shanglin Yang,Yang Zhou,Zikun Li,Zhuoming Chen,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将LLM压缩建模为终端任务，检验编码智能体能否自主完成"模型压缩"这一自改进子步骤：6个智能体×6种模型规模共36次提交中29次通过验证，最优设计平均PPL仅上升3.4%，下游准确率77.33%（BF16为79.56%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进要求AI系统能改进后续系统所用的模型与基础设施；LLM压缩提供了明确的效率目标——在更小的存储预算内保留预训练模型行为，但智能体能否自主完成该步骤尚不清楚。

Method: 将压缩形式化为终端任务：智能体接收源checkpoint、存储上限、推理环境与时间预算，自行构建压缩权重与可执行的推理代码；算法与实验由智能体自主控制，以困惑度反馈引导搜索，最终由独立验证环节检查模型。

Result: 6个智能体、6种模型—规模设置下36份提交中29份通过验证；按任务选取最低PPL的智能体设计，平均PPL较BF16仅上升3.4%，下游准确率77.33% vs. BF16的79.56%。记录显示：24次修订中21次在近乎固定存储下降低PPL；全模型反馈推翻了79个先前局部排序中的17个；生成与推理测试暴露了似然指标未能发现的失效。

Conclusion: 表征设计、实验判断与能力验证是智能体驱动模型压缩的三个核心环节，该压缩任务可作为衡量面向效率的自我改进的一个可量化组件。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires AI systems to improve the models and infrastructure used by subsequent systems. LLM compression offers a concrete efficiency objective: retain a pretrained model's behavior within a smaller model storage budget. We study whether coding agents can carry out this step autonomously. We formulate compression as a terminal task in which an agent receives a source checkpoint, storage limit, inference environment, and time budget, then constructs compressed weights and executable inference code. The agent controls its algorithm and experiments; perplexity feedback guides search, and independent verification checks the final model. Across six agents and six model--size settings, 29 of 36 submissions pass verification. Selecting the lowest-perplexity agent design per task yields an average 3.4% perplexity increase over BF16 and 77.33% downstream accuracy, compared with 79.56% for BF16. Recorded experiments reveal how agents achieve these outcomes and where improvement breaks down. Agents lower PPL in 21 of 24 revisions at nearly fixed storage; complete-model feedback reverses 17 of 79 prior local rankings; and generation and inference tests expose failures that likelihood alone misses. The results identify representation design, experimental judgment, and capability validation as central to agent-driven model compression, a measurable component of efficiency-oriented self-improvement.

</details>


### [223] [On Turn-Level Best-of-N Sampling for Scaling LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06868)
*Jalaj Bhandari,Dhawal Gupta,Hongli Zhan,Zhenting Wang,Varad Pimpalkhute,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing,Rupesh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了面向LLM智能体的"轮级best-of-N采样"（turn-level best-of-N sampling）推理时扩展方法：用生成器在每轮产生N个候选动作、由选择器挑选其一执行，在长时域终端任务上带来5–10%准确率提升及更高的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（如Zainullina等2025）用critic模型做推理时引导搜索；本文关注一个更简单的问题：现成的开源权重LLM是否本身就能有效比较不同候选动作，从而以极低工程成本实现推理时扩展。

Method: 提出并实例化generator–selector框架：在每个决策轮，generator生成N个候选动作，selector模型从中选择一个并在环境中执行；即轮级best-of-N采样，不依赖外部critic或价值模型。

Result: 在长时域终端任务上，该方法在准确率上提升5–10%，且重复试验间的一致性显著提高；轨迹分析显示智能体行为向有利模式转变：解题所需尝试次数减少、指令遵循更好、更多调试与测试行为。

Conclusion: 简单的轮级best-of-N采样配合开源LLM直接比较候选动作，即可有效实现LLM智能体的推理时扩展，无需专门训练的critic模型。

Abstract: In this work, we evaluate a simple inference-time scaling approach based on \textit{turn-level best-of-N sampling} (\method) for large language model (LLM) agents on complex, long-horizon tasks. We instantiate this using a \textit{generator-selector} framework with two LLMs; at each turn, a \emph{generator} model generates $N$ candidate actions and a \emph{selector} model selects one of them, which is then executed in the environment. This paper is closely related to the work of \cite{zainullina2025guided} who used critic models for guided search at inference time. Different from their work, we evaluate if off-the-shelf, open-weight LLMs can be effective at comparing different candidate actions directly. Experiments on long-horizon terminal tasks show that \method can lead to significant performance improvements, both in terms of accuracy (5--10\%) and consistency across repeated attempts. Analysis of agent trajectories reveal a shift in agent behaviour towards desirable patterns such as reduction in number of attempts to solve the task, improved task instruction following, debugging and testing.

</details>


### [224] [Learning to Decode Linear Codes with Generalized Belief Propagation Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06874)
*Samira Malek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出广义置信传播网络(GBPNet)，在保持消息传递对称性与Tanner图结构的前提下增强神经译码器学习能力，并引入渐进训练与高信噪比训练两种策略提升译码精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典迭代译码算法是纠错码的核心，近期神经译码器相较传统迭代算法表现出显著性能增益；但如何在保留BP消息传递对称性与Tanner图结构的同时提升可学习性仍是问题。

Method: GBPNet采用级联的变量节点更新与可学习的逐元素消息缩放，保留Tanner图结构以维持消息传递对称性；训练上引入渐进训练(progressive training)与高SNR训练两种策略。

Result: 在多种线性码上的实验表明，GBPNet的误比特率低于传统置信传播(BP)以及所评估的当前最优神经译码器。

Conclusion: 在保持BP结构先验的同时引入受限可学习参数与针对性训练策略，可有效提升纠错译码精度。

Abstract: Error correcting codes are a fundamental component of communication systems, enabling reliable data transmission over noisy channels. Recently, neural decoders have demonstrated notable performance gains over classical iterative algorithms. In this paper, we propose a Generalized Belief Propagation Network (GBPNet) that preserves message-passing symmetry while enhancing the decoder's learning capability. The architecture uses cascaded variable-node updates and learned element-wise message scaling while retaining the Tanner graph structure. We also introduce two training strategies, progressive training and high-SNR training, that improve decoding accuracy. Experimental results on several linear codes show that GBPNet achieves lower bit error rates than conventional belief propagation and the evaluated state-of-the-art neural decoders.

</details>


### [225] [Dull, Dirty, Dangerous? Working Conditions and the Limits of Occupational AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06888)
*Siyi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究利用754个美国职业的21项O*NET工作条件条目，考察"枯燥、肮脏、危险"（3D）工作条件与职业AI暴露度的关系；结果显示高AI暴露职业的3D条件得分更低，但控制体力要求、教育、培训经验与工资后，LLM暴露度的关联置信区间包含零，AIOE的关联仍为负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化常以"免除枯燥、肮脏、危险工作"作为正当性依据，但这一叙事缺乏对3D工作条件与职业AI暴露度之间实证关系的检验；作者希望厘清二者关联是否稳健，以及条目分组与职业特征如何影响暴露度估计的解释。

Method: 以754个美国职业为样本，选取21项O*NET工作条件条目构建3D指标；分别采用大语言模型（LLM）暴露度指标与AI Occupational Exposure（AIOE）指标，并分别在等职业权重与就业权重下估计关联；随后加入体力要求、所需教育、培训与经验、工资等协变量进行调整；并考察将"重复性"与"受限自主裁量权"分离后对合并"Dull"得分含义的影响。

Result: 在两种权重方案下，较高的AI暴露度均与较低的3D条件得分相关。调整体力要求、教育、培训与经验及工资后，复合关联减弱：LLM暴露度的调整后置信区间包含零，AIOE的置信区间仍位于零以下。将重复性与受限裁量权分开后，合并"Dull"得分所传达的信息发生变化。

Conclusion: 条目分组方式与职业特征共同塑造暴露度估计的解释，因此结论对指标构建与协变量设定敏感。职业画像可作为筛选对比性研究场景的起点；而判断自动化是否带来"期望的自动化"或"实际实现的减负"，仍需关于劳动者偏好与具体干预措施的证据。

Abstract: Automation is often justified as relief from dull, dirty, and dangerous work. I examine how these conditions relate to occupational AI exposure using 21 O*NET items across 754 US occupations. Higher exposure is associated with lower condition scores under a large language model (LLM) measure and AI Occupational Exposure (AIOE), with equal occupation weights and employment weights. Adjustment for physical demands, required education, training and experience, and wages reduces the composite association. The adjusted confidence intervals include zero for LLM exposure and remain below zero for AIOE. Separating repetition from restricted discretion also changes what the combined Dull score conveys. These comparisons show how item groupings and occupational characteristics shape the interpretation of exposure estimates. Occupational profiles offer a starting point for selecting contrasting research settings. Establishing desired automation or realized relief requires evidence about workers' preferences and specific interventions.

</details>


### [226] [FrontierPhysics: Evaluating Agents for End-to-End Physics Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06910)
*Bingran You,Wenjun Ke,Ria Rosenauer,Bo Liu,Wenbo Chen,Min Chen,Shuqi Xu,Yue Ma,Jiechao Feng,Bo Chen,Xiangkai Sun,Jiarui Liu,Jinen Guo,Zeyu Zhou,Xu Huang,Zhuohan Li,Haoyu Li,Heguang Lin,Lijie Ding,Yadong Zeng,Kangqi Zhang,Xinge Liu,Hanwen Liu,Zhenghong Li,Alexander von Recum,Yuchen Zhu,Felix Schwarzfischer,Yizhou Zhao,Jicheng Wang,Binxu Li,Qian Yu,Chan Li,Cheng Dai,Di Wang,Haoyu Zhang,Jiankai Sun,Kaituo Feng,Mian Qin,Ruihong Yin,Yiqun Luo,Weiwei Wu,Shihong Pan,Tsung Yeh Hsieh,Xiao Huang,Yan Zhang,Sihan Li,Zirui Ren,Zengji Tu,Kyoung Whan Choe,Ke Sun,Vincenzo Galia,Vaisakhi Mishra,Jiashu He,Hongyang Wang,Zhiheng Yang,Shubham Gaur,Yifan Gu,Shuyi Wang,Zihao Zuo,Steven Dillmann,Yiyou Sun,Xinyang Han,Hanchung Lee,Molei Tao,Yue Zhao,Shilong Liu,Yuchen Wu,Xuandong Zhao,Xiangyi Li,Florian Brand,Gerbrand Ceder,Hartmut Haeffner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出端到端物理研究基准 FrontierPhysics（56 个任务、42 位物理学家、九大领域），要求智能体完成文献调研到论文撰写全流程，并以确定性验证器加专家评分表评估正确性与可发表性；22 个模型 26 种配置中最优配置在 168 次 rollouts 中仅通过不超过 46 次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理基准仅评测固定题目、孤立子任务或给定解法的论文复现，且很少在检验正确性的同时考察研究质量，无法反映智能体独立开展物理研究的真实能力。

Method: 构建 FrontierPhysics 基准：56 个任务由 42 位物理学家基于自身研究设计，覆盖理论、计算、实验物理九大领域；每个任务要求智能体完成文献综述、制定研究计划、开展研究与实验、产出结构化结果与科学论文手稿。评测采用确定性验证器与专家撰写的评分量表（rubric）相结合的双重机制。

Result: 在 22 个模型、26 种配置的系统评测中，没有任何配置在 168 次 rollouts 中通过超过 46 次。对智能体轨迹的人工审查表明，前沿智能体的主要短板在于推理不够严谨与专家直觉不足，而非物理知识欠缺或执行错误。

Conclusion: FrontierPhysics 为端到端物理研究能力提供了同时衡量正确性与研究质量的评测框架，揭示当前智能体尚不具备独立完成可发表级物理研究的能力；未来改进方向应聚焦严谨推理与领域直觉的培养，而非单纯扩充知识或提升工具执行可靠性。

Abstract: AI agents are playing an increasingly important role in physics research. However, existing physics benchmarks only evaluate fixed questions, isolated sub-tasks, or paper reproduction with given solutions, and rarely check research quality alongside correctness. We introduce FrontierPhysics, an end-to-end benchmark with 56 tasks made by 42 physicists based on their own research, covering nine domains across theoretical, computational, and experimental physics. Each task requires agents to review the literature, develop a research plan, conduct research and experiments, and produce structured outputs and scientific manuscripts. Combining deterministic verifiers with expert-authored rubrics, FrontierPhysics evaluates whether agents' research is correct and publishable. Across 22 models in 26 configurations, no configuration passes more than 46 of its 168 rollouts. Manual review of agent trajectories shows that frontier agents' main weaknesses are insufficiently rigorous reasoning and limited expert intuition, rather than gaps in physics knowledge or execution errors.

</details>


### [227] [BEAKER: An Expert-Curated Benchmark for Embodied Brains in Self-Driving Chemical Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06911)
*Fei Lin,Tengchao Zhang,Ziyang Gong,Xiaotong Yu,Bohan Zhang,Yifan Zhou,Qihao Yang,Ji Dai,Dong Li,Yining Jiang,Qinghua Ni,Jun Huang,Qiang Zhang,Yue Zhou,Zhihang Zhong,Yonglin Tian,Fei-Yue Wang,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BEAKER基准，评测MLLMs在自驱动化学实验室中的具身可执行能力，含1000条专家标注的11类图像/视频任务，28个模型的评测显示粗粒度流程理解可行但精细动作落地仍困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动化学实验室（SDCLs）要求机器具备感知、推理与受约束动作的具身能力，而现有实验室基准多聚焦安全、异常检测或通用科学理解，缺乏对"执行导向具身能力"的评估。

Method: 构建BEAKER基准：1000条经化学与具身AI专家人工创建与审核（无AI辅助生成/标注）的问答样本，覆盖11类图像与视频任务，涵盖可供性定位、轨迹规划、设备/状态/安全识别、装置搭建、操作与步骤推理、时序流程推理；在离线单轮设定下评测28个通用、科学、化学专用及具身MLLM。

Result: 模型能在视频中跟随粗粒度实验流程，但在将动作落地到可操作区域与可行轨迹、识别细粒度实验器材/状态/安全规范、以及对中等长度流程排序步骤方面表现薄弱。

Conclusion: BEAKER揭示了粗粒度流程理解与实验室执行所需的精确、动作落地型感知之间存在明显差距，为未来SDCLs中的MLLM提供诊断性基准。

Abstract: Self-Driving Chemical Laboratories (SDCLs) are moving chemical experimentation toward embodied automation, where machines must perceive laboratory scenes, reason about experimental states, and act under physical, procedural, and safety constraints. Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly considered as embodied brains for such systems, existing laboratory benchmarks mainly focus on safety, anomaly detection, or general scientific understanding, leaving their execution-oriented embodied capabilities underexplored. To fill this gap, we introduce BEAKER, a Benchmark for evaluating Embodied Actionability, Knowledge, Experimentation, and Reasoning in SDCLs. BEAKER contains 1,000 expert-curated question-answer samples across 11 image and video task types, covering key judgments required for chemical laboratory execution, including affordance grounding, trajectory planning, equipment, state, and safety recognition, apparatus setup, operation and step reasoning, and temporal procedure reasoning. All questions and annotations are manually created and reviewed by chemistry and embodied Artificial Intelligence (AI) experts, without AI-assisted generation or annotation. We evaluate 28 general-purpose, science-oriented, chemistry-specialized, and embodied MLLMs in an offline, single-turn setting. Results show that current models can follow coarse experimental procedures in videos, but still struggle to ground actions into operable regions and feasible trajectories, to recognize fine-grained laboratory apparatus, states, and safety norms, and to order steps in mid-length procedures. BEAKER thus reveals a clear gap between coarse procedural understanding and the precise, action-grounded perception required for laboratory execution, providing a diagnostic benchmark for future MLLMs in SDCLs.

</details>
